16
tamiḻ intiyā
tōṟṟuvāy
teṉṉintiyāviṉ nila amippu ulakiṟ paḻamiyuṭiyatu.1 ataṉ varalāṟum av vakiyiṉatē. ataṉ ārāycci kallil nār urittal pōṉṟa kaṭumiyuṭiyatātaliṉ ataṉi ātimutal oḻuṅkupaṭutti eḻutiṉār yārumilli.2 varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ ituvariyil teṉ ṉintiyāviṉ paḻiya varalāṟṟiṉi eḻuta muyaṉṟirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu māttiram kūṟalām. ulaka makkaḷiṉ paḻiya varalāṟṟu ārāycciyil teṉṉintiya pūrva varalāṟṟiṉi viḷakkum pakutikaḷ iṭiyiṭiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. av vaki ātāraṅkaḷiyum varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ āṅkāṅkuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷa kuṟippukaḷiyum tuṇikkoṇṭu in nūl variyappaṭalāyiṟṟu. varalāṟu kaṟpaṉik katipōlak kūṟattakkataṉṟāka liṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟṟa mēṟkōḷkaḷ kāṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
iyal - 1
toṉmik kālam
tamiḻ intiyāviṉ paḻiya nila amippu
nila nūlār paḻiyakālattu ip pūmiyiṟṟōṉṟiyirunta kaṇṭaṅ kaḷiyum kaṭalkaḷiyumpaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟapaṭi, kiḻakkut tīvukaḷ āstirēliyā intiyā āppirikkā mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷ oru perum nilapparappāka viḷaṅkiṉa. ap periya nilapparappiṟku avarkaḷ konvāṉā (Gondwana) eṉap peyar vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. mēṟkuttoṭarccimali konvāṉāk kaṇṭattik kiḻakku mēṟkākap pirittatu. arāvali malikaḷukku vaṭakkē kaṭal iruntatu. konvāṉāk kaṇṭattiṉ perumpākam ciṟitu ciṟitākak kaṭaluḷ maṟintapōtu intiyā āppirikkā kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷ eṉpaṉa kaṭalāṟ pirikkappaṭṭaṉa. irāta kumutmukkēci eṉpavar teṉṉintiyāviṉ paḻiya nila amippic curukkamākavum viḷakkamākavum kūṟiyuḷḷār. atu varumāṟu:
konvāṉā allatu lemūriyā
“nila nūlār konvāṉā eṉap peyariṭṭuḷḷa periya pūkaṇṭattiṉ miccamāka uḷḷatu intiya nāṭu. ap pūkaṇṭam teṉṉāppirikkā mutal āstirēliyā teṉ amerikkā variyil parantu kiṭantatu. iḵtu ap pakutikaḷil kallākac camintu kiṭakkum pirāṇikaḷ tāvaraṅkaḷiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷik1 koṇṭu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. mēṟkut toṭarccimali konvāṉāk kaṇṭattik kiḻakku mēṟkākap pirikkum mukaṭupōlamin tiruntatu. ātaliṉālēyē intiyāviṉ āṟukaḷ arāpikkaṭali nōkki uṟpattiyāki vaṅkāḷak kuṭākkaṭaluḷ vīḻkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭakkē iretēk2 kaṭal mattiya airōppā ciṉṉa āciyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷi mūṭip parantu kiṭantatu. intiyāvilē arāvali malikaḷ māttiram kaṭalip pārttu niṉṟaṉa. nīṇṭa kālattiṉpiṉ mali (imayam) eḻa ārampittatu. iretēk kaṭal mēṟku nōkkip piṉvāṅkiṟṟu3.” viññāṉattiṉ curukkam eṉṉum nūlil konvāṉāk kaṇṭattippaṟṟi aticayappaṭat takka nikaḻcci oṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu varumāṟu:
“airōppāvil nilakkari uṇṭāyirukkiṉṟa kālattil āstirēliyā teṉṉamerikkā intiyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil orē vakit tāvaraṅkaḷ vaḷarntaṉa; ātaliṉāl in nilaṅkaḷ ellām oṉṟucērntu konvāṉāk kaṇṭa māka viḷaṅkiṉa” eṉṟu muṭivu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟatu.1 pērāciriyar veciṉar2 putiya koḷki oṉṟi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār: avar kūṟuvatu, “ik kaṇṭaṅkaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu aṇmiyil iruntaṉa. aṇṭār ṭikkā, āstirēliyā, teṉṉame rikkā, intiyā mutaliya nāṭu kaḷit teṉṉāppirikkāvic cuṟṟip porutti vikkalām. kaṭaliṉ aṭiyiluḷḷa karuṅkaṟ pāṟi veṭittamiyāl ik kaṇṭaṅkaḷ viṭupaṭṭup paṉikkaṭṭi taṇṇīril mitantu celvatupōla akaṉṟu ceṉṟuḷḷaṉa. itu nila nūlār kūṟum 3aintāvatu ulakattil nikaḻntatu.” eṉpatākum.
tāvara uyir nūlār konvāṉā eṉṉum kaṇṭatti lemūriyā eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar.
paḻiya pūmi cāttiram
intiya makkaḷiṉ paḻaṅkatikaḷik kūṟum purāṇaṅkaḷum itikācaṅkaḷum pūmiyiṉ naṭuvē periya pūkaṇṭamoṉṟu iruntatik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. mirukēntira ākamattiṟ kūṟappaṭuvatāka mātavac civañāṉa yōkikaḷ civañāṉapōta māpāṭiyattiṟ kāṭṭiyuḷḷa pakutikaḷ cila ap periya pūkaṇṭattippaṟṟi ōraḷavu viḷakkuvaṉavākum. avi varumāṟu: ēḻu perun tīviṉ naṭuttīvāvatu nūṟāyiram yōcaṉip parappuṭittāy vaṭṭamāy nilamakaṭku untit tāṉamāyuḷḷa nāvalan tīvu........in nāvalattīviṉ naṭuvē mēruvari........mēruvariyic cūḻnta nilamākiya iḷāvirutam.........imayattiṟkum teṉ kaṇṭattiṟkum naṭukkaṇṭamākiya pāratavaruṭattil vāḻvōr..........ivaṟṟuṭ1 pāratavaruṭamum oṉpatu kaṇṭamāy intirattīvum, kacēruttīvum, tāmiraparṇit tīvum, kapatittīvum, nākattīvum, cāntiramattīvum, kāntaruvattīvum, vāruṇattīvum, kumarittīvu meṉap peyar peṟṟup pala malikaḷum pala natikaḷum palavēṟu vakippaṭṭa cātikaḷumuṭittāy vayaṅkum. iv voṉpatuḷḷum kumarikkaṇṭamoṉṟē vētākamavaḻakkum, cātivarampum, kaṅki mutaliya puṇṇiya tīrttaṅkaḷum, kāci mutaliya puṇṇiya talaṅkaḷumuṭittāyc ciṟantatu. ēṉi eṭṭuk kaṇṭaṅkaḷum milēccar vāḻiṭaṅkaḷām.
“pūmiyiṉ mēṟparappu ēḻu kaṭalum ēḻu peruntīvum naṭuvē mēruvariyu muṭittāyp pūlōkameṉap peyar peṟum. in nāvalan tīviṉaṭuvē mēruvari nūṟāyiram yōcaṉi aḷavuṭittāy vayaṅkum....” itu mirukēntirattiṟ kaṇṭatu.
mēru ulakukku naṭu
purāṇaṅkaḷum itikācaṅkaḷum paḻiya kaṭal nilaṅkaḷippaṟṟip piṟaḻak kūṟumāyiṉum mēru ulakukku mattiyil uḷḷatu eṉpati ellām orutaliyāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷum. putta ciṉa nūlkaḷ mēruvi mantaram eṉak kūṟum. pākavata purāṇamum kantapurāṇamum oru kālattil kāṟṟuk kaṭavuḷukkum mērumalikkum nērntapōril kāṟṟuk kaṭavuḷāl muṟittuk kaṭaluḷ vīcappaṭṭa mēruviṉ cikaramoṉṟē ilaṅki eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itu veppa nāṭukaḷil aṭikkaṭi nērum kāṟṟukkuḻappam oṉṟiyum ak kālattil mēruviṉ oru pakuti kaṭaluḷ maṟinta varalāṟ ṟiṉiyum kuṟikkumeṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē im mali pūmattiya irēkiyi aṭuttu ulakukku mattiyil iruntateṉat terikiṉṟatu. piṟkālattil im mali imayamaliyi aṭuttō ataṟku vaṭakkilō iruntateṉak karutappaṭṭatu. ki. mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭil viḷaṅkiya mekas tīṉas eṉṉum kirēkkar mērumali pāṇṭi nāṭṭil uḷḷateṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār.1
pāskarāccāriyar eḻutiya vāṉanūṟ kuṟippil pūmattiya irēki paḻiya ilaṅkikku ūṭākac ceṉṟateṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pūmattiya irēki cumattirāvukku ūṭākac celkiṉṟatu. ilaṅkip putta nūlkaḷ ippoḻutuḷḷa ilaṅki, muṉṉiya ilaṅkiyil paṉṉiraṇ ṭil oru pakuti eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ippoḻutu ilaṅkikku 400 kal tūrattiluḷḷa mālittīvu eṉappaṭum tīvukkūṭṭaṅkaḷ ilaṅkiyiṉ pakutiyāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu nilanūlār kūṟuvar. at tīvukaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ āstirēliya makkaḷukkum intiyāviṟ kāṇappaṭum cila makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉarukkum iṉamuṭiyavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.1 ceṅ kōṉṟaric celavu eṉṉum nūlil kaṭal koṇṭa nāṭṭiṉ cila mali, āṟṟu iṭappeyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuri taliccaṅkamirunta maturiyik kaṭal koṇṭateṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ paḵṟuḷiyāṟu, kumariyāṟu mutaliyavikaḷik kaṭal koṇṭateṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. aṭiyārkku nallār kumarimuṉikkut teṟkē kiṭantu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭa nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭukaḷik kuṟip piṭṭatōṭu an nilapparappu 700 kāvata2 muṭiyateṉavuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivikaḷ ellām oru kālattil intiyak kuṭānāṭṭiṉ amippu vēṟu vakiyil iruntateṉpati naṉku viḷakkuvaṉa.
kaṭal kōḷ
iv vulaka makkaḷ ellōrum oru periya kaṭal kōḷippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉarum atu tattam nāṭṭil nikaḻntateṉpar. ik kati ciṟiya ciṟiya vēṟupāṭukaḷuṭaṉ orē vakiyāka ellōrālum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē ulaka makkaḷ mattiya iṭa moṉṟiliruntu pirintu celvataṉ muṉ ik kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻntateṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.3
intiya makkaḷ kaṭalkōḷip paṟṟik kūṟum varalāṟu catapatap pirāmaṇattil (ki. mu. 1300) mutaṉmutaṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. vaṭamoḻi nūliṟ ṟaṉittu niṟkum katiyāyiruppatāl itu akkēṭiyariṭamiruntō tamiḻariṭamiruntō āriyarukkuk kiṭittateṉa varalāṟṟu nūlār kūṟu kiṉṟaṉar. catapatap pirāmaṇattilum purāṇaṅkaḷilum kūṟappaṭum veḷḷap perukkiṉ varalāṟu varumāṟu:
maṉu taṉatu kāli vaḻipāṭṭic ceytu koṇṭirukkumpōtu oru mīṉ avaratu kiyil vantatu. am mīṉ maṉuvi nōkki, “eṉṉik kāppāṟṟu nāṉ uṉṉik kāppēṉ; oru veḷḷap perukku ellā vuyirkaḷiyum aḻittuviṭum” eṉṟatu. avar mīṉi oru muṭṭiyuḷ viṭṭār. atu peritāka vaḷarntapōtu avar ataṉi oru kuḷattil viṭṭār. atu iṉṉum peritāṉapōtu avar atik kaṭaluḷ viṭṭār. piṉpu mīṉ avari oru tōṇi ceyyumpaṭi vēṇṭiṟṟu. maṉu appaṭiyē ceytār. veḷḷap perukku nērntatu. mīṉ taliyil oṟṟik kompuṭaṉ vantatu. maṉu kompil oru kayiṟṟik kaṭṭiṉār. mīṉ avari vaṭamalikkuk koṇṭu pōyviṭṭatu. mīṉ, “yāṉ uṉṉik kāppāṟṟi viṭṭēṉ, tōṇiyi marattiṟ kaṭṭu,” eṉṟu maṉuviṭaṅ kūṟiṟṟu.
māpāratattil iv varalāṟu vēṟu vakiyiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. viva cutamaṉu vicālavaṉattil tavañ ceytu koṇṭiruntār. appoḻutu mīṉ āṟṟaṅkariyil vantu maṟṟa mīṉkaḷip pārttut taṉṉik kākkumpaṭi kēṭṭatu. maṉu am mīṉi eṭuttuc cāṭiyil viṭṭār. piṉ mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. maṉu ēḻu iruṭikaḷuṭaṉum palavaki vitikaḷuṭaṉum tōṇiyil ēṟiṉār. mīṉ tōṇiyi imayamalikkuk koṇṭu pōyiṟṟu. at tōṇi oru marattiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. piṉpu am mīṉ iruṭikaḷi nōkki, “yāṉē piramā; yāṉ mīṉ vaṭiveṭuttu uṅkaḷ ellōriyum kāttēṉ,” eṉak kūṟiṟṟu.
pākavata purāṇattil iv viṭam teṉṉintiyāveṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. atil maṉu allatu cattiyavirataṉ tirāviṭa vēntaṉ eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. aṅku maṉu maliya maliyil ūṟṟeṭuttu varukiṉṟa kirutamāliyil (viyiyil) pali celuttumpōtu mīṉ avaruṭiya kiyil varukiṉṟatu.
purāṇaṅkaḷ paḻiya ñāpakaṅkaḷi ōraḷaviṟ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉa. iv varalāṟṟuḷ cila uṇmikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu maṟuttaṟkilli. purāṇaṅkaḷ piṟkālaṅkaḷil kūṭṭiyum tiruttiyum kuṟittum eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1
ulakamakkaḷiṉ ōr uṟpattikkuriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ
ki.mu. 4000 variyil ulakamakkaḷi ellām oru kūṭṭattil iṇippataṟkuriya ātāraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟkuriya iraṇṭu ātāraṅkaḷ moḻiyum, vaḻipāṭumākum. ki.mu. 4000 variyil kiḻakkut tīvukaḷ cīṉā amerikkā cumēriyā papilōṉ kirēttā cipiras mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttukkaḷ orē uṟpattiyic cērntaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. pirittaṉ tēcam mutal yappāṉ, amerikkā variyil cūriyattampa vaḻipāṭu (civaliṅkavaḻipāṭu) kāṇap paṭṭatu. iv viṭaṅkaḷilellām pāmpum iṭapamum paricuttamuṭiyaṉa vākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. iv viraṇṭu periya ētukkaḷāl ulakamakkaḷ oru peruṅ kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum pirintu āṅkāṅku vāḻntārkaḷ eṉpatu teḷḷitiṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatu. itaṟkātāramāka mēlnāṭṭu varalāṟṟāciri yarkaḷ ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷavi cilavaṟṟi īṇṭut tarukiṉṟōm.
“cempuk kālattil kiḻakkē mattiyatari mutal cīṉā variyil orē vaki nākarikam nilaviyateṉa vikrar kiṟittiyaṉ eṉpavar kūṟiyuḷḷār.”1
“nīlāṟu, tikiras, cintu, kaṅki, intu āciyā, ocēṉiyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṉ tōṇikaḷ maṟantu pōkappaṭṭa makkaḷiṉ nākarika iṇippi veḷiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa.”2 (ivi orēvaki amippuṭiyaṉa.)
“amerikkariṉ muṉṉōr āriyamakkaḷukku muntiyavarkaḷāṉa turāṉiya vakuppāriṉ moḻiyi vaḻaṅkiyōrāyiruttal vēṇṭum. at turāṉiyaruḷ mukkiyam vāyntōr tirāviṭar eṉap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷa makkaḷāvar.”
“amerikka cātikaḷiṉ paḻiyamoḻi oṭṭuccoṟkaḷi uṭiyatu. coṟkaḷil vēṟupaṭṭiruntapōtum ilakkaṇa amivil aḵtu intiyamoḻikaḷi ottirukkiṉṟatu. paḻiya coṟkaḷ maṟintu pōkap putiya coṟkaḷ pukuntirukkalām. āṉāl moḻi amippu orē vakiyāka irukkiṉṟatu. ivviṉattiyē āppirikka, amerikka,. pacipikkiya, yappāṉiya, kōṟiya, tirāviṭa, tāttāriya, piṉṉiya, turukkiya, pāsk moḻikaḷ cērntaṉa.”
“ārāycciyil amerikka cātikaḷum teṉṉintiyat tamiḻarum cemittiya āriya makkaḷiṉiṉṟum vaḻakkaṅkaḷāl vēṟupaṭṭa oru potukkūṭṭattiliruntu pirintārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu.”3
“pala teyvaṅkaḷuṭaṉ cūriyacantirak kaṭavuḷar tirāviṭa (turūyitiya - Druid)k kaṭavuḷarāvar. papilōṉilum cālaṭiyāvilum cūriyakkaṭavuḷ vēl mārtuk1 eṉappaṭṭār. cantiratteyvam istār.2 av vālayaṅkaḷil naṭaṉamātar iruntārkaḷ. periya kiriyikaḷ naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. intiya ālayaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum tēvatāci vaḻakku, tirāviṭa vaḻakkip piṉpaṟṟiyatu. istār ālayattic cūḻntu cōlikaḷ iruntaṉa. ak kaṭavuḷiṉ aṭiyāḷam maramāka iruntatu.”
“pakaṟ (Baal) kaṭavuḷum, palipīṭamum, iṭapamum (cūriyaṉik kuṟikkum) ciluvi allatu kaṟṟūṇiṉāl kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. cālaṭiyariṉ ki. mu. 6000kkum ki. mu. 5000kkum iṭippaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ takaṭukaḷil tiruviṭa (Druvada) tirāpaṭa (Drapada) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ peritum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. cālaṭiyar tamiḻarēyāvar.”3
“tirāviṭa iṭappeyarkaḷ mecapetēmiyā, īrāṉ mutaliya tēcaṅ kaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mittaṉi,4 eṉṉumiṭattil pēcappaṭṭa moḻi ik kālat tirāviṭa moḻiyuṭaṉ mikavum ottuḷḷatu”5
“cōṭs pēṇvūṭ eṉpavar acīriya kampaḷaṅkaḷum intiya kampaḷaṅkaḷum orē vakiyāyiruttalik kāṭṭi avarkaḷiṉ cematti yarukkum āriyarukkum muṟpaṭṭa potu uṟpattiyip paṟṟip piṉ varumāṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār:”
“teṉṉintiya ālayaṅkaḷiṉ amippum paḻiya acīriyariṉ kaṭṭiṭa amippum orēvakiyākavuḷḷaṉa. acīriyariṉ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷilum tāmari moṭṭum malarum pōṉṟa mātirikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. acīriya intiya ciṟpak kalikaḷukkiṭiyil atika oṟṟumi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivviru makkaḷum oru potukkūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum pirintavarkaḷātaliṉ ivvoṟṟumi kāṇappaṭalām.”6
“teṉṉintiya ālayaṅkaḷum, cīṉā yappāṉ (Torii) ālayaṅkaḷum orē aṭippaṭiyiṉ tōṟṟaṅkaḷākum. tiruttamuṟāta piyūciyar (Figians) ekiptiya intiya ālayaṅkaḷiṉ mātiriyic cariyākap piṉpaṟṟamāṭṭā miyāl kōpuraṅkaḷiṉṟik kōyilkaḷi amittirukkiṟārkaḷ.”
“kirēttā (Crete) papilōṉ kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷil 7caṅku vātti yaṅkaḷum muttu alaṅkāramum kavaṉikkattakkaṉa. poṉīciyar iv vaḻakkatti micīṉiyariṭamiruntu peṟṟirukkalām. piṟkālap pirāmaṇa putta vaḻakkaṅkaḷukku aṭippaṭiyāyirunta tirāviṭa vaḻakkaṅkaḷuḷ itu naṉṟāka vērūṉṟi yiruntatu. taṟkālattil ip paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷic cilar parmā intucīṉā, cīṉā, yappāṉ variyum koṇṭuceṉṟaṉar. piṉpu ivi pacipikkiya tīvukaḷukkuk koṇṭupōkappaṭṭaṉa. kaṭiciyil ivi amerikkak karikaḷi aṭintaṉa.”1
“cintu kiḻakkut tīvukaḷiṉ eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟuvatip paṟṟi aiyappāṭu illi. paḻiya intiya eḻuttu evvāṟu pacipikkut tīvukaḷukkuc ceṉṟatu eṉpati oruvarum collamuṭiyātu. marakkaṭṭi kaḷil eḻutappaṭṭa kiḻakkuttīvu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālam aṟiyappaṭa villi. avarkaḷiṉ eḻuttu muḻuvatum maṟakkappaṭṭatu. itē eḻuttukkaḷ intiya muttirikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉa pōlap paḻiya cumēriyā, cūcā, tikiracukkut teṟkē uḷḷa karit tīvukaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. varalāṟu aṟiyappaṭāta oru makkaḷiṉ nākarikam iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.”
“cumēriyar, intiya eḻuttukkaḷic cumēriyāvukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟa intiya viyāpārikaḷiṭamiruntu avikaḷi aṟintirukkalām. ap paḻiya eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiṉ pakutikaḷ kāppāṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi kaḷuṭ cila varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa cintu eḻuttukkaḷip pōla eṉakkut tōṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. cintu moḻi pēcappaṭukiṉṟapōtum eḻutappaṭu kiṉṟapōtum ataṉi muḻutum aṟintavarkaḷ cumēriyarkaḷēyāvar.”
“pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ ellām arappā mokañcatarō eḻuttuk kaḷiliruntu piṟantaṉa. pirāmi eḻuttit teṉ cemattiya poṉīciya eḻuttukkaḷōṭu iṇitta āciriyarkaḷ ōraḷavil cariyākavē kūṟi yuḷḷārkaḷ. ivikaḷum arappā mekāñcatarō eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiṉa eṉpataṟkup pōtiya ātāraṅkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.”2
“cintu eḻuttukkaḷukkum mēṟku āciya, kiṭṭiya kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷiṉ arikuṟiyāṉa cittira eḻuttukkaḷukkum tōṟṟattil oṟṟumi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cintu eḻuttukkaḷi ārāyntōr avi cumēriya, ellam, miṉōva, kittiti eḻuttukkaḷukku oṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa eṉpatik kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. iv veḻuttukkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi oṉṟē. ivikaḷiṉ potu uṟpattikkuriya kālam putiya kaṟkālamākalām. ōr eḻuttiyē ovvoru vakiyārum vevvēṟu vakiyil virutti ceytaṉar.”3
ecci vels (H. G. Wells) eṉṉum āciriyar ulakil iṉṟu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟa makkaḷ ellōrum tōṉṟuvataṟku aṭippaṭiyiluḷḷōr mattiyatarikkum kiḻakkut tīvukaḷukkum iṭiyē kiṭanta periya pūkaṇṭattilē tōṉṟip perukiya kapilaniṟa makkaḷeṉṟum, mattiyatari nāṭukaḷil ātiyil vaḻaṅkiyatu pāskku (Basqu) moḻi eṉṟum, pāsk moḻiyum tirāviṭamum neruṅkiya uṟavuṭiyaṉaveṉṟum kūṟuvar.
ṭākṭar pāsṭav (Dr. Basedow) āstirēliyā intiyā teṟku āppirikkā eṉṉum nāṭukaḷ tariyāl iṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉak kūṟuvar. avar kūṟuvatu, “uyir nūlār ak kaṇṭatti lemūriyā veṉ ṟum, nila nūlār konvāṉā veṉṟum peyariṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ik kaṇṭa mirunta iṭattilēyē nām āti makkaḷiṉ toṭṭilit tēṭa vēṇṭum,” eṉpatākum.1
iriveṭ (Revert) eṉṉum āciriyar, cumēriya ocēṉiya moḻikaḷ mattiyatari mutal amerikkā, yappāṉ, tasmēṉiyā variyum ceṉṟu in nāṭṭu moḻikaḷi iṇittaṉa eṉak kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2
ārampakālap pottakam3 eṉṉum nūlil, ekiptiya moḻic coṟ kaḷōṭu oṟṟumiyuṭiya pala mayōriya (niyūcīlantu) moḻic coṟkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tēyilar (Tailor) eṉṉum pātiriyār mayōriya moḻi tamiḻukku iṉamuṭiyatātali naṉku viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
cumēriyarum tamiḻarum
“cēryōṉ mārcalum ṭākṭar hālum cumēriya makkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷit tōṟṟattil ottavarkaḷ eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. itu cumēri yarum intiyarum oru toṭarpiluḷḷavarkaḷeṉpati valiyuṟuttukiṉ ṟatu. irumakkaḷiṉ tōṟṟappolivum, taliyi alaṅkāram ceyyum muṟiyum orēvakiyiṉa. arappāviṟ kiṭitta kuttuvāḷkaḷ cumēri yariṉ avvaki vāḷi ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. cumēriyar cintu makkaḷiṉ nīraruntum kiṇṇaṅkaḷ orē vakiyiṉa. cintu makkaḷiṉ uruḷi vaṭi vāṉa veḷḷip pāttiraṅkaḷ cumēriyariṉ kalnārp pāttiraṅkaḷ pōṉ ṟaṉa. irumakkaḷum vācaṉippoṭi mutaliya mēṉiyalaṅkārap poruḷ kaḷi vikkap payaṉpaṭuttiya cimiḻkaḷ orēvakiyiṉa. pūcceṇṭukaḷ pūmālikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttum vakiyum, vaḻipāṭṭil mirukaṅkaḷit toṭarpupaṭuttum muṟiyum iru nāṭukaḷukku mottaṉa. iru nāṭṭavar kaḷum vaṇṭi ceyyum muṟiyit taṉittaṉi kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷeṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. iru nāṭukaḷiṉ vīṭṭu amippukkaḷiyum, muttiri veṭṭum muṟiyiyum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ cemmiyiyum nōkku miṭattu mūvāyiramāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ cintumakkaḷ pāpilōṉiyari viṭa uyarnili aṭintiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu pulappaṭum. itu intiya nākarika kālattiṉ kīḻ elliyākum. ataṟku muṟpaṭṭa intiya nākarika kālattiṉ vīṟṟiṉāl papilōṉiya nākarikam tōṟṟiyiruttalkūṭātā? appaṭi yāyiṉ cumēriya makkaḷ cintu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu allatu ataṉ nākarika vāṭi yippeṟṟa iṉṉoru iṭattiṉiṉṟu ceṉṟavarkaḷākamāṭṭārkaḷā? iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa iru makkaḷukku miṭiyiluḷḷa oṟṟumikaḷ kula oṟṟumiyik kāṭṭuvaṉa. cintuvil uḷḷa cilar allatu avarkaḷ eṇṇaṅkaḷiṟ paḻakiya cilar, cilluḷḷa vaṇṭukaḷiyum kiḷiñcalkaḷi yum maṟṟum cumēriyaratu aḻakiya poruḷkaḷiyum teṟkup pāpilō ṉukkuk koṇṭupōyiruttal kūṭum.”1
“iṉṟu kāṇappaṭum intiyaṉ oruvaṉuṭiya mukaveṭṭu āyirak kaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ vāḻnta cumēriya makkaḷiṉ vaṭivam pōṉṟatu. ikkāla intiyar kirēkkar ittāliyarkaḷiṭiyē āriya rallāta makkaḷiṉ cāyal kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. uruvac cilikaḷiliruntu aṟiyumaḷavil paḻiya cumēriyar intiyāviluḷḷa tirāviṭa makkaḷi uṭalamippil ottiruntaṉar. ak kālac cumēriyaṉ tamiḻi allatu atu toṭarpāṉa moḻikaḷi vaḻaṅkum iṉṟiya oru tirāviṭaṉi mika ottiruntāṉ. cumēriyar kaṭal vaḻiyālum tarivaḻiyālum tikiras yūpirātas paḷḷattākkukaḷi aṭinta intiya makkaḷ eṉpatu irukkak kūṭātataṉṟu. ivarkaḷiṉ nākarikam intiyāvilē cintunatip paḷḷattākku kaḷil vaḷarcciyaṭintirukkalāmeṉṟu yāṉ eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ.”2
ulaka makkaḷiṉ paḻiya varalāṟṟu ārāycciyāḷar ulakiṉ āṅ kāṅku vatiyum makkaḷiṉ uṭaṟkūṟu, moḻi, vaḻipāṭu ākiyaṉa ottirut tali ivvāṟu āṅkāṅkuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivi makkaḷiṉ oru potu uṟpattiyiyum ciṟappākat tamiḻariṉ paḻamiyi yum naṉku viḷakkuvaṉa. maṉita iyalār (Anthropologists) ōr ātit tāy tantiyari ṉiṉṟē ulakamakkaḷ perukiṉārkaḷ eṉa valiyuṟuttuvar.
ulakilē mikap paḻiya maṉita elumpu yāvātēcattiṟ kiṭittatu. itaṉ kālam aintulaṭcam āṇṭukaḷ eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē viñ ñāṉikaḷ, pūmiyiṉ mattiyiṟkiṭanta periya kaṇṭattilēyē makkaḷ tōṉṟip perukiṉārkaḷ eṉat tuṇikiṉṟaṉar. avvāṟāyiṉ periya veḷḷapperukku, pūmattiyil viḷaṅkiya kaṇṭattil nikaḻntatākalām eṉak kūṟutal piḻiyākātu. periya kaṭalkōḷukkup piṉ ciṟiya kaṭal kōḷkaḷ pala nikaḻntaṉavākalām. paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kumarikkōṭu mutaliyavi kaḷik kaṭalkoṇṭa varalāṟukaḷ ivvakiyiṉavē. mattiya irēkiyi aṭutta nāṭukaḷil aṭikkaṭi erimalik kuḻappaṅkaḷ nērtalum ataṉāl tari kaṭaluḷ maṟintupōtalum iyalpu. 1883il 1karakkaṟṟōvāvil nērnta periya erimalik kuḻappamē itaṟkuc cāṉṟu.
makkaḷiṉ toṭṭil
kīṉ (Keane) eṉṉum āciriyar, maṉita kuṭumpattiṉ toṭṭil malāya āciyā (yāvā maṉitaṉ) vākavirukkalām eṉṟum iṅkiruntu makkaḷ vaṭakku āciyā mēṟku āppirikkā kiḻakku mēṟku ocēṉiya ulakam eṉpavikaḷukkuc ceṉṟu paraviṉārkaḷeṉṟum ak kālattil tarit toṭarpu iruntateṉṟum piṟkālaṅkaḷil nilaṅkaḷ kaṭaluḷ maṟintu curuṅki viṭṭaṉaveṉṟum kūṟuvar.
kīt (Keith) eṉṉum āciriyar, pattulaṭcam āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ tōṉṟiya yāvā maṉitaṉiṉiṉṟum vaḷarntu tiruntiyavarkaḷē āsti rēliya makkaḷeṉṟum ak kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum perukiyavarkaḷē ulaka makkaḷeṉṟum makkaḷiṉ uṟpattit tāṉattik kuṟitta tamatu ārāycci yiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.2
kirām villiyam eṉpār, intiya nāṭṭilē tolluyirkaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭintu perukiṉaveṉṟum maṉitaṉum aṅkut tōṉṟit tattaḷittu mēl nōkki vantiruttal kūṭum eṉṟum pukalvar.3
tamiḻ makkaḷ
tamiḻ makkaḷ piṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantu intiyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉār kaḷā, allatu intiyāviṉ pūrvakuṭikaḷā eṉpaṉa varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷāl ārāyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āti makkaḷiṉ kiḷiyiṉarāy ak kālappōkku kaḷilum koḷkiyilum māṟupaṭātōrāyt toṉṟutoṭṭu 4intiya nāṭṭil vāḻntu koṇṭiruppōrē tamiḻ makkaḷeṉpatu iṟṟi ñāṉṟi varalāṟṟu āciriyar ellōrālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu.
intiyāviṟ kaṟkālam
ulaka makkaḷ paḻiya kaṟkālattiliruntu paṭippaṭiyāṉa muṉṉēṟṟam aṭintirukkiṟārkaḷeṉa makkaḷ nūlār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. intiya nāṭṭil paḻiya kaṟkālam mutal ulōka kālam variyil makkaḷ kiyiṉāṟceytu payaṉpaṭuttiya āyutaṅkaḷum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
narumatip paḷḷattākkil aṭi aḻinta vilaṅkiṉ eṉpukaḷōṭu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa paḻiya kaṟkāla āyutattiṉ kālam 400,000 āṇṭu variyil eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.1
ṭākṭar ṭi. terā (Dr. De. Terra) eṉpavar intiya makkaḷiṉ paḻiya kaṟkālam ki. mu. 30,000-kkum ki. mu. 20,000-kkum iṭiyil eṉak kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷār.2
‘vintiya maliyil paḻiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ tīṭṭiya cittiraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅku vēṭar īṭṭikaḷāl kāṇṭāmirukattit tākkum cittiram oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. av vakiyāṉa kāṇṭā mirukam ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭavilli. kāymūr (Kaimur) maliyil māṉi vēṭṭiyāṭum cittiraṅkaḷum, koslaṅkapāttil oṭṭic civiṅkikaḷiṉ cittiraṅkaḷum, ciṅkaṉpūril kaṅkāru māṉ kutiri mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ cittiraṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ic cittiraṅkaḷ variyap paṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil putiya kaṟkāla āyutaṅkaḷum kiṭittaṉa. paḻiya kālattavarkaḷ piṇaṅkaḷik kāṭṭil eṟintārkaḷ.’
putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ piṇaṅkaḷip putittārkaḷ. putiya kaṟkālamakkaḷiṉ elumpukaḷ misrappūril (Mirazapur) miṉukkamāṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. kōlāppūr eṉṉum iṭattil 54 putiya kaṟkālac camātikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. avikaḷuḷ maṭpāṇ ṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. pallāvarattiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa īmattāḻikaḷip pōlac ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu, nellūr, āṟkāṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇap paṭṭaṉa. palavakik kaṟcamātikaḷ ceṉṉi, pampāy, micūr, nicām irācci yaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ivikaḷuḷ irumpu āyutaṅkaḷum kiṭittaṉa. ak kālattiṟ piṇaṅkaḷic cuṭutalum uṇṭu. tāḻikaḷil cāmpal kāṇap paṭavilli; uṭali veṭṭic cila tuṇṭukaḷ tāḻiyil iṭappaṭṭa putiya kaṟkālattil īmattāḻikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. āticcanallūril 114 ‘ēkkar’ nilattil 1,000 tāḻikaḷ kiḷaṟi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. āticcanallūr tiru nelvēli māvaṭṭattil tāmiraparaṇi āṟṟōrattiluḷḷatu. cila ulōkap poruḷkaḷ illāviṭil avi putiya kaṟkālattaṉavākalām. avikaḷuḷ irumpāyutaṅkaḷum veṇkala āparaṇaṅkaḷum kāṇap paṭṭaṉa.
pirāmaṇapāttu, cintu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum tāḻip putiyalkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
takkaṇattilum, teṉṉintiyāvilum paḻiya kaṟkālap poruḷkaḷ atikam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi pūkōḷak kuṟukku irēkikku irupat tintu pākiyiṉ kīḻk kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. akkāla makkaḷ oruvakip paḷiṅkuk kallāl (Quartzite) āyutañ ceytārkaḷ. ivvaki āyutaṅkaḷ kuṇṭūr, kaṭappā eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷil atikam kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṉum pala iṭaṅkaḷilum paḻiya kaṟkāla āyutaṅkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ippoḻutu aṭi aḻintupōṉa kāṇṭāmiruka elumpōṭu ‘kuvārṭ ciṭ’ (Quartizite) eṉṉum paḷiṅkuk kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa āyuta moṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. mircāppūril putiya kaṟkāla āyutaṅkaḷ pala kiṭittaṉa. kaṅkip paḷḷattākkil kuṉcippūr mākāṇattil mīṉ muḷḷāṟ ceyta tūṇṭiloṉṟu āṟiṭu maṇalmēṭṭil akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭṭatu.
paḻiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ kaṭappā, ceṉṉik karikaḷil māttiram vāḻntārkaḷ. putiya kaṟkālattil avarkaḷ intiyā muḻumiyilum parantu vāḻa ārampittārkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭiya āyutaṅkaḷ ciṟiya tīttaṭṭik kaṟkaḷ, avi ari aṅkulam mutal oṉṟu allatu oṉṟari aṅkula nīḷam variyil uḷḷaṉa. amputtali, villuppiṟi ivi iraṇṭum kalanta vaṭivamuḷḷa kallāyutaṅkaḷ piṭiyil iṟukkip payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. ivi atikamāka vintiyamali pākel kaṇṭ (Boghel Kand), rēvā (Rewah), mircāppūr malit tāḻvāraṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷ vāḻnta otukkiṭaṅkaḷil aṭuppukari, elumpukaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. uḷi pōṉṟa oruvaki āyutaṅkaḷ cūṭiya nākapuri acām parmā intuc cīṉam malāyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. paḻiya kaṟkāla āyutaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ teṉṉintiyāviṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyutaṅkaḷ pāṟikaḷil tīṭṭik kūrākkap paṭṭaṉa. 10 allatu 14 aṅkulam nīḷamuḷḷa kuṭiyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ivikaḷōṭu uyartara maṭpāṇṭa uṭivukaḷum ceṅkaṟ kaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. intiyāvilē kaṟcamātikaḷil kōppi aṭi yāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
veṇkalak kālam
teṉṉintiyāvil kaṟkālatti aṭuttu veṇkalak kālamum irumpuk kālamum iruntaṉa. takarattiyum cempiyuṅ kalantu veṇ kalañ ceyyappaṭṭatu. teṉṉintiya camātikaḷil aḻakiya veṇkalap poruḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. mika mukkiyamāṉa cempup poruḷkaḷ mattiya intiyāvil kaṅkēriyā (Gungeria) eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāṇap paṭṭaṉa. iṅku 424 cempu āyutaṅkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi ki.mu. 2000 variyil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉavākalām eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅku 102 veḷḷit takaṭukaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avikaḷil cila vaṭṭavaṭivāyuḷḷaṉa. cila takaṭukaḷiṉmītu kompuḷḷa māṭṭut talikaḷ variyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. veḷḷi intiyāvil atikam kiṭiyāmiyāl atu piṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantirukkalām. cempu intiyāviṟ kiṭikkak kūṭiyatu. iviyallāmal, cempiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa uḷi, eṟiuḷi, vāḷ, īṭṭittali mutaliyaṉavum kiṭittaṉa. ivi kōṉpūr vātelkār (Fatelgarh) maṇipuri muttā eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷilum vaṭa intiyā muḻumi yilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
teṟkil vāḻnta makkaḷē vaṭakkiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉar
ārāycciyāḷar, teṉṉintiyāvil vāḻntukoṇṭirunta paḻiya kaṟkāla makkaḷē mattiya intiyā kaṅki yamuṉi veḷikaḷ vaṭa mēṟku intiyā mutal imayamali variyil ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ki. mu. 4,000 variyil intiyā mutal pāracīkam ekiptu kirēttā cipiras variyil orē iṉamakkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ.1
arappā, makañcotarō
arappā mokañcotarō kālam intiyāviṉ veṇkalak kāla nākarikatti veḷiyiṭukiṉṟatu. in nakaraṅkaḷiṉ nākarikam ki. mu. 3500 variyil eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. nākarikam vaḷarvataṟku iṉi iṭam illi eṉṉumpaṭi akkāla nākarikam uyarnili aṭintiruntatu. nakaraṅkaḷ naṉṟāka amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. māṭiyuṭiya vīṭukaḷ kiṇaṟu kaḷōṭu kūṭiyaṉavākavum, nīr ōṭum kālkaḷ uṭiyaṉavākavum vītikaḷiṉ iru maruṅkukaḷilum kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. makkaḷ payircceyki vāṇikam eṉpavikaḷāl poruḷīṭṭiṉar. vāṇikam mecapetēmiyā variyil naṭipeṟṟatu. yāṉi oṭṭakam nāy āṭu māṭu mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷ vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa. villu, ampukaḷ, cempiṉālum veṇkalattiṉālum ceyta kōṭarikaḷ, īṭṭikaḷ, mayir maḻikkuṅkattikaḷ, kattikaḷ mutaliyaṉa payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam, yāṉittantam, kaṇṇāṭi ōṭukaḷ mutaliyavikaḷāl āparaṇaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. pañciṉāl āṭi neyyappaṭṭatu. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ aḻakākavum niṟaṅ koṭuttum vaṉiyappaṭṭaṉa. ōviya eḻuttup pōṉṟa oruvaki eḻuttu muttirikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. tāykkaṭavuḷ, cūriyaṉ, pacupati, iṭapam, maram mutaliyaṉa vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.
āriyar varuki
teṟku intiyāvilē mēṟkil tāṉyūp natikkum, kiḻakkil kāspiyaṉ kaṭalukkumiṭiyē vāḻnta veṇṇiṟamakkaḷ āriyar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ manti mēykkum vāḻkkippaṭiyi aṭin tiruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ tam mantikaḷuṭaṉum peṇṭu piḷḷikaḷuṭaṉum kūṭāraṅkaḷip peyarttukkoṇṭu iṭamiṭamākat tirivar. ivvāṟu tiriyum mārkkattil ivarkaḷil oru piriviṉar kipar kaṇavāy vaḻiyāka intiyāvi aṭintaṉar. itu ki.mu. 2000 variyil eṉṟu collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. ivarkaḷ intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟku mūlikaḷil taṅkic ciṟitu ciṟitāka ēḻu natikaḷ pāykiṉṟa pañcāp tēcam muḻuvatiyum kippaṟṟi ṉar. āriyar varuṅkāl aṅku uṉṉata nākarikattiṉarāy vāḻntukoṇ ṭirunta tamiḻ makkaḷippaṟṟi āriyamakkaḷ taṅ kaṭavuḷari vaḻuttip pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. muṟkālaṅkaḷil cīrtiruntiya cāti yiṉari muraṭṭuc cātiyiṉar paṭiyeṭuttu veṉṟārkaḷ. itu kirēkkari urōmar veṉṟatum, haṉs (Huns) eṉṉum muraṭṭuc cātiyiṉar āciyā, airōppā mutaliya nāṭukaḷik kalaṅkac ceytatum pōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷi ṉāl naṉku aṟiyalākum.
āriya makkaḷ tammi etirtta tamiḻari acurar, tittiyar, tācar, nākar eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar. vēta pāṭalkaḷāl tācukkaḷippaṟṟi aṟiyak kiṭappaṉa varumāṟu:
tācukkaḷ nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻntaṉar. an nakaraṅkaḷil aracar vāḻn taṉar. avarkaḷiṭattil tiraṇṭa celvam iruntatu. ac celvam pacu tēr kutiri mutaliyaṉa. ivi nūṟu katavukaḷuḷḷa kōṭṭikaḷuḷ vittuk kākkappaṭṭaṉa. tācukkaḷ mitāṉaṅkaḷilum malikaḷilum cottukkaḷi vittirunta celvap perumakkaḷ. avarkaḷ poṉṉāparaṇaṅkaḷi aṇintārkaḷ. tācukkaḷ poṉ veḷḷi irumpuk kōṭṭikaḷil vāḻntaṉar. intiraṉ taṉṉi vaṇaṅkum tēvōtācarkaḷiṉ poruṭṭu nūṟu kaṟkōṭṭikaḷilirunta tācarkaḷi veṟṟikoṇṭāṉ. tācukkaḷ āriyariṉ kaṭavuḷarkaḷi ēṟṟukkoḷḷavilli. avarkaḷ mikac cīr tiruttam peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. kiruṭṭiṇaṉ eṉṉum tācaṉ taṉ patiṉā yiram vīrarkaḷuṭaṉ intiraṉi etirttāṉ. tācukkaḷil pāṇiyar eṉṉum oru piriviṉar iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ. kaṭalvaḻiyākavum tarivaḻiyākavum vāṇipañ ceyta tamiḻnāṭṭu vaṇikar.
ki. mu. 2000kkum ki. mu. 1400kku miṭiyil āriya makkaḷ pañcāp muḻumiyum kippaṟṟiṉar. intiyāvi aṭinta āriyamakkaḷi viṭa aṅku vāḻnta tirāviṭa makkaḷ kūṭiya tokiyiṉarāyiruntaṉar. āriya makkaḷukkum tamiḻ makkaḷukkumiṭiyil vivākak kalappukaḷ nērntaṉa. vētapāṭalkaḷic ceyta muṉivarkaḷ palar-vaciṭṭar, akattiyar, vicuvā mittirar mutaliyōr-tamiḻ marapiṉar eṉpatu avarkaḷ niṟampaṟṟit teriya varukiṉṟatu. varuṇaṉ eṉṉum kaṭavuḷē acuraṉ eṉappaṭukiṉṟāṉ.1
pi.ti. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār ‘mantira kālattil intiya makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki’ eṉṉum nūlil kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
paṭi eṭuttuvanta oruciṟu kūṭṭattiṉarāl periya cātimāṟṟam ēṟpaṭṭirukka iṭamilli. tirāviṭa moḻiyip pēciya makkaḷ vēṇṭiya aḷavu cīrtiruttamuḷḷavarkaḷākavum putiyavarkaḷi viḻuṅkiviṭakkūṭiya tokiyiṉarākavum iruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ. tamatu toḻil vaḷarcci kaḷukkēṟpac ceḻippaṭintu kiṭanta tam coṟkaḷi avarkaḷukku utaviṉārkaḷ.2
moḻiyaḷavil tamiḻarkaḷ evvaḷavukku āriyarkaḷāṉārkaḷō, avvaḷavukku āriyar koḷkikaḷil tamiḻarkaḷāṉārkaḷ eṉac cilāṟṟar eṉṉum āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. intiya makkaḷiṉ araciyal vāḻkki varalāṟukaḷ orē vakiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
vēḷvikaḷ
paḻiya ulakamakkaḷ ellōrum uyirppaliyiṭṭuk kaṭavuḷari vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.3 āriyamakkaḷ alintu tiriyum vāḻkkippaṭiyil irun tamiyiṉ avarkaḷ taṅ kaṭavuḷarukku niliyāṉa kōyilkaḷ amittup paliyiṭṭu vaḻipaṭavilli. ceṉṟa ceṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil kaṭavuḷik kuṟittup paliyiṭṭaṉar. ituvē maṟṟiya makkaḷiṉ vēḷvikaḷukkum āriya makkaḷiṉ vēḷvikaḷukkum vēṟṟumi. piṟkāla makkaḷ, vēḷvikaḷ allatu yākaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭuvaṉa āriyamakkaḷukku māttiram uriyaṉa veṉṟu taṭumāṟuvārāyiṉar. āriyar periya irācciyaṅkaḷik kōliya piṉ iv vēḷvikaḷ mikavum āṭamparamāka naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. ak kālaṅ kaḷil vēḷvikaḷ puriyum kiriyi muṟikaḷ (pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ) variyap paṭṭaṉa. ikkiriyi muṟikaḷip payiṉṟu avaṟṟil vallunarāṉōr pirāmaṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. piṟkālaṅkaḷil ip peyar, kōyiṟ kurukkaḷ purōkitariyum kuṟikka vaḻaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl pirāmaṇar eṉpōr āriyamakkaḷ eṉṉum eṇṇam palar karuttil patintuḷḷatu. iṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭiṟ kāṇum kōyiṟ kurukkaḷum purōkitarum muṟkālattil ekiptiya pāpilōṉiya nāṭukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa kurumār pōṉṟavarkaḷē.
pirāmaṇa kālam
ki.mu. 1400kkup piṉ āriyar (kalappu āriyar) kiḻakku nōkkic ceṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa tamiḻar irācciyaṅkaḷi veṟṟi koṇṭaṉar. ak kālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ yākaṅkaḷi āṭamparamākac ceyya ārampittaṉar. ataṉ poruṭṭu yākakkiriyi muṟikaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa.1 kaṅki āṟṟu veḷikaḷiluḷḷa makkaḷum āriya makkaḷum vivākak kalappāl oṟṟumi yaṭintu valuvaṭintaṉar. pirāmaṇaṅkaḷukkuppiṉ āraṇiyaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. āraṇiyaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa kāṭṭil vācañ ceyyumpōtu paṭittaṟkuriya pāṭalkaḷ. vēta pāṭalkaḷ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa kālattil kāṭṭiṟpōyt tavañceyyum vaḻakkuk kūṟappaṭavilli eṉṟum, iv vaḻakkup pūrva kuṭikaḷiṉ muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟi āriya makkaḷāṟ kik koḷḷappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar.1
pāratam
āriyar kaṅkic camaveḷikaḷil irācciyaṅkaḷit tāpitta kālattilēyē pāratappōr nikaḻntatu. itu ki. mu. 1300 variyil nikaḻntateṉap perumpālum ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu. pāratap pōril kalantu koṇṭavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷē. puṟanāṉūṟṟil tarumaṉip pukaḻntu pāṭiya pāṭal oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. viyācar, aruccuṉaṉ, turupati, kiruṭṭiṇaṉ mutalāṉōr kariya niṟattiṉarākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. cila peṇkaḷ poṉṉiṟa muṭiyavarkaḷākac collappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.2 veṇ ṇiṟamuṭiyavarāka yārum kūṟappaṭavilli.3
pāratam kālattukkuk kālam eḻutic cērkkappaṭṭa pala pāṭal kaḷuṭiyatu. itu yavaṉar pallavarkaḷip paṟṟiyum kūṟukiṉṟatu. alak cāntariṉ paṭiyeṭuppukkup piṉṉarē intiyar yavaṉarippaṟṟi aṟin tārkaḷ. itil ki. pi. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil cērkkappaṭṭa pāṭalkaḷ uṇṭu eṉpatu varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷatu kūṟṟu.
irāmāyaṇam
āriyar kaṅkic camaveḷikaḷi aṭintapiṉpu nikaḻntatākak kūṟappaṭum varalāṟē irāmāyaṇam.4 itu ki. mu. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil viḷaṅkiya vāṉmīkarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭateṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. irāmāyaṇak kati1 cātakakkatikaḷil vēṟu vakiyākak kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. vāṉmīki irāmāyaṇattil irāmar irāmēcuvarattil iliṅkam vittup pūcitta varalāṟu kāṇappaṭavilli. irāmar kariya niṟattiṉarākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟār. teṉṉāṭṭu varalāṟoṉṟiṉi eṭuttuc cōṭittu vāṉmīkar irāmāyaṇamāka eḻutiṉārō eṉṟu cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. cīti yiṉ pitāvākak kūṟappaṭum caṉakaṉ ki. mu. 1000 variyil viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. ic caṉakaṉē yākñavalkiyarukku uṇmi ñāṉatti upatēcittavaṉ. ivaṉ ak kālattil kalviyiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. in nūlil pala kālaṅkaḷiṟ pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷ cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vāṉmīki irāmāyaṇat til irāmar viṣṇuviṉ avatāramākak koḷḷappaṭṭilar.
upaniṭata kālam
āriya makkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu kaṭavuḷukku oṉṟik koṭuttut tamakku vēṇṭiya oṉṟippeṟum koḷkiyaḷavil iruntatu. avarkaḷ ulakam uyir kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum mupporuḷ uṇmikaḷippaṟṟi aṟiyātirun taṉar.2 maṟupiṟappippaṟṟi avarkaḷ vētaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭavilli. iṟanta piṉ uyirkaḷ nērē kūṟṟuvaṉulakukkuc ceṉṟu aṅku neṭuka vāḻn tirukkum eṉa avarkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. āriyar intiya nāṭṭi aṭiva taṉ muṉṉarē tamiḻ makkaḷ mupporuḷ uṇmikaḷi naṉku ārāyntu aṟintiruntārkaḷ. av vuṇmi ñāṉaṅkaḷiṉ veḷiyīṭākiya upaniṭa taṅkaḷē tamiḻarkaḷatu ñāṉamākum. itaṉi ārāycciyāḷar ellōrum ēṟṟukkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.
upaniṭataṅkaḷil ciṟappittuc collappaṭum yākñavalkiyar intiyāviṉ kiḻakkuppākaṅkaḷil vāḻntavarākalām. ivar eḻutiyuḷḷa nūlkaḷ kuru pāñcālam kōcalam vitēkam eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. yākñavalkiyar vāḻnta iṭam ak kālattil kalvit tuṟiyil uyarntu viḷaṅkiṟṟu. aṟivup poruḷit tēṭi aṅkup palar vantārkaḷ. yākñavalkiyar kalvi kaṟṟapiṉ uṇmi ñāṉattit tēṭi iṭaṅkaḷtōṟum alintu tirintār. ivarkaḷuṭaṉkūṭat tirintavarkaḷ cuvētakētu aruṇēyaṉ cōmacusma cattiyañāṉi eṉpōr. ivarkaḷ kalviyiṟ ciṟanta caṉakaṉ eṉṉum vitēkanāṭṭu aracaṉik kaṇṭārkaḷ. avaṉ viṉāviya kēḷvikaḷukku yākñavalkiyar ēṟṟa viṭiyaḷittār. caṉakaṉ avarukku nūṟu pacukkaḷit tāṉam vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. caṉakaṉ, tēril ēṟic celvataṉ muṉ taṉakkum avarkaḷukkum uṇmiñāṉa aṟivil evvaḷavu vēṟupāṭu irukkiṟateṉpati viḷakkiṉāṉ. itu pirāmaṇar kaḷākiya avarkaḷukku avamāṉamāka viruntatu. yākñavalkiyar caṉaka ṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟu avaṉiṭam ariya ñāṉaṅkaḷip payiṉṟār. avar kālat til avarē periya ñāṉiyāka viḷaṅkiṉār. yākñavalki ceyta1 upaniṭa tamē ñāṉa nūlkaḷuṭ ciṟantatu. yākñavalkiyar kālattil pirāmaṇar cattiriyariṭattil māṇākkarāka amarntu ñāṉam kaṟṟaṉar. caṉakaṉip pōlavē kāci aracaṉāṉa acantakētu, yivāli aracaṉ piravākaṉaṉ mutaliyōr viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ.2 yākñavalkiyar kālam ki. mu. 1000 variyil eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. ki.mu. 1000 variyil vintamalikku vaṭakkē tamiḻ moḻi vēṟu moḻikaḷākat tirintu vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.3 ākavē, ak kālam tamiḻ ulakattiṉ vaṭakkelli vintiya maliyāyiruntatu. vintiya malikku vaṭakku āriyavartta meṉappaṭṭatu.
pēccu vēṟupaṭuvataṉ kāraṇam
vīṭṭiṟ pēcuvatu pōla nām capikaḷiṟ pēcuvatilli. iv vaki yāṉa pēccukaḷum oru kūṭṭattiṉariṉ pēcci uṇṭākkum (māks mūlar). vēṟṟumoḻic coṟkaḷ vantu oru moḻiyuṭaṉ kalappatālum iru vēṟumoḻikkuriyavarkaḷukkiṭiyil vivākakkalappu uṇṭākic cantati perukuvatālum moḻikaḷ māṟupaṭum.
moḻikaḷi ilakkaṇa amippukkoṇṭu aṟiyalām. moḻikaḷ māṟiviṭiṉum ilakkaṇam ilakuvil māṟupaṭātu. ilakkaṇam āṟṟiṉ irumaruṅkumuḷḷa aṇiyum, coṟkaḷ avikaḷiṉiṭiyē ōṭum nīrum pōṉṟaṉa. tamiḻiṉ vaḻimoḻikaḷ tamiḻic cērntaṉa eṉṟu kāṭṭu vataṟku am moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷiviṭa ilakkaṇam utavipuri kiṉṟatu. oru moḻiyit tāṅki niṟpatu ilakkaṇam.
ippoḻutu vaḻaṅkum vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ amippil tamiḻi ottuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu moḻiyārāycciyāḷar nuvalkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē ak kālattil vaṭamoḻi ik kālam evvāṟu vaḻakkilirukkiṉṟatō avvāṟu ilakkiya moḻiyāka vaḻakkiliruntiruttal vēṇṭum; pēccumoḻi vēṟāka iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉa yūkikka vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu.
pirākirutam tamiḻ mayamākkappaṭṭatu
āriyar intiyāvi aṭinta kālattil avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya moḻi paḻiya pāracīkam pōṉṟa āriyam. itaṟku ilakkaṇa varampu illi. im moḻiyilēyē vēta pāṭalkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
“vēta pāṭal ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa kālattil aṅku ellōrālum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa oru vīṟuṭiya moḻi iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. am moḻi1 pirākirutattiṉiṉṟum uccarippu muṟiyil vēṟupaṭṭatu. vēta pāṭalkaḷil pirākiruta uccarippiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭa pala coṟkaḷ āḷappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ic coṟkaḷ maṟṟa moḻiyiṉiṉṟum kaṭaṉ vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpativiṭa vēṟu vakiyākak kūṟamuṭiyātu”2
“mantira kālattil mūṉṟu iṉamoḻikaḷ intiyāvil vaḻaṅkiṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ vētamoḻiyākiya intu cermaṉiya moḻi oṉṟu. iraṇṭāvatu, vētamoḻiyōṭu amippil māṟupaṭṭa tirāviṭa moḻi. itu, muṉṉatiṉ amippu, coṟkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟip peritum vēṟupaṭuttiṟṟu. āriyamoḻi, intiyāvil vaḷarcci yaṭintapōtu tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ valimiyāl pala māṟutalkaḷaṭintu tirāviṭa muṟikaḷit taḻuviṟṟu.”
“tirāviṭa makkaḷ poruḷkaḷukkum kalikaḷukkum iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷ ellām tamiḻ. avi piṟa moḻiccoṟkaḷalla. ik karuttu iṉṉum naṉku ārāyappaṭavilli. ārāycciyil camakkiruta moḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum kaliccoṟkaḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟum iraval vāṅkap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu pulappaṭutal kūṭum. intu cermaṉiya moḻiyākiya pirā kirutam intiyāvil vaḻaṅka ārampittapōtu ataṉ a, e, o eṉṉum eḻuttukkaḷ ellām a pōlāyiṉa; ataṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum ciṟitu ciṟitākat taṇivupaṭṭaṉa.”1
mantira kālattil āriyar
vintiyattukkut teṟkē kuṭiyēṟavilli
mantirakāla nūlkaḷ ak kālattil viḷaṅkiya nāṟpatu kūṭṭat tiṉarippaṟṟi māttiram kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avi vintiyamalikkut teṟkē uḷḷavarkaḷippaṟṟik kūṟavilli. ak kālattil teṉṉintiyāvukkum vaṭa intiyāvukkum pōkkuvarattiruntami mantiraṅkaḷil muttip paṟṟik kūṟappaṭutalāl aṟiyalām.2
puttar kālam (ki. mu. 557-477)
upaniṭata ñāṉam vaḷarcciyuṟṟa kālattil puttar tōṉṟiṉār. ivar tirāviṭa marapiṉar.3 ivaratu ñāṉamum upaniṭata ñāṉaṅkaḷi aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭatu. ivar vētamatattukku māṟāṉa puraṭciyik koṇṭu vantār. ivar tam upatēcaṅkaḷi makata moḻiyiṟ1 (pāḷi) ceytār. ākavē, puttar kālattil pāḷimoḻi makkaḷiṉ pēccumoḻiyāka irun tiruttalvēṇṭum. pāḷi puttar kālattukku iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷiṉ muṉ tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum. puttamatam kaliṅkanāṭṭil mikavum vēkamākap paraviṟṟu. ākavē, aṅkup pāḷimoḻi atikam payilap paṭṭatu. ataṉāl aṅku vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻ citintu vaṭuku allatu teluṅku āyiṟṟu. ilaṅkip puttamakkaḷ kaliṅka tēcak kalikaḷip piṉpaṟṟiṉar. aṅku vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻum pāḷi moḻik kalappiṉāl ciṅkaḷa māka māṟiṟṟu.2 makkaḷ emmoḻiyic camaya moḻiyākak koḷḷukiṟār kaḷō, am moḻic coṟkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ moḻiyiṟ kalantu moḻiyi vēṟupaṭutti viṭukiṉṟatu. peruntēvaṉār pāratattiṟ kāṇappaṭum maṇippiravāḷa naṭiyum itaṟku utāraṇamākum. tamiḻmakkaḷ tamiḻ uṇarcciyiṉāl āriyatti etirttup pōrāṭivantamiyiṉ, tamiḻ vēṟu moḻiyākac citintu viṭāmal piḻittatu āyiṉum, ataṉ ōraṅkaḷ kaṉṉaṭam teluṅku tuḷu maliyāḷam eṉa māṟupaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟiya tamiḻ moḻiyil vaṭamoḻik kaṟi atikam ēṟiyiruttali nām aṟiyalām. puttar kālattiṟkup piṉ kaliṅka nāṭu vaṭuku nāṭāka māṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. itu ki. mu. nālām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil eṉa uttēcamākak kūṟalām. tolkāppiyam ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭak kelli vaṭavēṅkaṭam eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu “centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum” eṉappaṭukiṉṟamiyāṉum ap paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷum cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷukkuṭpaṭa viruntamiyāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. ākavē tolkāppiyam ki. mu. nālām nūṟṟāṇṭukkuc ciṟitu muṉpiṉ ceyyappaṭṭiruttal kūṭum. “mali tēyntu maṇṇāṅkaṭṭiyāṉatu” eṉpatu nāṭṭup paḻamoḻi. ati oppak kumari mutal imayam variyil parantu kiṭanta tamiḻ nāṭṭip piṟamoḻikaḷ kavarntu koḷḷa iṉṟu tamiḻ intiyak kuṭānāṭṭiṉ ciṟiyavelliyuḷ māttiram vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ataṉ ōraṅkaḷiyum ataṉ vaḻimoḻikaḷākiya teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam hinti mutaliya moḻikaḷ ciṟitu ciṟitākak kavarntu varukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi vaḻaṅkum makkaḷiṉ eṇ 63,000,000 eṉak kaṇak kiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻ vaḻaṅkuvōriṉ eṇ 19,000,000.

iyal - 2
āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻar nākarikam
iṉṟu intiya makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum piṟavum paḻiya tamiḻar nākarikatti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭaṉaveṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. caṅka kālat tamiḻar nākarikam kalappillāta paḻantamiḻ nākarikamēyākum. oru moḻiyiluḷḷa paḻiya coṟkaḷ am moḻik kuriya makkaḷiṉ paḻiya nākarikatti naṉku viḷakkuvaṉa. arappā mokañ cotarō eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa paḻam poruḷkaḷikkoṇṭu aṅku vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟip paḻamporuḷ ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. oru moḻiyiluḷḷa ovvoru paḻiya collum ov voru paḻamporuḷukku nēr. pāri eṉṉum col kallāl ceyyap paṭṭa kiṇṭum karuviyik kuṟittatu. ap peyarē iṉṟu irumpu mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa avvakik karuviyik kuṟikka vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu. ulōkattippaṟṟi aṟiya muṉmakkaḷ kallāṟ ceyta pāriyip payaṉpaṭuttiṉārkaḷ eṉap pāri eṉṉum col aṟivikkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu paṇṭiyōr tāmaṟinta poruḷkaḷukkum, ceyalkaḷukkum iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ avarkaḷatu varalāṟṟiṉik kūṟuvaṉavākiṉṟaṉa. āriyar varukikku muṉ tamiḻar nākarikam evvāṟiruntateṉpatu im muṟiyil aṟintu kūṟutal iyaṉṟatu.
nilappirivum makkaṭ pirivum
ivvulakam maliyum kāṭum vayalum kaṭaṟkariyum pālinilamu meṉap palavaki iyaṟkip pirivukaḷuṭiyatu. ivvakip pirivukaḷ tamiḻar vāḻnta 1intiya nāṭṭilum uṇṭu. ovvoru nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷum vevvēṟu vaki vāḻkki naṭattiṉar. mali kāṭu vayal kaṭal eṉpaṉavum, avi cārnta nilaṅkaḷum kuṟiñci mulli marutam neytal eṉṉum peyarkaḷ peṟṟaṉa. maṇalveḷi pāli eṉappaṭṭatu.
an nilaṅkaḷil vāḻntōr muṟiyē kuṟavar iṭiyar uḻavar paratavar maṟavar eṉappeyar peṟṟaṉar. nilam paṟṟiya ip piriviyaṉṟi antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar aṭiyōr eṉat toḻiṟ pākupāṭu paṟṟiya piriviṉarum vāḻntārkaḷ. viṉivalar pala toḻil purivōrā yum, aṭiyōr kālvaḻiyākat toṇṭu purivōrāyu miruntaṉar. aṭimi kaḷi viṟṟu vāṅkum vaḻakkup paṇṭi nāṭkaḷil ellā nāṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭatu. cuntaramūrtti cuvāmikaḷ varalāṟum ariccantiraṉ katiyum itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷākum. viṉivalar aṭiyōr eṉṉum iru piriviṉarum kīḻōreṉavum ēṉiyōr mēlōreṉavum aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar.
maṟavar
ivarkaḷ eyiṉar vēṭar puḷiñar eṉavum kūṟappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ alintu tiriyum tiruntāta vāḻkkiyar; maṇal veḷikaḷiṉ iṭi yiṭiyē kiṭakkum paṟṟikaḷil taṅki vēṭṭiyāṭiyum, vaḻiccel vōri vaḻipaṟittum, mulli nilattē ceṉṟu pacukkaḷik koḷḷi yaṭittum vāḻunar. tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ip pālinila makkaḷiṉ koṭiya ceyalkaḷ palavāṟu varuṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avikaḷiṟ kūṟap paṭṭavikaḷiṉ curukkam varumāṟu: maṟavar cemmaṟikkaṭāviṉ kompu pōṉṟu muṟukkiya mīciyum puliyiṉ mīci pōṉṟa tāṭiyumuṭiyar; neruppil nīr viṭṭāṟpōṉṟa niṟattiṉar; cerupput toṭṭa kāliṉar; ippiyum cōkiyum kalantu kōttu aṇinta kaḻuttiṉar; muṟukiya caṭi yiṉar; villum ampum koṇṭa kiyiṉar; vaḻippōkkari etirppārt tirukkum iyalpiṉar; taṟukaṇmiyē urukkoṇṭālaṉṉa koṭumiyar; ivarkaḷ vaḻiccellum vaṇikar kūṭṭattavariṉ talikaḷik koytu avarkaḷiṉ viliyuyarnta paṇṭaṅkaḷik koḷḷiyiṭuvar; ap paṇṭaṅkaḷic ciṟu vilikku viṟpar; vaḻipōkkariṉ kaṭṭuc cōṟṟip paṟittuc cīli uṟiyiṟ pattirappaṭuttuvar; vaṇikariṭam koḷḷi koṇṭa peṇkaḷaṇiyum mēkalāparaṇaṅkaḷiyum acōkan taḻiyiyum uṭuppar. kiyiṟpaṭṭa vaḻippōkkari āṭeṉa niṉintu kāḷikku veṭṭip paliyiṭuvar.
arampōṉṟa kaṟkaḷuṭiya pāli nilattē iṟantavarkaḷi iṭṭu mūṭiya kaṟkumpaṅkaḷ pala kiṭakkum. taṭpak kālattum vemmi tōṉṟum avviṭattu vaṟanta cuṉikaḷum āṟukaḷum kūvalkaḷum kāṇappaṭum.1
maṟavariṉ kuṭil vīṭukaḷ eliyum aṇilum nuḻiyātapaṭi īntiṉ ōlikaḷāl vēyappaṭṭirukkum. maṟappeṇkaḷ pāriyikkoṇṭu eṟumpup puṟṟukaḷik kiṇṭip peṟṟa pullariciyi muṟṟattē kiṭakkum nila uraliṟ ṟīṭṭi, kiṇaṟṟu nīri moṇṭu pāṉiyil ulivārttu ākkiya cōṟṟi uppukkaṇṭattōṭu tēkkiliyil uṇpar.
maṟavar kūṭṭamāka vāḻum iṭaṅkaḷ paṟantali eṉappaṭum. ik kuṭiyiruppu ūkampullāl vēynta matilāṟ cūḻappaṭṭirukkum. tali vāyilil ampuk kaṭṭum paṟiyum tūṅkum; vāyiliṭattē caṅkiliyiṟ kaṭṭiya, nāy niṟkum. in nilattukkut teyvam kāḷi. koṟṟavi vinti, kāṭukiḻāḷ eṉpaviyum kāḷiyiṉ maṟupeyarkaḷ. kāḷi eṉpatu kaṟuppi eṉṉum poruḷ tarum peyar. kāḷikku narapaliyum piṟa uyirppaliyu miṭappaṭum.1
kuṟavar
maliyil vāḻum kuṟavar alivatum aliyātiruppatumākiya vāḻkkiyiṉar. avarkaḷ villum ampum koṇṭu vēṭṭiyāṭiṉar; vēṭṭiyāṭik koṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷiṉ tōli uṭuttiṉar. avar makaḷir maravuri taḻi uṭikaḷi aṇintaṉar. ākavē, maṉṟaṟkālaṅkaḷil maṇa makaṉ maṇamakaḷukkut taḻikaḷālum pūkkaḷālum kaṭṭiya uṭikaḷik koṭukkum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itu maliyāḷattil muṇṭu koṭukkum vaḻakkamāka iṉṟum nilavukiṉṟatu; maṟṟiya nāṭukaḷil kūṟi koṭukkum vaḻakkamāka nilipeṟukiṉṟatu. makaḷir mūṅkili vārntu kūṭi muṭinta ṉar. it toḻil avarkaḷiṭiyē iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. avar kaḷiṉ kulateyvam murukaṉ. murukakkaṭavuḷ ātikālam mutal mali uccikaḷil vittu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭār. muruka vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya āṭal vēlaṉ āṭal allatu veṟiyāṭṭu eṉappaṭum. muruka vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya ivvāṭal murukapūci ceyvōṉāl āṭappaṭṭatu. murukakkaṭavuḷip pōla vēlik kiyil vittiruttalāl avaṉum vēlaṉ eṉappaṭ ṭāṉ. tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi eṉṉum caṅka nūlil kuṟamātu muruka vaḻipāṭu ceyyum muṟi naṉku kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu varumāṟu:
maliyiṭattuḷḷa akaṉṟa nakariṭattē kuṟamātu kōḻikkoṭiyi naṭṭu ataṟku neyyum veṇ ciṟu kaṭukum appuvāḷ; vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya mantiraṅkaḷi uccarittu aḻakiya malarkaḷit tūvuvāḷ; māṟupaṭṭa iraṇṭu niṟamuṭiya paṭṭāṭikaḷi uḷḷoṉṟum puṟa moṉṟumāka uṭuppāḷ; civanta nūlik kāppākak kiyiṟ kaṭṭuvāḷ; veṇpori kaḷit tūvuvāḷ; irattattōṭu kalanta veḷḷariciyip paliyāka iṭuvāḷ; cevvalari mālikaḷiyum piṟa mālikaḷiyum orē aḷavāka naṟukki aciyumpaṭi tūkkuvāḷ; malippakkattē uḷḷa ūrkaḷip paciyum piṇiyum pakiyum varuttātoḻika eṉa vāḻtti naṟiya tūpaṅ kāṭṭuvāḷ; maliyiṭattu vīḻkiṉṟa aruviyōṭu vāttiyaṅkaḷ cērntu olikkac civanta malarmālikaḷit tūvi utiramaḷinta tiṉiyip parappi murukakkaṭavuḷ makiḻumpaṭi vaḻipāṭu ceyvāḷ.
murukaṉāṟ kuṟinērnta peṇkaḷukku muruka pūci ceyyum vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭi āṭṭukkuṭṭiyip paliyiṭṭu av virattattit toṭṭu avaṉ neṟṟiyiṟ ṟaṭavuvatum vaḻakku.
kuṟiñci nilattippaṟṟiyum an nilamakkaḷip paṟṟiyum caṅkanūl kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉa cila varumāṟu:-
1maliyiṭattē palāvum vāḻiyum ceḻittu vaḷarum. kuṟavariṉ vīṭukaḷ pullāl vēynta ciṟu kuṭicikaḷ. vīṭṭiṉ muṟṟattē vēṅki maramum palāvum niṟkum. kuṟavar ammaraṅkaḷiṉ kīḻ iruntu tēṭkaṭuppup pōṉṟa nāṭpaṭṭa kaḷḷik kuṭittu, cantaṉakkaṭṭi viṟakāṟ cuṭṭa iṟicciyi yuṇṭu makaḷirōṭu kuraviyāṭuvar. mali mukaṭukaḷilē tēṉkūṭukaḷ toṅkum. kuṟavar uyarnta mūṅkil ēṇikaḷic cārtti ēṟi at tēṉ kūṭukaḷi aḻittut tēṉ koḷvar. irākkālattē vēṭṭi yāṭac ceṉṟa kuṟavaṉ pakaṟkālattiṟ pulittōl mītu paṭuttu uṟaṅkuvāṉ. kaḷḷi uṇṭa kuṟavaṉ irākkālattē paraṇmītiruntu tiṉippuṉattik kāval kākkak, kuṟatti cantaṉampūciya taṉatu kūntalit tuḷarntu āṟṟik kuṟiñcip paṇpāṭuvāḷ. tiṉippuṉatti mēyavanta yāṉi av vōciyik kēṭṭu meymaṟantu tūṅkum. tiṉippuṉatti mēyavarum paṉṟi, kuṟavaṉ paraṇmītu māṭṭiya koḷḷiyik kaṇṭuniṟkum. kuṟac ciṟumiyar kiyiṟ caṅkuvaḷiyaṇintu2 mutukiṟ ṟūṅkumpaṭi kūntalip piṉṉi viṭuvar; tiṉimītu viḻum kiḷikaḷit taṭṭi kuḷiri mutaliya karuvikaḷit taṭṭiyum, acōkan taḷiri ōcciyum, āyō eṉac catta miṭṭum ōṭṭuvar; acōkilum tāḻiyilum kaṭṭiya ūcalkaḷil ēṟiyāṭu var; vēṅkip pūvik koytu pulipuliyeṉac cattamiṭuvar. ciṟumikkut teyvattāl nērnta kuṟiyip pōkka aṉṉi pūkkaḷip paliyākat tūvi vīṭṭik kāval ceytu veṟiyāṭum poruṭṭu muruka pūci ceyvōṉi vīṭṭukku aḻippāḷ. palāp paḻattāṟ camitta kaḷḷik kuṟattiyar vārkka vārkkak kuṟavar vāṅki uṇṭu makiḻvar. yāṉikaḷiṉ kompip peṟutaṟkuk kuṟavar yāṉikaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭuvar. miṉmiṉi moyttirukkum kaṟiyāṉ puṟṟik karaṭi irākkālattē iruntu tōṇṭum; akkāṭci kollaṉ irumpil vēli ceyvatu pōṉṟatu. yāṉiyi vēṭṭiyāṭap puli patuṅkiyirukkum. piṟipōṉṟa kom puṭiya paṉṟi paṟikkaṇ pōṉṟu niṟinta cuṉiyiṭattē nīrip parukic cēmpiṉ kiḻaṅki uṇṇum. civanta āramuṭiya kiḷi, paṟittut tūkka māṭṭātu pōṭṭuc ceṉṟa cāmik katirik kōḻikaḷ niṉṟu uṇṇum. muṟṟi viḷiyumpaṭi kaḷ mūṅkiṟ kuḻāykaḷil vikkappaṭṭirukkum. kuṟavar kaṭappa marattiṟ koṭikaṭṭi māli tūkkip palavaki vāttiyaṅ kaḷi olittuk kūttāṭi murukaṉi vaḻipaṭuvar.
vēḷāḷar
āṟṟōraṅkaḷil vāḻntu tāṉiyaṅkaḷi viḷivittōr vēḷāḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ āṟṟu nīrip payaṉpaṭutti vēḷāṇmi ceytaṉar. āṟṟukkuc cēymiyil vāḻntōr maḻi nīri ērikaḷilum kuḷaṅkaḷilum tēkkivittu ēṟṟam kapili piḻā iṭā mutaliyavikaḷāl iṟittuc cāmi avari tuvari mutaliya tāṉiya vakikaḷiyum viḷivittaṉar. ataṟku appāl parutti viḷintatu. kaṟkālattilēyē intiya makkaḷ parutti āṭi uṭuttārkaḷ. ak kālattil maṟṟiya nāṭukaḷil makkaḷ tōl kampaḷi caṇal āṭikaḷi uṭuttiṉar; allatu āṭiyiṉṟi yiruntaṉar. āṟṟuc camaveḷikaḷil āṭi neytaṟku vēṇṭiya kaṟkālap poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl akkāla makkaḷ āṭi neyvatil tērccipeṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. avarkaḷ marattiṉāl vīṭukaṭṭi vāḻntārkaḷ; neṟkaḷañciyaṅkaḷ marattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. vaṇṭiyum marattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. vaṇṭi ural ulakki toṭṭil mutaliyaṉa ik kālattiṟ pōlavē iruntaṉa. ik kālat taccar payaṉpaṭuttum āyutaṅkaḷ pōṉṟu kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavi putiya kaṟkālak kuṭiyiruppukaḷiṟ kaṇṭeṭuk kappaṭṭaṉa. in nilattukkuriya kulateyvam vēntaṉ. ak kāla makkaḷ nalla aracaṉiṉ āḷukiyil ellā naṉmikaḷum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu nampiṉārkaḷ. ākavē avarkaḷ ellā naṟkuṇaṅkaḷum aminta aracaṉit teyvamāka vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ.1 piṟkālattil vēntaṉ intiraṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. itu tamiḻar āriyar kalappu nērnta piṟkāla vaḻakku. āriyar, intiraṉ tēvarkaḷukku aracaṉ eṉak karutiṉar. vēḷāṇ makkaḷ intiraṉukku viḻākkaḷ ciṟappāka eṭuttaṉar. ivvaki viḻākkaḷip paṟṟic cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali mutaliya nūlkaḷ aḻakuṟak kūṟukiṉ ṟaṉa. vēḷāṇ makkaḷ uḻutuṇpōr uḻuvittuṇpōr eṉa iruvaki yiṉar. ivaruḷ uḻuvittuṇpōr vēḷ eṉappaṭṭuk kuṟunila maṉṉa rākavum āṭcipurintaṉar. iv vakuppiṉar aracaṉāl kaṇikkappaṭṭa peru makkaḷāvar.
māṭu orukālattiṟ celvamāka matikkappaṭṭatu. piṉpu tāṉiyam pōṉṟa uṇavuvakikaḷ celvamāka matikkappaṭṭaṉa. atika uṇavup poruḷkaḷi vittiruppavaṉē celvaṉāka matikkappaṭṭāṉ. vēḷāṇ makkaḷ atika tāṉiyaṅkaḷi viḷivittuc celvarāy vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ periya vīṭuvāyilkaḷiyum amittaṉar. avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, ulōkap pāttiraṅkaḷ ērttoḻilukku vēṇṭiya kalappi nukam, koḻu mutaliyaṉa, avar makaḷir aṇiyum āparaṇavaki, uṭukkum āṭikaḷ iviyum ivi pōṉṟavikaḷum ceytutavum toḻilāḷar marutanilattē vantu taṅkiṉar. piṟa nilaṅkaḷil vāḻvōr tam nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭikkum paṇṭaṅkaḷit tāṉiyaṅkaḷukkup paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyya vantaṉar. ivvāṟu marutanilam, makkaḷ pōkkuvarat tum āravāramumuṭiya nakaramāka māṟiṟṟu: iṅkup poḻutu pōkkuk kuriya āṭal, pāṭal, viḻā mutaliyaṉa nikaḻntaṉa, iṉpakkalikaḷ vaḷarcci yuṟṟaṉa. ovvoru toḻilāḷarum vāḻum vītikaḷuṭiya nakaraṅkaḷ iṅkē tāṉ tōṉṟiṉa. tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ marutanila makkaḷiṉ uṇṭāṭṭuk kaḷiyāṭṭu nīrāṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷiyellām aḻakākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
paratavar
kaṭaṟkari makkaḷ mīṉ piṭittum uppu viḷittum vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ uppuk kaṇṭattiyum uppiyum tamakku vēṇṭiya nelluk kum piṟaporuḷkaḷukkum paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ marattik kuṭintu kuṭivu tōṇikaḷiyum peruṅkaṭaliṟ cellattakunta nāvāy kaḷiyuñ ceytaṉar. muṉṉar avarkaḷ kariyi aṭuttut tōṇikaḷiṟ ceṉṟaṉar; piṉpu karuṅkaṭal vaḻiyākat tūratēcaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu teṉṉintiya āṭikaḷiyum maraṅkaḷiyum piṟanāṭṭup poruḷkaḷukku māṟṟiṉar.1 makaḷir taḻiyuṭi yuṭuttaṉar. tantiyar kaṇavar koṇarnta mīṉ iṟāl eṉpavikaḷik kāyaviṭṭup puṉṉi niḻalkaḷiliruntu puṭkaḷ oppik kāval kāttaṉar. neytal nilamakkaḷ paṉic ceṟivukaḷil ciṟu kuṭilkaḷamittu vāḻntaṉar. avi pullu allatu paṉiyōli yāl vēyappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ pūraṇik kālaṅkaḷil cuṟākkōṭu naṭṭup pūmāli aṇintu kūttāṭi varuṇaṉi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar; taliyil taḻi kaḷi aṇintaṉar. varuṇaṉ tamiḻar kaṭavuḷeṉṟum ik kaṭavuḷ āriya makkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭateṉṟum muṉ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.1
kaṭaṟkarit tuṟimukaṅkaḷil periya paṇṭacālikaḷ iruntaṉa. ivikaḷil arēpiyā, yavaṉa tēcam mutaliyavikaḷiliruntu vanta paṇṭaṅkaḷ cuṅkaṅkoḷḷum poruṭṭu aṭukkik kiṭantaṉa. piṟa nāṭu kaḷukku aṉuppum poruṭṭu vaṇṭikaḷilum kaḻutikaḷilum potimāṭu kaḷilum koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa mūṭikaḷ aracamuttiri kuttappaṭṭup paṇṭacāliyiṉ iṉṉōriṭattu aṭukkappaṭṭuk kiṭantaṉa. paṇṭacāli yil araca ūḻiyam puriyum kaṇakkarum cēvakarum palar kaṭami purintaṉar. kaṭaṟkarit tuṟimukaṅkaḷil vāṇikam vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. aṅku vaṇikar kūṭiṉar. periya māḷikikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. vaṇṭikaḷ celvataṟku nalla vītikaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa. māḷikikaḷ kuṉṟu nimirntatu pōṉṟa uyaramuṭiyaṉavākavum pala cāḷaraṅkaḷōṭu kūṭiya māṭikaḷum ōṭu kaṟṟi mutaliyavaṟṟāl vēyappaṭātu mūṭu cāntiṭṭu nilāmuṟṟaṅkaḷ viṭap paṭṭaṉavāyumiruntaṉa. avi ‘vēyā māṭam’ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. cāḷaraṅkaḷ māṉiṉ kaṇpōṉṟaṉavākac ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kaṭaliṟ cellum marakkalaṅkaḷ ticiyi aṟiyumporuṭṭu uyarnta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷil viḷakkukaḷ vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivikaḷ kalaṅkari viḷakkaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ik kalaṅkari viḷakkaṅkaḷ alakcāntiriyāvil tālami cōtar1 amitta veḷicca vīṭṭukkup paṉṉeṭuṅkālam muṟpaṭṭatu. kappalōṭṭum mālumi mīkāṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. mīkāṉ eṉpatē mīkāmaṉ eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkum. caṅka nūlkaḷil neytal nilattip paṟṟiyum an nilamakkaḷip paṟṟiyum kāṇappaṭuvaṉa cila varumāṟu:
kuṟac ciṟumiyar puṉṉi niḻaliliruntu karuvāṭṭik kāval kāttup puḷḷōppuvar. umaṇar erutukaḷ pūṭṭiya vaṇṭikaḷil uppi yēṟṟic ceṉṟu viṟpar. paṉimaraṅkaḷil irākkālattē aṉṟiṟ paṟavikaḷ taṅkum. iravil mīṉpiṭikkum paratavariṉ timil viḷakkup pācaṟiyil uṟiyum aracaratu yāṉimukattu neṟṟippaṭṭampōl viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum. nāvāykaḷiṉmītu periya koṭikaḷ aciyum. kaḻiyiṭattē viḷinta uppi umaṇap peṇkaḷ eṭuttu nellukku uppu eṉa vili kūṟi viṟpar. paratavar ciṟumiyar uppuk kuppaṅkaḷil ēṟi niṉṟu kaṭalil varum timilkaḷi eṇṇuvar. peṇkaḷ māntaḻi neytal ili mutaliyavaṟṟi uṭuppar. uppu vāṇikarōṭu avarkaḷ vaḷarkkum mantiyum kūṭac cellum. parattiyar mīṉkaḷi vilipakarntu celvar. ciṟumiyar kaṭaṟkariyil vaṇṭaṟ pāvi ceytum, ciṟṟil puṉintum ciṟucōṟaṭṭum viḷiyāṭuvar. paratavar muṟṟattē cuṟāk kiḻitta vali tūṅkum.
kōṉ
araciyal tamiḻariṭiyē mikap paḻamipeṟṟatu. kōṉ, ēntal, vēntaṉ, maṉṉaṉ, kuricil, iṟivaṉ, vaḷḷal, aṇṇal, kuṟunilamaṉṉar mutaliya paḻantamiḻc coṟkaḷē itaṟkuc cāṉṟu. aracaṉ kuṭikaḷi āḷvatu āṭciyeṉappaṭṭatu. āḷ eṉpatu makkaḷ orumi. āṭci eṉpatu iṭiyaṉ mantikaḷik kāppatupōla makkaḷip pakivariṉiṉṟum kāppatu eṉapporuḷpaṭṭatu. ēntal eṉpatu pōlavē vēntaṉ eṉpa tum. ēntu eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatākalām. aracaṉukkup paḻiya peyar kō allatu kōṉ. itaṟkup pacukkaḷi mēyppavaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. itu, āṭci mutalil mulli nilattil ārampittu vaḷarcci yaṭintateṉpati viḷakkum. in nilimiyilēyē makkaḷ māṭu pacu eṉappaṭum celvattit tēṭa ārampittārkaḷ. āyar tamatu celva mākiya mantikaḷuṭaṉ puṟṟarikaḷiṟ ṟaṅki vāḻavēṇṭiyavarkaḷāyiṉar. maṟṟac celvaṅkaḷpōlallāmal āṭumāṭukaḷ kaṉṟukaḷi īṉṟu perukum iyalpiṉa. āyariṉ putalvar taṅkuṭumpattukkuriya manti kaḷip pirittukkoḷḷalām. mēyccal nilaṅkaḷip pirittal muṭiyātu. mēyccal nilaṅkaḷ ōraḷavukkumēṟ ciṟiyaviyākkappaṭṭāl avi āṭu māṭukaḷ mēyvataṟkup payaṉpaṭā. ākavē kuṭumpaṅkaḷ oṉṟu cērntu vāḻum muṟi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kuṭumpattiṉ talivaṉ atikāra muṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. piṉ pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷukku atikāramuṭiya oruvaṉ talivaṉāṉāṉ. avaṉ ciṟappākak kōṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. ivvāṟu aracaṉ uṇṭāyiṉāṉ.
aracaṉ vāḻum maṉi kōṭṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. aracaṉatu atikāram uyara uyarap pōrum vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. kōṭṭiyic cūḻntu matil araṇ ceytatu. ākavē, kōṭṭi, araṇmaṉi eṉappaṭṭatu. matil maṇṇōṭu kēppik kaḻi kalantu kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. itu ceṅkal matiliṉum valivuṭiyatu. kōṭṭiyic cūḻntu akaḻ, akappā, allatu akaḻi iruntatu. ic coṟkaḷ akaḻ eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantaṉa. uṭu, ōṭi, kayam, parikam, pariki, pāmpuri eṉpaṉavum akaḻiṉ maṟupeyarkaḷ. pāmpiṉ uri pāmpi neruṅki mūṭiyiruppatupōl iruttaliṉ akaḻ pāmpuriyeṉap peyar peṟṟiruttal kūṭum. kōṭṭiyiṉ vāyil koṭṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. matiliṉ piṉpuṟattē nilattiliruntu mēlē yeḻuntu mēṭipōliruppatu kottaḷam eṉappaṭṭatu. matiliṉ mītu vīrar maṟintiruntu ampu eytaṟku amikkappaṭṭuḷḷa maṟiviṭam ñāyil ēvaṟi eṉappaṭṭatu. araṇmaṉiyiṟ pala aṟikaḷ iruntaṉa. tāṉiyakkaḷañciyam kottaṟi eṉavum, aracaṉ vīṟṟirukkum maṇṭapam koluvirukki eṉavum, āyutaṅkaḷ vikkum aṟi āyutak koṭṭil eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. koluvirukki koṭi tukil tōki pū ilikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. aracaṉ vaṭṭitta kūrnuti muṭi yaṇintāṉ. aracarum vaḷḷalkaḷum mukatti maḻittiruntārkaḷ. aracaṉ ariyil taṅkap paṭṭikiyum, kiyiṟ kaṭakamum, kamakkaṭṭukkum muḻaṅkikkumiṭiyil cataṅkikaḷiyuṭiya kaḻalum, kaḻuttil pavaḷa muttu mālikaḷum, kālil vīracceyal poṟitta vīra taṇṭiyum, kātiṟ kuṇṭalaṅkaḷum aṇintiruntāṉ. avaṉ koluvirukkum ācaṉam kaṭṭil eṉappaṭṭatu. aracaṉ cāyvataṟku ariyaṇiyiruntatu. avaṉic cūḻntu aṭiyār, uḻiyar, pukaḻ kūṟum akavar, kaṭṭaḷikaḷi veḷip paṭuttum vaḷḷuvar, aracaṉatu vīram koṭi ākiyavaṟṟip pāṭum pulavar, aṇṇal, vaḷḷal mutaliya perumakkaḷ iruntaṉar. aracaṉ kuṭimakkaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷi nēriṟ kēṭṭu muṟi ceytāṉ. avaṉ paricaṉar puṭicūḻa vīṟṟiruntāṉ. ari, kavari, kuyavu, kūviram, tikiri, viyam eṉpaṉa tēriṉ peyarkaḷ. ākavē, ak kālattup palavakit tērkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟu terikiṟatu. tērkaḷ yāṉi allatu erutukaḷāl iḻukkappaṭṭaṉa. tēriṉ naṭuppākam taṭṭu allatu nāppaṇ eṉṟum, ataṉ taṭṭup pār eṉṟum, tēric cuṟṟiyuḷḷa palakikaḷ kiṭuku eṉṟum, cakkaram ār eṉṟum, tēriṉmītu ēṟutaṟku amikkappaṭṭa paṭikaḷōṭu kūṭiya mēṭi, muṭṭi allatu pirampu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉa. tēr āṭi kaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. araca varuvāy aracaṉukkuriya nilaṅkaḷāl māttiramaṉṟu. vari, cuṅkam, tiṟi, kappam mutaliyavikaḷālum vantaṉa.
āṭci mulli nilattiṟṟōṉṟi marutanilattil vaḷarcciyuṟṟatu. ākavē, aracaṉatu nakar perumpālum maruta nilattilēyē iruntatu.
pārppār
ellāt tēcaṅkaḷilum ālayaṅkaḷil kaṭavuḷukkup palicelut tum kurumār iruntaṉar. avvakiyiṉar tamiḻnāṭṭilum iruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ pārppār eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kōyil toṭarpāṉa karumaṅkaḷip pārttaliṉ ippeyar avarkaḷukku iṭappaṭṭirukkalām. kaṭavuḷ vaḻi pāṭṭiṉum piṟavakiyiṉum ivarkaḷ potumakkaḷukku aṟivu koḷutti vantaṉar. im muṟiyil ivarkaḷ makkaḷiṉ purōkitarumāṉārkaḷ. purōkitam eṉpatu pura eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatākalām. purōkitaṉākiya pārppāṉē akapporuṭṭuṟiyil varum pārppaṉap pāṅkaṉāvaṉ. purōkitaṉ aracaṉatu uṟutic cuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ oruvaṉākavum iruntāṉ.
tolkāppiyattil, “nūlē karakam mukkōl maṇiyē, āyuṅ kāli antaṇark kuriya,” eṉakkūṟappaṭum antaṇar, tuṟavikaḷāy iṭam viṭṭu iṭam cellum tuṟavōrāvar. iṅku nūleṉak kūṟappaṭṭatu pottakak kaṭṭi. itu:
1“pūtiyu maṇṇum pottakak kaṭṭum
māṉuri maṭiyu mantirak kalappiyum
kāṉeṭu maṇiyuṅ kaṭṭuṟut tiyātta
kūṟi veḷḷurik kuṇṭikik kāviṉar” (utayaṇaṉ kati)
eṉpatāl naṉi viḷaṅkum.
kātal
muṟkālap pulavōr kātal, pōr eṉṉum iru tuṟikaḷilum pāṭalkaḷ puṉintaṉar. ip pāṭalkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum oḻukkaṅkaḷ varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṉtoṭṭu varuvaṉa. makaḷirum mintarum oruvari oruvar virumpik kātalittuk kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyarātalē paṇṭi nāḷ maṉṟal. piṟaraṟiyāvaki cilanāṭ karantoḻukum kātaloḻukkam kaḷa veṉavum, palaraṟiya maṇantu kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyarāy illiruntu nallaṟam naṭattum oḻukkam kaṟpeṉavumpaṭum. kūṭiya kātalar pirintaviṭattu iraṅkal, pirintavar varuvāreṉṟu avari etirnōkki āṟṟiyiruttal, oruvarōṭu oruvar piṇaṅki ūṭutal eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷip palavāṟu kaṟpittup paṇṭip pulavōr pāṭal puṉintaṉar. malināṭṭu makaḷirum mintarum ātikālattiṟ kaḷaviṟkūṭip piṉ maṇantu koṇ ṭaṉarātaliṉ puṇartalum atu kāraṇamāyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷum kuṟiñcikku urittākkappaṭṭaṉa. mulli nilattil maṇaṅkaḷ mutiyavarkaḷāl oḻuṅku ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. mullinilamakaḷir kāliyil mantikaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟa kātalar māliyil mīḷum variyum avari etirpārttiruttaliṉ mullikku iruttal urittākkappaṭṭatu. kaṭaṟkari makaḷir marakkalaṅ kaḷiṟ ṟoliviṭañ ceṉṟa kaṇavari niṉintu iraṅkiyiruttaliṉ, iraṅkal neytaṟku urittākkappaṭṭatu. poruḷ tēṭutal aracar ēvali niṟivēṟṟutal mutaliya ētukkaḷi muṉṉiṭṭuk kātalar pālinila vaḻiyē cellutaliṉ, pāli piritalukku urittākkappaṭṭatu. iv voḻukkaṅkaḷ ellām ellā nilaṅkaḷilum nikaḻattakkaṉa.
maṉṟal
ak kāla maṉṟal purōkitar iṉṟi naṭantaṉa. kaṇi eṉappaṭṭa cōtiṭar kaliyāṇattukkēṟṟa nalla nāḷi ārāyntu kūṟiṉar. caṅka nūl kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum maṉṟal muṟi varumāṟu:
urōkiṇi cantiraṉōṭu kūṭum pūraṇi nāḷil kaliyāṇa mukūrttam vikkappaṭṭatu. vīṭṭiṉ muṟṟattē kālkaḷ naṭṭu aḻakiya pantal iṭappaṭṭatu; tariyil āṟṟiṉ veṇmaṇal parappappaṭṭatu. aḻakiya pūmālikaḷiyum ilikaḷiyum tūkkip pantal alaṅkarikkap paṭṭatu. paruppu iṭṭu ākkiya cōṟṟukkuviyal palar kūṭiyiruntu uṇṭa piṉṉum mikuntu kiṭantatu. periya mēḷaṅkaḷum maṇavāttiyaṅkaḷum olittaṉa. aṟukaṅkiḻaṅki nūlāṟ cuṟṟic ceyta teyva uruvam vāḻi yiliyiṉ mītu vittup pūvāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. viḷakkukaḷ koḷuttappaṭṭaṉa. kaliyāṇattip pārppataṟkup pūppōṉṟu aḻakiya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya peṇkaḷ vantu kūṭiṉārkaḷ. putiya nīrkkuṭaṅkaḷit taliyilum, kiyil putiya vāyakaṉṟa pālikic caṭṭikaḷiyumuṭiya peṇkaḷ vantārkaḷ. appoḻutu peṇkaḷ āravārañ ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ ap pāttiraṅkaḷi oruvar kiyiliruntu maṟṟavar kikku māṟṟiṉar. putalvarip peṟṟōrum tālitarittōrumākiya aḻakiya nāṉku mutupeṇkaḷ an nīrk kuṭaṅkaḷi vāṅki maṇamakaḷi muḻuk kāṭṭiṉar. maṇamakaḷiṉ kariyakūntal nīruṭaṉ vanta nellālum malarālum maṟintatu. am mutupeṇṭir “nī koṇṭāṉukkiṉiyaḷāy avaṉip pēṇi iṉitu vāḻuti” eṉa vāḻttiṉar. maṇamakaḷukku āparaṇaṅkaḷ niṟiyap pūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avvāparaṇap poṟiyip poṟukkamāṭṭā miyāl avaḷukku viyarvi tōṉṟiṟṟu. aḻakiyapeṇkaḷ viciṟi koṇṭu vīciṉar. kuṟṟamillāta mukūrttam vantatu. tamar virivil vantu kiḻattiyik kiḻavaṉukku aḷittārkaḷ.
marutanilattē āṭal pāṭalkaḷiṟṟērnta kūttiyar viṟaliyar mutaliyōr vāḻntaṉar. ivarkaḷ parattimiyiṉar. ivarkaḷ nimittam marutanilat talivaṉ, taliviyarkaḷukkiṭiyil ūṭal, pulavi mutaliya piṇakkaṅkaḷ nērvatuṇṭu. kikkiḷi, peruntiṇi eṉṉum iru vakik kātalkaḷum caṅka nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kikkiḷi eṉpatu tammāṟ kātalikkappaṭṭa makaḷir tammik kātaliyātaviṭattu mintar tam kātali veḷippaṭuttip palakūṟutal. peruntiṇiyāvatu āṭavaṉ kātaliyātaviṭattu avaṉik kātalikkum orutti kātal mikuti yāl pala moḻintu karital. tām kātalitta makaḷir tammik kātalittu maṇakka uṭaṉpaṭātaviṭattu mintar, paṉaṅkarukkiṉāṟ ceyta maṇi kaṭṭiya kutiri mītu ēṟi āvirampū, erukkampū, elumpu mālikaḷic cūṭip, peṇṇiṉ vaṭivi ōliyil eḻutik, kiyiṟpiṭittu (ciṟuvar iḻukka) vītiyil ūrtalum peruntiṇi eṉappaṭum. itu nallatu iṉiyatu eṉap pulavar nāṭṭiya pulaneṟi vaḻakkeṉpa.
pōr
tamiḻnāṭṭu maṉṉar piṟa aracarik kīḻppaṭuttit tammāṇiyi mēmpaṭuttaṟ poruṭṭuc cerukkaḷ pala malintaṉar. ovvōr araca ṉukkum vevvēṟu koṭiyum māliyum iruntaṉa. pōrukkuc cellum pōtu vīrar tattam vēntarkkuriya mālikaḷiyaṇivar. māṟṟaracariṉ nirikaḷik kavartalē muṟkāla aracar pōr toṭaṅkum muṟiyākum. turiyōtaṉaṉ virāṭaṉiṉ niriyik kavarntatu pāratattiṟ kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟatu. māṟṟaracar niriyiṉik kavaraccellum vīrar tam muṭiyiṭattē veṭcip pūvic cūṭuvar. ākavē, niri kavartal veṭciyeṉappaṭṭatu. niri kavartalaṉṟip pakiyaracaṉiṉ nilattik kavartaṟ poruṭṭup paṭi yeṭuttuc cellutal vañciyeṉṟum, kōṭṭiyi muṟṟukiyiṭutal uḻiñi eṉṟum, etirttup porutal tumpi eṉṟum, veṟṟipeṟutal vāki eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. avvappōtu vīrar avvap pūkkaḷic cūṭiya kāraṇattāl av voḻukkaṅkaḷukku ip peyarkaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa.
pulavōr aracaṉiṉ pukaḻippāṭutal pāṭāṇ eṉavum, ulaka niliyāmiyi uṇarum pakuti kāñci eṉavumpaṭṭaṉa. pōr ceyyum nilam, kaḷari, paṟantali, mutunilam, kaḷam eṉappaṭṭatu. paṭikaḷ uṇṭi, ōṭṭu, aṇi, tāṉi eṉap pala pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭu iruntaṉa. muṉṉaṇippaṭi akkam, koṭippaṭi, tār, tūci, niri eṉṟum; piṉṉaṇippaṭi kūḻi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉa. piṟkālaṅkaḷil tēr, yāṉi, kutiri, kālāḷeṉap paṭikaḷ nāṉkākap pirikkappaṭṭaṉa.1 kutirip paṭi āriyar varukikkuppiṉ tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum. mokañcatarō makkaḷ kutiriyip paṟṟi aṟintirukkavilli.
yāṉivīraṉ ampu tuḷikkamuṭiyāta pulittōṟ kavacam pūṇṭu yāṉimītu vīṟṟiruntāṉ. yāṉi karuṅkaṭaliṟ cellum nāvāypōla vum, uṭukkūṭṭattiṉ naṭuvē cellum tiṅkaḷippōlavum ceṉṟatu. ataṉic cūḻntu pōrmaṟavar cuṟākkūṭṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟu ceṉṟaṉar. yāṉikaḷ naṉṟākap paḻakkappaṭṭuk kantukaḷilum, taṟikaḷilum āli yiṭattē kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avikaḷukku vellattōṭu kalanta arici ūṭṭappaṭṭatu. yāṉikaḷum, kutirikaḷum paḻakkappaṭumiṭam ceṇṭu veḷi eṉappaṭṭatu. kuḷaṅkaḷiṟ kuḷippāṭṭiyapiṉ avikaḷiṉ mukam niṟappoṭikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭapiṉ neṟṟi, ōṭi eṉṉum neṟṟip paṭṭattāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. tēril iraṇṭu allatu nāṉku kutirikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. tērōṭṭuvōṉ valavaṉ allatu pākaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. tēril palavaki uruvaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. atu palavaki niṟampūciya taṭikaḷālum tuṇikaḷālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉmītu tukiṟkoṭikaḷ acintaṉa.
vīrar palavaki āyutaṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avaṟṟuṭpala kaṟkālantoṭṭu māṟupaṭāmal vantaṉa. avi eṟuḻ, taṇṭu, vil, kokkari, cili, taṭi, tavar, kuṇil muṉi mutaliyaṉa. nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa āyutaṅkaḷik kuṟikkum tamiḻppeyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.1 avi kiviṭumpaṭi, kiviṭāppaṭi eṉa iruvakiyiṉa. vil nāṇil ampu vitteyyumiṭam uṭuveṉappaṭṭatu. taṟkāppukkuriya āyutam kēṭakam; atu kiṭuku kaṭakam taṭṭu parici palaki paṟi vaṭṭaṇi paṭṭam tōl tōṟpāram eṉap palavakiyiṉatu. mey kākkum caṭṭi mēlakam araṇi ācu eṉavum, kikkavacam kippuṭi eṉavum aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.
icik karuvikaḷ
pōrilē yāṉi kutiri kālāḷ mutaliya paṭikaḷi ūkka mūṭṭum poruṭṭup palavaki vāttiyaṅkaḷ olikkappaṭṭaṉa. iyam vāttiyam vācciyam eṉpaṉa icikkaruvikaḷik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkum potuppeyarkaḷ. avi tōṟkaruvi tuḷikkaruvi narampukkaruvi miṭaṟṟukkaruvi eṉa nālvakippaṭum. kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi uḷi iḷi viḷari tāram eṉpaṉa pāṭumiṭattup piṟappikkappaṭum ēḻu tamiḻ icikaḷākum. ūtappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ kuḻal mutaṉmiyuṭiyatu. paṉi āmpal koṉṟi mulli mutaliya palavakik kuḻalkaḷum iyaṅkaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉa. ūtappaṭum periya vāttiyaṅkaḷ kākaḷam, ammiyam, ciṉṉam, kompu, kōṭu, irali, vayir eṉpaṉa.
narampuk karuvikaḷil mutaṉmiyuṭiyatu yāḻ. ovvoru nilattum ovvoru vaki yāḻ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. nālu mutal patiṉāṉku patiṉēḻu irupattoru narampukaḷiyuṭiya yāḻkaḷ vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. ivi kiviralkaḷiṉāl taṭaviyum teṟittum vācikkappaṭṭaṉa. paḻiya yāḻkaḷ uruttira vīṇi pōṉṟa vaṭiviṉavāy mēl nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiya ‘kāp’ (Harp) eṉṉum yāḻ pōṉṟu narampu kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu karutalām. ivvaki yāḻkaḷ paḻiya ekiptil vaḻakkiliruntaṉa. ‘kāp’ pōṉṟa yāḻkaḷ paḻiya cumēriyarālum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
paṟikaḷ palavakiyiṉa.1 taṭāri, uṭukki mutaliyaṉa pāṇar kiyil iruntaṉa. taṭṭi, karaṭiki eṉṉum paṟikaḷ karaṭi kattum ōciyip piṟappittaṉa. pāli kuṟiñci marutam cevvaḻi eṉpaṉa ak kālattu vaḻaṅkiya paṇkaḷ. ici nuṇukkam pērici ciṟṟici mutaliya nūlkaḷ tamiḻiciyilakkaṇaṅkūṟa eḻuntaṉa eṉṟu paḻiya urikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. an nūlkaḷ iññāṉṟu iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. aracarum perumakkaḷum vēṭṭiyāṭumiṭattu mēṟkūṟiya paṟikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷum makkaḷ kuṭiyiruppukaḷum
aracaṉ vāḻum kōṭṭiyic cuṟṟip pēṭṭikaḷ iruntaṉa. ov voru pēṭṭiyilum ovvoru toḻil purivōr vāḻntaṉar. ivvāṟu pala kuṭiyiruppukaḷ nakaric cūḻntiruntaṉa. pēṭṭikaḷic cuṟṟi nel vayalkaḷ iruntaṉa. celvapperumakkaḷ vīṭukaḷ māṭaṅkaḷ allatu māḷikikaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ivi māṭikaḷuṭiyaṉavāyc ceṅkalliṉā lum marattiṉālum kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. iṟappu, iṟi, tāḻvāram, muṉṟil, muṟṟam, nilāmuṟṟam, uttiram, tūlam, cuṟṟuvāri, tāḻ, tuṭi, mukaṭu, viṭaṅkam, tiṇṇi, kuṟaṭu, oṭṭuttiṇṇi mutaliyaṉa vīṭṭiṉ uṟuppu kaḷiṟ cila. vīṭṭiṉ muṉṉē tiṇṇiyiruntatu. tiṇṇikku muṉṉāṟ kuṟaṭiruntatu. tiṇṇikaḷ maṇ kari cāṇi eṉpavi kalantu meḻuki aḻuttañ ceyyum karuviyāl miṉukkappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭukaḷukkuk katavuka ḷum nilikaḷum cāḷaraṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. vīṭukaḷiṉ kaḻivunīr veḷiyē cellac cākkaṭi allatu cālakaṅkaḷiruntaṉa. vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷil muk kōṇa vaṭivāṉa māṭāk kuḻikaḷ iruntaṉa. ivikaḷil maṇṇiṉāl allatu ulōkattiṉāṟ ceyta kiviḷakkukaḷ ēṟṟi vikkappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭṭiṟkup piṉṉāl kiṇaṟu iruntatu. kiṇaṟṟukku appāl kolliyum, kolliyil āṭu māṭu niṟkum koṭṭilum, appāl vayalumiruntaṉa.
ural, ulakki, ammi, tiriki, kuḻavi, āṭṭukkal, muṟam, callaṭi, caṭṭi, pāṉi, akappi, maṉi, kaṭṭuppeṭṭi, kaṭṭil, toṭṭil, ūñcal (vīcupalaki), uṟi (cumitūkku), kāvaṭi, cummāṭu, kaṇappu (tīccaṭṭi), paraṇ, ñelikōl (neruppuṇṭākkun taṭi), niṟikōl, kavaṇ, pāy mutaliyaṉa vīṭṭupporuḷkaḷiṟ cila.
vaṟiyōr kuṭicikaḷilum kuṭilkaḷilum vāḻntaṉar. cuvarkaḷ taṭikaḷi naṭṭu maṇṇi eṟintu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. avi perumpālum vaṭṭamāṉavi. kūriyiṉ mukaṭṭil kalacam vikkappaṭṭatu. im muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟiyē ālayaṅkaḷum kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. maṉṟam eṉṉum perunterukkaḷum, kōṇam eṉṉum kuṟunterukkaḷum iruntaṉa. kuṟiñcinilak kirāmam ciṟukuṭi eṉṟum, mulli nilak kirāmam pāṭi, cēri eṉṟum, maruta nilak kirāmam ūr eṉṟum, neytal nilak kirāmam pākkam eṉṟum, pālinilak kirāmam paṟantali eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. avi kaḷil kīḻōrum mēlōrum vāḻntaṉar.
uṭi
parutti intiyāvil viḷivatu. pañciliruntu nūlum nūli liruntu āṭiyum neyyappaṭṭaṉa. nīḷamāka neyyappaṭṭa āṭikaḷ tuṇṭukaḷākak kiḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē aṟuvi, tuṇṭu, tuṇi eṉpaṉa āṭiyiṉ peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅkiṉa. paṭṭu, mayir eṉpavikaḷālum āṭi neyyappaṭṭatu. nūliḻikaḷ ceṉṟa iṭamē teriyāta melliya āṭikaḷ akkāla makkaḷāl aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. palavaki āṭikaḷum uṭikaḷum akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpataṟku avikaḷik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷē pōtiya cāṉṟu.1 puṭivikaḷukkup palavakic cāyaṅkaḷ tōykkappaṭṭaṉa. karampu, paṇi, paraṇam, pāḷitam eṉpaṉa paṭ ṭāṭikaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ. kampaḷiyiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa āṭikaḷ mayirakam, uyirakam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. āṭikaḷ aḻakiya karikaḷum, pū vēlippāṭukaḷum, kaṇkavar vaṉappum uṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. āṭa varum makaḷirum kiḻittut tikkappaṭāta uṭikaḷiyē payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ūci, iḻi, vaṅki, caṉṉal, tiyal mutaliya coṟkaḷāl ak kālattavar tiyaṟṟoḻili aṟiyār eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu. iṟavukki eṉpatu iraṇ ṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ tamiḻ aracarukku meykāppāḷarāka amarn tirunta kirēkka urōmaṉ aṇaṅkukaḷāl tamiḻnāṭṭil pukuttappaṭṭatu. ēvalāṭkaḷum vīrarum caṭṭi iṭṭaṉar. kuppāyam tippi meyppi eṉpaṉa caṭṭiyiṉ peyarkaḷ. makkaḷ (talip) pāki, kuṭi, ceruppu, mitiyaṭi mutaliyavikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
āṭi āparaṇa alaṅkāram
tamiḻ makkaḷ uṭikaḷālum āparaṇaṅkaḷālum tammi naṉku alaṅkarittaṉar. vīṭṭuccuvarkaḷil aḻakiya cittiraṅkaḷ variyappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭṭu vāyilkaḷil iṉṟum peṇkaḷ kōlamiṭuvatu muṟkāla vaḻakkup paṟṟiyatu. celvap perumakkaḷ illaṅkaḷil vaṇṇamakaḷir eṉa vīṭṭi yum peṇkaḷiyum alaṅkarikkum peṇkaḷ iruntārkaḷ. vīṭṭiṟ payaṉ paṭuttum ovvoru poruḷum vēlippāṭuṭiyatākac ceyyappaṭṭatu. vīṭṭuk katavu nili kimaraṅkaḷiṉ antali tūṇ eṉpaṉavellām aḻakiya vēlippāṭukaḷāl aḻaku ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. curuṅkak kūṟumiṭattu makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya entap poruḷum aḻakiya vēlippāṭiṉṟic ceyyappaṭavilli. tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷ aḻakiya ciṟpaṅkaḷ amikkun tiṟamikaḷiyellām kōvilkaḷiṟ kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
makaḷir kūntalip palavāṟu kōtiyum piṉṉiyum muṭintu pala vakit tali aṇikaḷi aṇintu pūkkaḷāl vēyntaṉar. mēṉiyic cantaṉakkuḻampu kuṅkumam mutaliyavikaḷāl alaṅkarittaṉar;1 māṉmatam mutaliya vācaṉip poruḷkaḷip pūciṉar; kātiṟṟuḷiyiṭṭup poṉ ṉōliyaṇintaṉar. maṇam muṭitta peṇkaḷ tāli aṇintaṉar. muttu māli, pavaḷamāli, poṟcaṅkili, pātacaram, carappaḷi, neṟṟiccuṭṭi, caṅku vaḷi, poṉvaḷi, mōtiram mutaliya pala aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇiyappaṭ ṭaṉa. yāṉi kutiri erutu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum aṇikaḷ aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa.
poḻutupōkku
viḻākkālaṅkaḷil āṭal pāṭal uṇṭāṭṭuk kaḷiyāṭṭu mutaliya koṇṭāṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭantaṉa. vēṭṭiyāṭṭam, maṟpōr, vāṭpōr, cilampam, tēr, yāṉi, māṭṭup pantayaṅkaḷ, ammāṉi, pantu, 1ūñcal viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ, kāṭippōr, kutārippōr, āṭṭuppōr mutali yaṉa akkāla makkaḷ poḻutu pōkkukaḷ. icipāṭutalum poḻutu pōkkukaḷil oṉṟu. paḻiya kuṟiñcippaṇ iṉṟu kuṟiñci irākamākavum, marutam kētāra irākamākavum, neytal puṉṉākavarāḷiyākavum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru karumaṅkaḷukkum ēṟṟa vevvēṟu paṟikaḷ muḻakkap paṭṭaṉa. māṭu kavarac celvōr ēṟukōṭ paṟiyum, veṟiyāṭuvōr murukiyamum, tirumaṇam koṇṭāṭuvōr maṇamuracum, vēḷāṇmi yaṟuppōr nellarikiṇiyum, tēriḻuppōr tērōṭṭup paṟiyum, aracaṉatu allatu teyvattiṉ ūrvalatti aṟivippōr puṟappāṭṭup paṟiyum, mīṉ piṭikkac celvōr mīṉ kōṭ paṟiyum, cūṟi aṭippōr cūṟikōṭ paṟiyum koṭṭiṉar. ovvoru vāttiyamum vevvēṟu ōciyi eḻuppiṉa.
kūttukaḷ pala vakiyiṉa. kaḷikuṉippu eṉappaṭṭa kūttu, oru pāṭṭiluḷḷa poruḷi āṭṭattālum cikikaḷālum kāṭṭutal. iv vaḻakkup paṟṟiya kōviṟkiriyikaḷ iṉṟum naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. parata nāṭṭiyam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatum ituvē. kūtta makaḷir, kūttiyar eṉappaṭṭa ṉar. ippeyar parattiyarik kuṟippatāyirukkiṉṟatu. ciṟumiyar, kummi, teḷḷēṇam, cāḻal, ōri mutaliya āṭalkaḷ purintu kaḷittaṉar. ciṟuvar kiṭṭippuḷ viḷiyāṭiṉar. itu mēlnāṭṭavariṉ cāṭṭip pantaṭi (Cricket) viḷiyāṭṭup pōṉṟatu.
pāṇar yāḻ mattaḷam mutaliyavikaḷuṭaṉ alintu tirintu aracar, perumakkaḷ vāyilkaḷil pāṭiṉar. vēṭar valiyēṟṟi māṉ paṉṟi mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷip piṭittup poḻutu pōkkiṉar. maṟpōr potu vāṉa poḻutupōkkukaḷil oṉṟu.
pōkku varavu
akkāla makkaḷ palavaki vaṇṭikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. vaṇṭi, ūrti, oḻuki, cakaṭu, cākāṭu, viyam mutaliya peyarkaḷāl itu aṟiyap paṭum. vaḷiyam, vaṭṭil, vaṭṭam, vaṭṭaṇi, vaṭṭu, vaṇaṅku, vaṇar, vaḷi, vaḷḷam, vaḷḷi, accu, āṇi mutaliya vaṇṭiyiṉ uṟuppukaḷi uṇart tum peyarkaḷāl avarkaḷ vaṇṭiyiṉ payaṉi naṉku aṟintiruntār kaḷeṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. vaṇṭi perumpālum vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. aracarum peru makkaḷum tērkaḷilum pallakku kaḷilum ceṉṟaṉar; nīrilāyiṉ ōṭaṅkaḷil ceṉṟaṉar; tōṇikaḷiṉ mukappu yāṉi kutiri ciṅkam mutaliyavikaḷiṉ talikaḷpōlac cetukkappaṭṭiruntatu. ak kālam palavakit tōṇikaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa veṉpataṟkuk kappal, ōṭam, ampi, tōṇi, teppam, paricil, paṭaku, kalam, paḵṟi, catā, pōtam, toḷḷam, pakaṭu, paṭṭiki, paṭṭi, paṭuvi, puṇi, mitavi, vaḷḷam, timil mutaliya peyarkaḷē cāṉṟu.
uṇavu
tamiḻ makkaḷ ūṉik kaṟiyākavum tāvara vakikaḷiyum tāṉiyaṅ kaḷiyum mukkiya uṇavākavuṅ koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ iṟicciyi miḷaku tūḷ iṭṭu ākkik kaṟiyākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. miḷakāy piṟkālattil intiyāvōṭu vāṇikam ceyta airōppiya makkaḷāṟ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. itu teṉṉamerikkap payir. ākavē, ataṟku iṭukuṟippeyar illi. miriyal, kaṟi, kaliṉi, kāyam, tiraṅkam mutaliya peyarkaḷ miḷakik kuṟikkum. kirēkkarum urōmarum intiyāviṉiṉṟum atika miḷakik koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar avarkaḷ miḷakip pippili eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar.
tamiḻmakkaḷ, tāḷitakkaṟi (kuy), porikkaṟi, vaṟi, tuvaṭṭal, tuvi, puḷiṅkaṟi, kāṭi, ūṟukāy mutaliya palavakik kaṟikaḷi uṇṭaṉar. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ mukkiya uṇavu nel. nel puḻukki ularttik kutti ariciyāk kappaṭṭatu. avvāṟu kuttappaṭṭa arici puḻuṅkalarici eṉappaṭṭatu. arici cōṟāka aṭṭu uṇṇappaṭṭatu. aṭicil, amali, amutu, ayiṉi, avi, aviḻ, aḻuppu, uṇā, ūṇ, uṇkūḻ, cati, cātam, coṉṟi, cōṟu, tuppu, tōri, parukki, pāttu, pici, pukar, puḻukku, puṟki, pommal, poruku, maṭi, mici, mitavi, mūral, valci eṉpaṉa cōṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ. ovvoru peyarum vevvēṟu vakiyākac camikkappaṭṭa cōṟṟi uṇarttalām. paruppōṭu cērttup poṅkappaṭṭa cōṟu poṅkal eṉappaṭṭatu. kaḷi, kūḻ, tuḻavi, kañci, nīrāram, tōci, aṭi mutaliyaṉa vevvēṟu vakiyākac camikkappaṭṭa uṇavukaḷākum. appam, piṭṭu, aḵkulli, iliyaṭi, noli, melvaṭi, pollal, iṭi, caḵkulli, nuvaṇi, occi, tuvi, cīṭi, vaṭi mutaliyaṉa paṇiyāra vakiyuṭ cila. makkaḷ vāḻum iṭaṅkaḷukku ēṟpa uṇṇappaṭum tāṉiyaṅkaḷ vēṟu paṭṭaṉa. mulli nilamakkaḷ varaku, cāmi, mutiri mutaliya tāṉiyaṅkaḷi yum, kuṟiñcinila makkaḷ aivaṉam, tiṉi, mūṅkilarici mutaliyavikaḷiyum, marutanila makkaḷ cennel, veṇṇel mutaliyavikaḷiyum, neytal nilamakkaḷ mīṉukkum karuvāṭṭukkum māṟṟiya tāṉiyaṅkaḷiyum uṇa vākak koṇṭaṉar. tēṉun tiṉimāvum cuviyuḷḷa uṇavākum. maruta nila makkaḷ tēṉukkup patil vellattip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avarkaḷ karuppañcāṟṟik kāyccic carkkari kaṟkaṇṭu mutaliyavikaḷic ceytaṉar. pāl tayir mōr veṇṇey ney eṉpaṉa makkaḷāl virumpi uṇṇappaṭṭaṉa. palavakip paḻaṅkaḷ pucikkappaṭṭaṉa. paḻantamiḻar pala vaki matuvi aruntiṉar. uppu atikam viḷivikkappaṭṭatu. uppu viḷiyum vayalkaḷ uppaḷam, aḷakkar, uvarkkaḷam, kaḻi eṉappaṭṭaṉa. nilaṅkaḷ naṉcey puṉcey eṉap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. tirutti viḷi vikkappaṭāta nilam taricu eṉappaṭṭatu. naṉcey puṉcey nilaṅkaḷ cey eṉappaṭṭaṉa. vayal, akaṇi, kampali, kaḻaṉi, kiti, kōṭṭam, ceṟu, taṭi, paṇi, paṇṇi, paḻaṉam, pāṉal, pulam mutaliyaṉa vayaliṉ peyarkaḷ. payirc ceyki vēḷ āṇmi eṉappaṭṭatu.1 vēḷ - nilam; āṇmi - aracu allatu āḷuntaṉmi. vēḷāṇmi ellāt toḻilkaḷilum uyarntatākak karutappaṭṭatu. periya nilattiyuṭiyōr vēḷ allatu vēḷir eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷākavum iruntaṉar. vēḷāṇmi eṉpatu tamiḻil koṭi, īki eṉpavikaḷik kuṟikka vum vaḻaṅkum. vēḷāṇ māntar iyalpukaḷ pattu. avi, āṇivaḻi niṟṟal (cattiyam kaṭippiṭittal), aḻintōri niṟuttal, kikkaṭaṉāṟṟal (maṟṟavarkaḷik kaṭamippaṭuttal), kacivu akattuṇmi, okkal pōṟṟal, ōvāmuyaṟci, maṉṉiṟi karutal (aracaṉukkuc cella vēṇṭiya kaṭamiyic celuttal) oṟṟumi kōṭal, viruntu puṟantarutal, tiruntiya oḻukkam eṉpaṉa. vayal vēlikkuriya ovvoru toḻilukkum tamiḻp peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tottal, uḻakkal, mitittal, uḻutal mutaliyaṉa nilattip paṇpaṭuttum viṉikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ. vēḷāḷaṉatu mukkiya toḻiṟkaruvi kalappi. iti uḻupaṭi, kalaṉi, nāñcil, toṭuppu, paṭi, paṭivāḷ eṉpaṉa kalappiyiṉ peyarkaḷ.
vayalkaḷukku nīr āṟṟiliruntu pāyccappaṭṭatu. āṟukaḷukku aṇikkaṭṭukaḷ iruntaṉa. āṟṟunīr kālvāy allatu kālvaḻiyē ceṉṟatu. kiṇaṟu kuḷaṅkaḷiliruntu nīr ēttam, kapili allatu iṟi kūṭiyiṉāl iṟikkappaṭṭatu.
kittoḻil
ulōkaṅkaḷil vēli ceyvōr, kammāḷar, akkacāliyar, aṟivar, ōvar, kaṇṇāḷar, kaṇviṉiñar, kammiyar, kollar, karumār, taṭṭār, tuvaṭṭar, pulavar, puṉiyar, vittakar, vittiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ irumpu, urukku, cempu, veṇkalam, veḷḷi, poṉ mutaliyavaṟṟil vēli ceytaṉar. paḻiya camātik kuḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭṭa nakikaḷ ēṉaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa ak kālattil ulōkaṅkaḷil vēli ceyvōriṉ tiṟamiyik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. periya ulōkat tuṇṭukaḷi aṭittut takaṭākkip periya ēṉaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ulōkat takaṭukaḷ aṭar eṉappaṭṭaṉa. tiyalar, aṇikalaṉkaḷi mika virumpiṉar. ākavē aṇital aḻaku ceytali uṇartta ār, cūṭu, puṉi, pūṇ, mali, mili, miḷi, vēy, ey, vey eṉap pala coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. kaṭinar eṉpōr caṅki aṟuttu aḻakiya vaḷikaḷic ceytaṉar. kuyiṉar irattiṉak kaṟkaḷiṟ ṟuḷiyiṭṭaṉar.
āṭi neyvōr kammiyar, cēṇiyar, kārukar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. necavut toḻil mika vaḷarcciyuṟṟiruntatu.
vāṇikam
tamiḻilē vāṇikat toṭarpāṉa coṟkaḷ pala uṇṭu. niṟikōl, ñemaṉkōl, tarācu, vaṭṭi, mutal, vāci, kaṭi, aṅkāṭi, canti eṉpaṉa avaṟṟuṭ cila. neytal maruta nilaṅkaḷil vāṇikam toṭaṅki yirukkalām. umaṇar vayalkaḷil pātti kaṭṭi viḷivitta uppip potimāṭukaḷilum kaḻutikaḷilum koṇṭu ceṉṟu iṭaṅkaḷtōṟum viṟṟaṉar. vāṇikam vaḷarcciyaṭintapōtu vaṇṭikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. uruḷi, cūṭṭu, ār, kuṟaṭu mutaliyaṉa vaṇṭiyiṉ uṟuppukaḷi uṇarttum cila peyarkaḷ. vāṇikam ceyyum ceṭṭikaḷ palar iruntaṉar. vaṇikarukkuc cāttar eṉpatum caṅka kālattu vaḻaṅkiya iṉṉoru peyar. ceṭṭi eṉṉum col vaṭamoḻiyil ciṟesti ciṟastā eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. vētakālattil, muttu, pavaḷam, cantaṉakkaṭṭi, miḷaku mutaliyavikaḷi vaṭanāṭṭukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu viyāpārañ ceytōr pāṇiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. pāṇiyar eṉpatu vaṇikar eṉpataṉ tiripu eṉa ārāycciyāḷar ārāyntu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. camakkiruta paṇṭitarkaḷ camakkirutam kaṭavuḷ moḻiyāyiruttaliṉ atu maṉita moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum coṟkaḷi iraval peṟṟirukkamāṭṭātu eṉṉum kuruṭṭu nampikki yāl piṟa moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu camakkirutattukkuc ceṉṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷukku ovvāta camakkiruta uṟpattiyik kaṟpittuk kūṟuvar.1

iyal - 3
puttarukku muṟpaṭṭa intiyā
mekitā1 eṉpavar cātakak katikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟapaṭi ak kāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟikaḷiyum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum nirip paṭutti eḻutiyuḷḷār. avi perumpālum paḻiya tamiḻ ilaṅki makka ḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa pōlavē uḷḷaṉa. paṇṭit tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅ kaḷāl aṟiyakkūṭāta pala ceytikaḷ ataṉakattē kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, ataṉ cila pakutikaḷic curukki īṇṭut tamiḻil tarukiṉṟōm.
aracaṉ
aracaṉ ellā atikāramum uṭiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ. avaṉik kaṭṭuppaṭuttum piramāṇaṅkaḷ etuvum irukkavilli. aracariṉ kuḻantikaḷik kit tāyar pālūṭṭi vaḷarttaṉar; vaḷarntapiṉ avarkaḷ taṉi āciriyarkaḷiṭam kalvikaṟka viṭappaṭṭaṉar. (pāṇṭavar turiyōta ṉātiyōr turōṇariṭam kalvi payiṉṟami māpāratattiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉ ṟatu.) kalvi payiṉṟa piṉ avarkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ ovvōru pakutikku uva aracarkaḷāṉārkaḷ. aracaṉukkup pala tēviyar iruntaṉar. avaruḷ paṭṭattaraciyiṉ mūtta putalvaṉē paṭṭameytiṉāṉ. aracāṭci, tantiyi liruntu makaṉukku vaḻivaḻiyē ceṉṟatu. aracaṉukkup piḷḷikaḷ illātaviṭattu avaṉ uṭaṉ piṟantōṉum, uṭaṉ piṟantāṉ illāta viṭattu marumakaṉum āṭci ēṟṟaṉar. iḷavaracup paṭṭam kaṭṭum pōtu purōkitar valampuric caṅkilum poṟkuṭattilumiruntu iḷavaracaṉmītu nīr teḷittaṉar. ak kālattil avaṉ maṉiviyum uṭaṉiruttip paṭṭattut tēviyākkappaṭṭāḷ. piṉpu veṇkoṟṟak kuṭi virikkum kiriyi naṭattappaṭṭatu. nakara māntar nakari aṇi ceytārkaḷ. iḷavaracaṉ paṭṭattu yāṉi mītu nakar valam vantāṉ.
aracaṉatu maṉi nakariṉ naṭuviliruntatu. atu ēḻu māṭikaḷ allatu pala māṭikaḷuṭiyatu. tāṉiyakkaḷañciyamum nitiyum araṇmaṉiyi liruntaṉa. araṇmaṉiyiṉ potu maṇṭapam mikavum vicālamāṉatu. vaḻakkukaḷiṉ tīrppip peṟuvataṟku allatu aṅku naṭakkum koṇ ṭāṭṭaṅkaḷip pārppataṟku makkaḷ kūṭṭamākac ceṉṟārkaḷ. nakarattil naṭakkum ellāvakik kaḷiyāṭṭaṅkaḷiyum aracaṉ pārviyiṭṭāṉ. aracaṉ taṉatu cuṟṟattārōṭu makiḻntirukkum maṇṭapam cantaṉam paṉṉīr mutaliyaṉa teḷikkappeṟṟu naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻntatu. atu palavāṟu aḻaku ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu. aracirukki maṇṭapattil aracaṉ amiccarkaḷā lum naṭaṉa mātarālum cūḻappaṭṭiruntāṉ. aracaṉatu paḷḷiyaṟi māṭi mītu iruntatu. ataṉiṭattē aḻakiya kaṭṭilum ataṉ mītu aṇikaḷum iṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ataṉ ayalē cūtāṭum maṇṭapam. aṅku veḷḷi nāṟkālikaḷum poṉ cūtāṭu kāykaḷumiruntaṉa. aracaṉ potumaṇṭa pattē iruntu makkaḷiṉ vaḻakkukaḷi ucāvit tīrppu aḷittāṉ. araṇ maṉiyip periya matil cūḻntiruntatu. aṅku 16,000 naṭaṉamātar irun taṉar. iḷavaracarum iḷavaraciyarum uṟivataṟku vevvēṟu māḷiki kaḷiruntaṉa. aṭṭiṟcāliyil mīṉ iṟicci, cōṟu, kañci mutaliyaṉa ākkappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ uṇpataṟkuc ceyyappaṭum camiyal nūṟu vakiyiṉatu.
aracaṉatu nāvitaṉ poṉ kattiyiṉāl aracaṉiṉ mayirik kaḷintu, avaṉi muḻukkāṭṭi naṟumaṇam pūciṉāṉ. nāvitaṉ, araṇ maṉiyil kavaṉattukkuriyavaṉākaviruntāṉ. aracaṉ, aṇi ceyyap paṭṭa talippāki aṇintu paṭṭāṭi uṭuttup poṉ cerupput tarittāṉ.
avaṉ cellum tēril veḷḷik kutirikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.1 viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil avaṉ yāṉimītu ceṉṟāṉ. yāṉi poṉṉālum āṭiyālum aṇi ceyyappaṭṭatu.
aracaṉukkup periya māḷikikaḷ, pala maṉiviyar, vili uyarnta uṭikaḷ, maṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷ, poṉ, veḷḷi, muttu, irattiṉam, cempu, irumpu, tantam, cantaṉamaram, māṉtōl mutaliya pala celvaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. avaṉ vēṭṭi nāykaḷōṭu vēṭṭiyāṭac ceṉṟāṉ; matu arunti ṉāṉ; peṇkaḷum (perumpālum naṭaṉamātar) matuvaruntiṉar. aracaṉatu pūṅkāvaṉam patiṉeṭṭu muḻam uyarnta matilāṟ cūḻappaṭṭatu. ataṉ orupakkattē periyakatavukaḷ iruntaṉa. aṅku aracaṉum tēviyarum nīrāṭik kaḷikkum kēṇiyiruntatu. aṅku avaṉukkeṉa amikkappaṭṭa irukkiyil aracaṉ iruntāṉ appoḻutu avaṉetirē naṭaṉamātar nāṭṭiyam āṭiṉar. cilar iṉiya vāttiyaṅkaḷi olittaṉar. vāttiyaṅ kaḷi iyakkip pāṭutal ciṟanta poḻutupōkkākak karutappaṭṭatu.
nālvakip paṭikaḷ
aracaṉiṭattil tēr yāṉi kutiri kālāḷ eṉṉum nālvakip paṭikaḷ iruntaṉa. tērkaḷiṟ perumpālum iraṇṭu kutirikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avi mītu veḷḷik kuṭikaḷum palaniṟak koṭikaḷum nāṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kitērnta pākaṉ tēri yōṭṭiṉāṉ. avaṉ kavaca maṇintu kiyil villip piṭittiruntāṉ.
cila nēraṅkaḷil aracaṉē paṭiyi naṭattic ceṉṟāṉ. ak kālaṅ kaḷil avaṉ yāṉi mītiruntu pakivarit tākkiṉāṉ. yāṉikaḷ perumpālum matiṟ katavukaḷit tiṟappataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. aṟupatu vayatu vantatum uṟutiyāṉa kompuṭiyatumākiya yāṉi pōrukkuc ciṟantateṉak karutappaṭṭatu. pākar kuttuk kōlum tōṭṭi yum vittiruntārkaḷ. viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷaṇintu vāḷik kiyiṟpiṭitta aracar yāṉi mītiruntārkaḷ. valiya maṟavar koṭikaḷi vīcik koṇṭu pakkattē iruntaṉar. yāṉikaḷip paḻakkuvataṟkeṉat taṉi āṭkaḷ aracaṉiṭamiruntārkaḷ.
kutirikaḷukku irumpuk kavacaṅkaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa. kutiri vīrar villum vāḷum vittiruntārkaḷ.1 kālāṭkaḷ vil, vāḷ, vēl, kēṭakam mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar; avarkaḷ kavacam aṇintiruntaṉar.
paṭiyiṉ piṉṉē taccar āyutaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟārkaḷ.2 caṅku mēḷam mutaliyavikaḷi olikkum vāttiyakkārarum ceṉṟaṉar.
paṭi pala pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. ellāp paṭikkum talivaṉāka oru cēṉāpatiyiruntāṉ. avaṉ perumpālum araca kuṭumpattiliruntu terinteṭukkappaṭṭāṉ. amitiyāṉa kālaṅkaḷil avaṉ nītipatiyākak kaṭami pārttāṉ.
