vāṉanūl cōtiṭam eṉpaṉa vaḷarcciyaṭintiruntaṉa. kōḷkaḷiṉ celavukaḷ muṉṉamē aṟintu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. tiṅkaḷil muyal iruppatu tiṅkaḷi irāku viḻuṅkuvatu mutaliya nampikki iruntatu.1
ici
icikkaruvikaḷuḷ vīṇi mikavum mukkiyamuṭiyatu. āṭavarum makaḷirum virumpi atiṟ payiṉṟaṉar. atu taṉiyē allatu pāṭṭu kaḷuṭaṉ vācikkappaṭṭatu. itu kiviraliṉ nuṉikaḷāl narampukaḷit taṭaviyum teṟittum mīṭṭappaṭum. mayiṟpeṭikaḷum cēvalkaḷum kikoṭṭum tāḷattukku āṭumpaṭi payiṟṟappaṭṭaṉa.
ōviyamum, ciṟpamum
cuvarkaḷilum palakikaḷ mītum ōviyaṅkaḷ variyappaṭṭaṉa. acantāk kuki ōviyaṅkaḷē ak kālac cittiraṅkaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭu. ciṟuvar viḷiyāṭum aḻakiya pāvikaḷum, maṟṟum piṟa viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. cila araṇmaṉikaḷ oṟṟit tūṇuṭiyaṉa vākavum, cila eṭṭuc caturamuṭiya āyirantūṇuṭiyaṉavākavum kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. periya nilavaṟikaḷ amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. iṟantavarkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷip putittu avi mītu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eḻuppappaṭṭaṉa. aṅkup pūmālikaḷ tūkki naṟumpuki kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
cila nampikkikaḷum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum
matiyam, amāvācik kālaṅkaḷil makkaḷ viratamiruntārkaḷ. oruvaṉ tummiṉāl ‘cāvilli’ allatu ‘nīṭu vāḻka’ eṉṟu collap paṭṭatu. oruvari niṟivāka oru karumattic ceytāl avaṉ mutukiṟ ṟaṭṭi ataṉ makiḻcci aṟivikkappaṭṭatu. peṇṇiṉ valakkaṇ tuṭippatu tīmi nērvataṟku aṟikuṟi eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. peṇkaḷ tamakkut tuṉpam iḻittavarkaḷit tiṭṭiṉārkaḷ. kuruviṉ peyaric colvatu pāvam eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. kuruvi mūṉṟu muṟi valam vantu viḻuntu kumpiṭuvatu potuvaḻakku. maṉitaṉ nūṟu allatu nūṟṟirupatu āṇṭu vāḻvāṉ eṉa nampappaṭṭatu. vāyiliṟ kataviṭappaṭṭa cuṭalikaḷ iruntaṉa. pantu viḷiyāṭṭuc ciṟuvarāl mikavum virumpappaṭṭatu.
ūñcalil āṭuvati aracarum virumpiṉārkaḷ. aracarum peru makkaḷum āṟukaḷilum kuḷaṅkaḷilum nīntik kaḷittaṉar. viḻākkaḷ paṟi yaṟintu makkaḷukku aṟivikkappaṭṭaṉa. viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil vaṟiyavar kaḷum putu āṭi uṭuttup pūmāliyaṇintu naṟumaṇam pūci maṉiviya ruṭaṉ matuvaruntik kaḷittaṉar. kārttikit tiṅkaḷiṉ mattiyil varum viḻā mikac ciṟappuṭiyatu. aṉṟu aracaṉ nakaric cuṟṟi ulāvaruvāṉ. vaḻakkamāṉa matukkaṭikaḷit tavira viḻākkālaṅkaḷil putiya matuk kaṭikaḷ tiṟakkappaṭṭaṉa. makaḷir mintar ciṟuvar eṉpōr tiraṇṭu niṉṟu kūttu, vāttiyam, pāṭṭu, yāṉippōr, āṭṭuppōr, maṟpōr mutaliya vikaḷip pārttu āravārañ ceytaṉar.
maravaṇakkam
makkaḷ maraṅkaḷit teyvamāka vaṇaṅkavilli; āṉāl maraṅkaḷiluṟiyum teyvaṅkaḷi vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ. it teyvaṅkaḷukkuc cilavēḷi narapalikaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. marattil māliyum kompu kaḷil viḷakkukaḷum tūkkappaṭṭaṉa. marattiṉ aṭiyil āṭu kōḻi paṉṟi mutaliyaṉa paliyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. uyirp palikaḷ niṟuttappaṭṭapiṉ irattat tiṟkup patil marattil kuṅkumam pūcappaṭukiṉṟatāyiruttal kūṭum.
taṇṭaṉi
ak kālat taṇṭaṉi, taṇṭam, ciṟi, uṟuppukaḷik kaḷital, cirattik kōṭariyāṟ paṭṭaṭiyil vittut taṟittal pōṉṟaṉa.

iyal - 4
puttarukkup piṟpaṭṭa intiyā
mekastiṉ eṉṉum kirēkkar cantirakupta mayūraṉiṉ araṇmaṉi yil ki. mu. 302 mutal cilakālam taṅkiyiruntār. ivar intiyāvippaṟṟiya oru nūl eḻutiyiruntār. atiṟ kāṇappaṭum cila pakutikaḷ akkāla intiya makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟikaḷi naṉku viḷakkuvaṉa. avar kūṟuvaṉa:
intiya makkaḷiṉ ēḻu pirivukaḷ
intiyar ēḻu vakiyākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉar. tattuva cāttirat tiṟṟērnta oru kūṭṭattiṉar mutaṟ kulattiṉarākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. maṟṟik kūṭṭattiṉariviṭa ivarkaḷiṉ toki kuṟivāyiruntatu. ivarkaḷ ecamāṉarākavō vēlikkārarākavō irukkavilli. maṟu ulakattiṟkuriya poruḷkaḷi ellām ivarkaḷ naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉa makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. ākavē, avarkaḷ ivarkaḷip piṇaccaṭaṅ kukaḷ purivataṟkum uriya kālaṅkaḷiṟ kaṭavuṭpaliyic celuttuvataṟkum aḻittārkaḷ. iv vakik kiriyikaḷ purivataṟkuk kimmāṟāka ivarkaḷ mikunta poruḷip peṟṟaṉar. potumakkaḷ ivarkaḷāl periya naṉmi kaḷi aṭintaṉar. āṇṭiṉ ārampakālattil makkaḷ kūṭṭaṅkūṭi iruppārkaḷ. appoḻutu ivarkaḷ av vāṇṭil nikaḻavirukkum piṇi, pañcam, kāṟṟu, maḻi ākiyavaṟṟi aṟivippārkaḷ. ivaṟṟi aṟintukoṇṭa makkaḷum, aracaṉum nikaḻa virukkum tuṉpaṅkaḷukku āyattamākaviruntaṉar. tām kūṟiya varuṅkālap palaṉkaḷiṟ poytta tattuva cāttirikaḷ tamatu āyuḷiṉ mītikkālatti nikaḻkāla varuṅkāla palaṉkaḷik kūṟātu muṉamākak kaḻittaṉar.
ivarkaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyiluḷḷōr uḻutūṇ vāḻkkiyar. ivarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷilum kūṭiya tokiyiṉar. ivarkaḷ pōr mutaliya potuk karumaṅkaḷukkuriya kālattit tavira mīti nēram muḻuvatiyum nilattip paṇpaṭuttip payiriṭuvatiṟ celaviṭukiṉṟaṉar. nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭi eṭuttu varuvōr ivarkaḷit tuṉpuṟuttuvatilli. potu naṉmik kuriyōr eṉṉum eṇṇattiṉāl ivarkaḷ ellā āpattukkaḷi ṉiṉṟum kākkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. pakivarāl aḻikkappaṭātaṉavum, mikunta viḷivik koṭuppaṉavumākiya nilaṅkaḷiṉ payaṉ nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ uṇavukkup pōtumāṉatāyiruntatu. ivarkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷōṭu nāṭṭi uṟiviṭamākak koṇṭaṉar. nakara vāḻkkiyi ivarkaḷ virumpiṉārka ḷallar. intiya nāṭu muḻumiyilumuḷḷa nāṭu aracaṉukkuc contamāṉatu. evarēṉum contamāṉa nilam vittirukka aṉumatikkappaṭavilli. nila variyit tavira viḷivil nālil oṉṟum aracaṉukku uriyatāyiruntatu.
mūṉṟāvatu paṭiyiluḷḷōr iṭiyar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum āṭu māṭukaḷi mēyppar. ivarkaḷ nāṭṭilāvatu nakaraṅkaḷilāvatu niliyāka vāḻātu kūṭāraṅkaḷiṉ kīḻt taṅkiṉar; vēṭṭiyāṭiyum poṟi vittum tuṭṭa vilaṅkukaḷip piṭittaṉar.
nāṉkāvatu paṭiyiluḷḷōr ciṟpikaḷ. ivarkaḷiṟ cilar āyutaṅ kaḷic ceytaṉar. cilar payiriṭuvōrukkum piṟarukkum vēṇṭiya karuvi kaḷic ceytukoṭuttu avarkaḷukkut tuṇi purintaṉar.
aintāvatu paṭiyiluḷḷōr paṭiyāccikaḷ. ivarkaḷ pōr ceyyum muṟiyiṟ payiṉṟiruntaṉar. tokiyaḷavil ivarkaḷ iraṇṭāva tāka viruppar. pōr illāta kālaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ poḻutu pōkku kaḷilum cōmputtaṉattilum kālattip pōkkiṉar.
āṟāvatu paṭiyiluḷḷōr kaṇkāṇimār. nāṭṭil naṭakkum ellāvaṟṟiyum aṟintu colvatu ivarkaḷukku vēliyāyiruntatu; aracaṉ illāta viṭaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ tām aṟintavaṟṟi niyāyam vaḻaṅkum atikārikaḷiṭam kūṟiṉar.
ēḻāvatu paṭiyiluḷḷōr potuk karumaṅkaḷil ālōcaṉi kūṟum kūṭṭattiṉar. maṟṟavarkaḷ ellōriyumviṭa ivarkaḷ ciṟu kūṭṭat tiṉar. uyarnta oḻukkattukkum aṟivukkumāka ivarkaḷ matikkappaṭuki ṟārkaḷ. aracaṉatu karuvūlattukkum, aracaṉukku ālōcaṉi kūṟutaṟkum ik kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟutāṉ cilar terinteṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. paṭittali vaṉum talimi nītipatiyum iv vakuppic cērntavarkaḷē.
intiya aracāṅkam ivvāṟu pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. tattam kūṭṭattik kaṭantu maṇam muṭikka evarum aṉumatikkappaṭuvatilli. tamatu vakuppukkuriyatallāta toḻil purivataṟkum oruvarum aṉumatikkap paṭavilli. utāraṇamākap pōrvīraṉ orupōtum payiriṭuvōṉāka varamuṭiyātu; ciṟpi aṟivaṉāy (tattuva cāttiri) varamuṭiyātu.
makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkam
intiya makkaḷ ellōrum ceṭṭāṉa vāḻkkiyuṭiyavar. ciṟappāka, kūṭāraṅkaḷil taṅkumpōtu ivarkaḷ ivvāṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. oḻuṅkaṟṟa kūṭṭattiṉari avarkaḷ veṟuttaṉar. ākavē avarkaḷ oḻuṅkāka naṭantukoṇṭaṉar. kaḷavu ciṟitāka iruntatu. cantirakuptaṉiṉ kūṭārattil 400,000 vīrar taṅkiṉārkaḷ. eppoḻutāvatu 200 tiṟāmac1 poṉṉukku atikamākak kaḷavu naṭantatākak kūṟappaṭavilli. avar kaḷukku eḻuttu mūlam piramāṇaṅkaḷ kiṭiyā.
avarkaḷ eḻuttippaṟṟi aṟiyār. koṭukkal vāṅkalkaḷ ñāpa kattip poṟuttiruntaṉa. āṭamparamallāta vāḻkki naṭattiyapōtum avarkaḷ makiḻcciyuṭaṉ iruntārkaḷ. vēḷvikaḷilaṉṟi avarkaḷ matu aruntavilli. matu nelliliruntu vaṭikkappaṭṭatu. ariciyāṟ camitta kañci avarkaḷiṉ piratāṉa uṇavu. avarkaḷiṉ caṭṭaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkum pōtu avarkaḷ, arumiyāka vaḻakkukkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ. koṭukkal vāṅ kalkaḷ oruvar maṟṟavariṭattiṟ koṇṭa nampikkiyik koṇṭu naṭi peṟṟamiyāl pattiraṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭavilli. avarkaḷ veḷiyē ceṉṟapōtu vīṭṭiyum vīṭṭiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷiyum kāppataṟku evariyum niṟuttavilli. avarkaḷ eppoḻutum taṉimiyākavē uṇa varuntiṉārkaḷ. iṉṉa nērattil uṇavarunta vēṇṭum eṉṉum niyati irukkavilli; paci koṇṭa nēraṅkaḷil ovvoruvarum uṇṭaṉar.
uṭampi uriñcuvatiyē avarkaḷ ciṟanta uṭaṟ payiṟciyākak karutiṉar. ataṟkākap palamuṟikaḷ kiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa. aḻuttamāṉa karuṅ kāli uruḷikaḷi uṭampil aḻutti uruṭṭuvatē avaṟṟuṭ ciṟantatu. avarkaḷiṉ camātikaḷ āṭamparamiṉṟi eḷiyavakiyil iruntaṉa. camātikkumēl maṇkoṭṭi mēṭu ceyyappaṭṭatu. avarkaḷ aḻakiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum virumpiṉar. uṭikaḷ poṉṉiṉāl alaṅkarikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. āparaṇaṅkaḷil vayiraṅkaḷum viliyuyarnta kaṟkaḷum aḻuttappaṭṭiruntaṉa. pūvēli ceyta maciliṉ (callā) tuṇikaḷi avarkaḷ uṭuttaṉar. parivāraṅkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ piṉṉāṟ kuṭi piṭittuc ceṉṟaṉa. uṇmiyum pukaḻum pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. aṟivut tiṟamillāta mutiyavarkaḷukku matippuk koṭukkappaṭavilli.
avarkaḷ pala peṇkaḷi maṇantaṉar. avarkaḷ oru cōṭi erutip peṇkaḷukku viliyākak koṭuttu avarkaḷip peṟṟōr kaḷiṭamiruntu vāṅkiṉar. cilar tamakku vāḻkkit tuṇi vēṇṭumeṉṟu maṇañ ceykiṉṟaṉar. palar iṉpattiṟkāvum vīṭukaḷip pala kuḻanti kaḷāl nirappuvataṟkum maṇañ ceykiṉṟaṉar. vēḷvic cālikaḷilum kaṭavuṭ pūcikaḷilum oruvarum muṭi aṇiyavilli. paliyiṭum vilaṅ kukaḷ veṭṭik kollappaṭavilli; mūccip piṭittut tirukikkol lappaṭṭaṉa.
poyccāṭci colvōṉukkuk kaṭuntaṇṭam vitikkappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉatu uṟuppukkup paṅkam viḷittavaṉatu atē uṟuppu veṭṭap paṭṭatu; kiyum veṭṭappaṭṭatu. ciṟpikaḷiṉ kaṇṇukku allatu kikku ūṟu viḷittavaṉ koli ceyyappaṭṭāṉ.
aracaṉuṭiya uyir peṇkaḷiṉ kiyil iruntatu. ap peṇkaḷ tāy tantiyariṭamiruntu vilikku vāṅkappaṭṭavarkaḷ. vāyilukku veḷiyē kāvalāḷarum pōr vīrarum kāval purintaṉar. aracaṉ matu vuṇṭa mayakkattilirukkumpōtu avaṉik koṉṟavaḷ iṉivarum aracaṉukku maṉiviyākiṉṟāḷ. aracaṉ pakalil tuyil koḷḷāṉ. taṉakku māṟāka naṭakkakkūṭiya catikaḷukkup payantu avaṉ aṭikkaṭi taṉatu paṭukkiyi māṟṟuvatuṇṭu. pōrkkālaṅkaḷil maṭṭumallāmal vaḻakkut tīrppaḷittaṟkum avaṉ veḷiyē celkiṉṟāṉ. nīti celuttum maṇṭa pattil avaṉ muḻu nērattiyum celaviṭukiṟāṉ. an nērattil nāṉku vēliyāṭkaḷ avaṉatu uṭampi mara uruḷikaḷāl uriñcukiṉṟaṉar. uruḷikaḷāl uriñcumpōtē avaṉ vaḻakkukaḷi vicāraṇi cey kiṉṟāṉ. vēṭṭiyāṭutaṟkum, vēḷvic cālikaḷiṟ pali celuttu taṟkum avaṉ veḷiyē celkiṉṟāṉ. vēṭṭikkuc cellumpōtu peṇkaḷ palar avaṉic cūḻntu niṟkiṉṟaṉar.1 ivarkaḷukkup puṟattē īṭṭi taritta vīrar cūḻntu niṟkiṉṟaṉar. vītikaḷiṟ kayiṟukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa. kayiṟṟik kaṭantu cellum āṇ allatu peṇṇukku maraṇa taṇṭaṇi vitikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. mēḷam, callari mutaliya vāttiyaṅka ḷōṭu parivāraṅkaḷ celkiṉṟaṉa. aracaṉ vaḷittaṭikkappaṭṭa iṭattē uyaramāṉa iṭattil niṉṟu ampukaḷi eykiṟāṉ. avaṉukkup pakkattil iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu āyutantaritta peṇkaḷ niṟkiṉṟaṉar. veḷi yiṭaṅkaḷil vēṭṭiyāṭumpōtu aracaṉ yāṉiyiṉ ampārimītiruntu ampukaḷi eykiṟāṉ. peṇkaḷ cilar tērmītum cilar yāṉi mītum celkiṉṟaṉar. pōrukkuc celvati oppa avarkaḷ pala āyutaṅkaḷit tāṅkic celkiṉṟaṉar.
aracāṅka amippu
aracāṅka uttiyōkattariṟ cilar cantikaḷiyum, cilar irāṇu vattiyum, cilar nakarattiyum mēṟpārkkiṉṟaṉar. cilar āṟuvaḻiyāka nilaṅkaḷukku nīrpāyccuvatiyum, cilar nila aḷavikaḷiyum, cilar āṟṟiṉ ciṟiya kālvāykaḷāl nīrcellum maṭikaḷiyum kavaṉikkiṉ ṟaṉar. cilar viṟaku veṭṭuvōr, taccuvēli, kol vēli, curaṅkavēli mutaliyaṉa purivōri mēṟpārttu vari aṟa viṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ vīti kaḷiyum amikkiṉṟaṉar. tūrattiyum kiḷippātikaḷiyum aṟivik kum kaṟkaḷ ‘milukku’ oṉṟu naṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
nakara āṭci āṟu capiyiṉarāl naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉatu. mutaṟpiri viṉar kittoḻil toṭarpāṉa ellāvaṟṟiyum kavaṉippar. iraṇṭā vatu piriviṉar piṟa nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum varuvōrik kavaṉittu avarkaḷ taṅkuvataṟkēṟṟa vacatikaḷip purivar. ap putiyavarkaḷukkut tuṇiyāka viruppōr avarkaḷiṉ pōkkik kavaṉittu atikārikaḷukku aṟivik kiṉṟaṉar. piṟa nāṭṭavarkaḷ iṟakka nērntāl avarkaḷiṉ uṭikaḷ uṟavi ṉariṭam cērppikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mūṉṟāvatu piriviṉar nāṭṭil ēṟpaṭum iṟappup piṟappukaḷik kavaṉikkiṉṟaṉar. uyarntōr tāḻntōr eṉṟa vēṟupāṭiṉṟi ellōrukkiṭiyilum nērum iṟappup piṟappukkaḷ tavaṟāmal pativu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nāṉkāvatu piriviṉar cantikaḷik kavaṉikkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷē niṟikaḷukkum aḷavukaḷukkum poṟuppā yuḷḷōr. makkaḷukku viḷamparañ ceytapiṉ kālattiṟ kiṭikkum viḷiporuḷkaḷ pakiraṅkattil viṟkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟukku atikamāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷil vāṇikam puriya evarum aṉumatikkappaṭṭilar. aintāvatu piriviṉar kittoḻiṟ poruḷkaḷik kavaṉikkiṉṟaṉar. putiya poruḷ kaḷum paḻiya poruḷkaḷum vevvēṟāka vittu viṟkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. putiyavaṟṟiyum, paḻiyavaṟṟiyum kalantu viṟpōrukkut taṇṭam vitik kappaṭukiṉṟatu. āṟāvatu piriviṉar vāṇipap poruḷkaḷiṉ viliyiṟ pattiloṉṟi aṟaviṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu āṟu piriviṉarkaḷum kaṭami yāṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ oṉṟucērntu potukkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ puḻakkam, avaṟṟiṉ paḻutupārppu, paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vili, canti, tuṟi mukam, kōyilkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkup poṟuppāyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
nakariṉ niyāya atikārikaḷ irāṇuvatti naṭattuvikkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ aintu uṟuppiṉar aṭaṅkiya āṟu capikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar. oṉṟu, kaṭaṟpaṭi atikāriyōṭu ottuḻikkiṉṟatu. iraṇ ṭāvatu pōrukkuriya iyantiraṅkaḷiyum, pōrvīrariṉ uṇaviyum piṟa poruḷkaḷiyum koṇṭu cellum erutukaḷiyum vaṇṭikaḷiyum kavaṉikkiṉṟatu. itu, vēliyāṭkaḷiyum, paṟi koṭṭuvōriyum, callari pōṭuvōriyum, kutirip pākariyum, iyantiram paḻutu pārp pōriyum avarkaḷiṉ utaviyāḷariyum aḷikkiṉṟatu. callarikaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭum ōciyi aṟintu vēliyāṭkaḷ kutirikaḷukkup pul koṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. mūṉṟāvatu pirivu kālāṭpaṭikkup poṟuppā yirukkiṉṟatu. nālāvatu kutirippaṭikkum, aintāvatu tērkaḷukkum, āṟāvatu yāṉip paṭikkum poṟuppāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. aracaṉiṉ āyutaccāli uṇṭu. ovvoru pōr vīraṉum 1āyutaṅkaḷi āyutac cāliyilum, kutiriyi allatu yāṉiyip pantiyilum viṭṭuc cel kiṉṟāṉ. avarkaḷ yāṉikkuk kaṭivāḷamiṭuvatilli. tērkaḷi erutukaḷ iḻuttuc celkiṉṟaṉa. tēri iḻuttuk kaḷippaṭiyātapaṭi kutirikaḷ pakkattē naṭattic cellappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tērppākaṉiṉ iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷi lum ovvoru vīrar irukkiṉṟaṉar. pōr yāṉi nāṉku vīrarik koṇṭu celkiṉṟatu. oruvaṉ pākaṉum mūvar pōr ceyvōrumāvar.
tattuva cāttirikaḷ
tattuva cāttirikaḷiṉ oru piriviṉar pirācmēṉ (pirāmaṇar) eṉṟum, maṟṟap piriviṉar cārmēṉ1 eṉavum aḻikkappaṭuvar. pirāc mēṉiṉ koḷkikaḷ uṇmiyākavirukkiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, avarkaḷ mikavum matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. karuppattil iruppatu mutal avarkaḷ aṟi vāḷikaḷiṉ pātukāppil irukkiṉṟaṉar. tāyiṉatum kuḻantiyiṉatum nalattukkākac cila kiriyikaḷic ceyvatākap pācāṅku ceytu aṟivāḷikaḷ avaḷukkup pala puttimatikaḷik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ puttimatikaḷi āvalōṭu kēṭkiṉṟavaḷ mikavum puṇṇiyacāli eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉ ṟāḷ. piṟanta piṉ kuḻanti, oruvariṉpiṉ oruvarāka aṟivil uyarnta palariṉ pātukāppil iruntu varukiṟatu.
tattuva cāttirikaḷ, vaḷittaṭitta cōlikaḷiṉ mattiyil vāḻ kiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki eḷitāṉatu; pul, māṉtōl mutaliyavaṟ ṟiṉmītu avarkaḷ paṭukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟicciyip pucippatilli; peṇpōkatti avarkaḷ veṟuppar. avarkaḷ pēcu vatik kēṭpōr muṉamākavum amitiyākavum iruttal vēṇṭum. ip pirāmaṇattit tāṇṭum evarum irakkamiṉṟi ak kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum vilakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu vāḻum ovvoruvarum muppattēḻu āṇṭukaḷiṉpiṉ tattam illaṅkaḷukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar. appoḻutu avarkaḷ aḻakiya maciliṉ (callā) āṭikaḷi uṭukkiṉṟaṉar; viral kaḷil āḻikaḷum, kātiṟ kuṇṭalaṅkaḷum aṇikiṉṟaṉar; toḻil cey kiṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷallāta piṟavaṟṟiṉ ūṉip pucikkiṉṟaṉar. kāramuḷḷatum tāḷitañ ceytatumākiya uṇavi avarkaḷ virumpuvatilli. ettaṉi maṉiviyariyum avarkaḷ maṇantukoḷḷalām. aṭimikaḷiṉmiyiṉ kūṭiya toki maṉiviyarkaḷiyum, piḷḷikaḷiyum uṭiyarāyirut tal tamatu vāḻkkikku vacatiyākumeṉa avarkaḷ eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.
pirācmēṉiyar cāttira uṇmikaḷit tam maṉiviyarukkuc collikkoṭuppatilli. piṟarukku maṟikkappaṭṭa avvuṇmikaḷi veḷippaṭutti avaṟṟiṉ tūymiyik keṭuttuviṭuvārkaḷ, allatu uṇmi aṟivu mikuntu tammi viṭṭup pirintuviṭuvar eṉa avarkaḷ añcukiṉṟaṉar. nalla maṉiviyarum, kaṇavaṉum iṉpatuṉpaṅkaḷic camamākap pāvittu vāḻkki naṭattukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi pēcum poruḷkaḷuḷ maraṇam oṉṟākum. piṟappippōla iṟappum varum eṉṟu avarkaḷ cāttiraṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. maṉitaṉukku nērum iṉpatuṉpaṅka ḷellām kaṉavu pōṉṟa māyi eṉṟum, illāviṭiṉ oru poruḷ cilarukkut tuṉpattiyum vēṟu cilarukku iṉpattiyum koṭukkamuṭiyā teṉṟum, orē poruḷ vevvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil nērmāṟāṉa nukarvu kaḷik koṭukkak kāraṇameṉṉaveṉṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
iyaṟki nikaḻcciyik kuṟitta aḷavil avarkaḷ kuṟivāṉa viḷakkamuṭiyavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kaṭṭukkatikaḷi nampukiṉṟaṉar; ūki ārāyvilum pārkkac cātaṉiyil tiṟamiyuṭiya varkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ koḷkikaḷ palavakiyil kirēkkar koḷkikaḷōṭu oṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kirēkkarippōlavē avarkaḷum ulakam oru kālattil uṟpattiyāṉa teṉṟum, atu uruṇṭi vaṭiviṉateṉṟum, ataṉi uṇṭākkiya kaṭavuḷ ataṉ ellāp pākaṅkaḷilum ceṟintirukkiṟāreṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iv vulakiṉ uṟpattikku mutaṟ kāraṇamuṇṭeṉṟum, atu nīriṉiṉṟu uṇṭā yiṟṟeṉṟum, nilam, tī, nīr, kāṟṟu eṉṉum nālu pūtaṅkaḷiviṭa aintāvatu pūtam oṉṟu uṇṭeṉṟum, atiṉiṉṟum vāṉamum naṭcattiraṅkaḷum uṇṭāyiṉaveṉṟum avarkaḷ nampukiṉṟaṉar. pūmi ulakiṉ mattiyil iruppatākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ciruṭṭi uyiriṉ ilakkaṇam ivi pōṉṟa pala poruḷkaḷ kirēkkar koḷkikaḷōṭu ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. cārmēṉkaḷiṉ hilēpiyōy eṉṉum piriviṉar mikavum mariyāti kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kāṭṭil vacittu aṅkuk kiṭikkum ili kaḷiyum, paḻaṅkaḷiyum pucikkiṉṟaṉar: marappaṭṭiyāl titta uṭiyi uṭukkiṉṟaṉar. peṇ pōkamum, matuvum, ūṉum avarkaḷukku vilakku. aracaṉ tūtuvar mūlam ivarkaḷiṭam taṉakku vēṇṭiya ālōcaṉi kaḷik kēṭkiṟāṉ; ivarkaḷ mūlam kaṭavuḷukku vaḻipāṭu ceyvikkiṟāṉ.
ivarkaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyiluḷḷōr maruttuvar. ivarkaḷ maṉitaṉiṉ iyaṟkiyip paṟṟiya ārāycci ceykiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ eḷiyavi. avarkaḷ vayalkaḷil vacippatilli. avarkaḷiṉ uṇavu ariciyum piṟavum. ivaṟṟi avarkaḷ evariṭamēṉum kēṭṭup peṟṟukkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar; allatu viruntāḷikaḷākat taṅku miṭaṅkaḷiṟ peṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kalikaḷiṉ ciṟappiṉāl makiḻccikkuriya maṇaṅkaḷi oḻuṅkupaṭuttuvar; piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷ āṇ allatu peṇ eṉṟu muṉ aṟintu kūṟuvar. avarkaḷ uṇavu muṟi kaḷāl nōyi māṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. marunti arumiyākap payaṉpaṭuttu kiṉṟaṉar; maṟṟiya muṟikaḷ iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉ ṟaṉar. ivarkaḷum vēṟu cila vakuppiṉarum nāḷmuṟṟum aciyātu ōriṭattil niṟṟalpōṉṟa paḻakkaṅkaḷic ceykiṉṟaṉar.
ivarkaḷiyaṉṟi mantira vittikkārarum vēṭam pūṇṭōru mirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ tāmum uyarnta tiruttamuṭiyavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. maṟu ulakattippaṟṟiya niṉivu aṉpiyum tūymiyiyumuṇṭākkumeṉṟu avarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ cilariṭamiruntu peṇkaḷ camaya cāttiraṅ kaṟkiṉṟaṉar.
pāṭaliputrā
kaṅkiyāṟṟiṉ akalam 100 srēṭiyā.1 ataṉ kuṟinta āḻam 120 aṭi. kaṅkānatiyum iṉṉoru āṟum kūṭum iṭattil ‘palipōttirā’ eṉṉum oru nakar irukkiṟatu. itaṉ nīḷam eṇpatu srēṭiyā; akalam patiṉintu srēṭiyā. atu nīṇṭa catura vaṭivamāṉatu. atic cuṟṟi maratti ṉāl iṭappaṭṭa araṇ uṇṭu. ampukaḷ eyyakkūṭiyatākac cuvariṉ iṭiyiṭiyē cantukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. cuvariṉ muṉṉē akaḻi oṉṟu irukkiṉṟatu. nakariṉ kaḻivu nīr itaṉuḷ viḻukiṉṟatu. iṅku vāḻvōr intiyā muḻumiyilum vāḻvōriṉum ciṟantōr. aracaṉ taṉatu kuṭumpap peyarōṭu ‘palipōttirā’ allatu ‘cantira kōttirā’ eṉṉum peyaric cērttukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
akaḻikaḷ 600 aṭi akalamum, 30 muḻa āḻamumuṭiyaṉa. matil 570 kōpuraṅkaḷum aṟupattu nāṉku vāyilkaḷum uṭiyatāyiruntatu.1 intiya makkaḷ cutantira vāḻkkiyiṉar. aṭimikaḷ oruvarum kāṇappaṭa villi.
pāṇṭiya cātiyiṉar peṇkaḷāl āḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ mutal araci kekuliciṉ putalvi eṉappaṭukiṉṟāḷ. in nāṭṭiluḷḷa nicā (Nysa) eṉṉum paṭṭiṉamum mēṟkūṟiyappaṭi peyaruṭiya maliyum yūpitar kaṭavuḷiṉ paricutta iṭaṅkaḷ, immaliyiluḷḷa kukiyil pakkas kaṭavuḷ vaḷarntār eṉṟu intiyar uṟutiyākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ekulis, taṉatu putalvikkuk koṭutta nāṭṭi 365 kirāmaṅ kaḷākap pirittu ovvoru kirāmattiyum muṟiyē varuṭattiṉ 365 nāḷum tiṟi koṭukkumpaṭi vitittār.2
pakkas (Father Bacchus) kālam mutal alakcāntar kālam variyil pāṇṭināṭṭi āṇṭa aracar 154 pēr. ivarkaḷiṉ āṭci 6431 āṇṭu 3 tiṅkaḷ naṭipeṟṟatu.
tapirapēṉ eṉṉum tīvu intiyāviṉiṉṟum oru ciṟiya āṟṟiṉāṟ pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. aṅku intiyāviṟ kiṭippatilum atika poṉṉum muttum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. aṅkuḷḷa yāṉikaḷ intiya yāṉi kaḷilum periyaṉa. tapirapēṉ 7000 srēṭiyā nīḷamum, 5000 srēṭiyā akalamumuḷḷatu. aṅku 700 kirāmaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. kaṭal ōraṅkaḷil āmikaḷ atikam. avikaḷiṉ ōṭu 15 muḻa nīḷam. tāmpiraparṇi eṉṉum kaṭaluḷ irukkum it tīvil teṉṉikaḷ niriyāka naṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅku yāṉikaḷum atikam. at tīviṉ makkaḷ avikaḷip piṭittuk kaliṅka tēcattukku aṉuppukiṉṟaṉar. kari ōraṅkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ mīṉ piṭittu vāḻvar. ak kaṭalkaḷil ciṅkattali vilaṅkut talikaḷuḷḷa mīṉkaḷ ulāvukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷiṉ taliyuṭiya mīṉkaḷum aṅku uṇṭu; avikaḷukku mayirukkup patil muṭkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

iyal - 5
tamiḻ vēntarum avar āṭciyum
piṟkālat tamiḻnāṭṭuk kalveṭṭukaḷālum paṭṭayaṅkaḷālum tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇa muṭiyāta pala ariya ceytikaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. araciyal toṭarpāṉa taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭutaliṉ avvāṭci muṟi mikap paḻimi toṭṭē tamiḻnāṭṭil naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟa teṉat tuṇiyalām.
tamiḻnāṭṭilē mūvēntaratu āṭci muṟiyum orē vakiyāka naṭi peṟṟatu. ellā atikāramum aracaṉukku iruntatu. avaṉukku uṟutikūṟum amiccar nakarap perumakkaḷ mutaliya cuṟṟattiṉar iruntaṉar. araṇ maṉiyil ēval purivōr pala kūṭṭattiṉarākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉar. camiyal aṟi kuḷikkum aṟikaḷiṟ perumpālum āṭavarē vēli purintaṉar. vēliyāṭkaḷ vēḷam eṉṉum kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum terin teṭukkappaṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ, pōriṟ ciṟi piṭikkappaṭṭavarkaḷāvar.
cōyukuvā eṉṉum cīṉaṉ, cōḻar āṭciyip paṟṟi eḻutu miṭattuk kuṟippiṭṭiruppatu varumāṟu: araṇmaṉi viruntuk kālaṅ kaḷil aracaṉum nāṉku amiccarkaḷum ciṅkācaṉattiṉ aṭiyi vaṇaṅku kiṟārkaḷ; uṭaṉē aṅkuk kuḻumiyiruppōr vāttiyaṅkaḷi olittu āṭal pāṭalkaḷit toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. aracaṉ matu aruntamāṭṭāṉ; ūṉ aruntuvāṉ. nāṭṭu vaḻakkappaṭi avaṉ pañcu āṭiyi aṇintu koṇṭu tāṉiya māviṉāṟ ceyta paṇiyāraṅkaḷi uṇkiṟāṉ. uṇa varuntum aṟiyiṟ cēvippataṟkum taṉṉic cūḻntu tirivataṟkum avaṉ tiraḷāṉa naṭaṉa mātari vēlikku amarttiyirukkiṟāṉ. nāḷ oṉṟukku muṟi muṟiyāka avaṉic cēvikkum pēr mūvāyiram mātar.1 aracaṉukkum tēvikkum taṉic cēvakam puriyum paṇiyāḷarumiruntaṉar.
paṭiyil yāṉi kutiri vīrar, kālāḷ, tērvīrar eṉpōr irunta ṉar. yāṉivīrar yāṉiyāṭkaḷ kuñcaramaḷḷar eṉavum, kutiri vīrar kutiric cēvakar eṉavum, kālāṭpaṭiyiṉar kikōḷap perum paṭi yiṉar eṉavum, villāḷar villikaḷ eṉavum, vāṭ paṭiyiṉar vāṭpeṟṟa kikkōḷar eṉavum paṭṭaṉar. yutta kālattil vēlikku amarttik koḷḷappaṭum vīrar aracaṉiṉ valaṅki eṉappaṭṭaṉar. nakaraṅkaḷil paṭi taṅkumiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭakaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. paṭikaḷiṉ talivaṉ paṭittalivaṉ cēṉāpati allatu paṭimutali eṉappaṭṭāṉ. vari taṇṭum karumi (mēl uttiyōkattaṉ) nāṭuvāki eṉappaṭṭāṉ. vari taṇṭum vēliyil pōr vīrarkaḷum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉar. kālvaḻiyāka varum paṭi mūlappaṭi eṉappaṭṭatu.
mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi cīṉa āciriyaṉ 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil eḻutiyiruppatu varumāṟu: iv virācciyattil (cōḻa nāṭṭil) patiṉāyiram yāṉikaḷ niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoṉṟum ēḻu allatu eṭṭu muḻam uyara muṭiyatu. pōr puriyumpōtu avi mutukiṉmītu vīṭukaḷikkoṇṭu tirikiṉṟaṉa. vīṭukaḷ nirampa vīrarkaḷ iruntu ampukaḷi eykiṉṟaṉar. veṟṟiyaṭintapiṉ yāṉikaḷukkuk kuravap paṭṭaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. nāḷum avi aracaṉukkumuṉ koṇṭuvarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pōril koḷḷiyaṭikkappaṭum poruḷ aracaṉukkuc conta māṉatu.
aracaṉatu vāy uttaravu ‘tiruvāyk kēḷvi’ eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉik kēṭṭu eḻutum karumikaḷ palar avaṉuṭaṉ iruntaṉar. eḻutuvōṉ ‘tīṭṭukkāraṉ’ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. aracaṉ vaḻakkukaḷi nēriṟ kēṭṭu nāṭṭu vaḻakkappaṭi tīrppaḷittāṉ. periya karumikaḷukku ēṉāti, mārāyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. mārāyaṉ eṉpataṉ peṇ pāl mārāci. uyarnta karumikaḷ atikārikaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. mēl kīḻ atikārikaḷik kuṟikkap ‘peruntaram’ ‘ciṟutaram’ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅ kiṉa. uttiyōkattar ‘karumikaḷ’ eṉavum, vēliyāṭkaḷ ‘paṇi makkaḷ’ eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉar. niyāyātipatikaḷ ‘niyāyattār’ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. oru kirāmattiṉ allatu ataṉ pakutiyiṉ kaṭamikaḷip pārkkum karumikaḷ ‘uṭiyār’ allatu ‘kiḻār’ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ovvoru kirāmamum peru makkaḷ kūṭṭaṅkaḷāl āḷappaṭṭatu. kirāmaṅkaḷ pala cērntatu kūṟṟam, nāṭu allatu kōṭṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. pala kūṟṟaṅkaḷ cērntatu oru vaḷa nāṭu. vaḷa nāṭṭilum periyatu maṇṭalam.
1nāṭṭu āṭci
mācaṉaṅkaḷum caṉaṅkaḷum kūṭṭaṅkaḷum araciyaṟ karumaṅkaḷi naṭattiṉa. ovvoru kūṭṭamum taṉittaṉi ovvor karumattik kavaṉit tatu. kirāmaṅkaḷ, cērikaḷ, terukkaḷ eṉṉum vakiyākap pirivukaḷ cey yappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru teruviluḷḷōrum taṉittaṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷākac cērntu ovvoru karumattik kavaṉittārkaḷ. aṟac cottukaḷi mēṟpārvi ceytōr ‘tāṉattār’ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. tirukkōyilkaḷuk kuḷḷa nilaṅkaḷukku varikaḷ nīkkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kirāmam allatu paṭṭiṉam, ūr eṉappaṭṭatu. ūr, āḷaṉkaṇam eṉṉum kaṇattāl āḷappaṭṭatu. ūr āḷvār eṉpatum ak kūṭṭattiṉ peyar. ik kūṭṭattiṉ kīḻ ūrvāriyam eṉṉum kūṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ic col vāri (iṉam) eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. taṉippaṭṭa kaṭamikaḷip pārkka ik kūṭṭattārāl amikkappaṭṭōr vāriyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kūṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ kāriyakkūṭṭa uṟuppiṉar ‘vāriyap perumakkaḷ’ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ūriṉ ovvoru pirivum ‘kuṭumpu’ (Ward) eṉappaṭṭatu. ovvoru kuṭumpilum uḷḷa uṟuppiṉar ‘kuṭōli’ vāyilākat terinteṭukkappaṭṭaṉar.2 periya kuṟṟam ‘mēṟpaṭu kuṟṟam’ eṉappaṭṭatu. viṉikkēṭu (keṭṭa naṭatti) uḷḷavarkaḷum kikkūli vāṅkuvōrum taṇṭikkappaṭṭaṉar. ūravar kaḷiṭam uḷvari (maṟivāka vāṅkum vari) vāṅkutal ūrkkukkaravu (turōkam) ceytalākak karutappaṭṭatu.3 capi mācapi eṉpaṉa muṟiyē kuṟi, peruṅkuṟi eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉa. uṟuppiṉar perumakkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. capikaḷ perumpālum kōyil maṇṭapaṅkaḷiṟ kūṭiṉa. capi paṟi cāṟṟik kūṭṭappaṭṭatu. capi kūṭṭuvatu (cēmak) kalam allatu iraṭṭik kalam taṭṭi aṟivikkappaṭṭatu.
aracaṉatu vāykkaṭṭaḷi (tirumukam) tirumantira ōli eṉṉum karumiyāl eḻutappaṭṭup pottakattiṟ pukuttumuṉ ‘tirumantira ōli nāyakam’ eṉpavaṉāl oppu nōkkappaṭṭatu. piṉpu ōli nāyakam variyiḻuttuk koḷka eṉak karumi oruvaṉukkuk kaṭṭaḷiyiṭa, atu pottakattiṟ pukuttappaṭṭut tīṭṭu (Document) āṉatu. ēṟpāṭukaḷ (āvaṇaṅkaḷ) toṭarpākap puravu vari, varip pottakam, mukaveṭṭi, varip pottakak kaṇakku, variyiḻuttu, kaṇkāṇi, paṭṭōli, naṭuvirukki mutaliya karumikaḷ iruntaṉar.
nāṭṭiluḷḷa capikaḷukku aṉuppappaṭum ceyti ōlikaḷ ‘tirumukam’ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ‘naṭuvirukki’ eṉpavaṉ maṉuc ceypavaṉukkum karumikkum iṭiyil niṉṟu ceytikaḷi aṟivippavaṉ. āvaṇatti eṉpavaṉ kāriya (Executive) uttiyōkattaṉ. varip pottakam nāṭṭu vari toṭarpāṉa kaṇakkukaḷ tīṭṭappaṭum pottakam. ataṟkup poṟuppā yuḷḷa karumiyum ap peyarāl aṟiyappaṭṭāṉ. puravuvari eṉpatu nilavari. puravuvarit tiṇikkaḷam eṉpatu nilavarip pakuti (Land Tax Department). vaḻakkāḷiyiṉ vākku mūlam muṟippāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. nilam toṭarpāṉa vaḻakkukaḷ āṭci āvaṇam mutaliyavikaḷi nōkki muṭivu ceyyap paṭṭaṉa.
makkaṭ pirivu
makkaḷ toḻiṟ pirivukaḷ paṟṟik kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamākap pirintu vāḻntapōtum potuk karumaṅkaḷil ottuḻittārkaḷ. 1īḻavar ak kālattil paṉi teṉṉikaḷil ēṟi matu iṟakkavilli. varuṇam eṉpatu teṉṉintiya makkaḷāṟ kikkoḷḷappaṭavilli. cila piriviṉar ciṟcilavaṟṟic ceytal kūṭāteṉṉum vaḻakku iruntatu. itu kammā ḷarukkuc cōḻa aracaṉ eḻutiyaḷitta paṭṭayamoṉṟāl viḷaṅkukiṟatu. vīṭṭil naṭakkum naṉmi tīmikaḷil iraṇṭu caṅku olittal, mēḷamaṭit tal, vīṭṭi viṭṭu veḷiyē cellumpōtu mitiyaṭitarittal, vīṭṭukku veḷḷiyaṭittal iraṇṭu māṭi vīṭu kaṭṭutal vīṭṭiṉ muṉpuṟattit tāmari āmpal mutaliya pūmālikaḷāl alaṅkarittal pōṉṟa cakā yaṅkaḷ appaṭṭayattil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. toḻilāḷarkaḷiṭiyē ‘valaṅki’ ‘iṭaṅki’ eṉṉum kaṭcikaḷ iruntaṉa. iṭaṅkiyiṉar pirāma ṇarkaḷiṉ mitiyaṭikaḷiyum kuṭikaḷiyum tūkkic ceṉṟaṉar.
aracarum perumakkaḷum pala maṉiviyaruṭiyarāyiruntaṉar. maṉiviyar 1uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟum vaḻakkam iruntatu. varalāṟṟuk kālat tukku muṉtoṭṭu āṭal pāṭal mātar mikavum kavarccikkuriyavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷ. viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ varavu mikavum varavēṟpuk kuriyatāyiruntatu. avarkaḷ āṭavarkaḷuṭaṉ tārāḷamākap paḻakiṉārkaḷ. ampalaṅkaḷil (Rest Houses) taṇṇīr vikkappaṭṭiruntatu.
variyum varuvāyum
aracaṉatu niti niliyam talam eṉappaṭṭatu. nitiyiṟ perumpālaṉa, nakikaḷākavum viliyuyarnta maṇikaḷākavum iruntaṉa. apucā ceṭ (Apuza Zed) eṉpavaṉ pattām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutiyiruppatu varumāṟu: taṅkattil irattiṉakkaṟkaḷ patitta kuḻikaḷi intiya aracar aṇi kiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kaḻuttil irattiṉam viṭūriyam mutaliyavikaḷāṟ ceyta mālikaḷ ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, muttukkaḷ mikavum vili yēṟappeṟṟavi. aracariṉ nitic cēmippup perumpālum muttukkaḷā yirukkiṉṟatu. atikārikaḷum uyarnta karumikaḷum aracaṉ aṇivatu pōṉṟa viliyuyarnta muttu mālikaḷi aṇikiṟārkaḷ.
vari perumpālum iṟi eṉappaṭṭatu. paṇam iṟukkumpaṭi vitik kappaṭum taṇṭaṉi taṇṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. āyam eṉṉum collum iṟi eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. aracaṉatu vāyil ‘tirukkoṟṟa vāyil’ eṉappaṭṭatu.
mattiya aracic cēravēṇṭiya nilavarikkuk kirāma capikaḷ poṟuppāyiruntaṉa. varikaḷ nila viḷivukaḷiliruntum piṟa vakikaḷi liruntum vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. 2veṭṭi eṉpatu kūli illāta kaṭṭāya vēli. nakaravari (ūriṭuvari), ērivari, vētaṉivari (aṇikaṭṭum kaṭṭāya vēli), cilavari, makaṉmivari, pērvari (Poll Tax), kārttiki yarici, kārttikip pacci (kācu āyam), kaṭi iṟi, paṭikāval, nakarkāval eṉap pala varikaḷ iruntaṉa.
cila nēraṅkaḷil vari koṭātārit taṇṇīriliṭṭum veyilil niṟuttiyum vētaṉi koṭuttum vari vāṅkappaṭṭatu.
vāṇikam
viyāpārattiṉ poruṭṭu makkaḷ iṭaṅkaḷtōṟum ceṉṟārkaḷ. periya terukkaḷ ‘peruvaḻi’ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. paṇṭaṅkaḷ kaḻuti, poti māṭu, erumikaḷil koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa. paṇam kaṭaṉ koṭuttavar, vāṅkiyavariṭattiṟ peṟum cīṭṭu (Pronote) ‘kiyeḻuttōli’ eṉap paṭṭatu. tamiḻ viyāpārikaḷ malāyat tīpakaṟpam. intu cīṉam, cīṉā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟaṉar. māvalipuram, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam, cāliyūr, koṟki mutaliyaṉa kiḻakkilum, kollam mēṟkilum periya viyāpārap patikaḷāyiruntaṉa. ilaṅki nikkavāram (Nicobars) ilakka tīvu, māli tīvukaḷ mēṟkilum, kappalkaḷ taṅkutaṟku ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷāyiruntaṉa. cīṉa nāvāykaḷ maliyāḷakkariyiluḷḷa kollamvariyiṟ ceṉṟaṉa. arāpiyāviliruntu kutiri viyāpārikaḷ kutirikaḷikkoṇṭu vantaṉar.
aṭimikaḷ
ak kālattil paṇattukku viṟṟu vāṅkappaṭum aṭimikaḷ irun tārkaḷ. uyarkulattavarkaḷum cila camayaṅkaḷil aṭimikaḷāka vantārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ perumpālum tammik kōyilkaḷukku viṟṟārkaḷ. aṭimi kaḷi viṟṟu vāṅkuvataṟku araciyal āvaṇaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. kōyilkaḷukku viṟkappaṭum peṇkaḷ ‘tēvarāṭṭiyar’ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. vēliyāṭkaḷukkuk kūli tāṉiyaṅkaḷākavē koṭukkappaṭṭatu.
kirāmam
kirāmaṅkaḷil makkaḷ eḷiyavāḻkki naṭattiṉar. paṇṇikkāraṉ allatu nilam vittiruppōṉ mēlāṉa nilimiyil iruntāṉ. kirāma capikaḷ perumpālum nilam vittirukkum perumakkaḷiṉ kūṭṭamā yiruntatu. kirāmaṅkaḷic cuṟṟic cila pakutikaḷ ūrp potuvākaviruntaṉa. vari yiṟukkum makkaḷ kirāma āṭciyil perum paṅku paṟṟiṉar. ovvoru kirāmattilum kālvaḻiyākavarum vēliyāṭkaḷ iruntaṉar. cēkkiḻār, periya purāṇattil nantaṉārippaṟṟik kūṟum pakuti, akkālak kirāma vāḻkkikku eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. tāṉiyam cērttu vikkappaṭum iṭam ‘koṭṭakāram’ eṉappaṭṭatu. nilam ceyvōṉ kāṇiyāḷaṉukkuk koṭukkum pākam mēlvāram eṉappaṭṭatu.
aḷavikaḷ
nilaṅkaḷ kōlāl aḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. oru kōl 16 cāṇ; 18 kōl koṇṭatu oru kuḻi; 256 kuḻi koṇṭatu oru mā; 20 mā koṇṭatu oru vēli. iṭaṅkaḷtōṟum kuḻi aḷavu māṟupaṭṭatu.
āṇṭu 360 nāṭkaḷākavum, cila kālaṅkaḷil 365 nāṭkaḷākavum kaṇakkuc ceyyappaṭṭatu.
tāṉiya aḷavi
2 ceviṭu orupiṭi; 5 ceviṭu ōr āḻākku; 2 āḻākku 1 uḻakku;
2 uḻakku 1 uri; 2 uri oru nāḻi; 8 nāḻi oru kuṟuṇi; 2 kuṟuṇi 1 patakku;
2 patakku 1 tūṇi allatu kāṭi; 3 tūṇi oru kalam.
poṉ aḷavi
oru mañcāṭi 2 kuṉṟi; 1 kuṉṟi 5 mā; 1 mā 4 kāṇi; (oru mañcāṭi=2 kuṟuṇi=10 mā=40 kāṇi) 20 mañcāṭi oru kaḻañcu (68 mutal 72 tāṉiya eṭi;) mā 1\20-yum kāṇi 1\80-yum kuṟikkum. paṇa eṭi eṉpatu 1\10 kaḻañcu.
nīṭṭalaḷavi
8 tōri (tāṉiyam) oru viral; 12 viral oru cāṇ; 2 cāṇ
oru muḻam.
paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ
muṟkālam paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ marattaṭi, 1kōyiṟcāli allatu maṭaṅkaḷ allatu uvāttiyāyar vīṭṭut tiṇṇiyiṟ kūṭiṉa. pēr periya tiṇṇiccaṭṭampiyār eṉpatu iṉṟum vaḻakku. āciriyaṉukku māṇavar uṟṟuḻi utaviyum uṟuporuḷ koṭuttum kalvi payiṉṟaṉar. ēṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭa puttakaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ēṭum eḻuttāṇiyum kaṇak kāyarāṟ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟuvar maṇalil eḻutip payiṉṟārkaḷ. āciriyaṉiṉ mutal māṇavaṉ avaṉukku utaviyāḷaṉāyiruntāṉ; avaṉ caṭṭāmpiḷḷi eṉappaṭṭāṉ. māṇavar avaṉukku aṭaṅki naṭan tārkaḷ. kāliyil māṇavar marattaṭiyilō tiṇṇiyilō kūṭiṉārkaḷ. āciriyaṉ vantatum avarkaḷ ellōrum eḻuntu iruveṉa iruntu avaṉ kūṟuvatik karuttākak kēṭṭup payiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyar kālattil iv vakip paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷē iruntaṉa. māṇākkar āṇ ṭil oru muṟiyō iru muṟiyō uvāttiyāyarukku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟi (perumpālum tam vayalkaḷilum kollikaḷilum kiṭippavikaḷi) kiyuṟiyākak koṭuttaṉar. māṇākkaṉ kalviyi niṟuttuṅkāl periya kūṭṭaṅkūṭi avaṉ payiṉṟa nūlic capiyōrukkup piracaṅkippittu avaṉ tiṟamiyik kāṇpippatum marapāyiruntatu.
ilaṅki aracar varalāṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭuvaṉa cila
ilaṅki aracar perumpālum tamiḻarkaḷ allatu tamiḻ irattak kalappuṭiyavarkaḷāka iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ araṇmaṉip paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ tamiḻ aracaruṭiyaṉapōlavē iruntaṉa. aracaṉukkup pakkattil ‘valaṅki’ ‘iṭaṅki’ eṉṉum perumakkaḷ iruntaṉar. cēṉāpati aracaṉukku aṭuttapaṭiyil atikāra muṭiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ. araṇmaṉic camiyalaṟiyil āṭavarē vēli ceytaṉar. camiyalaṟi yil puḻaṅkum taṭṭukkaḷum pāttiraṅkaḷum veḷḷi taṅkam eṉpavi kaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavi. nāṭōṟum kāliyil maruttuvaṉ aracaṉatu uṭal niliyic cōtittu ataṟkēṟṟa uṇavukaḷic camikkumpaṭi paṭṭōli eḻutic camiyalaṟit talivaṉiṭam koṭuttāṉ. uṇavu camittatum aracaṉ uṇpataṟku oru maṇi nērattukku muṉ camiya laṟit talivaṉukkuc, camitta ellāvaki uṇavilum ciṟitu ciṟitu eṭuttuk koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṉ uṇṭu oru maṇi nērattukkuppiṉ aracaṉ uṇavu aruntiṉāṉ. akkāla aracar uṇavil nañcu iṭappaṭu vatik kuṟittup peritum añciṉar.1 aracaṉukkuc cōṟum, nūṟu (pala) vakiyāṉa kaṟikaḷum camikkappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ avikaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟi maṭṭum koṇṭu varumpaṭi ceytu uṇṭāṉ. avaṉukkuc camayalaṟiyil cēvippōrellōrum vāyit tuṇiyāṟ kaṭṭiyiruntaṉar. periyōrkku muṉ kikaṭṭi vāy putittu niṟṟal eṉpaṉa paḻiya marapukaḷ. iṉṟum katirkāmat tirukōyiliṟ pūci ceyyum ciṅkaḷa kurumār pūci nērattil tamatu vāyit tuṇiyāṟ kaṭṭikkoḷvar. purōkitaṉ, aracaṉ veḷiyē celvataṟkum piṟa karumaṅkaḷic ceyvataṟkum nalla nēram aṟintu kūṟiṉāṉ. aracaṉik kāṇac celvōr avaṉip pārttapiṉ ciṟitu tūram piṉ nōkki naṭantu ceṉṟu appāl tirumpic celvar. araca ṉukkup piṉkāṭṭic celvatu mariyātikkuṟivu eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu. makkaḷ aracaṉuṭaṉ pēcumpōtu tammi aṭimikaḷ (aṭiyēṉ) nāykaḷ (nāyēṉ) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭup pēcuvārkaḷ.2

iyal - 6
kiḻakkē tamiḻariṉ kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷ
kiṟittava āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉar tamiḻmakkaḷ cumattirā, yāvā, pāli mutaliya tīvukaḷilum, parmā, cīyam, intuc cīṉam, malāy, kuṭānāṭu mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum kuṭiyēṟi irācciyaṅkaḷik kōli āṭcipurintaṉar. ak kālattil ak kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷil civamatam paraviyiruntatu. an nāṭukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟa paḻiya aḻipāṭukaḷum kalveṭṭukaḷum paḻaṅkatikaḷum naṉku ārāyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avikaḷi ātāramākak koṇṭu annāṭṭu varalāṟukaḷ eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. av varalāṟu kaḷāl ak kālattuk kaṭal kaṭantu ceṉṟa tamiḻarippaṟṟi yām naṉku aṟital kūṭum. maṇimēkali eṉṉum tamiḻnūl (ki.pi. 200) cāvakat tīvilē nākapurattiliruntu aracāṇṭa pūmicantiraṉ, puṇṇiyarācaṉ eṉṉum aracar iruvarik kuṟippiṭuvatōṭu tamiḻvaṇikar marakkalaṅkaḷil carakkukaḷi ēṟṟit toliviṭaṅkaḷiṟ koṇṭu ceṉṟu viṟṟup perum poruḷīṭṭiṉārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
tiritirayatā eṉṉum pirāmaṇaṉ kampōtiyāviṉiṉṟum eṇṇūṟu kiḷiṅkuk (Kling) kuṭumpaṅkaḷōṭu ceṉṟu piramāri kāḷi eṉṉum yāvaka māti maṇantu an nāṭṭukku aracaṉāṉāṉ eṉa an nāṭṭup paḻaṅ katikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. yāvakattiṉ mutal aracaṉukku srīmāṟaṉ eṉṟu peyar. ip peyar cāvaka nāṭṭiṉ araca parampari tamiḻ uṟpattiyuṭiya teṉpati naṉku viḷakkum. yāvāviluḷḷa perok eṉṉum makkaḷiṭiyē iṉṟum tirāviṭak kuṭumpap peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.1 yāvāvilē mikap periya cila ālayaṅkaḷiṉ aḻipāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḻiya purāṇaṅkaḷil yāvakat tīvu, cuvarṇa pūmi eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. yāvakat tīvilum ataṉi aṭuttuḷḷa tīvukaḷilum kāṇappaṭum aḻipāṭukaḷālum, kiṭittuḷḷa paḻamporuḷkaḷālum piṟavaṟṟālum aṟiyakkiṭappaṉa piṉvarumāṟu: 1yāvāt tīviṟ kāṇappaṭum tirukkōyilkaḷuṭ pala civaṉukku uriyaṉa. yāvaka makkaḷiṉ amaramāli eṉṉum nūl paḻami yuṭiyatu. aḵtu am makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa teyvaṅkaḷippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉ ṟatu. an nūliṉāl an nāṭṭiṉar civaṉiyē talimiyākakkoṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷeṉat terikiṉṟatu. civaṉ, kuru, īsvaraṉ eṉṉum peyar kaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. cumattiyāvilē lārā yoṅṟāṅ (Lara Yongrang) eṉṉumiṭattil ālayak kūṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ periya aḻipāṭu uḷḷatu. itaṉ naṭuvil periya civaṉ kōyilum, irumaruṅkilum piramaṉ, tirumāl ālayaṅkaḷum etirē nantiyiṉ ālayamum uḷḷaṉa. valappāti civaṉum iṭappāti umiyumākavuḷḷa arttanārīcuvarat tiruvaṭivamum aṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. vināyaka vikkirakaṅkaḷ mikap pala iṅkuḷḷaṉa. ivi intiya ālayaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum tiruvuruvaṅkaḷic cālavum ottaṉa.
pōrk kaṭavuḷākiya kārttikēyak kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvuruvaṅkaḷum yāvāviṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṉita vaṭivam mayilmītu ēṟiccelvatu pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷē perumpālum uḷḷaṉa. āṟu taliyum paṉṉi raṇṭu kiyumuḷḷa tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ cilavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. civaṉ iliṅka vaṭivilum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār.
caṅku cakkaram tāmari kēṭakam eṉpavikaḷip piṭittirukkum nāṉku kikaḷuṭiya tirumāliṉ tiruvuruvaṅkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. pāmpiṉmītu paṭuttut tuyil koḷḷum tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ cilavum iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ilakkumiyum cattiyapāmiyum iru pakkaṅkaḷilum niṟkum tiruvuruvam pālit tīviṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. pūmāli cāmari tāmari karakam eṉpavikaḷik kikaḷil vittirukkum nāṉku mukamuṭiya piramāviṉ vikkirakam yāvāviṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cēram (Seram) eṉṉum pakutiyil amarāya eṉṉumiṭattil mummūrttikaḷiṉ tiruvuruvaṅkaḷōṭu poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa civaṉ tiruvuruvam oṉṟuṅ kiṭittatu.
celipiciyil (Celebes) civa vaṇakkattukkuriya ciṉṉaṅkaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. an nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ paḻaṅkatikaḷil civaṉ pārata kuru eṉappaṭukiṉṟār. kiṟāpāṭ (Crawfurd) eṉpār niyukiṉiyiṟ kāṇap paṭum camayakkiriyikaḷ civavaḻipāṭṭiṉ tēyvukaḷ eṉak kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. mēṟkup pōrṉiyōvil nanti, kaṇēcar, iliṅkam mutaliya uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ivikaḷuṭaṉ oru poṉ iliṅkamum kāṇappaṭṭatu iṅkuk kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
1malāyk kuṭā nāṭṭiluḷḷa tīvukkuc ciṅkapuram eṉṉum peyar an nāṭṭu makkaḷāṟ koṭukkappaṭṭataṉṟu; yāvāt tīviṟ kuṭiyēṟi aṅku niṉṟum veḷippōnta teṉṉintiya makkaḷāl iṭappaṭṭatu. ataṟkut tamacak allatu tamacā2 eṉṉum peyar iruntateṉak koḷḷi ṉum, atuvum intiya uṟpattikku uṭiyatākum. cumattirā eṉpatum intiya makkaḷāṟ koṭukkappaṭṭa peyarē. cumattirā eṉpataṟku nalla poruḷ eṉṟa karuttu.3 ki.pi. 1601 mutal ciṅkapuram ciṅkappūr eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟatu malāyk kuṭā nāṭṭilē kopurā icuvam (Hopura-Isuom) eṉṉumiṭattil kāṇappaṭṭa kaṇēca vikkirakattil teṉṉintiya eḻuttukkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṅku aḻakiya naṭarācat tiruvuruva moṉṟum kāṇappaṭṭatu. iṉṟu malāyk kuṭā nāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭum makkaḷ tamiḻariṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyavarkaḷāvar eṉac cuvaṟṟiṉkām eṉṉum āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kampōtiya makkaḷiṉ paḻaṅkatikaḷil kampucuvāyam pava eṉpavarukkuc civaṉ aḷitta mērā allatu pērā eṉpavaḷiṭam piṟantu perukiyavarkaḷē an nāṭṭu makkaḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. kampōtiyā kocciṉ cīṉā teṟkuc cīyam mutaliyaṉa aṭaṅkiya pakutiyic cīṉar pūṉāṉ (Funan) eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar. kavuṇṭiṉiyā eṉṉum pirāmaṇaṉ nākiṉi cōmā eṉṉum aracakulap peṇṇi maṇantu kampōtiyāvukku aracaṉāṉāṉ eṉa an nāṭṭup paḻaṅkatiyuḷḷatu.4 ilaṅki teṉṉintiya kiḻakkuk kariyōraṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷē kampōtiyāvil kuṭiyēṟi ṉārkaḷ.5 kampōtiya aracar, varmaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭappeyar puṉintu muṭicūṭṭappeṟṟaṉar. teṉṉintiya pallava aracarkaḷum varmaṉ eṉṉum peyar peṟṟiruntaṉar. cantiravarmaṉ eṉṉum kampōtiya aracaṉ ki. pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiyāvukku vantāṉ. aṅkōrvāṭ (Ankor-Vat) eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāṇappaṭum kōviṟ kōpuraṅkaḷ teṉṉintiya kōyiṟ kōpuraṅkaḷi ottuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum naṭarācat tiruvuru vaṅkaḷum piṟacāṉṟukaḷum iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiṉōr teṉṉintiya makkaḷē eṉpati viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.1 kampōtiya aracaruḷ pāvavarmaṉ eṉpōṉ ciṟanta civapattaṉ. ivaṉ ki. pi. 616il kampīravāṇar eṉṉum oru civaliṅkatti nāṭṭi vaḻipaṭṭu vantāṉ. kampōtiyar kaṭavuḷaruḷ civaṉē talimipeṟṟiruntār. piracār, ciṉeṅ, kaṟāpi mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ civaṉ umiyōṭu iṭapattil vīṟṟirukkum tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. iṭapattiṉmītu vīṟṟirukkum vaṭivattilum pārkka naṭarāca vaṭivamē makkaḷāl peritum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. aracarum potumakkaḷum iliṅkaṅkaḷi nāṭṭiyatik kuṟippiṭum kalveṭṭukaḷ pala kampōti yāviṟ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
cīyattilē pāṅkok nakarukku arukēyuḷḷa kuḷam oṉṟiṉ pakkattē kālaṉ iṭapam iliṅkam eṉṉum cilikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.2 ivi kaḷiṉ muṉṉē potumakkaḷ cattiyañ ceytārkaḷ. pāccima eṉṉum putta ālayattiṉ veḷiyē civaliṅkamoṉṟu uḷḷatu. peṇkaḷ itaṉic cuṟṟivantu tutittup piḷḷivaram vēṇṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivvālayam muṉpu civaṉ kōyilāyiruntateṉpatiṟ ciṟitum aiyamilli. malāyar yāvakar makamatiyar mutaliya ellāc camayattiṉarum civaṉukkuc ceyvatu pōlap pūvum paḻamum poṅkalum vittu iv vālayattil vaḻipaṭukiṉ ṟaṉar. kocciṉ cīṉa aracaṉ oruvaṉ vicaya īsvarar ālayaṅkaṭṭi aṅkuc civaliṅkap piratiṭṭi ceyta varalāṟu kalveṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu.
kampōtiyāvilē civaṉ kōyil oṉṟiṟ cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷa kalveṭṭiṟ kāṇum viparam piṉvarumāṟu:
yōkavarmaṉ,3 ācciramattukkuk koṭuttuḷḷa muttu, poṉ, veḷḷikkācu, kutiri, erumi, yāṉi, āṭavar, makaḷir, pūmāli mutaliya vaṟṟi aracar evarum kavarntukoḷḷutal ākātu. pirāmaṇar, aracaṉ, aracaṉiṉ piḷḷikaḷ māttiram āparaṇaṅkaḷik kaḻaṟṟātu kōviliṉ uḷḷē cellalām. perumakkaḷiṉ parivāraṅkaḷākac cellum potumakka ḷum āṭamparamillāta uṭiyuṭaṉ uḷḷē cellām; avarkaḷ nanti yāvartta māliyiyaṉṟi vēṟu mālikaḷi aṇintukoṇṭu cellutal ākātu. ālayattuḷ iruntu evarēṉum veṟṟili pōṭṭukkoḷḷavō uṇavu aruntavō kūṭātu. potumakkaḷ ālayattiṉ uḷḷē cellutal ākātu; uḷḷē caṇṭi allatu parikācam mutaliyaṉavum ceytal kūṭātu. civaṉiyum tirumāliyum vaṇaṅkum pirāmaṇarum nalla paḻakka vaḻakkamuṭiyavarkaḷum māttiram uḷḷē taṅki niṉṟu metuvākat tutipāṭik kaṭavuḷit tiyāṉikkalām. aracaṉiyaṉṟi vēṟevarāvatu ālayattukku muṉṉāl cella nērntāl tēriṉiṉṟum kīḻē iṟaṅkik kuṭipiṭiyātu naṭantu cellutal vēṇṭum. piṟa nāṭṭavarkaḷukku ivviti vilakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pirāmaṇar, aracar, mantirimār, aracaṉiṉ piḷḷikaḷ, tirumāli vaḻipaṭum kurumār, potumakkaḷuḷ ciṟantōr mutaliyavarkaḷ viruntāka vantāl ālayattiṉ talimik kuru ivar kaḷi varavēṟṟuc coṉṉa muṟippaṭi uṇavu, nīr, veṟṟili mutaliyaṉa koṭuttum avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiyaṉa purintum upacarittal vēṇṭum.
itil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa kaṭṭaḷikaḷik kaṭakkiṉṟavar evarum ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum uḷḷa aḷavum narakattic cērvārkaḷ; ik kaṭṭaḷi kaḷukkuṭpaṭṭu avikaḷi valuppaṭuttukiṉṟavarkaḷ it tarumatti uṇṭākkiyavar peṟum palaṉiṟ pātiyip peṟuvar.
kaliṅka nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum eḻunūṟu tōḻarōṭu ceṉṟa vicayaṉeṉ ṉum irācakumāraṉē ilaṅkiyiṉ mutal aracaṉ eṉappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. kaliṅkar tamiḻarkaḷē eṉpatu evarumaṟinta uṇmi. muṉ ‘ellam’ eṉa vaḻaṅkiya ilaṅkittīvu ciṅka vamicattavaṉākiya kaliṅka aracaṉ āṇṭa miyiṉ ciṅkalam (ciṅkaḷam) eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. iṉṉum ciṅkaḷa makkaḷ tām vicayaṉatum avaṉ 700 tōḻarkaḷatum vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷ eṉa nampi varukiṉṟaṉar.
cumattirā, yāvā, intuccīṉam, malāyā, ilaṅki mutaliya nāṭukaḷi lellām nākavaḻipāṭu kāṇappaṭṭatu. itu kāraṇamāka an nāṭukaḷiṉ makkaḷ nākar eṉavum aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. yāvāt tīvilē aracaṉum avaṉic cūḻntu tiriyum perumakkaḷum poṉ pāmpu, poṉ yāṉi, poṉ māṉ, poṉ kōḻi, arccuṉaṉiṉ aṉumakkoṭi eṉpavikaḷik kiyil vittiruntārkaḷ. iṅkuk ki. pi. 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ ṟamiḻiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭa paṭṭiyamoṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu.1 itaṉāl oru kālattil tamiḻ yāvāttīvil araciṉar moḻiyāka iruntateṉa naṉku aṟiyalām. aruccuṉaṉ nākar kulak koṭiyākiya yāvakamāti maṇantu aravāṉip peṟṟāṉeṉṟu yāvaka makkaḷ nampukiṉṟaṉar.
intiyāvilē putta camayam taliyeṭuttapiṉ pali marapiṉar kaṭal vaḻiyākac ceṉṟu pālittīvilum ayalēyuḷḷa tīvukaḷilum kuṭi yēṟiṉar. it tīvukaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷi otta oḻukka muṭiyavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉa akaram eṉpavar kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.1 māpali acuraṉ eṉappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. acurarkaḷ vētakālattukku muṉpum ataṉ muṟpakutiyilum tēvarkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. piṟkālattil avarkaḷ irākkatar allatu tēvarkaḷiṉ pakivar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.2 irāmāyaṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭum vāliyum civaliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉaṉ. vāliyiṉ marapum paliyiṉ marapum oṉṟōveṉpatu ārāyattakkatu.

iyal-7
tamiḻar camaya varalāṟu
iṉṟu iv vulakil palvēṟu niṟattiṉarum moḻiyiṉarumākiya makkaḷ āṅkāṅku vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. maṉitavaralāṟṟu, moḻi varalāṟṟu ārāycciyāḷar, ivarkaḷ ellōrum oru miyattiṉiṉṟum pirintu ceṉṟavarkaḷēyeṉa ārāyntu aṟutiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. itaṉi oppavē makkaḷiṉ camayamum oṉṟāyiruntu vaḷarcciyuṟṟup piṉ palavēṟu kiḷikaḷākap pirintateṉpatu uyttaṟiyappaṭum.
ammi vaḻipāṭu
makkaḷiṭiyē ātiyiṟṟōṉṟiyatu tāy vaḻipāṭu. vīram celvam kalvi pōṉṟavikaḷukkellām talimi peṟṟirukkum kaṭavuḷar peṇpāliṉarē. ulakil ātiyiṟṟōṉṟiyatu peṇ āṭci. ak kālattil maṇat toṭarpukaḷ iṟukkam aṭiyavilli. tāy peṟum kuḻantikaḷ kuṭumpattukkuc contamāṉavi; tantikku avikaḷiṭam urimi uṇṭākavilli.1 iṉṟum iv vaḻakkut tiruntāta makkaḷiṭi yum tiruntiya makkaḷ cilar iṭiyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. maliyāḷa nāṭṭil marumakkaṭṭāyam eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇṭu. itaṉpaṭi tantiyiṉ cottup piḷḷikaḷic cērātu avaṉ cakōtariyiṉ piḷḷikaḷi aṭikiṉṟatu.2 tiruvitāṅkūr aracurimiyum peṇvaḻiyiyē cērkiṉ ṟatu. aracaṉiṉ marumakaṉum marumakaḷumē iḷiya irācar, iḷiya irāṇi eṉṉum paṭṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉar. aracaṉiṉ piḷḷikaḷukku irācciyattil oruvaki urimiyum illi.1 iv vaḻakkukaḷellām peṇ āṭci uṇṭāyirunta kālattiṉ etirolikaḷē. cakōtariyiṉ makaṉ makaḷi maṇam muṭittaliṉālēyē peṇṇiṉ tantiyiṉ cottu marumakaṉic cītaṉamāka aṭikiṟateṉat tēyilar eṉṉum maṉitaviyalār kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. ‘tāyam’ eṉpatu peṇ āṭci uṇṭāyirunta kālattut tōṉṟiya col. itu, cottut tāyiṭamiruntu piḷḷikaḷi aṭintatu eṉpati naṉku viḷakkuvatu. tāyāṭci uṇṭāyirunta kālattil piḷḷikaḷ tāyic cūḻntiruntārkaḷ. tāyiṭattil ellā atikāramum iruntaṉa. ellā atikāramuṭiya tāy teyvamāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭāḷ. ‘tāyiṟ ciṟan toru kōyililli’ allavā? iv vaḻipāṭu makkaḷ vāḻkkip paruvattiṉ mika muṟkālattilēyē tōṉṟiyiruntatu. iṉṟu tāykkaṭavuḷiṉ cilikaḷ, vaḷarnta kaṭivāyp paṟkaḷuṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teṉṉintiya makkaḷiṉ nākarikam paṭippukkālam mutal paṭippaṭiyē vaḷarntuvantuḷḷateṉpatu muṉṉamē kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
it tāykkaṭavuḷiṉ vaṭivamum ātikālattil makkaḷ koṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭa vaṭivip piṉpaṟṟiyē veṭṭappaṭṭum eḻutappaṭṭum varu kiṉṟateṉakkoḷḷutal tavaṟākātu. tārviṉ (Darwin) eṉpār ellā rālum uccimēl vittup pukaḻappaṭum uyirnūṟ pulavar. ivar ātikāla makkaḷukku vaḷarnta kaṭivāyppaṟkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟum, avi pacci iṟicciyik kiḻippataṟkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa veṉṟum, avikaḷiṉ payaṉ kuṟintakālattil avi vaḷarccik kuṉṟi vēṭṭip pal (Canine teeth) aḷavil irukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.2 ākavē, tāykkaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu makkaḷukkuk kaṭivāyp paṟkaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭintirunta oru kālattil ārampittateṉak koḷḷalām.
tāykkaṭavuḷiṉ vākaṉam ciṅkam. avaḷ kiyilirukkum āyu taṅkaḷ taṇṭum kēṭakamum. kaṭavuḷarukku nāṉku kikaḷum pala kikaḷum koṭukkum vaḻakkam kiṟittava āṇṭukkup piṉṉiyatu eṉa 1mākṭaṉal eṉṉum āciriyar ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. avar kūṟuvatu:
“intuk kaṭavuḷariṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷi amikkum muṟiyil kiṟittava āṇṭiṉ ārampattil cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. ataṟku muṉ ik kaṭavuṭcilikaḷ maṉitaṉippōla iraṇṭu kikaḷuṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. ivvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷē putta ciṟpaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. iraṇṭām kaṟpīs (Kadphis II; 50. A.D.) eṉṉum kirēkka intiya aracaṉiṉ nāṇayattil civaṉ iraṇṭu kikaḷuṭiyava rākavē kāṇappaṭukiṟār.” mokañ cotarōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa civaṉvaṭivukku iraṇṭu kikaḷ iruppatum mēṟpaṭi kūṟṟi valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu. ciṉṉa āciyāvilē kititi eṉṉum nāṭu oṉṟu uḷḷatu. im makkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu nākarikam mutaliyaṉa tamiḻ makkaḷuṭiyaṉa pōṉṟaṉa. iṅkuk kāṇappaṭṭa paḻiya nāṇayamoṉṟil oru kiyiṟ ṟaṇṭum maṟṟak kiyiṟ kēṭakamum piṭittuc ciṅkattiṉ mītu vīṟṟirukkum tāyk kaṭavuḷiṉ uruvam poṟikkappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu. itaṉāl paṇṭiya tāykkaṭavuḷiṉ vaṭivam iraṇṭu kika ḷuṭiyatākavē iruntateṉat tuṇiyappaṭum. peṇ ciṅkattiṉ mītirukkum kuṭicu eṉṉum tāykkaṭavuḷē ekiptiyaratu pōrt teyvamākum.
tāykkaṭavuḷ mēṟku āciya makkaḷāl asreṟet herā, ṟiyā mutaliya pala peyarkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. makkaḷ mikat tāḻnili aṭintirunta kālattē tāyk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ārampittateṉpatu ataṉ vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷāl naṉku aṟitum. tamiḻ makkaḷ, kāṭukiḻāḷ, koṟṟavi, vinti, kāḷi mutaliya peyarāl tāykkaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭ ṭārkaḷ. tāyk kaṭavuḷukku narapaliyum, erumi āṭu mutaliya piṟa palikaḷum iṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷāl aṟikiṉṟōm. kāḷi pāli nilatteyvam. pālinila makkaḷ, vēṭar, iṭiyar, uḻavar eṉṉum makka ḷiṉ vāḻkkip paṭikaḷil ārampap paṭiyiluḷḷōr. ākavē, tāyk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu, makkaḷ payiriṭātum, mirukaṅ kaḷip piṭittup paḻakki vaḷarkka aṟiyā tum irunta oru kālattil ārampitta tākum. tāyk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṉpōtu pāmpu vaḻipāṭu ārampikka villi pōlum.
nāṇayattil iṭapattiṉ mītu irukkum apparum, arukil ciṅka vāka ṉattiṉ mītirukkum ammiyum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉar. ciriyā, pāpilōṉ, ekiptiya nāṭukaḷilum ciṅka vākaṉamuṭiya tāyk kaṭavuḷē mēlāṉa teyvamāka vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu1.
appar vaḻipāṭu
tāykkaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ivvāṟu tōṉṟi naṭipeṟṟu varumpōtu tiru maṇak kaṭṭuppāṭu iṟukkamaṭintatu; tanti āṭci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. tantiyē ellā atikāramum uṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. ākavē, tanti vaḻipāṭu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. oḷavippirāṭṭiyar. “aṉṉiyum pitāvum muṉṉaṟi teyvam” eṉa aruḷicceytirukkiṉṟamiyum ammi appar vaḻipāṭṭi ōraḷavu viḷakkuvatākum. tanti vaḻipāṭu, kālattil vēntaṉ vaḻipāṭāka māṟiṟṟu. tāykkaṭavuḷi oppap paḻiya makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa tanti vaṭivam iṉṟiya civaṉ vaṭivamē eṉat terikiṉṟatu. kittiti nāṭṭilē kāṇappaṭṭa paḻiya nāṇayattil tāyk kaṭavuḷukku valappakkattē, muttalivēlum kaṇṭakōṭariyum kiyiṟ ṟāṅkiya appar vaṭivam oṉṟu iṭapattiṉmītu niṟkiṉṟatu.1 itē vaṭiviṉa tākak kallāṟ cetukkappaṭṭa cilikaḷiyum annāṭṭu makkaḷ vaḻi paṭṭārkaḷ. civaṉukkuriyaṉavākac collappaṭum elumpu, ūmatti māli, caṅka veṇkuḻi, pulittōluṭi mutaliyavikaḷāl makkaḷ, tōli uṭiyākavum, elumpu ippi mutaliya vikaḷi aṇikaḷākavum koṇṭa oru kālat til iv vaḻipāṭu tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉṟu naṉku cintit taṟiyalākum. muṟ kālattil intiya makkaḷukku vākaṉamākap erutukaḷē payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟum ālayaṅkaḷil erutukaḷiṉmītu vāttiyakkārar ēṟiyiruntu pēriki koṭṭiccelvati nām pārkkalām. ākavē, ak kālat tantikkaṭa vuḷukku iṭapam ciṟanta ūrti ākkappaṭṭatu. kititi, cīriyā, kirēttā, cumēriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṉ2 nāṇayaṅkaḷil imil uḷḷa intiya iṭapam poṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ākavē iṭapatti ūrtiyākavuṭiya tanti vaḻipāṭu intiya nāṭṭilēyē ārampittiruttal vēṇṭum.
kititi nāṭṭu aracaṉukkum iraṇṭām iramce eṉṉum ekiptiya aracaṉukkum iṭiyil eḻutappaṭṭa uṭaṉpaṭikki oṉṟu cūḷiyiṭṭa kaḷimaṇ takaṭukaḷuṭ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. av vuṭaṉ paṭikki ki. mu. 1290il eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atil iṭiyēṟṟik kiyiṟ piṭittirukkum tantik kaṭavuḷē ellāk kaṭavuḷarilum mēlāṉavar eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. Golden Bough-IV, P. 137.
“His hands are rised, in his right he holds a single headed axe or hammer, in the left a trident of wavy lines, which is thought to stand for forked lightning or a bundle of thunderbolts...... The combination of the bull with the thunderbolt as emblems of deity suggests that the animal may have been chosen to represent the sky God not merely of its virility but of its voice; for in the peal of thunder premitive man may well have heard the bellowing of a cellestial bull.-Ibid.
It is from the Indian mythology that we get our nearest insight into the character of these deities and the meanings of those sculptures. For the bull represents Siva whose emblem was a trident and also an exe-The Hittite empire-John Garstang.
kittiti nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭṭa vēṟu cila ciṟpaṅkaḷil ak kaṭavuḷ mukkōṇa vaṭivāṉa villum cūlamum piṭittu iṭapattiṉmītu niṟkiṉṟār. iṭapattiṉ kompukaḷ pūmālikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
“In his right hand which is drawn pack there is a triangular bow. The horns of the bull are wreathed with garlands-Ibid P. 202.
ciriyā makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa aṭāṭ eṉṉum civaṉ kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaṭivam oṉṟu av viṭattiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa veṇkalat taṭṭu oṉṟiṉ mītu poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ak kaṭavuḷ oru kiyil iraṇṭu alakuḷḷa kōṭariyiyum (maḻu) maṟṟak kiyil kīḻum mēlum muttalikaḷuṭiya vēli yum piṭittirukkiṉṟār. mēṟku āciya makkaḷ mut tali vēl allatu cūlatti iṭiyēṟu eṉa vaḻaṅ kiṉārkaḷ. “At his(The Jupiter of Syria) side hangs his sword in its scabbard. His right hand brandishes a double axe; his left grasps a thunderbolt consisting of six spirally-twisted lines, each of which is tipped with arrow head. The bull that supports the God has a rosette on the forehead between eyes-Zeus P. 619.
iṭapattiṉ neṟṟiyil pūmāli kaṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. “....for in the Roman art he reappears under the title of Jupiter Dolichenus, wearing a phrygian cap, standing on a bull, and weilding a double axe in one hand and a thunderbolt on the other. In this form his worship was transported from the native home by soldiers and slaves till it had spread over a large part of the Roman empire, specially on the frontiers where it flourished in the camps of the legions”-The golden bough-a study of art and religion-part IV, P. 134-J.G. Fracer.
ammi appar vaḻipāṭu
ivvāṟu ammi appar vaḻipāṭukaḷ ātiyiṟ ṟaṉittaṉiyē tōṉṟiṉa. piṉpu makkaḷ ivarkaḷi maṉivi kaṇavaṉ eṉṉum muṟiyiṉa rākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭalāyiṉar. kititi nāṭṭil nāṇayattiṟ kāṇap paṭṭa vaṭivaṅkaḷum itaṉiyē kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēṟku āciya nāṭu kaḷil ammi appar vaḻipāṭu iṉṉoru vakiyilum ārampittatu. ñāyiṟṟut, tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭukaḷ avarkaḷiṭiyē tōṉṟiya kālattil avarkaḷ tiṅkaḷit tāykkaṭavuḷākavum ñāyiṟṟit tantik kaṭavuḷāka vum koṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. ākavē, paḻiya ammi appar vaḻipāṭukaḷ mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil ñāyiṟṟut, tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭukaḷuḷ nuḻin tuḷḷaṉa.
ñāyiṟṟu vaḻipāṭu
itaṉmēl ivvulakil iruḷippōkki oḷiyik koṭukkum ñāyiṟē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ eṉṟu makkaḷ uṇara ārampittaṉar. avarkaḷ civantavaṉ allatu civanta oḷiyuṭiyavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil ñāyiṟṟukkuc civaṉ eṉṉum peyar koṭuttu vaḻipaṭuvārāyiṉar. ip peyarē ātiyil ñāyiṟṟukkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatic civaṉ eṉṉum talippiṉkīḻ viḷakkutum. civaṉ eṉpataṟku aṭuttapaṭiyil el eṉṉum peyar katiravaṉiṉ peyarāka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. mēṟku āciyāvil elliṉ tiripākiya peyarkaḷē ñāyiṟṟik kuṟikkap peritum vaḻaṅkiṉa. ulaka makkaḷ ellōrum kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarāka āṇṭa peyarkaḷi ārāyu miṭattu avi oḷi allatu oḷiyuṭiyavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷiyē koṭukkiṉṟaṉa.1 el eṉpatu ñāyiṟṟiyum oḷiyiyum kuṟikkat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya cālap paḻiya peyar. “ellē ilakkam” eṉpatu tolkāp piyam. ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷi makkaḷ malimukaṭukaḷiṟ kaṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. ākavē ulakam muḻumiyilum ik kaṭavuḷiṉ iruppiṭam malimukaṭu eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭatu. iṉṟum civaṉ kilāya maliyil irukkiṟār eṉap potumakkaḷum piṟarum nampi varukiṉṟaṉar. malikaḷ illāta nāṭukaḷil ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ ceykuṉṟukaḷ mītu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. iv vakic ceykuṉṟukaḷi loṉṟē pēpel eṉṉum kōpuram2 ivvakic ceykuṉṟukaḷ mēṟku āciyā, āpirikkā, amerikkā eṉṉum nāṭu kaḷiṟ pala uḷḷaṉa. ulaka viyappukkaḷ eṉappaṭum ekiptiya kūrnutik kōpuraṅkaḷum iv vakiyiṉavē. ivi ñāyiṟu naṭu uccikku varum nāḷil atu eḻum ticiyip pārkkumpaṭi kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1 teṉṉin tiya ālayaṅkaḷum kiṟittavarkaḷiṉ ālayaṅkaḷum ivvāṟē kaṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. calamaṉiṉ ālayamum kiḻakkut tikki nōkkiyē niṉṟatu.
iliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉ toṭakkam
aṭuttapaṭiyil makkaḷ ceykuṉṟukaḷukkup patilaṭi nimirntu talikuvinta kaṟkaḷic cōlikaḷilum veḷikaḷilum naṭṭu vaḻipaṭuvā rāyiṉar. tiruvāṉikkā, tilliyampalam, tiruvālaṅkāṭu mutaliya teṉṉintiya ālayaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷāl itaṉ uṇmi naṉku teḷitum. muṉ iliṅkaṅkaḷ niṟutti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷilēyē piṉpu ālayaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. ulakam muḻumiyilum ivvaki vaḻipāṭu oru kālattiṟ kāṇappaṭṭatu.
ālamar kaṭavuḷ
ovvoru kirāmaṅkaḷilum maṉṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. maṉṟameṉpatu ūrukku naṭuvē makkaḷ kūṭum marattaṭi. marattic cuṟṟit tiṇṇiyiruntatu. ñāyat tīrppu, paḷḷikkūṭam, āṭal pāṭal mutaliya poḻutu pōkkukaḷ iṉṉum kirāma makkaḷiṉ ellāk kiḷarcci, nikaḻcci eṉpavikaḷukku iṭam ituvē. maṉṟattē perumpālum ālamaram niṉṟatu. niḻal maraṅkaḷil āl talimiyuṭiyatu. maṉṟattu maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ iliṅkam niṟuttap paṭṭiruntatu. itaṉic cuṟṟit tiṉamum alakum meḻukkumiṭappaṭṭu viḷakku ēṟṟappaṭṭatu.1 āliṉ kīḻ iruntamiyiṉ kaṭavuḷukku ‘ālamar kaṭavuḷ’ ‘ālamar celvaṉ’ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ip peyarkaḷē civaṉik kuṟikkap peritum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āl aiyaṉ iṭam ‘ālayam’ eṉappaṭṭatu.
mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷilum aruṭkuṟikaḷ karuvāli maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa;2 kirēttāvil yūkāvipras maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ kaṟṟūṇkaḷ niṟutti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptiyarum kirēkkarum āli ṉattic cērnta atti marattip paricuttamuṭiyatākak koṇṭu, ataṉāl iliṅkaṅkaḷ ceytaṉar.
tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭu
ñāyiṟṟu vaḻipāṭṭukkup piṉ tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭu tōṉṟiṟṟu. intiya nāṭṭil cantiraṉ āṇ teyvamākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. mēṟku āciya nāṭṭavar itaṉip peṇ teyvamākak koṇṭaṉar. aṉṉōr marattiṉkīḻ niṟuttappaṭṭa kaṟṟūṇi ñāyiṟākavum marattit tiṅka ḷākavum koḷvārāyiṉar. piṉpu tiṅkaṭ kaṭavuḷ ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷiṉ maṉiviyākkappaṭṭatu. maraṅkaḷum cōlikaḷum tiṅkaṭkaṭavuḷāka vaḻi paṭappaṭṭaṉa. marattukkup patil marattūṇkaḷum niṟutti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil āṇ kaṭavuḷ peṇ kaṭavuḷarik kuṟikka iliṅkaṅkaḷ niṟuttappaṭalāyiṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭil ivvāṟu nikaḻa villi.
iliṅka vaṇakkam ulakam muḻumiyum kāṇappaṭṭatu
ec. ci. vels eṉṉum āciriyar iliṅka vaḻipāṭu iṅkilāntu mutal kiḻakkē yappāṉ variyum kāṇappaṭṭateṉak kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. amerikkāviṉ cila pākaṅkaḷil iṉṟum iv vaṇakkam kāṇappaṭuvati ārāycciyāḷar ārāyntu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. mukkērci eṉpavar intiya nākarikamum ataṉ paḻamiyum eṉṉum nūliṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa vaṟṟiṉ curukkatti īṇṭut tarukiṉṟōm.
1“intiya makkaḷ civaṉiṉ aruṭkuṟiyākiya civaliṅkattit toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. iv vaṇakkam intiyāvuḷ māttiram niṟkavilli. iḵtu ulakiṉ ellā nāṭukaḷilum orukāṟ paraviyiruntateṉpati viḷakkuvataṟku vēṇṭiya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭit tuḷḷaṉa. cīṉā, yappāṉ, intuk kaṭal, pacipikkaṭaṟ ṟīvukaḷ mutaliya iṭaṅ kaḷil iv vaḻipāṭu muṟṟākat tūrntu viṭavilli. āpirikka amerikka2 makkaḷiṭiyum iḵtu orukāṟ paraviyiruntatu. acīriya, yūtēya, cīriya, ciṉṉa āciya, papilōṉiya makkaḷiṭiyum iv vaṇakkam kāṇappaṭṭa teṉpatik kiṟittava maṟi vāyilāka aṟikiṉṟōm. ciṉṉāṭkaḷiṉmuṉ papilōṉ nāṭṭil civaliṅkaṅkaḷ pala akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptiṉ cila pakutikaḷil hem, hōṟas, isṟas, carāpis mutaliya peyarkaḷāl ik kaṭavuḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu. civaliṅkaṅkaḷuṭaṉ pulikaḷum pāmpukaḷum vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptiya camātic cuvarkaḷil civaliṅ kaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. paḻiya airōppāvil iv vaṇakkam eṅkum paraviyiruntatu. itaṉi ōṭṭuvataṟkuk kiṟittava kurumār mika ūkkam eṭuttārkaḷ. kirīcilē vicā (Viza) eṉṉumiṭattil civaliṅkat toṭarpāṉa kiriyikaḷ iṉṟum naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. ayarlāntil civaliṅkaṅkaḷ, pala kiṟittava ālayaṅkaḷuṭ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi ‘cīlanākik’ (Sheila-na-gig) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. iḵtu orupōtu civa liṅkam eṉpataṉ tiripu ākalām. paḻamporuḷ ārāycciyāḷar iṅki lāntilum iskottilāntilum civaliṅkaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṇṭu piṭittaṉar. ivi kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ urōmar kōṭṭikaḷi yum māḷikikaḷiyum kaṭṭi vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷākum. urōmar iv vaṇakkatti iṅkilāntilē parappiyiruttalkūṭum. cermaṉiyilum pirāṉciyilum iv vaṇakkam mikku vaḻaṅkiyiruntataṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. lituvēṉiya makkaḷ (Lithuanians) 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil iv vaṇakkattiyē kikkoṇṭiruntu piṉpu kiṟittava matattit taḻuviṉārkaḷ. tipettu, puttāṉ (Bhutan) eṉṉu miṭaṅkaḷil civaliṅka vaṇakkam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cintōyicim (Shin-toism) eṉṉum yappāṉiya matattil civaliṅkam mutaṉmiyāṉatu. amerikkāviṉ pala pakutikaḷil ciṟappāka meccikkō, pēru, kiti tīvu (Hyti)kaḷil civaliṅkam vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. spāṉiyar mutal mutal amerikkāvukkuc ceṉṟapōtu ālayaṅkaḷil civaliṅkaṅkaḷ vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatik kaṇṭārkaḷ. āppirikkāvil takōmi (Dahomi) eṉṉum iṭattil iliṅkam leṅpā (Lengba) eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu.
“iliṅka vaṇakkam acīriyāviṟṟōṉṟi ayal nāṭukaḷiṟ paraviṟṟu eṉṟu cilar niṉikkiṟārkaḷ. kirēkka nāṭṭil pakkas (Bacchus) tayōṉicas (Dionysus) vaḻipāṭukaḷ toṭarpākac civaliṅkam vaḻipaṭap paṭṭatu. kirīcil pakkas viḻāvil iliṅkam vītikaḷ vaḻiyē ulāk koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. ekiptiyariṉ ōsṟis eṉṉum teyvam pakkas eṉpataṉ iṉṉoru vēṟupāṭē. pakkas vaḻipāṭu ittāliyil mikavum parantiruntatu. pakkas kepaṉ (Hebin) allatu kampāṉiyā (Campania) eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. kepaṉ kepi (Hebe - kuri) eṉṉum tēviyuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. pakkacukku iṭapamum puliyum mikap piriyamāṉavi. avar 1pulittōli yuṭuttuk kiyil tiricūlattiyum vittiruntār. ātalāl kepaṉ civaṉ eṉṟum, kepi kuri eṉṟum kūṟalām. pakkas kaṭavuḷip piṉtoṭarntu iṭapamum puliyum ceṉṟaṉa. avar kiyil aruntum maṇṭiyum iruntatu. ciṉṉa āciyāvil iliṅkakkaṭavuḷ kemōs (Chemos) molōc (Moloch) meratok (Merodock) ātōṉiyis (Adonis) capāciyas (Sabazius) pakkas allatu pākēōs (Bagios) eṉṉum peyarkaḷuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. ves top (Westropp) eṉpavar atōṉicas eṉpatu arttanārīcuvarar eṉṟu ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ciṉṉāṭkaḷiṉmuṉ ekiptilē ‘turkkammā’ eṉa aṭiyil eḻutiya oru turkki uruvam oṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu.”
ṟoṭēciyāvil (āppirikkā) kāṇappaṭṭa iliṅkaṅkaḷilum ciriyāviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa iliṅkaṅkaḷilum pūmālikaḷ veṭṭappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa. poṉīciya makkaḷ tayar nakarilē pakalavaṉ ālayattil marakatat tiṉālum poṉṉiṉālum ceyta iliṅkaṅkaḷi vittu vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ. ivvaki iliṅkaṅkaḷi avarkaḷ cipiṟālṭaril nāṭṭiyiruntamiyiṉ avviṭam iṉṟum “kekkuviciṉ tūṇkaḷ” eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷiṟ1 pēḻikaḷ (peṭṭi) vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟu ilaṅkiyiluḷḷa katirkāmam eṉṉum ālayattil vaḻipaṭappaṭu vatum pēḻiyē. mēṟku āciya nāṭṭup pēḻikaḷuḷ ciṟiya iliṅkam vikkappaṭṭiruntatu. nilipōla iraṇṭu kaṟkaḷi niṟutti mēl oru pāvu kalli vittu mūṭiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉkīḻ (Dolmen) vittu iliṅkaṅkaḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. itiliruntē pēḻiyil iliṅkatti vittu vaḻipaṭum muṟi ārampittatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. viviliya maṟiyil pēḻiyil vikkappaṭṭa cāṭcip pattiram eṉpatu nammavariṉ “iyantirat takaṭu” eṉpati ottatē.
iliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉ vaḷarcci
kaṭavuḷ oruvarē uḷar eṉa uṇarnta makkaḷ ammi apparkaḷiṉ aruṭkuṟikaḷē iṟivaṉiṉ āṇ taṉmi peṇ taṉmi eṉpavi kaḷi uṇarttum eṉak koṇṭaṉar. āṇ taṉmi peṇ taṉmikaḷi viḷakkum aṟikuṟiyāka avarkaḷ āṇkuṟi peṇkuṟi vaṭivāṉa aṭi yāḷaṅkaḷiyum vittu vaḻipaṭuvārāyiṉar. piṉpu iv viraṇṭum oṉṟu cērkkappaṭṭuk kaṭavuḷ āṇ peṇ iyalpiṉar eṉṉum ilappiṉum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. iv vaṭivaṅkaḷ elōkim eṉappaṭṭaṉa. mokeñ cotarōvilum āṇ kaṭavuḷik kuṟikka āṇkuṟiyum, peṇ kaṭavuḷik kuṟikkap peṇ kuṟiyum vaḻaṅkiṉa.2 elōkim vaḻipāṭē intiyāvil āvuṭiyār vaḻipāṭākum. paṇṭiya iliṅkaṅkaḷ tūṇ vaṭiviṉa. āṇ peṇkuṟi vaṭivukaḷ airōppāvilē kiṟittava ālayaṅ kaḷil iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.3 erucalēmilē oru kālattil ov voru vītiyilum iliṅkaṅkaḷ vittu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.1 ñāyiṟṟiyum tiṅkaḷiyum oppat tīyum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. iliṅkam tīyiṉ vaṭivākavum koṇṭu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu.
tī vaḻipāṭu
ñāyiṟṟiyum tiṅkaḷiyum oppa oḷiyuṭip poruḷākiya tīyum makkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. viviliya maṟiyiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭil nim ṟoṭ eṉṉum aracaṉ tīvaḻipāṭṭip palastīṉ nāṭṭil ārampittāṉ eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv vaḻipāṭu iṉṉum pāracīkariṭiyē corāsriya matak koḷkiyāka nilavukiṉṟatu. tīyum iliṅka vaṭivil vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. pīṭattōṭu kūṭiya iliṅkattukkut tīkkaṭi kōli oppiṭṭut, tīk kaṭikōliyē iliṅka vaṭivamāka amittu makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ eṉac cila ārāycciyāḷar karutiṉar. tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē muccuṭar vaḻi pāṭukaḷum iruntaṉa. tīyi enta nēramum uṇṭākki vaḻipaṭutal kūṭum. ñāyiṟṟiyum tiṅkaḷiyum enta nērattilum kaṇṭu vaḻipaṭutal aritu. ākavē avarkaḷ ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī eṉṉum muccuṭarkaḷiṉ vaṭivik kuṟikkum vaṭṭam, vil allatu piṟi, mukkōṇam vaṭivāṉa kuṇṭaṅ kaḷi amittu avikaḷil tī vaḷarttuk kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭuvārāyiṉar. ituvē muttī vēṭṭal eṉṉum vaḻakku. āriya makkaḷiṉ tī vaḻipāṭu iv vuṇmikaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu eḻuntataṉṟu. avarkaḷ kāliyil eḻuntu tōyntu, tīmūṭṭi erittuk kuḷir kāyntu ataṉ pakkattē niṉṟu kaṭavuḷit tutittaṉar. iv vaḻakkiliruntē avarkaḷatu akkiṉikōttiram eṉappaṭum tī vaḻipāṭu eḻuntatu. caṉakaṉ yākñaval kiyiyum avaruṭaṉ niṉṟa pirāmaṇariyum nōkki nīvir akkiṉikōt tiram eppaṭic ceykiṟīrkaḷ eṉṟu kēṭṭapōtu avarkaḷ cariyāṉa viṭi aḷikkavilli. itaṟkuk kāraṇam āriyaratu tī vaḻipāṭum tamiḻaratu tī vaḻipāṭum vevvēṟu vakiyiṉavātalē. ākamaṅkaḷum vētaṅkaḷum nērmāṟāṉavi eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar muṭivu. tī vaḻipāṭu turāṉiya makkaḷukkuriyateṉṟē varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.2
“koṭinili kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉṟa
vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ mutalaṉa mūṉṟum
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu kaṇṇiya varumē” (tol. puṟa. 33)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭum vaḻipāṭukaḷ muṟiyē ñāyiṟu tī, tiṅkaḷkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭukaḷē. paḻaṅkālap palipīṭaṅkaḷum piṟkālam iliṅkamāka vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa.1 tiruvaṇṇāmaliyil iṟivaṉ cōti vaṭivāy niṉṟāṉeṉpatu iliṅkavaḻipāṭu tī vaḻipāṭṭiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟi yatu eṉṉum aitīkattik kuṟippatu. muṟkālattu oṭuṅkiya koṭumuṭi kaḷuṭaṉ nimirntu vaḷarnta malikaḷ iliṅkaṅkaḷāka vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa.2
vēntaṉ
makkaḷ ñāyiṟṟit taṉipperuṅ kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭa kālattil aracaṉ ñāyiṟṟiṉ 3putalvaṉeṉṟum iṟantapiṉ avaṉ ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷāka māṟukiṟāṉ eṉṟum makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ.4 ākavē aracaṉiṉ cāmātiyiṉmītu ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷiṉ aruṭkuṟiyāka iliṅkam naṭap paṭṭatu. av viṭaṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭṭukkuriya ālayaṅkaḷākavum iruntaṉa. iv vaki ālayaṅkaḷ pala ekiptil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavu ḷāka māṟiya aracarkaḷ ekiptil iṟā (iravi) eṉṉum peyar peṟṟaṉar. ṟā eṉpatiliruntu irācaṉ eṉṉum col piṟantateṉac cilar kūṟuvar.
nantivaḻipāṭu
appaṉ eṉṉum tantik kaṭavuḷiṉ ūrtiyākiya nanti mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriyatāyiruntatu. palastīṉ nāṭṭil āṉkaṉṟu allatu nanti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭami kiṟittava maṟiyiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. tāvītiṉ (David) maṉivikkē āṉkaṉṟu eṉṟu peyar.1 kititi nāṭṭilē eyuk (Eyuk) eṉṉumiṭattilē nantic cili karuṅkaṟ pīṭattil vittu makkaḷ ellōrālum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. iv viṭapak kaṭavuḷic cēvikkat taṉikkuru iruntār. ekiptiya makkaḷ ōciṟis kaṭavuḷiṉ vākaṉamākaya apis eṉṉum iṭapatti vaḻipaṭ ṭārkaḷ. ellā mattiyatari nāṭukaḷālum iṭapam vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. iṭapat tiṉmītu cattiyañ ceyvati makkaḷ peritum añciṉārkaḷ.2
mukkaṭ ceylavar
makkaḷ, ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ tī eṉpavikaḷukku mēlāṉa taṉip perumporuḷ oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉṟu koṇṭa kālattil avarkaḷ paḻiya appaṉ vaṭivattukku muccuṭarkaḷik kaṇkaḷ eṉak koṇṭaṉar. āṅkāṅku makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa pēykaḷ avariṉ parivāraṅkaḷāyiṉa. ulaka makkaḷellārālum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa pāmpu avariṉ āparaṇaṅkaḷāyiṉa; payaṅkaramāṉa tāyk kaṭavuḷ ippoḻutu cānta vaṭiviṉaḷāy avariṉ pāriyāyiṉaḷ. umi, eññāṉṟum kaṉṉi eṉappaṭṭār.3 mokañcota rōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa civaṉ tiruvuruvattukku mūṉṟu kaṇkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.4
mātorupāti iṟivaṉ
ammi appark kaṭavuḷar, pāti āṇ, pāti peṇ vaṭivilum vittu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉar. iv vaṭivu “artta nārīcurar” vaṭivam eṉap paṭum. arttanārīcura vaṭivamē ātōṉis eṉa mēṟkuttici nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉa muṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu. iv vaṭivam paḻiya cumēriya makka ḷālum vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu.5 mokañcotarō makkaḷ appari āṇ eṉṟum ammiyi ammi eṉṟumē vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. cumēriyā nāṭṭi lum itē peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ammi appariṉ putalvar cumēriyarāl eṉlil eṉappaṭṭār. mokañcotarōvil ammi mīṉkaṇṇi eṉappaṭṭār. ippeyarē mīṉāṭci eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
pāmpu vaṇakkam
iv vulakiṉ ellāp pākaṅkaḷilum pāmpu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu. mocē veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyta pāmpi isravēlar vaṇaṅki vanta varalāṟu, kiṟittuva maṟiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kiṟittuva maṟiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum 
paṭippu varalāṟu mutaliyaṉa cālaṭiya makkaḷiṭamiruntu kiṭittaṉa eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cālaṭiya makkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷē eṉpatu varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷatu muṭivu. pāmpu vaṇakkam illāta nāṭu iv vulakil illi. itaṉi ārāyntu viḷakkum taṉi nūlkaḷ pala āṅkila moḻiyil eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṟum cila nāṭukaḷil uyirp pāmpukaḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa; cila nāṭukaḷil pāmpiṉ cilikaḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
“āppirikka makkaḷiṉ mukkiya teyvam pāmpu. maḻiyiṉmi, pañcam, piṇi mutaliya kālaṅkaḷil makkaḷ pāmpukaḷic ciṟappāka vaḻipaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. iv vakiyāka urōmarum orukālattiṟ ceytaṉar. oru nikirō vaṉāvatu vēṇṭumeṉṟu pāmpukku oruvakiyāṉa tīmiyuñ ceyyamāṭ ṭāṉ. taṟceyalāyp pāmpu oṉṟik kāyappaṭuttukiṟavaṉ niccayamākak kollappaṭuvāṉ. orumuṟi āṅkilak kappaṟkārar cilar tāṅkaḷ taṅkiya viṭṭiṉuḷ vanta pāmpu oṉṟi aṭittukkoṉṟārkaḷ. ataṟkāka ivarkaḷ aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷāl koṭumiyākat tākkik kollappaṭ ṭārkaḷ. pāmpukaḷ uṟivataṟkeṉak kaṭṭappaṭṭa kuṭicikaḷ nāṭu eṅkum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷukku mutiya peṇkaḷ tiṉamum uṇavu koṭukkiṟārkaḷ. pāmpukaḷukku aḻakiya periya kōyilkaḷum irukkiṉ ṟaṉa. ovvoru vakip pāmpik kavaṉippataṟkum ovvoru vēlik kāraṉ irukkiṟāṉ.”
“niyukiṉiyil cōlikaḷil pāmpukaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ iruk kiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷukkup paruva kālaṅkaḷukkēṟṟavāṟu paṉṟi, āṭu, kōḻi mutaliyaṉa uṇavāka aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa”1 itu pāmpu vaṇakkattiṟku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
ivvaṇakkam evvāṟu uṇṭāṉateṉṟu varalāṟṟu āciriyar kaḷāl naṉku aṟintu kūṟa muṭiyavilli. itaṉ toṭakkam maṟivāka irukkiṉṟatu. ulakil kāṇappaṭum makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉar tattam kulakkuṟi kaḷāka (Totem) mayil, āṭu, muyal, āmi pōṉṟa yātēṉum oṉṟik koṇṭiruntaṉar; iṉṟum koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ik kulakkuṟikaḷ piṟpāṭu vaḻipaṭavumpaṭṭaṉa. iv vakik kāraṇattip pāmpu vaṇakkat tiṟkum koḷḷalāmō eṉpatu ārāyattakkatu. intiya nāṭṭil pāmpu vaḻipāṭu naṭipeṟṟu varuvatik kūṟavēṇṭiyatilli. pāmpuc cilikaḷ illāta ālayaṅkaḷē illi eṉak kūṟalām.1
muruka vaḻipāṭu
muruka vaḻipāṭu ñāyiṟṟu vaṇakkattiṉ iṉṉoru vaki. kaṭalmīteḻum utayakāla ñāyiṟu,2 mayilmīteḻuntaruḷum murukaṉāka uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭaṭuḷḷatu. murukaṉ eṉpataṟku iḷiyavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ. yūtamakkaḷum pakaṟ kaṭavuḷi mutiyapakal, iḷampakal (Ba-al) eṉa iruvakiyāka vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. mutiya pakal tāṭiyuṭiya mutiya vaṭivuṭaṉum, iḷampakal kuḻanti vaṭivuṭaṉum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. yūta makkaḷukkuc camaya aṟivu koḷuttiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ marapic cērntōr eṉappaṭukiṉṟamiyiṉ avarkaḷiṭiyē tamiḻarkaḷuṭ kāṇappaṭum pala koḷkikaḷ nilaviṉa. iḷam kaṭavuḷ yūtarāl pōrk kaṭavuḷākavum koḷḷappaṭṭatu. iḷampakalē piṉ urōmariṉ mārs eṉṉum pōrk kaṭavuḷākavum māṟiṟṟu. murukakkaṭavuḷukkum acurar eṉappaṭṭa makka ḷukkum iṭiyil nikaḻnta pōrkaḷ eṉpaṉa muruka vaḻipāṭṭiṉarukkum civavaḻipāṭṭiṉarukkum nikaḻnta pōrkaḷākum. acurar civavaḻipāṭṭiṉar eṉpatu muṉṉamē kūṟappaṭṭatu. yekōvā eṉpatu el kaṭavuḷiyē kuṟikkum peyareṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. yekōvā vaḻipāṭ ṭiṉar elliṉ vēṟuvaki vaḻipāṭukaḷākiya pakal (Ba-al) moloc mutaliya vaḻipāṭukaḷi aḻikka muṉintu niṉṟa varalāṟṟuṭaṉ itaṉi oppu nōkkuka. makkaḷiṭiyē nikaḻnta pōrkaḷ makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa tey vaṅkaḷmītu ēṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. vēta pāṭalkaḷ, intiraṉ tācukkaḷiṉ celvaṅkaḷik koḷḷikoṇṭu avarkaḷi aḻittāṉeṉṟu kūṟu kiṉṟaṉa. āriya makkaḷ tām peṟṟa veṟṟikaḷit tām vaḻipaṭṭa kaṭavuḷ mītu ēṟṟik kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. ivvāṟu kūṟutal akkāla marapupōlum.
māl vaṇakkam
māl (mā+1el) vaṇakkamum ñāyiṟṟu vaṇakkattiṉ iṉṉoru vaki. tirumāliṉ kiyil irukkum cakkaram ñāyiṟṟiyē kuṟikkiṉṟatu. “ariyallāṟ ṟēviyilli aiyaṉiyāṟaṉārkkē”. perumāḷ (peru māṉiṉ peṇpāḵ) eṉa varuvaṉa ārāyattakkaṉa. orupōtu kariya tāyk kaṭavuḷ māel eṉa vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatō eṉpatu cintikkattakkatu. pārata kālattukkup piṉ tirumāl vaḻipāṭu kaṇṇaṉ vaḻipāṭāka māṟiyuḷḷatu. tirumāl mūvulaki aḷantāreṉa varum purāṇakkati ñāyiṟu kāli, naṇpakal, māli eṉṉum mūṉṟu kālaṅkaḷil cellum celaviṉi uṇarttumeṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
civaṉ
ñāyiṟē civaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl ātiyil vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa teṉpatu ulakamakkaḷiṉ paḻiya varalāṟukaḷi nōkkumiṭattu naṉku pulaṉākum.2 “arukkaṉ pātam vaṇaṅkuvar antiyil-arukkaṉāvāṉ araṉuruvallaṉō” (tirunā-tē) eṉpatum itaṉiyē uṇarttum. ñāyiṟu, āṇṭiṉ ovvoru mātattilum ovvoru vīṭṭil taṅkum. ovvoru vīṭṭil niṟkumpōtum atu vevvēṟu vaṭivil vaḻipaṭap paṭṭatu.3 ākavē, ovvoru tiṅkaḷum ovvoru kaṭavuḷukku urittākkappaṭṭiruntatu. iv vaḻakku mēṟku āciya ekiptiya intiya nāṭukaḷukkellām potu. epirēya makkaḷiṉ āṇṭiṉ mūṉṟāvatu mātam civaṉ eṉa aṟiyappaṭṭatu.1 āciyāvilum, pāpilōṉilum āṇ ṭiṉ mūṉṟām mātam civaṉ allatu cimāṉu eṉappaṭṭatu. pāpilōṉil itē peyaruṭiya iṭam oṉṟum iruntatu.
iḵtu aṅkē kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷil eḻutivikkappaṭṭa uṟuti (Documents) kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.2 itaṉāl pāpilōṉ āciyā pālastīṉa nāṭukaḷil civaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa kaṭavuḷ oṉṟu iruntateṉpatu teḷḷitiṟ pulappaṭum. arēpiyarum yūtarum cālatiyāvi luḷḷa ūr eṉṉum nakariṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu kāṉāṉ tēcattiṟ kuṭiyēṟiya āpirakāmiṉ (ki. mu. 2153) cantatiyiṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ākavē yūta makkaḷ cālatiyarip piṉpaṟṟiyē tiṅkaḷ peyarkaḷiyum vaḻaṅki ṉārkaḷātal vēṇṭum. cālatiyāvil ip peyar cālap paḻiyatāy vaḻaṅkiṉamiyiṉālēyē atu tiṅkaḷukkup peyarākkappaṭṭatu. pāpilōṉ makkaḷum pālastīṉarum elcaṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkiya kaṭavuḷ, civaṉip pōlavē malimukaṭukaḷiliruppavarākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.3 kiṟittuva maṟiyiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭu ki.mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum,
ki.mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum iṭiyil eḻutappaṭṭatu.4 itil, mocē eṉpavar, yekōvā aruḷiya pattuk kaṭṭaḷikaḷic ciṉāy malimītu niṉṟu civaṉ mātattiṉ āṟāvatunāḷ makkaḷukku veḷiyi ṭṭār eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl civaṉ eṉṉum peyar tiṅkaḷiṉ peyarākak ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭilum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatu ūkittaṟiyappaṭum. kiṟittuva maṟiyilē ciyaṉ eṉak kūṟappaṭum kaṭavuḷ civaṉē eṉpati mēl nāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷē ārāyntu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.1 ātarlilli eṉpār kūṟuvatu piṉvarumāṟu: “civaṉ eṉṉum col valittum melittum intiyāviṉ pala pakutikaḷil uccarikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. pirāṉcu makkaḷ ‘ccivaṉ’ eṉa uccarippar. kiṟittuva maṟiyilē (Amos. V. 26) varum ciyaṉ eṉpatu civaṉē eṉa ārāycciyāḷar kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷārkaḷ”. epirē yariṉ yekōvā eṉṉum col civā eṉpatu pōṉṟa hevā, cevā eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.2 ekiptilē civaṉ eṉṉum pālinilap pacuntari oṉṟu uṇṭu. iṅkup paḻiya amaṉ ālayam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. amaṉ ekiptiyariṉ ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ. amaṉ vaḻipāṭu paṇṭu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟatāka avarkaḷ varalāṟukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. amaṉ vaḻipāṭu ciyas (Zeus) yūpita r vaḻipāṭukaḷākak kirēkka urōmi nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. ciyas yūpitar vaḻipāṭukaḷum ñāyiṟṟu vaḻipāṭukaḷē. ip pālivaṉap pacun tariyil, ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ, civaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭami yiṉālēyē ataṟkuc civaṉ eṉṉum peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉa eḷitil ūkittaṟiyalām. civaṉ eṉpatē amaṉ eṉap piṟkālan tirintu vaḻaṅ kutalkūṭum. amaṉ (Amon) eṉpatē kiṟittavarkaḷ vaḻipāṭṭu muṭivil kūṟum āmeṉ eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟateṉpatu varalāṟṟu āciri yarkaḷ cilaratu karuttu. mēṟku āciyāvil iṭaṅkaḷukkum mātattukkum māttiramaṉṟu; makkaḷukkum civaṉ eṉṉum peyar iṭṭu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. 3coyṟō rōyis eṉṉum cumēriya aracaṉatu maṉiviyiṉ peyar civaṉ. ivaḷukku māttiram civaṉ eṉṉum peyar iṭappaṭṭateṉak karututal kūṭātu. ak kāla makkaḷ palarukku ip peyar iṭṭu vaḻaṅkap paṭṭateṉṟum am marapupaṟṟi ivaḷukkum ip peyar koṭukkappaṭṭa teṉṟum naṉku uyttaṟiyalākum. amerikkāvilē kolaṟaṭōp paḷḷattākkiluḷḷa pīṭapūmiyil pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa civaṉ ālayam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. kirēkkariṉ ciyas eṉṉum kaṭavuṭ peyarum civaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripē. itu, ciyas maliyil iruppavarāyum mūṉṟu kaṇkaḷuṭiyarāyum kūṟappaṭutalāl naṉku aṟitum. yūpitar eṉṉum peyarum cevā eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar.1 ukkirō piṉṉiya turāṉiya moḻikaḷil civaṉ civō eṉa vaḻaṅkum. ilāppiyariṉ kāttaṟ kaṭavuḷ civaṉ. turukki moḻiyil cev eṉpatu aṉpi uṇarttum. akkēṭiya moḻiyil ce karuṇiyiyum, cep uḷḷattiyum uṇarttum.2 āriya makkaḷiṭiyē vētakālattil civākkaḷ (Sivas) eṉṉum oru kūṭṭattiṉar iruntaṉar. pāṇiṉiyiṉ ilakkaṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa civapuram3, ivarkaḷiṉ nakaramākalāmeṉak karutap paṭukiṉṟatu. mecapetēmiyāvilē paḻiya pāpilōṉ makkaḷiṉ marapiṉa rākiya ciṟukūṭṭattiṉar cilar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ kapil civā, ṟapil civā eṉṉum kaṭavuḷari vaṇaṅkiṉar. paḻiya ilaṅki aracar palar civaṉ eṉṉum peyarit tamatu peyariṉ iṟutiyiṟ cērttuvaḻaṅ kiṉar. aracarallāta cilarukkuc civaṉeṉṟum peyariruntami makāvamica meṉṉum putta nūlālaṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. civaṉ oḷimali eṉṉum peyar puttar kālattukku muṟpaṭṭatu. kirēttā(Crete)viluḷḷa ōr iṭattukkum civaṉ eṉṟu peyar.
maravaṇakkam
maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ kaṭavuḷ vaṭivaṅkaḷ vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭamiyiṉ, makkaḷ maraṅkaḷilum kaṭavuḷ uṟivatāka niṉintu avikaḷi vaḻipaṭalāyiṉar. iv vaṇakkamum ulakam muḻumiyilum kāṇappaṭṭatu; iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.4 iṉṟu kiṟittavariṉ tēvālayattukkum peyarāka vaḻaṅkum ‘cēṭc’ eṉṉum peyar, karuvāli marattikkuṟikkum ‘kērk’ eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kāṭṭi yuḷḷārkaḷ.5 maravaṇakkattil maraṅkaḷiṉ uccikkiḷikaḷmītu āṭit tuṇṭukaḷik koṭipōlak kaṭṭip paṟakkaviṭuvatu vaḻakku.
naṭarāca vaṇakkam
naṭarāca vaṭivil iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭum vaḻakku mokañcotarō kālattilēyē kāṇappaṭṭatu.1 aṅkuḷḷa muttirikaḷil iṟivaṉik kuṟikkat tāṇṭavaṉ eṉṉum peyar poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu; aracaṉ oruvaṉ tāṉē oppaṟṟavaṉ eṉṉum perumitattiṉāl āṭum āṭali iv vaṭivam kuṟikkiṉṟatu. kāliṉ kīḻ oruvaṉi mitittu niṟṟal ‘oṉṉārtteṟal’ eṉpatiyum, amartti niṟkum karam aṭintāri ātarittu aṭikkalam kāttaliyum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. tammip paṇiyum ciṟṟaracarkaḷ muṭimītu tamatu kālittūkki vittu aruḷvatu ak kālap perumaṉṉar marapu. av vaḻakkē ‘tiruvaṭit tīkki’yumām.
kōyil
maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ irunta aruṭkuṟikaḷic cuṟṟik kaṟkaḷi aṭukki matil allatu vēli ceyvatiliruntu kōyil ārampamāyiṟṟu. maṉita ṉiṉ vīṭu oṉṟip pārttu amikkappaṭṭatē kōyil.2 kōyil eṉpatu aracaṉiṉ vīṭu eṉap poruḷpaṭum. ālayam kōyil eṉappaṭṭatu pōlavē aracaṉatu illam kōyil eṉappaṭṭatu.3 mokañcotarō vil, ciṟitu aṟikaḷum taṭitta cuvarkaḷumuṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭam ak kāla ālayam eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ataṉ amippik koṇṭu atu pala māṭiyuṭiyatāyiruntateṉak karutappaṭṭatu. māṭikaḷē iṉṟu kōpuraṅkaḷaḷavil curuṅkiviṭṭaṉa pōlum.
mēṟku ulakattilum ātiyil ālayaṅkaḷ maramum iliṅkamum kiṇaṟum palipīṭamum aḷavil iruntaṉa. palipīṭameṉpatu mēlē ciṟiya tī mūṭṭakkūṭiya oru kal. “ārampattil maramum, piṉ cōliyum kiṇaṟum, piṉ iliṅkamum, piṉ cetukki maṭṭañ ceyyappaṭāta kallākiya pali pīṭamum, piṉ koṭikaḷ allatu pāmpukaḷum muṟiyē ōriṭattil kūṭi ālayaṅkaḷāyiṉa. mekkāvil uḷḷa kāpā, maramum iliṅkamum kiṇaṟumākiya ivvaḷavil uḷḷa kōyilāka iruntatu. iṅkilāntiluḷḷa kal vaṭṭaṅkaḷukku ayalil tōppukkaḷum, kiṇaṟukaḷum iruntaṉa.”1 eṉṟu parlāṅ eṉṉum āciriyar ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷār. “ātiyil, caturamāṉa allatu vaṭṭamāṉa kal vēlikaḷiṉ naṭuvē iliṅkamum ataṉ muṉṉāl palipīṭamum vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. ivvakik kalvēli yiṉ orupuṟattē mūṉṟu kalliṉāl eṭukkappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ (kaviḻnta ‘pa’ pōṉṟaṉa) poṉiciyāviṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ik koḷkiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē pāpilas (Babel) pālpek2 ālayaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. mekkāvil uḷḷa kāpā ālayam muṟkūṟiya vaki ālayat tukku utāraṇamākum” eṉa ‘cīriyāvil kaṟkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum varalāṟu’ eṉṉum nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu.3
teṉṉintiyāvil mikap paḻiya ālayaṅkaḷiṉ aḻipāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli. teṉṉintiyāvil ālayaṅkaḷ marattiṉāṟ kaṭṭap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, piṉpu am marakkōyilkaḷiṉ amippip piṉ paṟṟik kalliṉāl ālayaṅkaḷ eṭukkappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, ataṉālēyē mikap paḻiya ālayaṅkaḷum avikaḷiṉ aḻipāṭukaḷum teṉṉintiyā viṟ kiṭikkavilliyeṉṟum paḻiya kaṭṭiṭak kali ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. teṉṉintiya kaṭṭiṭakkali ārāycciyāḷaruḷ ciṟanta yōvu tepuruil (Jouveau Dubreuil) eṉpār teṉṉintiya kaṭṭiṭak kaliyippaṟṟik kūṟiyiruppaṉa cila piṉvaruvaṉa: “tirāviṭariṉ kaṭṭiṭak kali 1500 āṇṭukaḷ variyil maṟṟiya nāṭṭu av vakikkali yiṉāl pātikkappaṭātu taṉimiyāka niṉṟatu. itu maṟṟiya nāṭṭavariṉ kaṭṭiṭak kalikaḷiṉiṉṟum etiyum kaṭaṉpeṟavilli. itu paṭippaṭiyē vaḷarcciyaṭintirukkiṉṟatu. tirāviṭa nāṭṭiṉ ovvoru kālattuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiyum nōkkumpōtu avikaḷiṉ amippu metuvāka vaḷarcciyaṭintuḷḷateṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. itu maṉita nūlār kuraṅku pōṉṟa maṉitaṉuṭiya maṇṭi mutal iṉṟiya maṉitaṉiṉ maṇṭi variyil paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarcciyaṭintu vantuḷḷamiyik kāṭṭutal pōṉṟatu. māmallapurattil kāṇappaṭum tērkaḷukkum tiruppāppuli yūriṟ kāṇappaṭum tērkaḷukkum mātiriyil vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. itu koramāṅkaṉ1 maṉitaṉ maṇṭikkum iṉṟiya maṉitaṉ maṇṭikku muḷḷa vēṟupāṭu pōṉṟatu.”
“kaṟcippikaḷ tam muṉṉōr tamakkuk kaṟpitta parampari muṟip paṭiyē kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷi amittārkaḷ. tamiḻilē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ vaṭivukaḷ kālantōṟum māṟupaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa. itu pōlavē moḻiyum, moḻi vaḻakkukaḷum, makkaḷiṉ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum māṟu paṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa; camayamum kiriyi muṟikaḷum camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷum taṇivu peṟṟu vantuḷḷaṉa. mikap paḻiya uruvac cilikaḷukkum iṉṟiya avvakic cilikaḷukkum vēṟṟumi kāṇalām. nila nūlār nilattil aṭukku aṭukkākak kāṇappaṭum pāṟikaḷip pārttu avaṟ ṟiṉ vayatik kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. itupōlavē kaṭṭiṭakkaliyil tērnta oruvaṉukkuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum kūṭu, allatu pōtikiyiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷ ārāyccikkup pōtumāṉavi. itu pāṟikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum tolluyirkaḷiṉ eṉpuk kūṭukaḷ (Fossils) ap pāṟikaḷi iṉṉa iṉṉa pāṟi eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟku nilaviyalārukkup payaṉpaṭuvatupōlākum.”
verkūcaṉ (Fergussion) eṉpār teṉṉintiya ālaya amippukaḷ ekiptiya kōyilkaḷi ottaṉaveṉṟu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2 tēsraṉ eṉpār intiyāviṉ paḻiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷil ekiptiya cālaṭiya poṉīciya amippu muṟikaḷ kalantuḷḷaṉa eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār.1 maliyāḷattil cila ālayaṅkaḷ kuṭilpōla vaṭṭavaṭivāka amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vaṭṭa vaṭiviliruntē piṉ catura vaṭivu ārampittiruttal vēṇṭum. makkaḷ ātiyil kaṭṭi vāḻnta vīṭukaḷ vaṭṭavaṭiviṉavē. karuvukku (karppak kirukam) mēl uḷḷa pākam iṉṟum vaṭṭavaṭivākavē amikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. muṭi eṉpatu kuṭil vīṭukaḷukku ucciyil vikkappaṭṭa pāṉiyē yākum. aracaṉ illattic cuṟṟi avaṉuṭiya cuṟṟattiṉar cēṉāpatiyar iruppatupōlak kōyiliṉ uḷ vītiyic cuṟṟip parivāra teyvaṅkaḷukku ālayaṅkaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa.2 ātiyil maramum iliṅkamuṅ kiṇaṟumākavirunta ālayam ivvāṟu vaḷarcciyuṟṟatu.
talaviruṭcam
iliṅkattic cuṟṟik kaṭṭiṭameḻuntatu. appoḻutu maram iṭam peṟavilli. enta marattiṉkīḻ iliṅkam ātiyil vittu vaḻipaṭap paṭṭatō am maram av vālayattiṉ talaviruṭcam eṉappaṭṭatu. mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷilum ekiptilum kirēttāvilum teṉṉintiya ālayaṅ kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum koṭimaram pōṉṟa marattūṇkaḷ ālayaṅkaḷil niṟut tappaṭṭu āṇṭuviḻāk kālaṅkaḷil kiriyikaḷuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. koṭi marattiṉ nuṉiyil mūṉṟu kiḷikaḷ kāṇappaṭutalpōla am maraṅkaḷukkum kiḷikaḷ iruntaṉa. talaviruṭcamē, kālattil koṭimaramāka māṟiyuḷḷatō veṉpatu ārāyattakkatu.3 iliṅkam vikkappaṭṭuḷḷa maraṅkaḷiṉ kiḷikaḷil, āṇṭil orumuṟi ciṟappāka naṭipeṟum poṅki maṭiyiṭutal pōṉṟa vaḻipāṭṭi aṟivikka āṭit tuṇiyik kaṭṭi viṭutal intiya nāṭṭilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa vaḻakku.
tiri
aracaṉ uṟiyum uḷ maṇṭapattiṉ etirē tiri iṭappaṭṭiruk kum.1 ataṉi oppa ālayaṅkaḷilum uḷmaṇṭapattiṉ etirilum tiric cīli iṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ālayaṅkaḷil iṉṟum tiric cīliyiṭṭirup pati nām kāṇalām. mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷilum ālayaṅkaḷukkut tiriccīliyiṭappaṭṭiruntatu.2
aiyar
araṇmaṉiyil aracaṉic cēvikkum paṇiyāḷar palar irunta ṉar. avarkaḷ palataraṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntārkaḷ, avaruḷ oruvaṉ talivaṉākaviruntāṉ. itaṉi oppavē ālayaṅkaḷilum aiyaṉic cēvikkum aṭiyavarkaḷ palar iruntaṉar. aracaṉ tuyilviṭṭu eḻuntatu mutal avaṉ tuyilkoḷḷap pōkum variyil avaṉ aṭiyōr eṉṉa eṉṉa paṇikaḷic ceykiṉṟārkaḷō av vēlikaḷiyellām aiyaṉatu aṭiyārum ceytaṉar.3 aiyaṉic cēvittaliṉ ivarkaḷ aiyareṉ ṟum, kōyiṟ karumaṅkaḷip pārttaliṟ pārppār eṉṟum peyar peṟṟa ṉar. aṉṉōr paḻiya nāḷil āṟu taraṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉar.4 kiṟittuva maṟiyil cērmiyā, nēciyā eṉa aiyāvil muṭiyum pala peyarkaḷik kāṇalām. tamiḻmoḻiyil māttiramaṉṟu, mēṟku āciya moḻikaḷilum aiyar eṉpatu kaṭavuḷik kuṟittatu. arāpi makkaḷ pāpilōṉ nāṭṭic ciṉ-aiyar eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar. ciṉ-aiyā eṉpataṟku ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ eṉṟu poruḷ. paḻiya keltiya moḻiyilum aiyar eṉpatu kaṭavuḷik kuṟittatu.1
aiyar eṉpatu āriyar eṉpataṉ tiripu eṉac cilar karutalāyiṉar. itu ciṟitum poruntuvataṉṟu. tolkāppiyattil “aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇameṉpa” eṉa aiyar eṉṉum peyar vantuḷḷatu. ārāycciyāḷar aiyar eṉpatu tūya tamiḻccol eṉṟē aṟutiyiṭṭuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.2 vaṭamoḻip piraḷayam teṉ tēcattē paṭi eṭuttu vanta oru kālattil, tamiḻppeyarkaḷiṉ iṭatti vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷ eṭuttaṉa. avvāṟu vanta peyarkaḷuḷ pirāmaṇar eṉpatum oṉṟu. iṉṟum kōyiṟṟoṇṭu ceyyum kulattavar aiyar, aiyaṅkār, nampi, paṭṭar mutaliya tamiḻp peyarkaḷālēyē aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kurumāri mēṟku āciya nāṭṭuk kurumārōṭu oppiṭṭup pārkkumiṭattup pala uṇmikaḷ veḷiyākiṉṟaṉa. ekiptiya kurumāriṉ oḻukkaṅkaḷ perumpālum tamiḻnāṭṭuk kurumāriṉ oḻukkaṅkaḷi ottaṉaveṉpatu keratōtacu āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷavikaḷāl viḷaṅkum.3
purōkitar
