aiyar, makkaḷukku camayap poruḷil vaḻikāṭṭiyāka viruntār. avar iṟantavar, iruppavarkaḷuṭiya naṉmiyi muṉṉiṭṭup pala kiriyikaḷic ceyya ārampittaṉar. makkaḷ avar puriyum kiriyikaḷil aḷavu kaṭanta nampikki vittaṉar. makkaḷi ellāt tīmikaḷiṉiṉṟum kāppavar eṉṉum poruḷil avar purōkitar eṉappaṭṭār. cōtiṭam, vāṉa ārāycci, mantira vitti mutaliyavikaḷ ellām muṟkālattiṟ kurumārkaḷiṭattilēyē iruntaṉa. itaṉāl potu makkaḷ kurumārukkup peritum aṭaṅki naṭantārkaḷ. itu ulaka varalāṟṟil ellā nāṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭa uṇmi. purōkitaṉ aracaṉatu uṟutic cuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ oruva ṉākavum iruntāṉ. aracar avikaḷil aiyarukkut taṉippaṭṭa matippuk koṭukkappaṭṭatu. araca capikaḷil aiyarukkut taṉatu karuttukkaḷi eṭuttukkūṟum atikāram iruntatu.1 aiyarum purōkitaṉum āriyaṉ eṉṟu niṉippatu tavaṟuṭittu.
kōyiṟ kiriyikaḷ
ōr aracaṉukku avaṉ vēlikkāraṉ nittiri viṭṭeḻuntatu mutal nittirikkuc cellumvariyil eṉṉeṉṉa vēlikaḷic ceytāṉō, av vaki vēlikaḷi ellām kaṭavuḷukkuc ceyvatē ālayak kiriyikaḷākum.2 viḻākkaḷ eṉpaṉa aracaṉ ulāvarum kālat til naṭipeṟuvatupōla naṭattik kaṇṭu makiḻtal, viḻākkaḷiyum nittiya kiriyikaḷiyum koṇṭu paṇṭi aracariṉ vāḻkkiyi iṉitu aṟintu koḷḷutal kūṭum. avvattēca vaḻakkukaḷukkēṟpa ik kiriyikaḷ ciṟitu vēṟupaṭum. tiruvuruvattukku naṟumaṇattilam tēyttal, tirumuḻuk kāṭṭutal, āṭi uṭuttal, naṟum pukiyum tīpamum (ālatti) kāṭṭutal, uṇavu paṭittal pōṉṟa kiriyikaḷ ellā nāṭukaḷukkum potu vāṉavi. kiṟittuva ālayaṅkaḷil naṭipeṟuvaṉa pōṉṟavē. cālamaṉ kaṭṭiya ālayattil naṭipeṟṟa kiriyikaḷum iv vakiyiṉa. cīriyā, ṟoṭēciyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa iliṅkaṅkaḷil pūmālikaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.1 cīriyāvilē kaṭāṭ (Hadad) eṉṉum civaṉ kaṭavuḷuṭiya iṭapattiṉ neṟṟiyil pūmāli kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.2 itaṉāl poṉīciyā cīriyā āppirikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil vaḻipāṭṭil pūmālikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu terikiṉṟatu. yūtar vaḻipāṭu paḻiya akkēṭiya poṉīciya vaḻipāṭukaḷi ottatu. ātaliṉ ak kālattu an nāṭukaḷilum iv vakik kiriyikaḷē naṭipeṟṟaṉa veṉpatu teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭum. ālatti kāṭṭutal teṉṉāṭṭup paḻiya vaḻakku. aracarukku muṉṉālum itu kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. tīpam ñāyiṟṟik kuṟikkumeṉac cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.3
makkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu
aracaṉik kāṇuvataṟku makkaḷ eppaṭic cellukiṟārkaḷō appaṭiyē ālayattukkum ceṉṟaṉar. perumakkaḷik kāṇac cellu miṭattu veṟuṅkiyōṭu celvatu tamiḻar marapaṉṟu. “pōṉavaṉ veṟuṅ kiyōṭē pōkāvaṇṇañ ceṉṟu ñāṉa caṟkuruvi naṉṟāy vaṇaṅki niṉṟu” (kivalyam); ākavē, avarkaḷ cellumiṭattuk kiyuṟiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉar. naṭuvirukki eṉṉum karumimūlam aracaṉukkut tamatu ceyti kaḷi aṟivittalpōla avarkaḷ tamatu kiyuṟiyi naṭuvirukkikaḷākiya kurumār (aiyar) mūlam kaṭavuḷiṭattil cērppittut tamkuṟikaḷiyum avar mūlam uṇarttiṉar. itaṟkuk kimāṟākak kurumār kūliyum peṟṟaṉar. pattarkaḷ kaṭavuḷiṉ etirē niṉṟu avaṉ pukaḻpāṭik kumpiṭṭuk kūttumāṭiṉar.1 mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil iv vakik kūttu iṉṟum naṭipeṟuvati varalāṟukaḷiṟ kāṇkiṉṟōm. cālamaṉ aracaṉiṉ tanti yākiya tāvītu (David) pēḻikku muṉṉāl tuḷḷi āṭiṉa varalāṟu kiṟittava maṟiyil (2. Sam VI, 14, 15) kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tēvatācikaḷ
aracariṉ araṇmaṉikaḷil aracaṉip pāṭṭālum kūttālum makiḻvikkum makaḷir palar iruntaṉar. avvāṟē ālayaṅkaḷilum av vaki makaḷir palar iruntaṉar. intiya nāṭṭil māttiramaṉṟu, mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷilum urōmilum iv vaki makaḷir iruntaṉar. ivar kaḷiṉ oḻukkam iṉṟu intiya nāṭṭut tēvatācikaḷiṉ oḻukkattip peritum ottatu.2 pālastīṉ ālayaṅkaḷilum tēvatācikaḷ iruntārkaḷ. itaṟku ātāram kiṟittuva maṟiyiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. irācarācaṉ kaṭṭiya tañcāvūr ālayattil nāṉūṟu tēvatācikaḷ iruntārkaḷ eṉṟu oru kalveṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. makamat kacaṉi kālattil cōmanāta ālayattil 300 tēvatācikaḷ iruntaṉar.
caṅku vāttiyam
teṉṉintiya ālayaṅkaḷil caṅku vāttiyam oru ciṟappu. civaṉ, kātil aṇintiruppatu “caṅka veṇkuḻi”; tirumāl kiyil vittirup patu caṅku. “caṅkāpiṭēkam” eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ iṉṟum ālayaṅkaḷil māperum kiriyikaḷ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. intiya makkaḷ paricuttamākak koḷḷum poruḷkaḷuḷ caṅkum oṉṟu. caṅku intiyanāṭṭil māttira maṉṟu; iv vulakam muḻumiyilum tūymiyuṭiyatākak karutappaṭṭu ālayaṅkaḷil vāttiyamākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantatu. eliyaṭ cimit eṉṉum āciriyar, caṅki vāttiyamākakkoḷḷum vaḻakkam teṉṉinti yāvil ārampittuk kiḻakku nōkkic cīṉā yappāṉ kīḻittīvukaḷ pacupik kaṭaṟṟīvukaḷukku ūṭākac ceṉṟu amerikkāviyum aṭintateṉak karutiṉār.1 kirēttāt tīviṉ paḻiya makkaḷ, ālayaṅkaḷil kaṭavuḷi vīti vaḻiyē aḻittu varumpōtu caṅku vāttiyam ūtiṉārkaḷ.2 iṉṟum kiṟittuva ālayaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟil caṅku vāttiyam ūtappaṭuvati varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟum cīṉāvilē putta ālayaṅkaḷil caṅku ūtappaṭukiṉṟatu. kiṟittuvamaṟiyiṉ putiya ēṟpāṭṭil caṅku oli, uyirtteḻuvatiṉ aṭiyāḷamākak karutap paṭṭatu.1 mekcikkōvil paḻiya kiyeḻuttuc cuvaṭi oṉṟil, iraṇṭu kurumār taṅkaḷ kātukaḷit tuḷittu ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷukkup pali celuttuvatupōlavum, iraṇṭu kurumār cāmpirāṇip puki iṭuvatu pōlavum, iṉṉum iraṇṭu kurumār caṅku ūtuvatupōlavum uḷḷa paṭam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ik kiyeḻuttuc cuvaṭi ippoḻutu puḷō ṟaṉs (Florance) eṉṉumiṭattil uḷḷatu.2 ñāyiṟṟu vaṇakkam eṅkeṅku kāṇappaṭṭatō aṅku ellām caṅku vāttiyamum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. 
kaṭal uyirkaḷiṉ ōṭukaḷ mika muṟpaṭṭa kāla makkaḷāl puṉitamākak karutappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ kavaṭi (cōki) pōṉṟavi vaḻipaṭap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, kaṭavuḷ taṉmiyuṭiyaṉavātaliṉ kavaṭikaḷ makkaḷāl aṇiyappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, piṟkālaṅkaḷil avikaḷukkuppatil avi (caṅku) koṭukkum muttu cīva oḷi aḷippaṉa eṉakkaruti aṇiyappaṭa lāyiṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. muttukkaḷi aṇiyum vaḻakkiliruntē piṟkālattil cīvacattu uṭiyatākiya uruttirākkam aṇiyum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. muttu naki, muttup pallakku, muttu āparaṇam eṉpaṉa aracar, kaṭavuḷarkaḷukkuc ciṟantaṉavākak koḷḷappaṭalāyiṉa.
tuvarāṭi
civaṉ, tirumāl aṭiyavarkaḷ tuvarāṭi uṭukkiṉṟaṉar. tuvarāṭi uṭuttaṟkum paḻiya varalāṟu uṇṭu. iv vaḻakkup paṇṭi makkaḷ pala riṭiyē kāṇappaṭṭatu. caṅku vāttiyam eṅkeṅkup payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatō aṅkellām oruvakic civappuc cāyam uṟpattiyākkap paṭṭatu.3 poṉīciya makkaḷ civappuc cāyam ūṭṭiya āṭikaḷi ulakiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu vāṇikam purintaṉar. poṉīciyāviṟ ceyyappaṭṭa civanta āṭikaḷ ak kālattu uyarnta vāḻkkip poruḷ kaḷuḷ oṉṟāka viruntaṉa. cila camayaṅkaḷil ataṉ vili poṉṉukkuc camaṉākaviruntatu.1 ak kāla aracarum perumakkaḷum kurumārum iv vāṭikaḷiyē uṭuttaṉar. kōyilkaḷilum iv vāṭikaḷ alaṅ kārattiṉ poruṭṭut tūkkappaṭṭaṉa. mōcē, yekōvāviṉ kūṭṭukku (vimāṉam) civappu āṭiyiyē payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. kurumārum anniṟa uṭiyi aṇintaṉar. pāpilōṉiya teyvaṅkaḷukkuc civappu āṭi kaḷē uṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptiya pāpilōṉiya aracarum inniṟa uṭi kaḷiyē uṭuttiṉar. kirīcilum urōmilum aracarum perumakkaḷum kuru mārum uyarnta karumikaḷum māttiram civappu uṭi aṇintaṉar. ēṉiya makkaḷukku iv vurimi maṟukkappaṭṭiruntatu. ik kaṭṭuppāṭṭi mīṟic civappu āṭi aṇiyum potumakkaḷ kaṭuntaṇṭaṉi aṭintārkaḷ; cila camayaṅkaḷil kolit taṇṭaṉiyum peṟṟaṉar. pappuṟā (PauPra) eṉṉum collē civappi uṇarttum pēppiḷ (Purple) eṉṉum collāka māṟiṟṟu. poṉīciya makkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ koṇṭuvantu viṟṟa civappu āṭi paccivaṭam eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. pacci eṉpatu pappuṟā eṉpataṉ uccarippu vēṟupāṭu eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
intiya nāṭṭilum civappu uṭi uyarntatākak karutappaṭṭatu. teyvaṅkaḷukkuc civanta āṭikaḷē uṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. tirumurukāṟṟup paṭiyil teyva makaḷir tampalappūcci pōṉṟa niṟa āṭi uṭuppavarkaḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. paripāṭal, tirumāl civanta paṭṭāṭi uṭutti yiruppavarākak kūṟukiṉṟatu. murukak kaṭavuḷ uṭuttiyiruppatu civanta āṭi (muruku). maṟṟum kaṭavuḷarum civanta āṭiyiyē uṭuttiyirup patākap paṭaṅkaḷ variyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. māṇikkavācakar tirupperuntuṟi yil tīkki peṟṟapōtu aṇintiruntatu civappu āṭi (tiruvā. pu).
itaṉāl kaṭavuḷar perumakkaḷ kurumāriṉ uṭikaḷ civappu niṟamā yiruntaṉa veṉṟum, ammarapip piṉpaṟṟiyē kaṭavuḷ aṭiyār tuvar āṭi aṇiyum vaḻakku ēṟpaṭṭateṉṟum naṉku tuṇiyappaṭukiṉṟatu.
cuvattikam
cuvattika aṭiyāḷam puttar kālattukkup piṉpē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa teṉap palar karutalām. ñāyiṟṟu vaḻipāṭu, caṅku vāttiyam eṉpavi kaḷippōlavē ik kuṟiyum mikap paḻamiyuṭiyatu. varalāṟṟuk kālat tukku muṟpaṭṭa cuvattikam poṟitta paḻamporuḷkaḷ teṉ intiyāviṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1 ātikāla makkaḷ ñāyiṟṟik kuṟikkap puḷḷaṭiyi (X) allatu puḷḷaṭiyiccuṟṟi vaṭṭamiṭṭa kuṟiyi vaḻaṅ kiṉar.2 ippuḷḷaṭi kuricu (Cross) eṉa āṅkila moḻiyil vaḻaṅkum. puḷ ḷaṭiyiluḷḷa kōṭukaḷiṉ vaḷivē cuvattikak kuṟiyākum. itaṉāl puḷḷaṭi cuvattikamāka vaḷarcciyuṟṟateṉa aṟiyalām. cuvattikam eṉpatu vaṭamoḻippeyar. āṅkilō cākcaṉiyar itaṉi ‘vavarvoṟ’ (Fowerfot) eṉṟum, pirāṉciyar kuṟōyikskiṟāmi (Croix Grammee) eṉṟum, skāntiṉēviyar tōrkkaṭavuḷiṉ cutti (Thor’s Hammer) eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉar. cuvattikam eṉpataṟku makiḻcci eṉṟu poruḷ. patañ caliyiṉ yōka cūttirattil yōkācaṉattil oṉṟu cuvattikam eṉappaṭu kiṉṟatu. itu intiyāvilō kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷ oṉṟilō tōṉṟi iv vulakam muṟṟilum paraviyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉa ārāycciyāḷar tuṇikiṉ ṟaṉar. māntar nūlār ulakil ellāp pākaṅkaḷiṉum iv vaṭiyāḷam camayakkuṟiyāka vaḻaṅkiyateṉak kūṟi ulakappaṭattiṉ ellāp pākaṅkaḷi lum puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.3 civaṉ eṉṉum peyar ñāyiṟ ṟukkuc cālap paḻiya peyar eṉṟu muṉ viḷakkappaṭṭatu. cuva eṉpatē civa eṉa vantateṉa moḻiyārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar. civaṉ campantamāṉa kuṟi eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyē ñāyiṟṟukkuṟikku cuvattikam eṉap peyar iṭappaṭṭatu eṉa nām naṉku uyttuṇaralām. cuvattikakkuṟi āriyariṉ intiya varukikku muṟpaṭṭatu. 
heras pātiriyār kūṟuvatu: mokañcotarōvil vāḻnta tirāviṭamakkaḷ ciṟiya caturamāṉa nakikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avikaḷil cuvattikam poṟikkappaṭṭiruntatu. aṅkuk kiṭitta paṭṭiyaṅkaḷilum ik kuṟi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv vaṭiyāḷam tirāviṭa makkaḷuṭiyatu. putitāka intiyāvi aṭinta āriyartām putitākak kikkoṇṭa palavaṟṟōṭu iv vaṭiyāḷattiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ic cuvattika aṭiyāḷam tamiḻaruṭaṉkūṭa mēṟkē kirēttā (crete), kirīs, ēṟṟūṉā (Etruna), aipīriyā (speyiṉ) mutaliyavum piṟavumākiya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟatu. mokañcotarōvilum, ilaṅkiyilum vaḻaṅkiyatupōla atu an nāṭukaḷilum cukam eṉṉum poruḷik kuṟittatu. kuṭikkum kiṇṇat til iv vaṭiyāḷam irukkumāyiṉ atu kuṭippavaṉukkuc cukam taruva tāka eṉṉum poruḷik kuṟittatu. iv vaṭiyāḷam eṟṟuskiya (Etrus-cian) camātit tūṇkaḷilum, kiṟittavariṉ kallaṟikaḷilum poṟikkap paṭṭaṉa. itu, iv vaṭiyāḷattiṉ kīḻ aṭakkañceyyappaṭṭavar maṟu ulakil cukamāka irukkaṭṭum eṉṉum poruḷik kuṟittatu.
ākamaṅkaḷ1
ālayam amikkum muṟikaḷiyum, aṅku naṭakkavēṇṭiya kiriyi vakikaḷiyum, makkaḷ ālayaṅkaḷil kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ceyya vēṇṭiya muṟikaḷiyum kūṟum nūlkaḷ ākamaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭum. vētaṅ kaḷum ākamaṅkaḷum nērvirōtamuṭiyaṉa.2 vētaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum teyvaṅkaḷum ākamaṅkaḷiṟ kiḷakkappaṭum teyvaṅkaḷum vēṟu. vētaṅkaḷ varuṇāccirama tarumaṅkaḷi valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. ākamaṅkaḷ cariyi kiriyi mutaliyavikaḷik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ālaya vaḻipāṭu vētaṅ kaḷukku aṉṉiyamāṉatu. ākamaṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭirut taliṉ avi tamiḻaruṭiyaṉavākā eṉac cilar karututalkūṭum. em moḻi yil ākamaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉavēṉum avikaḷiṟ potintuḷḷa poruḷkaḷikkoṇṭu avi evarukkuc contam eṉa aṟital eḷitu. ākamaṅkaḷ tamiḻaruṭiyaṉavē eṉpatu varalāṟṟu nūlār naṉku ārāyntu aṟutiyiṭṭa muṭivu. upaniṭataṅkaḷ pāracīka moḻiyilum ilattīṉ moḻi yilum moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāracīkarum, ittāliyarum an nūlkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭiyaṉaveṉṟu vātāṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. ālayam kaṭṭum ciṟpakkali, tiruvuruvaṅkaḷic ceyyummuṟi mutaliyaṉavum ivi pōṉṟaṉavum perumpālum avvat toḻilkaḷukkuriya kūṭṭattiṉa riṭiyē ēṭṭileḻutappaṭātu parampariyākat toṭarntu vantuḷḷaṉa. iv vaki vaḻakkukaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum tiraṭṭip piṟkālattil aiyarmār eḻutivittaṉar. tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ ulōkaṅkaḷil amikkum muṟikaḷ nīṇṭakālam eḻutappaṭātu parampariyāka vantateṉpatik kaṉkūli eṉpār teṉṉintiya veṇkalat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ eṉṉum nūliṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.1
ākamaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭum yōkam, ñāṉam mutaliya pakutikaḷ piṟ kālattu eḻutappaṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum. tamiḻil aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam eṉṉum muppālāka nūl ceytalē vaḻakku. vīṭupaṟṟi nūl ceytal vaḻak kiṉṟu.2
“annili maruṅkiṉ aṟamutal ākiya
mummutaṟ poruṭkum uriya veṉpa” (tol. ce. 106)
iv vaḻakkuppaṟṟiyē tiruvaḷḷuvarum muppālāka nūl ceytār. āriya riṉ muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟiyē tamiḻil nūlkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu cilar vātippar. itu tantikku makaṉ muppāṭṭaṉ eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōr koḷki. nāṟpālāka nūl ceytalē āriyar marapu.
purāṇaṅkaḷ
purāṇaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa paḻiya varalāṟukaḷ. purāṇaṅkaḷil paḻiya aracar varalāṟukaḷum avarkaḷ kālaṅkaḷil nikaḻnta periya ceyalkaḷum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aracarkaḷatu capikaḷilē pulavarkaḷ iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aracaṉatu parampariyiluḷḷa varalāṟukaḷi ellām tiraṭṭip perunāṭkaḷil araca capikaḷil kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. ivvāṟu vaṭanāṭṭu aracar capikaḷil irunta pulavarkaḷ cūtar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉālēyē purāṇaṅkaḷ cūta muṉivarāṟ collappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu kaṉṉa parampariyil vanta varalāṟukaḷ piṟkālattut tiraṭṭi eḻutappaṭalāyiṉa. ivikaḷē paḻiya intiya makkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷākum. maṟṟiya nāṭukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum paḻiya varalāṟukaḷ purāṇa varalāṟukaḷi viṭac ciṟantaṉa eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu. apastampar (ki. mu. 400), kuṭaliyar mutalāṉōr purāṇaṅkaḷik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷamiyiṉ ak kālattilēyē purāṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruntaṉaveṉa pārkittar (Pargiter) eṉṉum āriyar kūṟuvar.
purāṇaṅkaḷi naṉku ārāyntu, intiya makkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟuk katikaḷ eṉa nūl eḻutiya pārkittar eṉpār kūṟiyiruppatiṉ curukkam piṉ varumāṟu:
purāṇakkatikaḷ eṉappaṭum paḻiya varalāṟukaḷil pirāmaṇar ki yiṭṭu avikaḷi vēṇṭiyaḷavu perukkiyum, pirāmaṇak katikaḷiyum kaṭṭukkatikaḷiyum cērttum, taṅkaḷ cāttirak koḷkikaḷiyum kiriyikaḷiyum pukuttiyum irukkiṉṟaṉar. pirāmaṇariṉ katikaḷ pirāmaṇarukku eppaṭip pulappaṭumō avvāṟu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ak katikaḷil pirāmaṇariṉ karuttukkaḷum, pirāmaṇariṉ uyarvum, avar kaḷiṉ puṉitat taṉmikaḷum, avarkaḷ maṉitarukku appāṟpaṭṭa nili yil iruppatum, ivipōṉṟaṉavumākiya avarkku māttiram vāyp puṭiya koḷkikaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ak katikaḷ ciṟitum porutta maṟṟaṉa. pirāmaṇarkaḷiṉ piḻiyiṉāl purāṇaṅkaḷ matippi iḻantu viṭṭaṉa.1 varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷākiya purāṇaṅkaḷ pirāmaṇariṉ kiyīṭṭiṉāl camaya nūlkaḷāka māṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
ciṟuvaṉ oruvaṉ ciṟitāyuḷḷa irapparp piyi (Baloon) eppaṭi ūtip perukkac ceykiṟāṉō avvāṟu ciṟiya aḷavil irunta paṇṭi makkaḷ varalāṟṟi ataṉiṉum paṉmaṭaṅku peritākap pirāmaṇar tam conta nalam karutip perukkiyuḷḷaṉar.
itikācaṅkaḷ
itikācam eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vaḻaṅkuvaṉavum purāṇaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa paḻiya varalāṟukaḷē. ivvaralāṟukaḷilum pala kaṭṭukkatikaḷ nuḻintuḷḷaṉa. purāṇaṅkaḷil kiyiṭṭatupōlavē itikācaṅkaḷilum pirāmaṇar kiyiṭṭu avikaḷiṉ perumiyik kuṟittuviṭṭaṉar. pārkittar kūṟuvatu: “pirāmaṇariṉ katikaḷ potuvil nampattakkaṉa valla. irāmāyaṇattil pirāmaṇak koḷkikaḷ peritum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. katiyum putumuṟiyākavum nampakkūṭātatākavum irukkiṉṟatu.”1
mattiyatari makkaḷ ellōrukkum ākamam oṉṟē
mēl nāṭṭu makkaṭkula nūlār mattiyataric cātiyiṉar eṉappirik kum peruṅkūṭṭattil polinīciyar, tamiḻar, ekiptiyar, pāpilōṉiyar, kirēttā makkaḷ paḻiya ittāliyar mutaliyōrum, iṉṉum mattiya tarikkaṭali aṭutta nāṭukaḷil vāḻum makkaḷum aṭaṅkuvar. im makkaḷ ellōrum orukālattu oru kūṭṭamāka vāḻntu piṉpu pirintu ceṉṟōr. mattiyatari nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa makkaḷē orukālattil intiyāvi yaṭintu tamiḻar eṉṉum cātiyiṉarākap perukiṉārkaḷ eṉak karutap paṭṭatu. paḻiya makkaḷ varalāṟukaḷ ōraḷavu veḷiccam aṭintuḷḷa ik kālattu, tamiḻ makkaḷē mattiyatari nāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ eṉṉuṅ koḷki valuttuḷḷatu. heras pātiriyār ārampattil muṉṉiya koḷkiyuṭiyavarākavē iruntār. mokañcotarō, arappāviṟ kiṭitta āyirattu eṇṇūṟu eḻuttuppoṟitta muttiri kaḷi vācittapiṉ avar tamiḻarkaḷē mattiyatari nāṭukaḷiṟceṉṟu kuṭi yēṟiṉārkaḷ eṉap palavakiyil ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2
viviliya vētattiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭil, mattiyatari makkaḷ ellōruṭiya vaḻipāṭukaḷum oruvakiyiṉa eṉpataṟkuriya ātāraṅkaḷ niṟiyak kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟi īṇṭut tarukiṉṟōm. avaṟṟi iṉṟum intiyanāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaḻakkukaḷōṭu oppu nōkki uṇmi ōrka.
“yekōvā mocēyi nōkki atiuttama cukanta tiraviyaṅ kaḷākiya cittamāṉa veḷḷippōḷattil paricutta snāṉattiṟkuc cēkkaliṉpaṭi 5000 cēkkalum, karuvāppaṭṭiyil atiṟ pātiyākiya 2500 cēkkalum cukanta vacampu 2500 cēkkalum, ilavaṅkappaṭṭiyil 500 cēkka lum, cītaviruṭcatteṇṇeyil oru kiṉṉum eṭuttu, parimaḷa tilak kāraṉuṭiya ceykiyākac ceyyappaṭṭa parimaḷa tilattippōla, avi kaḷiṉāṟ cutta tilattic ceyvāyāka. atuvē apiṣēka tilamāka irukkavēṇṭum. ataṉālē capiyiṉ āvācattukkum, cāṭcip peṭ ṭikkum, pīṭattukkum kiḷi viḷakkukkum tūpavētikikaḷukkum, takaṉa palivētikikkum apiṣēkam paṇṇakkaṭavāyāka. ārōṉum avaṉ puttirarum eṉakku ācāriyattoṇṭu puriyumpaṭi nī avarkaḷukku apiṣēkam paṇṇi avarkaḷip paricuttappaṭuttuvāy. (yāt: 30. 22-33) (yāt: 25. 30) eṉ cannitiyil eppoḻutum pīṭattiṉmītu appam vikkavēṇṭum. nivēttiyattukku oru kūṭiyil eṇṇeyiṟ picinta puḷippillāta melliya māviṉāṟ ceyta atiracaṅkaḷiyum eṇṇey taṭavappaṭṭa puḷippillāta aṭikaḷiyum koṇṭuvarak kaṭavaṉ. avikaḷi ācāriyaṉ koṇṭuvantu yekōvāviṉ cannati yil... paliyāka iṭakkaṭavaṉ. nivēttiyamākum poruṭṭu aṭukkun tōṟum cuttakunturuvi iṭakkaṭavāy.......nittiyamāka yekōvāvukku akkiṉiyiliṭappaṭum nivēttiyaṅkaḷuḷ avi mikavum paricuttamāṉa vikaḷākiyāl avikaḷ ārōṉukkum avaṉ puttirarkaḷukkum urittākum; avikaḷip paricutta stāṉattiṟ pucikkakkaṭavarkaḷ. (lēvi. 16 12-13.) yekōvāviṉ cannitiyilirukkum vētikiyileriyum nerupput taṇalkaḷiṉālē takaṉatūpa kalacatti niṟittu, tūḷākkappaṭṭa cukanta tūpa varkkattil taṉ ki niṟiya aḷḷit tirikkuḷḷē koṇṭu vantu cāṭcip pattirattiṉ mēlirukkum kirupācayatti mūṭattakkatāka, yekōvāviṉ cannitiyil akkiṉiyiṉmēl tūpa varkkattip pōṭak kaṭavaṉ.
(lēvi. 24. 1-4) yekōvā mocēyi nōkki nittamum viḷak kēṟṟumpaṭikku isravēlar iṭittuppiḻinta cītaviruṭcattiṉ cuttamāṉa eṇṇeyi uṉṉiṭattil koṇṭuvarumpaṭi avarkaḷukkuk kaṭṭaḷi yiṭu. ārōṉāṉavaṉ tiriccīlikku veḷiyē cāyaṅkālam toṭakkam viṭiyaṟkāla pariyantam yekōvāviṉ cannitiyil nittamum viḷakku ēṟṟakkaṭavaṉ; (eṇ 8. 1-4) yekōvā mocēyi nōkki ārōṉukku nī viḷakkēṟṟumpōtu ēḻu tīpamum, kiḷiviḷakkut taṇṭukaḷukku nērē oḷikoṭukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu col eṉṟār.
(nāḷā 15. 15-16) yekōvāviṉ vākkiṉpaṭi, mocē kaṭṭaḷi yiṭṭa pirakāram tēvaṉuṭiya peṭṭiyit taṇṭōṭum taṅkaḷ tōḷiṉ mēl eṭuttukkoṇṭu vantārkaḷ; taṅkaḷ cakōtararit tampuruvum, kiṉṉaramum, kittāḷaṅkaḷumākiya kītavāttiyaṅkaḷ muḻaṅka āṉantak kūttuṭaṉ uratta cattamāyp pāṭumpaṭi ēṟpaṭuttumpaṭi tāvītu kaṭṭaḷi yiṭṭāṉ (mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi ati. 2.). ūtu kompōṭum, pūrikikaḷōṭum, kittāḷattōṭum, tampuruvum, kiṉṉaramum muḻaṅka isravēlaraṉi varum yekōvāviṉ uṭaṉpaṭikkip peṭṭiyik koṇṭuvantārkaḷ.
(eṇ. 8-10) uṅkaḷ āṉanta tiṉattilum, uṟcava nāḷilum mācāram paṅkaḷilum uṅkaḷ takaṉa palikaḷiyum stōttira palikaḷiyum celuttumpōtu ekkāḷaṅkaḷi ūtavēṇṭum. (caṅ. 18-5) kiṉṉaratti ṉālum kiṉṉarattōṭu, kītattiṉōciyiṉālum yekōvāvip pāṭuṅkaḷ. (2. cāmu 6.5) tāvītum isravēl cantatiyār camastarum yekō vāviṉ camukattil tēvatāruviṉāṟ ceyta kiṉṉaramum, tampuruvum, miru taṅkamum, vīṇiyum, kittāḷamumākiya nāṉāvita vāttiyaṅkaḷiyum muḻakkiṉārkaḷ.
(lēvi. 2-3) ōyvunāḷum mācappiṟappum amāvāciyum, pūraṇi yum, uṟcava nāḷkaḷum puṇṇiya kālaṅkaḷ. antak kālaṅkaḷilē nitti yākkiṉiyilē iṟicci mutaliyavikaḷiyiṭṭut takaṉapali ceytal vēṇṭum. taṅkaḷ caṅkaṟpattiṉpaṭiyē niyamitta tiṉaṅkaḷilē upavacittu viratamaṉuṭṭittal vēṇṭumeṉṟuñ collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. (lēvi. 12. 1-5) oru stiri karuppavatiyāki āṇpiḷḷi peṟṟāl acuciyiṉ nimittam rutumatiyippōla ēḻunāḷ acuciyāyirukkakkaṭavaḷ. piṉ avaḷ utiracuci nimittam muppattu mūṉṟunāḷ variyum acuciyāyirup pāḷāka. cucikkēṟṟa tiṉaṅkaḷ niṟivēṟumaḷavum entap paricutta vastu viyum paricikkavum paricutta stāṉattiṟ piravēcikkavum kūṭātu. (nāḷā. 15. 19-24) rutumatiyāṉa stiri taṉ carīrattiṉ ūṟaliṉimittam ēḻu nāḷa ḷavum acuciyāyiruppāḷ. avaḷip paricikkiṟavaṉ evaṉoruvaṉō avaṉ cāyaṅkāla pariyantam acuciyākaviruppāṉ. avaḷ acuciyāyiruk kiyil avaḷ paṭutta entap paṭukkiyum avaḷirunta enta ācaṉamum acuciyāyirukkum.”
1ācāriyapiṭēkam, viratam aṉuṭṭittal, maraṇa, ceṉaṉa tīṭṭuk kāttal, ālayaṅkaḷil kiriyipurital, tīyil paliyi iṭutal, iviyum ivi pōṉṟaṉavum tamiḻar āriya makkaḷiṭamiruntu kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉa valla veṉpataṟku mēṟkāṭṭiyavi ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷākum. paḻiya tamiḻnāṭṭu vaḻakkukaḷiyē nammavar tavaṟāka āriyak koḷki kaḷeṉak karuti varukiṉṟaṉar.
camayattiṉ akavaḷarcci
itukāṟum kūṟiyaṉa camayattiṉ puṟavaḷarcci muṟikaḷ. camayam iṉṉoru vakiyilum vaḷarcciyaṭivatāyiṟṟu. ataṉi akavaḷarcci eṉak kūṟalām. makkaḷ iv vulakattiyum, tammiyum oruṅkē iyakkum mēlāṉa oru poruḷiyumpaṟṟi ārāyat talippaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ am mēlāṉa poruḷ tamatu cintikku varātateṉṟum atuvē ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum iyakkukiṉṟateṉṟum kaṇṭārkaḷ. ākavē avarkaḷ apperum poruḷukku ‘uḷḷattik kaṭantatu’ eṉṉum poruḷil kaṭavuḷ eṉṟum, ellāvaṟṟiyum iyakkuvatu eṉṉum poruḷil iyavuḷ eṉṟum peyarkaḷi iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar. ip peyarkaḷ. tamiḻil mikap paḻamiyuṭiyaṉa. mokañcotarō muttirikaḷil kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum peyar iruttali heras pātiriyār kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.1 mokañcota rōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa tiruvuruvam oṉṟu, kaṭavuḷ, pacupati eṉṉum karutti viḷakkā niṟkiṉṟatu. avvuruvam yōkattilamarntirukkum tirukkōlat tuṭaṉumuḷḷatu.2 itaṉāl ak kāla makkaḷ mupporuḷ uṇmikaḷi yum, cintiyiyaṭakkic cummāvirukkiṉṟa yōkamuṟikaḷiyum naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷeṉpaṉa teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭum. maṟṟiya nāṭukaḷil iv vuṇmikaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭiruntamikkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭa villi. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ camaya akavaḷarcci mika uyarnili peṟṟiruntami yiṉālēyē avarkaḷ putitāka vanta āriya makkaḷiṉ camayakkoḷki kaḷukku uṭampaṭārāyiṉar. āriyarukkum, tācukkaḷ eṉa avarkaḷāl iḻittuk kūṟappaṭṭa tamiḻarkaḷukkumiṭiyil nērnta pōrkaḷukku mukkiya kāraṇam, tācukkaḷ āriyar teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭa maṟuttatōṭu avarkaḷ vēḷvikaḷiyum veṟuttamiyēyākum. irāvaṇaṉ ātiyōr vaṭa nāṭṭu muṉivarkaḷ uñaṟṟum vēḷvikaḷukku iṭiyūṟāy niṉṟu avi kaḷi aḻittu vanta varalāṟum irāmāyaṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
āriyar, intiyāvi aṭintapōtu ulakam, uyir, kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum mupporuḷ uṇmikaḷi aṟintirukkavilli. maṟupiṟappip paṟṟiyē vētaṅkaḷ aṟiyā eṉṟu muṉ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. vētamatam tattuvak koḷki (Philosophy) illāta matam. āriyamakkaḷ intiya nāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟiya kālattil āriyar tamiḻar eṉṉum iru kūṭṭattiṉa rum kalantu oṉṟupaṭṭaṉar. ak kālattil pirāmaṇar pirāmaṇarallātār āriyar āriyarallātārkaḷiṉ kalapput tirumaṇaṅkaḷik kaṭṭuppaṭuttum caṭṭaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyirukkavilli.3 ak kālattil tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ koḷkikaḷ palavaṟṟi āriyamakkaḷ piṉpaṟṟalāyiṉar. avaṟṟuḷ kāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu tavañceytalākiya vaḻakku oṉṟu. kāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu tavañceytalippaṟṟi irukkuvētattiṟ kāṇappaṭavilli eṉa ār. ci. tattar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. itaṉāl iv vaḻakkut tamiḻarkaḷuṭiyatē eṉṟu naṉku tuṇitum.
“kāmañ cāṉṟa kaṭikkōṭ kāli
ēmañ cāṉṟa makkaḷoṭu tuvaṉṟi
aṟampuri cuṟṟamoṭu kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum
1ciṟantatu payiṟṟal iṟantataṉ payaṉē.” (tol. kaṟ-51)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiramum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum.
upaniṭataṅkaḷ
upaniṭataṅkaḷē tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paṇṭimaṟikaḷ eṉpatu muṉ ōriṭattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu. āriyap pirāmaṇarkaḷuṭ cilar uṇmiñāṉaṅ kaḷi aṟiyum tākam uṭiyavarkaḷāy iṭaṅkaḷtōṟum alintu tirintaṉar. ivvāṟu tirinta yākñavalkiyum avaruṭaṉ ceṉṟa maṟṟiya pirāmaṇarkaḷum vitēka nāṭṭaracaṉākiya caṉakaṉik kaṇṭārkaḷ. yākña valki caṉakaṉiṭattil māṇākkaṉāka amarntu uṇmi ñāṉattip payiṉṟār. itaṉpiṉ pirāmaṇar, tām muṉṉorupōtum aṟintirāta uṇmiñāṉaṅkaḷi aracamarapiṉariṉ pātaṅkaḷiṉkīḻ māṇākkarāyiruntu payiṉṟaṉar. ataṉpiṉpē āriyaruṭiya matam tattuvak koḷkiyuṭiya tāka māṟiṟṟu. viṉraṇic2 eṉṉum aṟiñar upaniṭataṅkaḷip paṟṟi ārāyntu kūṟiyiruppatiṉ curukkam piṉvarumāṟu:
“upaniṭatañāṉam aracarkaḷiṭam māttiramaṉṟu, peṇkaḷiṭamum aiyamāṉa kulattavarkaḷiṭiyum iruntatu. pirāmaṇaṉ māttiram āciriya ṉāka amarntu mūvarukkum vētaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkalām eṉa vaṟpuṟutti āriyarkaḷuṭiya nīti nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.3 āṉāl aracarum aracar kulattavarkaḷumē uyarnta ñāṉattip peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṟum, avarka ḷiṭamiruntē pirāmaṇar avvaṟivip payiṉṟaṉareṉṟum upaniṭataṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. aracar avvāṟu aḷitta ñāṉam maṟupiṟapput toṭarpuṭi yatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iññāṉam pirāmaṇar ciṟitum aṟiyātatu. upaniṭata meṉpataṟkuk kiṭṭa iruppatu eṉṟu poruḷ. āciriyaṉ oruva ṉiṭattil cila maṟivāṉa uṇmikaḷip paṭittaṟku māṇākkaṉāyirut tal eṉa itu poruḷpaṭum. avvāṟu koṭukkappaṭum ñāṉam maṟi (irakaciyam) eṉappaṭṭatu. upaniṭataṅkaḷ, ammaṟiyit takutiyillātavar kaḷukku aḷittal kūṭāteṉa vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu, im maṟiyit tanti taṉ mūtta putalvaṉukku allatu uṇmiyāṉa māṇākkaṉukku aḷikkalām; iv vulakam muḻuvatiyum peṟuvatāyirun tālum maṟṟavarkaḷukku aḷittal kūṭātu. oruvaṉ āciriyaṉiṭam māṇāk kaṉāka amarntu palamuṟi varuntik kēṭṭapōtu iññāṉam veḷi yiṭappaṭṭateṉa upaniṭataṅkūṟukiṉṟatu.” upaniṭata kālatti ārāycci yāḷar palavāṟu kūṟuvar. 1ṟaṉēṭ eṉpār upaniṭata kālam ki.mu. 1200kkum ki.mu. 600kkum iṭiyil eṉak kūṟuvar. itu kāṟum maṟivāka maṟi eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ nilaviya tamiḻarkaḷiṉ uṇmiñāṉaṅkaḷ av vāriya makkaḷ vāyilāka upaniṭatam eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ veḷivaralāyiṉa. ak kālattilēyē civavaḻipāṭu āriyarukkuḷ talimi peṟṟiruntatu. āriyariṉ uruttiraṉ eṉṉum kaṭavuḷum civaṉum oruvarākak karutap paṭṭaṉar.2
upaniṭataṅkaḷ uyir, ulakam, iṟivaṉ eṉṉum mupporuḷ kaḷiṉ uṇmikaḷi ārāykiṉṟaṉa; yōkattippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa; ōṅkāratti uccarikkumpaṭi valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa; māyi uḷporuḷ eṉak koḷkiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum uṇmikaḷ ellāk kālattukkum ellāmakkaḷukkum potuvāṉavi.1 upaniṭatañāṉam ñāyattukku uṭpaṭṭatu; vētaṅkaḷukku māṟāṉatu.2
upaniṭataṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭum piraṇavam tamiḻukkē uriyatu. vaṭa moḻiyil e, o eṉṉum kuṟilkaḷ illi. enta moḻiyil e, o eṉṉum kuṟilkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō am moḻikkē ō, ē eṉṉum neṭilkaḷum uriyaṉa. im muṟiyikkoṇṭu, pantākkar, pālimoḻi yil e, ō eṉṉum kuṟilkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟamiyiṉ makatanāṭṭil tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiyateṉṟum, ekara okaraṅkaḷ tamiḻukkē uriyaṉaveṉṟum kāṭṭi yuḷḷār.
cāṅkiyam
upaniṭataṅkaḷukkuppiṉ tōṉṟiyatu kapilariṉ cāṅkiya nūl. cāṅkiya matakkoḷki ‘catkāriyavātam’ eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. itu māyi uḷ poruḷeṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu; viṉi, viṉippayaṉkaḷi valiyuṟuttu kiṉṟatu. itu kaṭavuḷippaṟṟi yātum pēcavilli. ākavē, itu nīrīccura cāṅkiyam eṉavumpaṭum. kapilariṉ nūṟkoḷkikaḷukku aṭippaṭi upaniṭataṅkaḷ. cāṅkiya nūl ki. mu. 760 variyiṟ ceyyappaṭṭateṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. kirēkka tattuvacāttiriyākiya patakōras eṉpavar intiyāvukku vantu kapilariṉ cāṅkiyanūṟ karuttukkaḷip payiṉṟu avaṟṟik kirīcil veḷiyiṭṭār eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.3 purāṇaṅ kaḷilē kapilariṉ vēḷvik kutiriyip piṭittukkaṭṭiya kapilar oruvar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟār. avar tamiḻmarapiṉar eṉa olṭkām eṉṉum āciriyar ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷār.4 kutamaputtar kapilarmarapil vantavareṉṟum, avar tamiḻmarapiṉareṉṟum karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.1 cāṅkiyanūl ovvoṉ ṟiyum niyāyavāyilāka ārāykiṉṟatu. cāṅkiyam vētamārkkattukku muṟṟum muraṇpaṭṭatu.
puttam
kapilarukkuppiṉ tōṉṟiyatu puttamatakkoḷki. im matakkoḷ kikku aṭippaṭi upaniṭataṅkaḷum cāṅkiyanūlum. puttamatam māyiyi ilporuḷeṉak kūṟum. ākavē, am matak koḷki cūṉiyavātam eṉappaṭum. cāṅkiyam, puttam mutaliya mataṅkaḷ, upaniṭatak koḷkikaḷ evvāṟu ciṟitu ciṟitāka māṟupaṭṭu vevvēṟu matak koḷkikaḷāka māṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kāṇpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷākum. puttamatam vēta matattukku māṟāṉatu. puttariṉñāṉam tamiḻar ñāṉatti aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭateṉa mēlnāṭṭu āciriyar tāmum eḷitilaṟintu kūṟiyuḷ ḷār.2 ālṭaṉpēk eṉṉum āciriyar upaniṭataṅkaḷ puttarukku evvāṟu vaḻikāṭṭikaḷāyiruntaṉaveṉpati naṉku ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.3
yōkam
kuru māṇākka muṟiyil neṭuṅkālam vantaṉavum upaniṭataṅ kaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉavumākiya yōkanūṟ poruḷkaḷip patañcali muṉivar tiraṭṭi (ki.mu. 150) yōka cūttiraṅkaḷākac ceytār.4 yōkam eṉpatu maṉatti ōriṭattil niṟuttic ceyyappaṭum tiyāṉam. yōkanūlil maṉatti ōriṭattil niṟuttutaṟkuriya payiṟcikaḷum yōkattiṉāl aṭiyumpēṟum naṉku viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. yōkanūlum niyāya muṟiyāka uṇmikaḷi ārāyntu. yōkattiṉ muṭivu, piṟappi oḻittal. kaṭavuḷi aṭital eṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ itu, ‘īcuvara cāṅkiyam’ eṉappaṭum.1 yōka nūl piraṇavatti uccarikkavēṇṭum eṉa vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟatu. maṉam cārntataṉ taṉmiyi aṭikiṉṟateṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu;2 paṟṟi aṟukka vēṇṭumeṉa moḻikiṉṟatu; ap paṟṟi aṟuppataṟku īcuvaraṉippaṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉa navilkiṉṟatu. kaṭavuḷ patti, yōkaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟantateṉa aṟikiṉṟatu.3 akattūymi puṟattūymikaḷip paṟṟip pēcukiṉṟatu. nallavar iṇakkattippaṟṟic cāṟṟukiṉṟatu; maṉam uyiriṉ akak karuvi yātalippaṟṟip pukalkiṉṟatu. cāṅkiyanūlum yōkanūlum uyirkaḷ pala eṉak koḷkiṉṟaṉa.4 iv vaki yōkavaḻikaḷi ellām tamiḻmakkaḷ mikamika muṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu mokañ cotarōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa tiruvuruvaṅkaḷāl naṉku teḷivuṟukiṉṟa teṉpatu muṉṉōriṭattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
yōkamum ālaya vaḻipāṭum
yōkap paḻakkattukku oru vaṭivi maṉattil niṟuttutalvēṇṭum. allāviṭiṉ maṉam ōr iṭattil nillātu. ākavē, kuṇaṅkuṟiyillāta kaṭavuḷi oru vaṭivam koṭuttu niṉikka vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. ataṟku ālayaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ payaṉaḷittaṉa. camaya tīkki, mantiraṅ kaḷi nūṟṟeṭṭu āyiratteṭṭu eṉṟu uccarittal mutaliyaṉa. avvāṟu ceyyumiṭattu maṇikaḷi eṇṇutal mūccuppayiṟci purital pōlvaṉa yōkap payiṟciyiṉ mutaṟpaṭikaḷākum. ippoḻutu, ālaya pūci aracaṉukkuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭupōl karutappaṭātu, yōkaniliyiṟ ki vantār oruvar iv vulaka makkaḷ ellōrum tīviṉikaḷiṉiṉṟum nīṅki nalvaḻippaṭavēṇṭumeṉa niṉintu kaṭavuḷi niṉippatu eṉak karutap paṭuvatāyiṟṟu. itu parārtta pūci (piṟarporuṭṭucceyyum pūci) eṉappaṭalāyiṟṟu.
“vāḻka antaṇar vāṉavar āṉiṉam
vīḻka taṇpuṉal vēntaṉum ōṅkuka
āḻka tīyatellām araṉ nāmamē
cūḻka viyakamum tuyar tīrkavē.”
eṉṟavāṟu vēṇṭutal parārtta pūcikku eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. yōka nili kivarappeṟāta pārppāṉiyē tirumūlar “pērkoṇṭa pārppāṉ” eṉak kūṟiṉār ātalkāṇka.
yōkam kivanta periyōr niṉippaṉa niṉittapaṭi ākum. eṉa makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ.1 cittarkaḷ eṉappaṭṭōr yōkattiṉ makimiyi ṉāl pala putumikaḷic ceytōrkaḷēyāvar. ellāc camayattavarkaḷum tattam camaya mutalvarkaḷ putumikaḷi viḷittārkaḷeṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iṟivaṉiṭattē, uḷḷam paṭintu niṟkum uyirkaḷiṭattu iṟivaṉiṉ iṟimik kuṇaṅkaḷ cila uṇṭāvatu iyalpu eṉac civacittānta nūlkaḷ kūṟum. maruttuvam cōtiṭam pōṉṟa periya uṇmikaḷ yōki kaḷāl tamatu teḷinta uḷḷakkāṭciyiṟ kaṇṭu kūṟappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu nampappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tirumantiram
tamiḻmakkaḷiṭiyē vaḻaṅkiya uṇmi aṟivukaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṉ tiraṭṭāka veḷivantuḷḷatu tirumūlar aruḷiya tirumantiram eṉṉum nūl. in nūliṟ kāṇappaṭum akaccāṉṟukaḷāl itiṟ kūṟappaṭum poruḷkaḷ tamiḻmakkaḷiṭiyē vaḻaṅkiyavi eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu. paṟṟaṟuttal, aṉpu kaṉintu iḷakiya uḷḷattar ātal, kaṭavuḷiṭattil patti ceytal pōṉṟaṉavē muttikku vaḻi eṉa ivar kūṟuvar. ellā ñāṉaṅkaḷum pattiyil aṭakkam.
“eṉpē viṟakā iṟicci yaṟuttiṭṭup
poṉpōṟ kaṉaliṟ poriya vaṟuppiṉum
aṉpō ṭuruki akaṅkuḻi vārkkaṉṟi
eṉpoṉ maṇiyiṉi eytavoṇ ṇātē”
“aṉpuñ civamum iraṇṭeṉpar aṟivilār
aṉpē civamāva tāru maṟikilār
aṉpē civamāva tāru maṟintapiṉ
aṉpē civamāy amarntirun tārē”
“āci aṟumiṉkaḷ āci aṟumiṉkaḷ
īcaṉō ṭāyiṉum āci yaṟumiṉkaḷ
āci paṭappaṭa āyvarun tuṉpam
āci viṭaviṭa āṉanta māmē” (tirumantiram)
“paṟṟuka paṟṟaṟṟāṉ paṟṟiṉi yappaṟṟip
paṟṟuka paṟṟu viṭaṟku” (kuṟaḷ. 350)
tirumantiram ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ ceyyappaṭṭateṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. cittar nūlkaḷilum tirumantirattiṟ kūṟappaṭum uṇmikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tirumuṟikaḷ
tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ camayak karuttukkaḷ tirumuṟikaḷilum āḻvār pāṭalkaḷilum tatumpikkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. tōttiraṅkaḷuṭaṉ avi miṭintu kiṭattaliṉ avi cāttiramākap payaṉpaṭā. āyiṉum cāttiraṅ kaḷukku mēṟkōḷ at tōttiraṅkaḷē.
cittānta nūlkaḷ
paṇṭutoṭṭut tamiḻmakkaḷiṭiyē ‘maṟi’ eṉappeyar peṟṟu vanta camaya tattuvakkoḷkikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum tokuttu muṟip paṭuttic ceyyappaṭṭaṉavē civacittānta nūlkaḷ patiṉāṉkum.1 “ōrum vētāntameṉ ṟucciyiṟ paḻutta-cāraṅ koṇṭa civa cittāntat tēṉamutu” (kumarakuruparar) vētāntam - upaniṭatam.
vīṭu
tamiḻmakkaḷ vīṭu eṉakkoṇṭatu meyyuṇarviṉāṟ paṟṟaṟṟut tiruvaruṭ paṇpuṟutalēyākum. “paraṉiniṉintimmūṉṟum viṭṭatu pēriṉpavīṭu” (oḷaviyār). tolkāppiyattilum vīṭupeṟa niṟṟal tuṟavu eṉak kūṟappaṭṭatu. tirukkuṟaḷil nīttār perumiyum itaṉiyē kuṟik kiṉṟatu. civacamayattiṉ muṭivu, “avaṉaruḷālē avaṉṟāḷ vaṇaṅki” avaṉi aṭital.
“kāṇuṅ kaṇṇukkuk kāṭṭumuḷam pōṟ
kāṇavuḷ ḷattik kaṇṭu kāṭṭaliṉ
ayarā vaṉpiṉ araṉkaḻal celumē” (ci. pō. -10cū.)
civa vaḻipāṭṭiṉ toṉmi
civa vaḻipāṭu aṟiya muṭiyāta paḻimiyuṭiyatu. cintuveḷip putipporuḷ ārāycci nikaḻttiya cēryōṉmārcal eṉpār civa matattiṉ paḻimi kaṟkālattukkum appāṟ celkiṉṟateṉṟum, ip poḻutu ulakiṟ kāṇappaṭum camayaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum atu muṟpaṭṭa teṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.1 paṉēci eṉpār, “mokañcotarō arappā eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭitta, kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷum, kaṭavuḷ vaṭivaṅkaḷum iliṅkaṅkaḷum nām itukāṟum eṇṇiyati viṭat turkki civa vaḻipāṭukaḷ mikap paḻimiyuṭiyaṉaveṉṟu aṟi vikkiṉṟaṉa” eṉak kuṟippiṭṭaṉar.2
paḻiya camayanūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭamikkuk kāraṇam
muṉṉōriṭattiṟ kūṟiyāṅkut tamiḻmakkaḷ vīṭṭu neṟikkuriya uṇmi ñāṉaṅkaḷik kurumāṇākkar muṟiyiṟ payiṉṟuvantaṉar. iv vuṇmikaḷit tamiḻarvāyilāka aṟinta āriyamakkaḷ cilar avaṟṟi maṟṟiya tam kuḻuviṉarkaḷukku muṇarttuvāṉ viḻintu, avikaḷi avarkaḷ paṭittu viḷaṅkakkūṭiya moḻiyil veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. vaṭakki luḷḷavarkaḷiṉ moḻiyit teṟkiluḷḷavarkaḷum aṟintiruntaṉar. teṟkiluḷḷavarkaḷ tammiṭattiluḷḷaṉavum vaṭakkiluḷḷavarkaḷ naṉku aṟiyātaṉavumākiya uṇmip poruḷkaḷi avarkaḷ kaṇṭu viyakku māṟum nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiṉar. itu, ik kālam tirukkuṟaḷ, cilappatikāram, tamiḻar nākarikam pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷit, tamiḻ aṟiyāta mēlnāṭṭu aṟiñarum vaṭanāṭṭu aṟiñarum kaṇṭu viyakkumāṟu tamiḻ aṟiñar āṅkilamoḻiyil veḷiyiṭṭu varutal pōṉṟatōr vaḻakku. āṅkila moḻiyil eḻutappaṭum nūlkaḷ ellām āṅkilamakkaḷ eḻutiyaṉavākā taṉapōla vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ ellām vaṭavar eḻutiyaṉa vākā. vaṭamoḻi vaṭakkē uḷḷavarkaḷatu eṉṟu karutappaṭiṉum teṟkē uḷḷavarkaḷē ataṉiyum naṉku kaṟṟu am moḻiyil ariya nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟi ataṉiyum vaḷampaṭuttiṉar. pōtāyaṉar, kāttiyāyaṉar, cāṇakkiyar, irāmāṉucar, caṅkarar, nīlakaṇṭar pōṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ciṟanta nūlkaḷ ceytu am moḻiyi vaḷampaṭuttiyavarkaḷ yāru milli. kapilarum avarmarapil vanta cittārtta puttarum tamiḻarkaḷē eṉpatu muṉpu viḷakkappaṭṭatu. paḻiya kaṭṭiṭakkali irukkiṉṟatu; entat tēcattiṟ kāṇappaṭum paḻiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiyum nōkki, avi iṉṉa nāṭṭuk kaṭṭiṭakkali amippuḷḷaṉa eṉa, an nūl aṟiñar eḷitil aṟintukūṟa vallunarāvar. ataṉi oppavē emmoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭiruppiṉum nūlkaḷil poruḷiyum pōkkiyumkoṇṭu avi evarukkuriyaṉa eṉa eḷitil niccayam ceyyalām. tamiḻtteyvaṅ kaḷukku aiyarmār vaṭamoḻiyic cāṟṟiyē pūci ceykiṉṟārkaḷ eṉiṉ, itu vaṭamoḻiyil nampikki vitta piṟkāla aracarkaḷiṉ āṇiyāl uṇṭāṉa vaḻakku. “vallāṉ vakuttatē vāykkāl” araciṉar taliyīṭṭi ṉāl periya māṟṟaṅkaḷ eḷitil uṇṭākum. itaṟkuc cāṉṟu tāḻn tōrum tāḻttappaṭṭōrum ālayaṅkaḷiṟ pukuttappaṭuvatu oṉṟu; inti kaṭṭāyak kalviyāka vantatu maṟṟoṉṟu.
camayac coṟkaḷ
camayat toṭarpākat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum, āṇavam, kaṉmam, māyi, pati, pacu, pācam pōṉṟa coṟkaḷum piṟavum vaṭamoḻic coṟka ḷeṉap perumpālum karutappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻmakkaḷil orucārār paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷi māttiram tūya taṉit tamiḻc coṟkaḷākakkoṇṭu ēṉiyavikaḷip piṟamoḻic coṟka ḷeṉa otukkivarukiṉṟaṉar. mēlnāṭṭu moḻikaḷil eḻutappaṭum nūl kaḷil ovvoru kalikkum uriya taṉiccoṟkaḷ (kaliccoṟkaḷ) āḷap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. im muṟi ellāmoḻiyiṉarukkum potu. yāpparuṅkala viruttiyil, āṭi nūl aṇikalanūl eṉa, ettaṉiyō tuṟikaḷit taṉittaṉi virittukkūṟum palanūlkaḷ tamiḻil nilaviṉa eṉṟu teḷivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ovvoru tuṟikaḷiyum viḷakka eḻunta taṉinūl kaḷil avvattuṟikaḷukkuriya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (kaliccoṟkaḷ) āḷap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟutal kaṟpaṉi eṉal cālātu. paṟpala kāraṇacceṟivāl paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cila nīṅkalāka ēṉiya māṇṭoḻintaṉaveṉpa tum kaṟpaṉiyaṉṟu. ippoḻutu paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉa perumpālaṉa ilakkiyac coṟkaḷē. iv vilakkiyac coṟkaḷi māttiram paḻantamiḻc coṟkaḷ eṉakkoṇṭu aviyallāta piṟavaṟṟip piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷ eṉa otukkiviṭutal niyāyamaṉṟu. iṉṟum, ōṭañcey vōr, āṭineyvōr, ciṟpavēlipurivōr, pāṉicaṭṭivaṉivōr pōṉṟu ovvoru toḻilpuripavarkaḷiṭiyē avvattoḻilkaḷuk kuriyaṉavum yām aṟiyātaṉavumākiya pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkutalik kāṇalām. yōvuṭupiṟiyil (Jouveau Dubreuil) eṉṉum pirāṉciyar eḻutiya teṉṉintiyak kaṭṭiṭakkali eṉṉum nūlil kaṭṭiṭa uṟuppukaḷi uṇarttum pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi namakkup putiyaṉavākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. oru kali enta makkaḷiṭattil, ātiyiṟ ṟōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭikiṉṟatō, am makkaḷiṉ moḻiyil ataṟkuriya kaliccoṟkaḷ pala tōṉṟivaḻaṅkum. akkali iṉṉoru nāṭṭukkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭu atu aṅku vaḷarcciyaṭiyuṅkāl ak kalic coṟkaḷ ayal moḻikaḷil karuttiṟkēṟpa moḻipeyarttu allatu, am moḻikaḷukkēṟṟa uccarippu muṟiyil tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭutal iyalpu. muṟkālattu intiyāviṉiṉṟum ekiptu, kirīcu, pāpilōṉ, pālastiṉ mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭa kuraṅku, mayil, arici, iñci, karuvā, tantam, tippili, āṭi mutaliyavikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ an nāṭṭu moḻikaḷil tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭamiyē1 itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu ākum.
āriyamakkaḷ intiyāvukku veḷiyēyuḷḷa nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum vantōr. ivarkaḷatu moḻiyum paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum kirēkka, urōma, tiyutōṉiya, cilāviya makkaḷiṉ moḻikkum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷukkum iṉamuṭiyaṉa. makkaḷ tammic cūḻntuḷḷa piṟamakkaḷiṉ cērkkik kēṟpap paḻakkavaḻakkam, moḻi, kuṇam ākiyavaṟṟāl māṟupaṭum iyal piṉar; “nilattiyalpāl nīrtirin taṟṟākum māntarkku-iṉattiyalpa tāku maṟivu” (kuṟaḷ. 452). intu airōppiya makkaḷukkup potuvākiya coṟkaḷum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum palavuṇṭu. putitāka vanta āriyamakkaḷ putitākak ki yāṇṭa paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum putiya coṟkaḷiyum aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭumāṉāl intu airōppiya makkaḷukkumāttiram potuvāyuḷḷa avikaḷi intu āriya makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟiliruntu kaḻittup pārttalvēṇṭum. intu airōppiya makkaḷukkup potu allātaṉavellām intiya āriya makkaḷukku, intiya pūrvamakkaḷiṭa miruntu kiṭittaṉavē. āriya makkaḷukkuḷḷavaṟṟi āriyarukku illātavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu pirittaṟiya vēṇṭumāyiṉ im muṟiyik kiyāḷa vēṇṭumeṉa ārāycci nuṇṇuṇarvu paṭittār nuvalkiṉṟaṉar.2
āriyar muṉ vāḻnta nāṭu mikka kuḷir uṭiyatu. avarkaḷ putitāka vantaṭinta mattiya veppataṭpa niliyuṭiya nāṭṭil tām muṉ aṟintirunta kuḷirtēcat tāvaraṅkaḷiyum paṟavikaḷiyum vilaṅku kaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum kāṇamuṭiyātu. avarkaḷ intiyanāṭṭi aṭintapiṉ tām putitāka aṟinta poruḷkaḷukku, āti makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷiyē iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉpatil oru putumiyum illi. ivvāṟu kol (Kol) moḻiyiṉiṉṟum vēta moḻiyiṟ ceṉṟu ēṟiyuḷḷa pala coṟkaḷi piṟisluski eṉpār, ‘āriyarukkum tamiḻarukkum muṟpaṭṭa intiyā’ eṉṉum nūlil naṉku ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. koṇṭar, muṇṭar, cāntāl, eṉappaṭum makkaḷ tamiḻariṉ piriviṉarē yeṉṟum, avarkaḷ moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ vaḻakku niṉṟupōṉa paḻantamiḻc coṟkaḷeṉṟum ārāycciyāḷar kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ. itaṉi muṇṭar eṉṉum talippiṉkīḻ viḷakkutum. cattēci eṉpār irukku vētattiṟ kāṇappaṭum aṇu, araṇi, kapi, karumārā, kālam, kāṉam, nāṉā (pala) nīlam, puṭpam, pūcāṉa pala (paḻam), pīcam, mayūram, irāttiri, uruvam mutaliya pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.1 tamiḻc coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ceṉṟapōtu, muttā (muttā) mīṉā (mīṉ) eṉpaṉapōlac ciṟitu vēṟupāṭukaḷi aṭintaṉa. ivvāṟu kalanta pala coṟkaḷ mēlum mēlum tirintu urutteriyāvaki karantaṉavāyiṉum, moḻi ārāycciyil ac col mūlaṅkaḷ tamiḻē eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iv vakic coṉmūlaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟi yāḻppāṇam, vaṇa. cuvāmi ñāṉappirakācar veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻ oppiyal akarātiyiṟ kāṇalākum.
airōppiya āriyar intu āriyarkaḷiṉ camayaṅkaḷ orē vaki yiṉavalla. ippōtiya allatu vētakāla āriyamatattiliruntu intu airōppiyarukkup potuvāṉa camayak koḷkikaḷi kaḻittup pārppiṉ eñci niṟpaṉa tamiḻarkaḷuṭiyaṉavē. im muṟiyikkoṇṭu vēta kālattilēyē tamiḻar camayakkoḷkikaḷ āriya matattit taṉ vaṇṇa mākat tirikkattalippaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa veṉpatu teḷitalkūṭum. mokañ cotarō kālattil tamiḻarukku ālayaṅkaḷum ālayak kiriyikaḷum iruntaṉa. ālayaṅkaṭṭum vitikaḷiyum ālaya vaḻipāṭṭiyum paṟṟik (kiriyikaḷ) kūṟum nūlkaḷ ākamam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. vētaṅkaḷuḷ ākamak koḷkikaḷ (kiriyimuṟikaḷ) kalantuḷḷaṉaveṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ākamak koḷkikaḷ vētaṅkaḷil mukiḻttu āraṇi yakaṅkaḷ kālattil arumpi moṭṭākip pirāmaṇa kālattil muṟukkaviḻa lāyiṉa.1 pirāmaṇak kiriyikaḷ ākamaṅkaḷip piṉpaṟṟiṉaviyē. āraṇiyakaṅkaḷ kālattu ākamaṅkaḷippaṟṟiya pukaḻ iruntatu.2 tamiḻar uḷḷattiṟṟōṉṟi malarnta ākamak koḷkikaḷ, upaniṭata ñāṉaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu toṭarpuṭiya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉpatu naṉku uyttaṟitum. ‘paṇṭi intiyāvil makkaḷ vāḻkki’ eṉṉum nūl eḻutiya pi. ti. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār avarkaḷ, “tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toḻil, kalit toṭarpāka vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ ellām, tamiḻ camakkirutatti ṉiṉṟu iraval peṟṟaṉavalla iv vārāycci naṉṟāka ārāyappaṭṭu muṭivu peṟavilli; ārāycciyil itu uṇmiyāka muṭital kūṭumāṉatu. kalit toṭarpāka vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ tirāviṭa moḻiyi ṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉpeṟṟavi eṉṟu tirumpalām”3 eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
tirumūlar aruḷiya tirumantirattil camayattoṭarpāṉa coṟkaḷ nirampak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tām kūṟum ñāṉaṅkaḷ it tamiḻ ulakil muṉṉamē uḷḷaṉa eṉpatit “tamiḻmaṇ ṭalamintum tāviya ñāṉam” “centami ḻāti teḷintu vaḻipaṭu - nanti yitaṉi navamurittāṉē” eṉap palaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tirumūlar nūlil vētameṉa varuvatu āriyamakkaḷiṉ pāṭalkaḷeṉat teriyavilli. vētam caṉakāti muṉivarukku iṟivaṉāṟ kallāla nīḻaliṉkīḻiruntu aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭa teṉpatu, paṇṭu toṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ aitīkam. iṟivaṉ muṉamā yiruntē vētatti aṟivuṟuttār eṉpataṉāl tamiḻar kūṟum maṟi, pāṭal kaḷum pāṭaṟ ṟokuppukaḷumaṉṟu; tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷukku muṉatēcikar aruḷiyatupōṉṟa upatēcam. tamiḻariṉ paṇṭic camaya ñāṉaṅkaḷik kūṟum tirumantirattil vantuḷḷa camayaccoṟkaḷ tamiḻ eṉak koḷḷalā meṉat terikiṉṟatu. civacittāntameṉpatu tamiḻariṉ conta uṭimi. cittāntapporuḷkaḷi uṇartta vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ ārāycciyiṉṟip piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷeṉa vilakkaṟpālaṉavalla. tamiḻccoṟkaḷ vaṭa moḻiyiṟṟirintu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu, avi maṟupaṭiyum tamiḻil vēṟu uruvat tuṭaṉ pukuntiruttal kūṭum. itaṟku utāraṇam ceṉṉiyiluḷḷa ampaṭṭaṉ pālamē. ātiyil hāmilṭaṉ pālameṉappaṭṭa ip pālam uccarippōratu cōrviṉāl ampaṭṭaṉ pālamāka nilavukiṉṟatu. āṅkila moḻiyil atu “pārpars piriṭj” eṉa vaḻaṅkum. “hāmilṭaṉ” “pārpar” (ampaṭṭaṉ) āṉatupōlap pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pukuntu tamatu uruvatti māṟṟikkoṇṭu mīṇṭum tamiḻukku vantiruk kiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu yām karutukiṉṟōm.
“āriyamakkaḷ intiyanāṭṭi aṭivataṉmuṉ tamiḻarukku, nākarikamilli; camayamilli; kalikaḷilli. āriyar vanta piṉputāṉ tamiḻar āriyarip pārttu mellamella nākarikamaṭintu miruka niliyi ṉiṉṟu mēlnōkki vantārkaḷ” eṉṉum pollāta koḷki paḻiya varalāṟṟāciriyariṭattiṟ kuṭikoṇṭiruntamiyāl avarkaḷ tamiḻar kaḷiṭattuk kāṇappaṭum uyarvukaḷellām āriyamakkaḷiṉ cērkki yāl nērntaṉaveṉṟu kuruṭṭuttaṉamākak kuṟippiṭṭaṉar. cēryōṉ mārcal eṉpavarē (mokañcotarō aḻipāṭṭu ārāyccikku) oru kaṇattukkumuṉ “tamiḻarkaḷ oru kālattil nākarikamaṭintiruntārka ḷeṉṟu ivvulakam aṟiyavilli” eṉa viyappuṟṟuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.1
vaṭa intiya makkaḷ taṉi āriyarē eṉṟu karutiviṭutal kūṭātu. avarkaḷiṭiyē neruṅkiya tirāviṭak kalappu uṇṭu. itaṉāl avar kaḷiṉ moḻiyum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum māṟupaṭṭaṉa.2
mokañcotarōt tamiḻariṉ camayam
heras pātiriyār mokañcotarōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa āyirat teṇṇūṟu paṭṭiyaṅkaḷi naṉku ārāyntum, avikaḷil eḻutap paṭṭavaṟṟi olimuṟiyil vācittum aṟinta uṇmikaḷ cilavaṟṟi mātaveḷiyīṭukaḷ vāyilākap pulappaṭuttiyuḷḷār. avaṟṟiṉ curukkamō virivō itukāṟum tamiḻil veḷivantila. pampāyp palkalikkaḻaka veḷiyīṭṭil (yūlāy-1936) avar veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa kaṭṭuri oṉṟic curukki īṇṭut tarukiṉṟōm.
oru paṭṭiyattil “iṟuvaṉ kommiṉṉir” eṉap poṟikkappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu. (kaṭavuḷiṉ) kompu vāttiyamūtum mīṉavar eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. iṉṉoru paṭṭiyattil “kōyil ellāk kaṭavuḷ āṇ” eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ālayattiluḷḷa ellāk kaṭavuḷukkum talivar eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ.
iṉṉoru paṭṭiyattil nālūriyirukkum kaṭavuḷ “viṭukaṇ” eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. viṭukaṇ eṉpatu tiṟanta kaṇ. kaṭavuḷ imiyā tavar (“imiyā nāṭṭam” uṭiyavar) ik kālat tamiḻar aitīkattōṭu itu ottuḷḷatu. kaṭavuḷ makkaḷukku ellā utaviyum aḷippavar eṉavum ak kālattavar nampiṉar. itu, “itu utavu miṉ aṭu āṇ” eṉap poṟik kappaṭṭa paṭṭiyattāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. (kaṭavuḷ) utavi ceykiṉṟa mīṉavariṉ kaṭavuḷ eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ.
oru muttiriyil mūṉṟu mukamum yōkattil vīṟṟirukkum kōlamumuṭiya oru vaṭivam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv vaṭivic cuṟṟip pala vilaṅkukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. itu, kaṭavuḷ pacupati eṉṉum karutti viḷakka amikkappaṭṭa vaṭivam eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. pala kulak kuṟi kaḷi (Totem) yuṭiya nāṭṭu makkaḷ ellārum vaḻipaṭum kaṭavuḷ eṉpati viḷakka itu kaṟ ciṟpiyāl veṭṭappaṭṭatākalāṉ.
ōriṭattil kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarāka eṇmi eṉṉum col kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. iṉṉoru muttiri kaṭavuḷ eṭṭāka irukkiṟār eṉak kūṟu kiṉṟatu. kaṭavuḷ mīṉ vaṭivil nāṭṭil pala pakutikaḷil vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. taliyilē cūlam uṭiya kaṭavuḷ vaṭivaṅkaḷ muttirikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. kaṭavuḷ mūṉṟu kaṇṇuṭiyavareṉṟu muttirikaḷ teḷivākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru kaṇṇum taṉittaṉi vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. paṭṭi yaṅkaḷil mūṉṟu kaṇ eṉpatu “mūṉkaṇ” eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. mūṉṟu kaṇṇuṭiya periya mīṉ eṉa oru paṭṭiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. tāykkaṭavuḷ mīṉ kaṇṇi eṉappaṭukiṉṟār. mīṉ kaṇṇiyē mīṉāṭci eṉa ik kālattu maruvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu pōlum.
mēṟku āciyā nāṭukaḷilum kaṭavuḷariṉ taliyil iraṇṭu kom pukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aracarum kompukaḷi aṇintaṉar. kompukaḷ mēlāṉa atikārattik kuṟippaṉa veṉṟum avi ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir kaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷeṉṟum karutappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
“The husband God like Baal of Tarsus was identified by the Greeks with Zeus and Lucian tells us that the resemblance of his image to the images of Zeus was in all respects unmistakable. But his image unlike those of Zeus was seated upon bulls. A similar God of thunder and lightning was worshipped from early times by the Babylonians and Assyrians. His image is represented.........wearing a cap with two horns - Golden Bough.
viṭukaṇ, eṇmi, āṇ, kaṭavuḷ, pērāṇ, tāṇṭavaṉ eṉpaṉa kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarkaḷāka mokañcotarō makkaḷāl āḷappaṭṭaṉa. nālu maraṅkaḷiṉ iṭiyiluḷḷa tāṇṭavaṉ eṉa oru paṭṭiyaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
mokañcotarō makkaḷ iliṅkatti vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ eṉpatu aiyamiṉṟi viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. mokañcotarō arappā eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷil iv vakik kaṟkaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. mīṉavar iv vaḻipāṭṭi ārampattil etirttārkaḷeṉat terikiṉṟatu. mīṉavar piṟkālattil maccayar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. iliṅkaṅkaḷākiya kaṭavuḷarukku vīṭukaḷ mutaliya māṉiyaṅkaḷ viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
cumēriya makkaḷiṉ mummūrttikaḷ āṇ, ammā, eṉlil eṉpaṉa. tamiḻariṉ mummūrttikaḷ āṇ, ammaṉ, murukaṉ. ammaṉ mīṉkaṇṇi eṉap paṭṭiyaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷār. pākam peṇṇāyuḷḷa vaṭivam (arttanārīcura vaṭivam) kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv vaṭivam cumēriyāvilu muṇṭu. kaḻaṉikaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ, iṭikkaṭavuḷ, cākkaṭavuḷ (yamaṉ), mīṉ kaṭavuḷ eṉpaṉa akkāla makkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. cūlaṅkaḷ ālayaṅ kaḷilum vīṭukaḷilum vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. oru paṭṭiyattil pāmpu mūṉṟu kaṇṇuṭiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. cūlattukkup patil kōṭariyum naṭṭu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu.
cila paṭṭiyaṅkaḷil makkaḷ kaṭavuḷiṉ mūṉṟu kaṇkaḷiyum tiyā ṉittārkaḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. puṉitamuṭiya kaṭavuḷ tiru uruvaṅ kaḷmītu taṇṇīr allatu pāl teḷikkappaṭṭatu. (av vaḻakku iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu). tuti pāṭutal, kaṭavuḷiṉ peyari uccarittal, (cepittal) nērttikkaṭaṉ ceytalpōṉṟa vaṇakkaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. aracaṉ uḻavaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. aracaṉē ālayaṅkaḷil aiyarākaviruntāṉ.
viruntē ak kāla viḻāvākat terikiṟatu. (poṅkal, kurupūci, caṅkāpiṭēkam, viḻākkaḷ eṉpavikaḷil iṉṟum makkaḷukku uṇavu aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.) ovvoru kirāmattilum vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa cūlam allatu cūlattit taliyiluṭiya kaṭavuḷ aṭutta kirāmattukkuk koṇṭuppōkappaṭṭatu. akkālam periya viḻākkaḷ koṇṭāṭap paṭṭaṉa. nakarattil vītikaḷ cantikkumiṭattil ālayaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. teṉṉantōppukaḷiṉ mattiyil iliṅkaṅkaḷ vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭukaḷilum ciṟiya kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉa.
iṟantavarkaḷic cuṭutalum putittalum akkāla vaḻakku. iṟantapiṉ niyāyat tīrppu naṭappatāka makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. ñāyattīrppu eṉpataṟkut “tīrppu” eṉṉum col muttirikaḷil āḷappaṭṭiruttal nōkkattakkatu.

iyal - 8
intiyāvum mēṟku ulakamum
nōvā pēḻi kaṭṭutaṟku muṉpu ekiptiyar tamiḻ nāṭṭukku marakkalaṅkaḷiṟ payaṇañceyta varalāṟu avarkaḷiṉ ōviya eḻuttu nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. paṇṭu nāṭṭukkup payaṇañceyta kaṉṉu eṉṉum mālumi kūṟiyiruppataṉ viparam varumāṟu: “nāṉ cēpā eṉṉum tuṟimukatti aṭintēṉ. yāṉ koṇṭuvaravēṇṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷ ellām kappalil ēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. yāṉ erutukaḷiyum pacukkaḷiyum āṭu kaḷiyum pali koṭuttēṉ. yāṉ paṇṭu eṉṉum paricutta nāṭṭiliruntu aracaṉukku vēṇṭiya ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum koṇṭuvantēṉ.”
ki. mu. 1600il paṇṭu tēcattukkuc ceṉṟa iṉṉoru payaṇattiṉ muḻu viparamum ōviya eḻuttu nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. appoḻutu isravēlar ekiptil maṟiyaṟpaṭuttappaṭṭiruntārkaḷ. ataṉ viparam varu māṟu: “patiṉoru kappalkaḷil paṇṭutēcattiṉ viyappu mikka poruḷkaḷ ēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. avi vili uyarnta maraṅkaḷ kuṅkuliyampōṉṟa maṇapporuḷkaḷ, maṇamaraṅkaḷ, amu eṉṉum nāṭṭiṟ kiṭikkum poṉṉaḻuttiya yāṉittantam, kaṇṇukkut tīṭṭum mi, nāyttalik kuraṅku, vēṭṭināy, pulittōl, an nāṭṭu makkaḷ kuḻantikaḷ eṉpaṉa.”
ekiptiya moḻiyil kuraṅkiyum yāṉit tantattiyum kuṟikka kavu, ep eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ivi muṟiyē kavi ipam eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ tiripu.1
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉ toṭṭu intiyāvukkum mēṟku āciyāvukkumiṭiyil mūṉṟu vaṇikap perumpātikaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mika ilakuvāṉatu cintunati mukattuvārattiliruntu arāpiyak kuṭākkaṭal vaḻiyāka yūpirātas mukattuvāratti aṭiyum kaṭaṟpāti. iṉṉoṉṟu pāracīkattiṉūṭākat tarivaḻiyē celvatu. ceṅkaṭal vaḻiyākac celvatu maṟṟoṉṟu.
cintunati mukattuvārattukkum yūpirātas paḷḷattākkukaḷukkum iṭiyiluḷḷa vāṇikam mikattoṉmiyuṭiyatu. iv vāṇikattik kuṟitta ceyti mittaṉi (Mitanni) nāṭṭiyāṇṭa kititi aracaṉiṉ paṭṭayaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ip paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ ki.mu. 14ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭavi. acurpāṇip pāliṉ (Assurbanipal) puttaka niliyattil (ki. mu. 668-626) kāṇappaṭṭa cintu1 eṉṉuñcol intiyap pañcu eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ic col mikap paḻami yuṭiyatu. ic col akkēṭiya moḻiyil tāvara āṭi eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. acurpāṇippāl eṉṉum aracaṉ perumpayirt toḻil purivōṉāyiruntāṉ. avaṉ intiyāviṉiṉṟum ‘kampaḷi kāykkum maraṅkaḷi’p peṟṟāṉ. mēṟku āciyāvil naṭipeṟṟa pañcu viyāpāram mikap paḻamiyuṭiyatu. kirēkkar mutal mutal intiyarip paṟṟi aṟiyavantapōtu avarkaḷ ‘marattiṟ kāykkum kampaḷiyi’ uṭuttiruntārkaḷ. irukku vētattil iravum pakalum iraṇṭu necavu ceyyum peṇkaḷāka uvamikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1 mukayarilē (Mugheir-ur of Chaldees) tiṅkaṭ kaṭavuḷiṉ ālayattilum nēpucaṇ nicariṉ (ki. mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) araṇmaṉiyilum intiyat tēkkamarat tuṇṭukaḷ kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. tēkku, karuṅkāli, cantaṉam mutaliya maraṅkaḷ parkācā (Bargaza) puṟūc eṉappaṭṭa intiyāviṉ mēṟkuk kariyiluḷḷa tuṟi mukattiliruntu tikiracukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. iv vāṇikam ki. pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil naṭipeṟṟatu.
cālamaṉ nēcariṉ (Salamanesar III ki. mu. 860) tūṇil kuraṅku, intiya yāṉi, paktīriya (paracīka) oṭṭakam mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. pavēru cātakam (ki. mu. 500) intiya vaṇikar pavērukku (pāpilōṉ) kaṭalvaḻiyākac ceṉṟa varalāṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu. aṅkup paṟavikaḷ arumi. intiya vaṇikar mutaṉmuṟi oru kākatti aṅkuk koṇṭuceṉṟaṉar. an nāṭṭu makkaḷ ataṉ aḻakip pārttu atikam viyantārkaḷ. aṭutta payaṇattil av vaṇikar naṉṟāka āṭum mayil oṉṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. mayiliṉ aḻakāṟ kavarappaṭṭa an nāṭṭumakkaḷ kākkiyiṉ aḻakik koṇṭāṭiṉārkaḷallar.
paṇṭik kālattil intiyar aṉupavam vāynta kaṭalōṭikaḷāka viruntaṉar. paḻiya intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ intiyariṉ tuṇikaramāṉa kappaṟ payaṇaṅkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. piṟkālaṅkaḷil mattiya tarikaḷil ōṭiya kappalkaḷiviṭap periya kappalkaḷi intiyar kaṭṭiṉar. ki. mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kaliṅka nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ilaṅkikkuc ceṉṟa vicaya irācaṉiṉ kappal 700 makkaḷi ēṟṟicceṉṟatu. cātakak katikaḷ munnūṟu mutal eḻunūṟu pēri ēṟṟiccellakkūṭiya marakkalaṅkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. kiḻakkuk kariyiluḷḷa periya tuṟimukaṅkaḷ campā, tāmiralipti eṉpaṉa; mēṟkiluḷḷa paṟukācā cuppirā eṉpaṉa. vinōta māṉa paṟavikaḷ, cintukkutirikaḷ, tantam, āṭikaḷ, aṇikalaṉkaḷ, poṉ, veḷḷi mutaliyaṉa it tuṟimukaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum veḷināṭu kaḷukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. ak kāla makkaḷ kaṭalvaḻiyāka ayalnāṭu kaḷukkuc celvatu cātāraṇa nikaḻcci. keveṭṭucūtta (Keveddhusutta) eṉṉum nūlil kariteriyāta kaṭalil kariyik kāṭṭum paṟavikaḷuṭaṉ vaṇikar payaṇamceyta varalāṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. paṟavikaḷ eppoḻu tum kariyirukkum ticiyi nōkkip paṟakkum iyalpuṭiyaṉa. kariyi aṟivataṟku nōvā paṟavikaḷit tiṟantuviṭṭa varalāṟu kiṟittuvamaṟi yiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. piḷiṉi, kosmas eṉpōr kariyi aṟi vataṟkuk kuruvikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkam ciṅkaḷa makkaḷiṭiyē uṇṭu eṉa eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
pāracīkak kuṭākkaṭal viyāpāram ārampattil cālaṭiyarvacam iruntatu. ivarkaḷ kaṭaṟkoḷḷikkārarākaviruntaṉar. ki. mu. 694il ik kaṭaṟkoḷḷikkārar ceṉṉācerip (Sennacherib) eṉṉum cālaṭiya aracaṉāl oḻikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. iv vāṇikam piṉpu poṉīciyar kikku māṟiṟṟu. pāracīkak kuṭāviluḷḷa pakēriṉ (Bharein) tīvukaḷil ivarkaḷ vāṇikam naṭattiyatik kuṟippiṭum paḻiya putiporuḷkaḷ kiṭit tuḷḷaṉa.
ki. mu. 606il pāpilōṉiyar acīriyari veṉṟaṉar. appoḻutu niṉēvā kiḻakkuttēcaṅkaḷiṉ periya cantiyāka viḷaṅkiṟṟu. aṅku, poṉīciyar, yūta aṭimikaḷ, ayōṉiyar, intiyar mutaliyōr tamatu viyāpārap paṇṭaṅkaḷi viṟkum poruṭṭuk kūṭiṉārkaḷ. ak kālattil pāpilōṉiyar intiyakkarikaḷiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyiruntiruntār kaḷātal kūṭum. alekcāntar intiyāvi aṭintapōtu pāpilōṉil naṭattappaṭuvatu pōṉṟa vivākaccantikaḷ1 aṅku naṭattappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu srāpō kūṟiyuḷḷār.
intiyāvukkum cemattiya makkaḷukkumiṭiyiluḷḷa vaṇikam perumpālum kaṭalvaḻiyāka naṭipeṟṟatu.
paḻiya āciya nākarikam intiyā, cīṉā, ilaṅki, mattiyatari nāṭukaḷ variyil parantu kāṇappaṭṭatu. iv viṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭitta paḻiyamaṇṭapaṅkaḷ, ulōkap poruḷkaḷ, necavupporuḷkaḷ eṉpaṉa orēvakiyiṉa. kiḻakku mattiyatari, nākarika ulakiṉ mēṟku elliyāka orupōtu iruntatu.
kaḻuti oṭṭakam potimāṭu eṉpavikaḷil paṇṭaṅkaḷi ēṟṟiccellum vaṇikar kūṭṭattiṉar, mika muṟkālantoṭṭu intiyā, cīṉā, mēṟku āciyā eṉpavikaḷit toṭuttirunta perumpātiyāṟ pira yāṇañ ceytaṉar. okcas (Oxus) āṟṟiliruntu kaspiyaṉ kaṭalvari yil paṇṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭumaraṅkaḷ vāyilākak koṇṭupōkappaṭṭaṉa.
intiyat tuṟimukaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum carakkukaḷ ēṭiṉ tuṟimukat tukkuc ceṉṟaṉa. calamaṉ aracaṉ poṉīciya aracaṉiṉ naṭpippeṟṟup poṉīciya mālumikaḷiṉ utaviyāl poṉ, veḷḷi, yāṉittantam, vālillāk kuraṅku, mayil, akil, irattiṉakkaṟkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷi intiyāviṉiṉṟum peṟṟāṉ. opir poṉṉukkup pērpōṉatu; ākavē, epirēyattil ‘opirp poṉ’ eṉṉum mutumoḻi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. opir eṉpatu intiyāviluḷḷateṉpatu uṭaṉē teḷivākiṉṟatu. yūtar nūl kaḷil intiyamoḻic coṟkaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
paṇṭik kirēkkap pulavarākiya hōmar āppirikka etiyōp piyar, kiḻakku etiyōppiyar eṉṉum iru makkaḷippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. avar kūṟiya kiḻakku etiyōppiyar eṉpatu tirāviṭarik kuṟikkum. hōmar kālattil intiyāvum āppirikkāvum oṉṟāka iṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉaveṉṟum, intumākkaṭal, mattiyatarikkaṭal iruppatupōla muṉpu iṭiyil iruntateṉṟum nampappaṭṭatu. epi rēyattilum camakkirutattilum kirēkkarik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya col yavaṉa eṉpatu.
keratōtacu ki. mu. 484il piṟantār. ivar tamatu nūlil intiyarip paṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷār. avar kūṟuvatu: “intiya makkaḷ vevvēṟu moḻi kaḷi vaḻaṅkum pala cātiyiṉarāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. teṟkēyiruppōr etiyōppiyarip pōṉṟōr. aṅku ōr uyiriyuṅ kollāta oru kūṭṭattiṉarum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tāṉiyaṅkaḷiyum tāvaraṅ kaḷiyum uṇavākak koḷvar.”
“intiyāvil oruvaki eṟumpukaḷ uṇṭu. ivi puṟṟeṭukkum pōtu veḷiyē taḷḷum maṇaloṭu poṉtūḷum veḷiyē varukiṉ ṟatu. av veṟumpukaḷ nāyiṟ ciṟiyaṉavum nariyiṟ periyaṉavumāy iruk kum. avi poṉṉik kāval kāttukkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. naṭuppakalil avi puṟṟukkuḷ iṟaṅkicceṉṟu nittiri koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. intiyar peṇ oṭṭakaṅkaḷil ceṉṟu poṉtūḷkaḷic cākkukaḷil kaṭṭik koṇṭu virintu celkiṉṟaṉar. av veṟumpukaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟip pāracīka aracaṉ piṭittu vittirukkiṉṟāṉ.
ik kati, camakkirutattiluḷḷa pappulikkā (Paippilika) eṉṉum eṟumput taṅkam eṉṉum poruḷ tarum colliṉiṉṟum muḷittatā yirukkalām. ak kālattil tatistāṉ (Dardistan) eṉṉumiṭattil taṅkam arikkappaṭṭatu. keratōtacu intiyap pañcu kampaḷiyilum nayamāṉatu eṉṟum, intiya makkaḷ ataṉāl āṭi neytārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟi yuḷḷār.
keratōtacuvukkuppiṉ intiyāvippaṟṟik kūṟiyavar keṟciyas (Ktesias) eṉṉum kirēkkar (ki. mu. 416) ivar intiyāvippaṟṟip piṟar vāyilāka aṟintavikaḷit tiraṭṭi intikkā (Indika) eṉṉum nūl ceytuḷḷār. ivar kūṟiyiruppaṉa perumpālum kaṭṭukkatipōlvaṉa. ivar karuvāppaṭṭiyippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṉṟār. ivar ataṉik ‘kāruppu’ (Karuppu) eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār. intiyāvil nañci aṟiyum puṟāvum kāyatti āṟṟum kallum iruntaṉaveṉṟum, aṅku oṟṟik kompu muḷitta kaḻutikaḷum kutirikaḷum uṇṭu eṉṟum, ak kompukaḷiṟ ceyta cimiḻkaḷiṟ pāṉañceyyiṉ viṭattiṉvalimi tali kāṭṭa muṭiyāteṉṟum avar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. (kākki nañci aṟiyum iyalpiṉateṉṟum, kāṇṭāmirukattiṉ kompil ūṟṟi uṇṇum pāṉaṅkaḷil nañcu kalantiruppiṉum tīmi nērāteṉṟum iṉṟum intiyamakkaḷ nampi varukiṉṟaṉar.)pāṇiṉi muṉivar peṇpāl pōṉṟa yavaṉi eṉṉum collik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. itaṟku uriyeḻutiya kārttiyāyaṉar atu yavaṉa ilipiyik kuṟikkumeṉa varintuḷḷār.
alakcāntar ki. mu. 329il intiyāmītu paṭi eṭuttuvantār. ki.mu. 315il pañcāppil kirēkkar āṭci oḻintatu. ataṉmēlum kirēkka intiyar kalappiṟṟōṉṟiya makkaḷ palar pañcāppiṟ kāṇappaṭṭārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ kālattil aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷōṭu kalantu maṟintuviṭṭaṉar. cantirakupta aracaṉē kirēkkap peṇṇi maṇantiruntāṉ. vaṭa intiyā vil cakar eṉappaṭum skittiyar āṭci ki. mu. 93il toṭaṅkiyatu.
1tālami piḷāppis (ki. mu. 321) kālattil intiya vēṭṭi nāy kaḷum, intiyap pacumāṭukaḷum ekiptil kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. intiyap peṇ kaḷum aṅkuk kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. intiya maṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷ oṭṭakaṅkaḷi lēṟṟikkoṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa.
peripuḷūs eṉṉum nūl (ki. mu. 60) intiyāvukkum ekiptukkum vāṇikam nērāka naṭakkavilliyeṉṟum, akkāla vaṇikar intiyā vukku nērē payaṇañ ceyyat tuṇiyāmiyāl eyuṭāmaṉ (Eudemon) eṉṉumiṭattiṟ cantittārkaḷeṉṟum, alakcāntiriyāvippōla eyu ṭāmaṉ ak kāla vāṇipa naṭu iṭamāyiruntateṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.
cīṉāviliruntu paṭṭum, intiyat tuṟimukaṅkaḷiliruntu nalla maciliṉ āṭiyum, aṇikalaṉkaḷum, marakatam, muttu, maṇaccarakku mutali yaṉavum urōmukkuc ceṉṟaṉa. maruntuccarakkum maṇapporuḷkaḷum nalla vilikku viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. miḷakukku māṉam iruntatu. piḷiṉi kālattil urōmil oru irāttal miḷakiṉ vili 15 ṭaṉāri (Denari). naṭukkālam variyil miḷaku mēl nāṭukaḷil uyarnta vāḻkkip poruḷ kaḷil oṉṟāka iruntu vantatu. 15ām nūṟṟāṇṭil oru irāttal miḷakiṉ vili iraṇṭu ‘ciliṉ’. alārik (Alaric) eṉṉum kirēkkaṉ urōm nakarmītu tāṉ iṭṭa muṟṟukiyi eṭuppataṟkuk kēṭṭa poruḷkaḷuḷ 3000 irāttal miḷakum oṉṟu eṉak kipaṉ (Gibbon) eṉpār kūṟiyuḷḷār.
piḷiṉi eṉpār urōmariṉ āṭampara vāḻkkiyāl intiyā vukku mikutiyākap paṇam celvatip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. intiyā vil nāṇayap puḻakkam kuṟivu. mutal aintu ‘urōmic cakkaravarttikaḷ’ kāla nāṇayaṅkaḷ ērāḷamāka intiyāvil eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nirō (Niro) kālattil intiyāviṉiṉṟum urōmukkuc ceṉṟa uyartara vāḻkkip poruḷkaḷiṉ aḷavu uccanili aṭintatu. itaṟkuppiṉ aṅku nērnta uḷ nāṭṭuk kuḻappaṅkaḷāl av vāṇikam kuṟintu viṭṭatu. urōmi aracaṉiṉ 612 poṉ nāṇayaṅkaḷum 1187 veḷḷi nāṇayaṅkaḷum intiyāvil nilattiṉkīḻ iruntu kiṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi pāṉi nirampiya, āṭcumiyuḷḷa eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭa nāṇayaṅ kaḷiviṭa vēṟāṉavi. ivikaḷuṭ perumpālaṉa ākastas kālattaṉa. tipēriyas piḷiṉi (Tiberious-Pliny) eṉpār, intiyā, cīṉā, arāpiyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷ ōr āṇṭil nūṟukōṭi ‘cesrarces’ taṅkattik kavarntukoḷkiṉṟaṉa eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār. it toki 11,00,000 taṅka nāṇa yaṅkaḷukkuc cari eṉak kaṇakkiṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. itiṟ pāti intiyāvi aṭintatu. ak kāla urōmar iṟantavariṉ uṭalmītu ērāḷamāṉa maṇac carakkukaḷik koṭṭi erittārkaḷ. nirō aracaṉ (ki. pi. 66) popōkā (Popoka)viṉ uṭalmītu koṭṭi eritta maṇac carakku arāpiyāviṟ kiṭikkum ōrāṇṭu viḷivukku nēr eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭatu. ivvīṇ āṭamparam intiyā arāpiyā ēṭiṉ mutaliya nāṭukaḷukku atika varuvāyik koṭuttatu.
srāpō eṉpavar ākastas kālattavar. ivar āciya kirēkkar. “meōs kōmās” (Myos Homas) eṉṉum mattiyatarit tīvukaḷukku arukāmiyil intiyāvukkup payaṇappaṭṭuc cellum 120 kappalkaḷi yāṉ pārttēṉ” eṉa avar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
kuḷōṭiyas aracaṉ kālattil ilaṅkikku arukē kutiri muṉikku aṇittil kirēkka kappal oṉṟu uṭintupōyiṟṟu. ati ṉiṉṟu tappi mīṇṭavarkaḷiṭam kēṭṭaṟintavaṟṟi ātāramākakkoṇṭu piḷiṉi ilaṅkippaṭam varintār. avar intiyanāṭṭiṉ paṭamoṉṟum varintuḷḷār. avar ki. pi. 150 variyil vāḻntār.
pāṇṭiya aracaṉ oruvaṉ ākastas aracaṉiṭam tūtaṉuppiṉāṉ; (ki. mu. 21) ilaṅki aracaṉum tūtaṉuppiyiruntāṉ. kippālus (Hippalus) eṉpār kappalkaḷ intiyāvukkup pāyeṭuttuc celvataṟkēṟṟa paruvak kāṟṟik kaṇṭupiṭittār. muciṟiyil kaṭaṟ koḷḷikkārar1 mikutiyāka iruntamiyiṉ, ‘muciṟiyil iṟaṅkuvatilum paṟakki (Barake)yil iṟaṅku vati vaṇikar virumpiṉārkaḷ’ eṉap piḷiṉi kūṟiyuḷḷār. paṟakki miḷaku varttakattiṟkup pērpōṉatu. miḷaku viḷiyum nāṭukaḷiṉ naṭuvi luḷḷatu telliccēri. parakācāviliruntu cantaṉakkaṭṭi tēkku karuṅkāli mutaliya intiya maraṅkaḷ aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu peripuḷus eṉṉum nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu. cintunati mukattuvārattiliruntu iliciyam eṉṉum oruvaki vācaṉic carakku, irattiṉakkal, nīlakkal, tōl, cīṉappaṭṭu mutaliyaṉa ēṟṟumatiyāyiṉa. piṟkālaṅkaḷil paṭṭu tari vaḻiyāka urō mukkuk koṇṭupōkappaṭṭatu. urōmar ārampattil paṭṭu eppaṭiyuṇ ṭākiṟateṉṟu aṟiyātiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ atu marattil kāyppatu eṉa nampivantaṉar. parkācā eṉpatu ik kālam puṟūc (Brooch) eṉa aṟiyap paṭum. tamiḻakam urōmi āciriyarkaḷāl tamiṟikē (Damirike) eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. teṉṉintiyat tuṟimukaṅkaḷuṭ ciṟantatu muciṟi. itu ippoḻutu karaṅkaṉūr eṉa vaḻaṅkum. muciṟiyil akastas aracaṉukku ālayam oṉṟu iruntateṉap peturuṅkēriyar aṭṭavaṇi (ki. pi. 222) kūṟukiṉṟatu. yavaṉavaṇikar āṭimātattil vantu mārkaḻi mātattiṟ puṟap paṭṭārkaḷ. kumari muṉiyil kumarit teyvattiṉ ālayam irun tamiyip peturuṅkēriyariṉ aṭṭavaṇi kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷatu.
kiṟisrōsram (Dio-Chrisrostam ki.pi. 117) eṉpār intiya vaṇikarit tām alakcāntiriyāviṟ cantittamiyik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.1
parakācāt tuṟimukattilē (puṟūc) aṭikkaṭi kirēkkappeṇkaḷ iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭārkaḷ. intiya aracariṉ araṇmaṉikaḷil yavaṉappeṇkaḷ iruntārkaḷ; cakuntali nāṭakattil ivvāṟu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. teṉṉintiyāvilē aracaṉiṉ meykāppāḷar ūmarākiya milēccarāyiruntaṉar.
paratar ceyta nāṭṭiya cāttirattil araṅkil orē muṟiyil tōṟṟakkūṭiya naṭikariṉ kūṭiya eṇ aintu eṉa variyaṟukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kirēkka nāṭakaṅkaḷiṟ pōla intiya nāṭakaṅkaḷilum payaṅkaramāṉa allatu pārppataṟku iṉpam aḷiyāta kāṭcikaḷ tavirkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intiya nāṭakaṅkaḷil yavaṉikā eṉṉum tiriccīli payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatu. yavaṉi eṉṉum kirēkkap peṇkaḷ aracar allatu araca kuṭumpattiṉariṉ parivāraṅkaḷākaviruntaṉar. tiriccīli kirēkkarip pārttuc ceyyappaṭṭataṉṟu. kirēkkar tiric cīliyip payaṉpaṭutta villi.
cātakak katikaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa kālam mutal intiya nāṭṭuk katikaḷ mēlnāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyamikkuc cāṉṟukaḷuṇṭu.
urōmaṉ varttakap pātukāppiṉ poruṭṭu iraṇṭu urōmip paṭṭāḷaṅkaḷ muciṟiyil niṟuttappaṭṭiruntaṉaveṉṟu peturuṅkēri 
yariṉ aṭṭavaṇi eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. piḷiṉi eṉpavar urōmi aṇaṅkukaḷiṉ āṭampara vāḻkkiyip paṟṟi eḻuti yiruppatu piṉvarumāṟu:
1eṅkaḷ nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ kiviralkaḷil allatu kātil iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu muttukkaḷi kaṭṭit toṅkaviṭukiṟārkaḷ. avi oṉ ṟōṭu oṉṟu muṭṭi acivatik kaṇṭu avarkaḷ makiḻkiṉṟaṉar. vaṟiya varkaḷ tāmum muttukkaḷi aṇikiṉṟaṉar! ceruppukaḷum ataṉvār kaḷum muttukkaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mutti aṇivatu māttira mallāmal avarkaḷ muttiṉ mītu mitittum naṭakkiṟārkaḷ. orupōtu yāṉ kayas (Caius) cakkaravarttiyiṉ maṉiviyip pārttēṉ. yāṉ pārtta nēram viḻākkālam allatu paricuttamāṉa kiriyikaḷip puriyum nēramaṉṟu. avaḷ aṇintirunta muttukkaḷum irattiṉak kaṟkaḷum avaḷ talimītu panti pantiyākak kiṭantu oḷiviṭṭaṉa. kūntalmītum, cūṭi yirunta pūmālikaḷilum, kātukaḷilum, kaḻuttilum, kaḻuttu mālikaḷi lum kiviralkaḷilum avaḷ aṇintirunta muttu pavaḷam eṉpavi kaḷiṉ peṟumati 40,000,000 cesrarces (Sesterces). avikaḷiṉ peṟumati at toki eṉṟu uṟutippaṭuttuvataṟkuḷḷa pattiraṅkaḷ avaḷiṭam iruntaṉa. avi piṟarāl vekumati aḷikkappaṭṭaṉavalla; āṉāl nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ paṇattikkoṇṭu vāṅkit talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka aṇiyappaṭṭu varuvaṉa. makkaḷik koḷḷiyiṭum muṟi ivvāṟiruk kiṉṟatu. laliyāpōliṉā eṉpavaḷi mūṭiyirunta nāṉku kōṭi cesrar ces peṟumatiyuḷḷa aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ veḷiccattil avaḷ pērt tiyip pārkkak kūṭiyatākaviruntatu.
āṇṭil 55,000,000 cesrarcesci (486,979 taṅka nāṇayam) intiyā kavarntu koḷḷukiṟateṉṟum intiyāviṉiṉṟum vāṅkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ urōmil nūṟu maṭaṅku atikavilikku viṟkappaṭṭaṉaveṉ ṟum avar kūṟiyuḷḷār.
yūliyar cīcar, puṟuttaciṉ (Brutus) tāyākiya cēviliyā (Servilia) eṉpavaḷukku oru mutti vekumatiyāka aḷittār. ataṉ peṟumati 47,457 taṅka nāṇayam (pavuṉ). kiḷiyapattirā eṉṉum ekiptiya irāṇi kātil aṇintirunta muttuk kaṭukkaṉkaḷiṉ vili 151,450 taṅka nāṇayam.
perippuḷus eṉṉum nūl intiya mēlnāṭṭu vāṇikattip paṟṟik kūṟuvatu piṉvarumāṟu:
intiyāvil miḷakum muttum atikam vāṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tantamum, ‘cilantivali’1 eṉa urōmarāl vāṅkappaṭṭa paṭṭum, spik nāṭ (Spikenard) eṉappaṭṭa maṇapporuḷum, kaṅki mukattuvārattil vilikkuk kiṭittaṉa. malippāttiram (Malabothram) itaṟkuk kiḻakkē yuḷḷa nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭittatu. ellāvakip paḷiṅkuk kaṟkaḷum, irattiṉak kaṟkaḷum, pavaḷaṅkaḷum āmiyōṭum tamiḻakattukku appāluḷḷa ilaṅkit tīviṉiṉṟum vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa.
muciṟit tuṟimukattil malippāttiram, miḷaku eṉpavikaḷi vāṅki aṉuppuvataṟkēṟṟa vacatikaḷ uṇṭu. vaṇikar ivikaḷiyum piṟa maṇapporuḷkaḷiyum iṅkuk koṇṭuvarukiṉṟaṉar. iṉṉum iṅku iṟakkumatiyākum poruḷkaḷ niṟakkaṟkaḷ, pavaḷam, paṭikam, pittaḷi, īyam, matu (Wine), cātiliṅkam, āṭi, pāṣāṇam (Arsenic) mutaliyaṉa. muttuk kuḷippip paṟṟik kūṟumiṭattuk kolittaṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭavar kaḷē muttuk kuḷippiṟ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉa avar kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.
ak kālattē viyāpārattiṉ poruṭṭu vanta kirēkkarum piṟanāṭṭu makkaḷum maturi, pukār pōṉṟa nakarkaḷiṟṟaṅki vāḻntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ vāḻum vītikaḷip paṟṟic cilappatikāram kūṟukiṉṟatu.
2“intiyāvukku aṉuppappaṭum paṇṭaṅkaḷ alakcāntiriyāvi liruntu iraṇṭu mil tūrattiluḷḷa yūliyōpolis (Juliopolis) eṉṉum iṭattil iṟakkappaṭṭaṉa. piṉpu avi nīlanati vaḻiyāka koptas (Coptus) eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattukkuk koṇṭupōkappaṭṭaṉa. yūliyōpolicukkum koptacukkum iṭiyē uḷḷa tūram 303 mil. ittūram 12 nāṭkaḷil kaṭakkappaṭṭatu. koptaciliruntu perṉis (Ber-nes) eṉṉum arāpiyak kuṭākkaṭaliluḷḷa paṭṭiṉattukku maṟupaṭiyum ac carakkukaḷ koṇṭupōkappaṭṭaṉa. iv viraṇṭu paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷukku miṭi yiluḷḷa tūram 258 mil. cūṭu mikutiyāl vaṇikar kūṭṭaṅkaḷ iravil māttiram payaṇañ ceytaṉa. it tūrattik kaṭakka 12 nāṭkaḷ piṭittaṉa. perṇis eṉṉum tuṟimukattiliruntu maṟupaṭiyum carakkukaḷ arāpiyak kuṭākkaṭaliluḷḷa kēṉi (Cane) eṉṉum tuṟimukattukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa. aṅku niṉṟum kappalkaḷ muciṟit tuṟimukatti nāṟpatu nāṭkaḷil aṭintaṉa. piṉpu mārkaḻi mātattiṟ kappalkaḷ tirumpiṉa. ivvāṟu kappalkaḷ pōyt tirumpa oru varuṭam piṭittatu.
ak kālattil urōmāpuri ālayaṅkaḷil atika cāmpirāṇi erikkappaṭṭatu. urōmar vili uyarnta vācaṉip poruḷkaḷāl iṟantavarkaḷi mūṭi erittārkaḷ. ciciliyā eṉpavaḷiṉ cavattiṉ mītu 210 mūṭi maṇap poruḷkaḷ koṭṭi erikkappaṭṭaṉa. nirō (maraṇam ki. pi. 68) eṉpavar pappōyi (Pappoe) eṉpavariṉ cavattiṉmītu oru nāṭṭil ōrāṇṭil viḷivākum maṇapporuḷ avvaḷavu koṭṭi erittār. iṟantavarkaḷ mītu koṭṭi erikkappaṭum maṇapporuḷkaḷiyum, tey vaṅkaḷukku erikkappaṭum av vakip poruḷkaḷiyum oppiṭumiṭattut teyvaṅkaḷukku erikkappaṭuvatu tāṉiya niṟitāṉum illi eṉap piḷiṉi eṉpavar kūṟiyuḷḷār.
ayurēliyaṉ (Aurelian) kālammutal paṭṭāṭikaḷ urōmil poṉṉukkuc cariyāka niṟuttu viṟkappaṭṭaṉa.
intiya miḷaku vāṇikam
toṉmitoṭṭu intiyāviṉiṉṟum miḷaku veḷi nāṭukaḷukku aṉuppappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 47 variyil iv vāṇikam mummaramāka naṭi peṟṟatu. ak kālattuc ceṅkaṭal vaḻiyāka intiyāvukkum alakcāntiri yāvukku miṭiyil marakkalap pōkkuvarattu iruntatu. intiya arāpiya viyāpārikaḷ paruvakkāṟṟuk kālaṅkaḷi naṉku aṟintu kappaṟpayaṇañ ceytaṉar. ceṅkaṭal mukattuvārattiluḷḷa ōcilis (Ocelis) eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattiliruntu pāyeṭutta kappalkaḷ tuṭuppukaḷāl ōṭṭappaṭṭu muciṟip paṭṭiṉatti nāṟpatu nāṭkaḷil aṭintaṉa. ivvāṟu alakcān tiriyā vaḻiyāka intiyāvukkum urōmikkumiṭiyil ki. pi. 215 vari yum iv vāṇikam mummuramāka naṭipeṟṟatu. kiḻakkiliruntu urō mukkuc ceṉṟa poruḷkaḷuḷ mukkāṟpakuti miḷaku. ak kāla vāṇipat tiṟkut tēvippaṭṭa miḷaku muḻumiyum maliyāḷak kariyiliruntu ceṉṟatu. mikap paḻaṅkālantoṭṭu miḷaku airōppiya nāṭukaḷil iṟic ciyip paḻutupaṭāmaṟ pātukākkavum, uṇavukaḷukkuc cērkkavum payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭatu. kirēkkar maruntu muṟiyil itaṉi mikutiyākap payaṉ paṭuttiṉar. kippōkkiṟēṟcu (Hippocrates) eṉpavar itaṉi intiya maruntu eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ākastas kālattil miḷaku urōmil mika matippuḷḷa carakkākak karutappaṭṭatu. ak kālattu ōr irāttal miḷakiṉ vili ēḻu rūpāy; intiyāvil ataṉ vili itaṉ pāti aḷavu tāṉum illi. paruvakālatti aṟintu kappalkaḷ mikutiyāka intiyā vukku ōṭattoṭaṅkiya kālattil miḷakiṉ vili kuṟintuviṭṭatu. atu māvāka iṭittup poṭṭaṇaṅkaḷāka viṟkappaṭṭatu. alāṟik eṉṉum kotiyaṉ urōmi muṟṟuki iṭṭapōtu, muṟṟukiyi viṭuvataṟku, avaṉ ēṉiya poruḷkaḷōṭu 3,000 irāttal miḷakiyum kēṭṭāṉ. urōmiliruntu intiyāvukku aṉuppappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ mikakkuṟivu. ākavē, urōmiliruntu ērāḷamāṉa celvam intiyāvi aṭintatu. urōmiliruntu ērāḷamāṉa taṅka, veḷḷi nāṇayaṅkaḷ intiyāvukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. intiyāviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa urōmaṉ nāṇayaṅkaḷuṭ perumpālaṉa maliyāḷakkariyilum ataṉi yaṭutta kōyamuttūrk kōṭṭam, maturi eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷilum kāṇap paṭṭaṉa. in nāṇayaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa tipēriyas kuḷōṭiyas kēyas (Tiberius, Gaius and Cludius) kālattaṉa.
“yavaṉartanta viṉimāṇ naṉkalam
poṉṉoṭu vantu kaṟiyoṭu peyarum”
eṉa akanāṉūṟṟiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
muciṟi maliyāḷakkariyilē pēr āṟṟu mukattuvārattiluḷḷatu. tamiḻ nūlkaḷ itaṉip periya tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉamākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. poṉīciyārum arāpiyarum it tuṟimukattil viyāpāram naṭattiṉar. urōmiliruntu pāvi, aṉṉa viḷakkukaḷ, nakippēḻikaḷ, maṇa muḷḷa matu eṉpaṉa iṟakkumatiyāyiṉa. aracarum celvarum im matu viṉi uṇṭaṉar. peturuṅkēriyariṉ aṭṭavaṇiyiṉpaṭi muciṟiyil ākastasiṉ kōyil oṉṟiruntatu. tamiḻ aracariṉ paṇṭacālikaḷi yavaṉa vīrar kāval purintaṉar. muciṟiyil yūtar arāpiyar pāracīkar kuṭiyēṟi yiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ taṉittaṉi vītikaḷil vāḻntaṉar. tōmas ñāṉi yār (Apostle Thomas) muciṟikku ayaliluḷḷa mallaṉkārāvil ki. pi. 50il vantu iṟaṅkiṉāreṉac cīriya kiṟittavarkaḷ nampi varukiṉṟaṉar.
ki.pi. 215kkuppiṉ iv vāṇikam viḻuntuviṭṭatu; pāracīkak kuṭāk kaṭal vaḻiyākavum tari mārkkamākavum ōraḷavu naṭipeṟṟatu. cīṉar tam marakkalaṅkaḷil cīṉāvukku miḷaku koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar.
iṭikkālattil kōyilkaḷukku miḷaku kāṇikkiyākak koṭukkappaṭṭatu. ak kālattil ōr irāttal miḷakiṉ vili nālu civiṉ. atu taccu vēli ceypavaṉ oruvaṉiṉ nālu nāṭkūli. iv vāṇikam veṉis, ceṉōvā (Genoa) viyāpārikaḷiṉ kiyil irun tatu. avarkaḷ itaṉi arāpiyariṭamiruntu vāṅkiṉar. iv viyāpārikaḷ paṇañ campātippati aṟinta mēṟku airōppiya makkaḷ kiḷarcci koṇ ṭaṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷ intiyāvukkuc cellum kiṭṭiya pātiyi aṟiya muyaṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl kolampas amerikkāvik kaṇṭupiṭit tāṉ. vāskōṭakāmā eṉṉum pōrttukkēyar kaḷḷikkōṭṭiyi 1498ām āṇṭu aṭintār. 16ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil ṭaccukkārar airōppāvil miḷaku viyāpārattik kippaṟṟiṉar. ak kālattil ōr irāttal miḷakiṉ vili mūṉṟu ‘civiṉ’; muṉ ‘āṟuciviṉ’ variyi liruntatu. itaṉāl ilaṇṭaṉ viyāpārikaḷ kiḷarcci koṇṭārkaḷ. cilar elicapet irāṇiyiṭam uttaravupeṟṟuk kiḻakku nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ viyā pārañ ceyyap puṟappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ kiḻakkē aracukaḷik kōliṉar.
