iyal - 9
moḻi
avarkaḷ tam eṇṇaṅkaḷip piṟarkkuṇartta viḻikiṉṟaṉar. appoḻutu moḻi uṇṭākiṉṟatu. ātiyil makkaḷ ūmarum ceviṭa rum pēcuvatupōlac cāṭikaḷālum cikikaḷālum mukakkuṟikaḷālum pēciṉar. itu naṭippu muṟiyil uḷḷa pēccu. im muṟiyiliruntē nāṭakat tamiḻ vaḷarcciyuṟṟatu. makkaḷic cūḻntu palavaki iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷum kuruvikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷum iruntaṉa. ivi ovvoṉṟum ovvōr oliyi eḻuppiṉa. itu ātimakkaḷiṉ kavaṉattukku vantatu. avarkaḷ ivvolikaḷi uṟṟukkēṭṭu av volik kuṟikaḷi avvap poruḷkaḷik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiṉar. ‘kā kā’ eṉak kattum paṟaviyik kākam eṉṟum, ‘kū kū’ eṉak kūvum kuruviyik kuyileṉ ṟum, ‘mā’ eṉṟu aḻum vilaṅki māṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkutal itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu. ippoḻutu naṭippōṭu olikkuṟikaḷum kalantu moḻi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ataṉ piṉpu avarkaḷ tammic cūḻntirunta ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum, kuṇaṅkaḷiyum, ceyalkaḷiyum kuṟikka ovvōr olikkuṟikaḷi amittu olimuṟiyāṉa moḻiyi ākkikkoṇṭa ṉar. iṉṟum pēccāḷar mēṭikaḷil niṉṟu pēcum pōtum pēccukkēṟṟa pala avinayaṅkaḷik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivi paḻiya pēccu vaḻakkiṉ tēyvukaḷē. āti makkaḷ coṟkaḷi ākkuvataṟkuk kikkoṇṭa 1kāraṇaṅkaḷ (moḻip poruṭkāraṇam) perumpālum iṉṟu nammāl aṟintu kūṟamuṭiyāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa.
coṟkaḷ talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka nīṇṭa kālam toṭarntu varukiṉṟaṉa. kālantōṟum putitāka aṟiyappaṭum poruḷkaḷiyum ceyalḷiyum kuṟikkap putiya coṟkaḷ tōṉṟum. ivvāṟu oruvakiyil moḻi vaḷarntukoṇṭu cellumpōtu paḻiya coṟkaḷ cila vaḻak koḻiyum; avaṟṟiṉ iṭattip putiya coṟkaḷ cila ēṟkum. itaṉāl makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum moḻiyil kālantōṟum vēṟupāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭum. muṉṉōr evvāṟu coṟkaḷi uccarittārkaḷō avvāṟu avikaḷi uccarikka aṟitalum, avikaḷi avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya marapil payaṉpaṭuttutalumē tiruntiya moḻippayiṟciyākum. paratanāṭṭiyam pōṉṟu naṭippu māttirattiluḷḷa kūttit tamiḻar nāṭakattamiḻ eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar. makkaḷiṉ aka uṇarccikaḷukkēṟpa eḻum iyaṟki oli kaḷip piṉpaṟṟi, makiḻcci, vīram, cōkam pōṉṟa uṇarccikaḷi eḻuppun toṉiyiṟ pāṭappaṭum pāṭalkaḷ icittamiḻ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ivi pōlallāta pāṭalkaḷum, ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum iyaṟṟamiḻ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
ātikālat tamiḻukkum piṟkālat tamiḻukkum ciṟitu vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. “moḻikaḷ taṟkālam tāṅki niṟkum vikuti, vēṟṟumi yurupu mutaliya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ yāvum, nāḷaṭivil vantu pukuntaṉa. itu moḻiyārāycci yāḷar kaṇṭu aṟutiyiṭṭa muṭivu. taṉiyē niṉṟu poruḷ payavāk kuṟiyīṭu kaḷākiya iv vurupukaḷ yāvum taṉiyē niṉṟu poruḷ tantu utaviya moḻikaḷiṉ citivil vanta miccameṉavum avarkaḷ tuṇintuḷḷārkaḷ. oru kālattil tiṇi pāl iṭam cuṭṭā viṉimuṟṟu āṭci naṭipeṟ ṟirukkalām eṉṟum tuṇiyalām. maliyāḷattil tiṇipāl iṭam cuṭṭātu kālam oṉṟaṉiyē cuṭṭum viṉi muṟṟu āṭci kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu.”
moḻi, eḻuttunili ilakkiyanili ilakkaṇanili eṉṉum nilikaḷi muṟiyē eytukiṟatu. ulakiyal moḻiyiṉ teḷivē centamiḻ.
pēccumoḻi, eḻuttunili aṭiyumpōtu oru cīrtiruttamum, eḻuttunili kaṭantu ilakkiyanili peṟum pōtu oru tiruttamum, ilakkiya niliyil niṉṟu ilakkaṇanili aṭiyumiṭattu iṉṉoru tiruttamum aṭikiṉṟatu.
1koṭuntamiḻeṉpatu makkaḷiṭiyē eḷiya vaḻakkiluḷḷa tamiḻ. koṭumi - vaḷivu. “iyaṟkik kaliyiṉ tiṭṭattiyum moḻivaralāṟ ṟuṇmiyiyum kikkoṇṭu avaṟṟiṉvaḻi nōkkiṉ tiṇi pāl iṭam vikutik kuṟiyīṭaṉittum viṉimuṟṟuc coṟkaḷukkup piṟkālattut tāṉ vantaṭintaṉaveṉpatu pōtarum. paṉṉeṟippaṭṭu varampu uṇarttaṟkiṉṟip pēccuniliyil māttiramirunta moḻiyi ilakkaṇa varampu koṭuttuc centamiḻākkiṉa kālattukkuc ciṟitu muṉṉar ivi vaḻakkiṟ pukuntirukkalām.
“ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟappeṟṟa kālattil an nūlkaḷ vititta varampukku uṭpaṭātu niṉṟa iyaṟki moḻi oṉṟu iruntirukka vēṇṭu meṉap pōtiya niyāyaṅkaḷāl ūkikkalām. avviyaṟki moḻiyil tiṇi pāl iṭañcuṭṭā viṉimuṟṟu vaḻakkup pirayōkam naṭipeṟ ṟirukka vēṇṭumeṉavun tuṇiyalām.
“moḻiyuṟpattik kālattil mutaṟṟōṉṟiyaṉavākiya poruṭkuṟip pum, iyakkappaṇpukkuṟippum, nilippaṇpuk kuṟippum paṟṟiya peyarc col viṉiccol uriccol eṉṉum mūṉṟiyum nīkkiya piṟacoṟka ḷellām iṭiccol eṉṉum oru peyaruḷ aṭakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.”1
arappā mokañcotarōp putiporuḷ ārāycciyiṟ kiṭitta paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟi heras pātiriyār vācittuḷḷār. ip paṭṭiyaṅkaḷi olimuṟiyil vācippataṟkuk karuviyāyiruntavi paḻiya cumēriya eḻuttukkaḷēyākum. moḻikaḷiṉ iyaṟki eṉṉum nūlil māksmūlar eṉṉum paṇṭitar cīrtiruntiya makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷ māṟṟameytum iyalpiyē iḻantuviṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉap pukaṉṟuḷḷār. itaṟkut tamiḻmoḻi ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭu.2
intiya nāṭṭuḷ āriyar, pāracīkar airōppiya kirēkkar, skītiyar, kaṉs (Huns), apkāṉiyar, tāttāriyar, maṅkōliyar, arāpiyar mutaliya pala iṉamakkaḷ pukuntu intiyamakkaḷuṭaṉ kalantuḷḷārkaḷ. ataṉāl putiya makkaṭ kūṭṭaṅkaḷum putiya moḻikaḷum eḻuntaṉa. 3iṟisli eṉṉum āciriyar intiya makkaḷ eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
mēṟku intiyāvil vāḻvōr cittiya tirāviṭar. ip pirivil marāṭṭiyap pirāmaṇar kuṉpiyar (Kunbis) kuṟukkar mutaliyōraṭaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷ cittiyar tirāviṭar kalappiṟṟōṉṟiṉōr. pañcāp, cintu, kūrccaram, irācaputtāṉa, mattiya intiyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil skitiyar orupōtu kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. āriyat tirāviṭar aikkiya mākāṇattiluḷḷa akrā, ayōtti, irācaputtāṉā, pikār mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. maṅkōliya tirāviṭar vaṅkāḷattiṉ teṉpākattilum, oricāvi lum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. nēpāḷam, acām, parmā, imayamalip pakkaṅkaḷi luḷḷa tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷ maṅkōliya makkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa. parmiyar, maṅkōliyaruṭaṉ kalanta tamiḻar. turukkiya irāṉiya tamiḻar paluccistāṉattilum mēṟku ellip puṟaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. arāpiya allatu apkāṉiya kalappuṭiya tamiḻar tōṟṟattil skitiyar allatu turukkiyarip pōṉṟōr. ivarkaḷ pala iṉa makkaḷiṉ kalappi ṉāl tōṉṟiyavarkaḷ ākalām. tirāviṭar ilaṅki mutal kaṅkiveḷi variyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
eḻuttu
makkaḷ pēcciṉāl tam eṇṇaṅkaḷi aṇmiyiluḷḷavar kaḷukku viḷakkiṉārkaḷ. cēymiyiluḷḷavarkaḷukkut tam eṇṇaṅ kaḷi viḷakkuvataṟku atu payaṉ aḷikkavilli. ākavē cēymik kaṇ uḷḷavarkaḷukkut tam karuttukkaḷi uṇarttum mukattāṉ eḻut tukkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ātiyil makkaḷ taṅkaruttukkaḷic cittiraṅkaḷ vāyi lāka viḷakkiṉar. oruvaṉ maṟṟoruvaṉi vāḷiṉāṟ koṉṟāṉ eṉṉum karutti veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭumāyiṉ, kīḻē oruvaṉ utiram nāṟpuṟamum paravumpaṭi paṭuttukkiṭappatu pōlavum, maṟṟoruvaṉ kiyil vāḷi vittukkoṇṭu ōṅki veṭṭuvatupōlavum eḻutikkāṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. iv vakic cittiraṅkaḷum, uruvak kāṭcikaḷum maṉitaruṭiya puṟat tuṟuppip paṟṟiya ceyalkaḷik kāṭṭuvataṟkāka māttiram payaṉpaṭṭu vantaṉavēyaṉṟi, maṉattiṉ nikaḻccikaḷākiya kōpam, poṟāmi, tukkam, makiḻvu, cerukku, vīram, payam mutaliya tōṟṟaṅkaḷik kāṭṭuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭavilli. ākavē makkaḷ iv vaki maṉattōṟṟaṅkaḷiyum mēṟpaṭi uruvaṅkaḷ mūlamākat terivikkaveṇṇip pāṭupaṭṭu vantami yāl, avarkaḷ, cittiravēliyilum kal, maram mutaliyavaṟṟāl uruvaṅ kaḷic cetukkuvatip paṟṟiya vēlikaḷilum tiṟamiyaṭintaṉar. eḻut tukkaḷ uṇṭāyirāta kāraṇattiṉālēyē ātiyil cittira vēlikaḷum uruvam cetukkum vēlikaḷum vaḷarcciyaṭintiruntaṉaveṉṟu kūṟalām.
cittiraṅkaḷ perumpālum kaṭpulaṉākum poruḷkaḷi yaṉṟik kaṭpulaṉākāta maṉavuṇarccikaḷiyum piṟa paṇpukaḷiyum viḷakkuva taṟkup payaṉpaṭāmaliruntaṉa. ākavē maṉa uṇarccikaḷiyum paṇpu kaḷiyum uṇarttum cila kuṟiyīṭukaḷ cittiraṅkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu eḻutappaṭ ṭaṉa. itu aṟivik kuṟikkak kaṇṇiyum, kālattik kuṟikka vaṭṭatti yum, naṉṟiyiṉmikku viriyaṉ pāmpiyum, viṭāmuyaṟcikkut tēṉīyi yum, veṟṟikkuk karuṭaṉiyum eḻutik kāṭṭutal pōṉṟatu. pēru (teṉ amerikkā) tēcamakkaḷ eḻuttukkup patil kayiṟukaḷil palavāṟu muṭiccu kaḷiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar.1
ivvāṟu eḻutuvatālum makkaḷ uccarippu muṟiyāṉa moḻiyil tam eṇṇaṅkaḷip piṟarkku uṇartta māṭṭātavarkaḷ āyiṉar. ākavē, avarkaḷ cila cittiraṅkaḷi eḻuti ac cittiraṅkaḷ kuṟikkum poruḷ kaḷukkuriya peyarkaḷiṉ mutal acikaḷic cērttu, vācikkak karutiya coṟkaḷiṉ uccarippu varum muṟiyil eḻuta ārampittārkaḷ. itu kattarikkāyiyum ataṉ pakkattē oru kōliyum eḻutik kattarikkōl eṉa vācittal pōṉṟatu. im muṟiyil eḻutuvatum mikak kaṭiṉam aḷittamiyiṉ ovvoru colliṉ uccarippukkum ovvoru kuṟiyīṭu amikkappaṭṭatu. in nilimiyiluḷḷaṉavē cīṉa eḻuttuk kaḷum, mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷumākum. piṉpu moḻiyil ettaṉi olikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu uccarittu aṟintu ovvoru oliyiyum kuṟikka ovvōr kuṟiyīṭu amikkappaṭṭatu. ak kuṟiyīṭukaḷākiya eḻut tukkaḷic cērttuc colli uccarikkum muṟi kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. ekiptiya moḻiyil cittira eḻuttukkaḷ aṭinta palavaki māṟṟaṅkaḷ aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cittira eḻuttukkaḷ maṇ takaṭukaḷil caturamāṉa nāṇaṟ kuccikaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭapōtu kīṟukaḷāka amintaṉa. avvaki eḻuttukkaḷ āppeḻuttukkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ cittira eḻuttil ārampittup paṭippaṭiyāṉa pala māṟṟaṅkaḷ aṭintuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻar payaṉpaṭuttiya av vaki eḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟēṉum namakkuk kiṭittila; cīṉa eḻuttukkaḷ pōṉṟa arappā mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷē kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. cittira eḻut tukkaḷ tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpataṟkuriya pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ yāpparuṅkala virutti mēṟkōḷ, tivākaram mutaliya nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
“kāṇap paṭṭa vuruva mellām
māṇak kāṭṭum vakimi nāṭi
vaḻuvi lōviyaṉ kiviṉi pōla
eḻutap paṭuva turuveḻut tākum.” (yāpparuṅkalavirutti. mēṟkōḷ)
“uruvē yuṇarvē yoliyē taṉmi
eṉavī reḻuttu mīriru pakutiya.” (ilakkaṇak kottu. mēṟkōḷ)
mokañcotarō eḻuttiṉiṉṟum pirāmi eḻuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. itu acōkaṉ kālattum ataṟku muṉpum vaṭapirāmi, teṉpirāmi eṉa iru vakiyāka naṭipeṟṟatu. pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ ki. mu. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉpu uḷḷateṉpataṟku ātāramuṇṭu. iv vaki eḻuttup poṉiciyā vilē mōpnakariṉ (Moab) aracaṉatu (ki.mu. 850) paṭṭiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. ik kāraṇattiṉāl poṉīciya viyāpārikaḷiṭamiruntu intiya makkaḷ eḻutteḻutum muṟiyi aṟintārkaḷ eṉṟu varalāṟṟu nūlār ellōrun tuṇiyalāyiṉar.1 āṉāl, mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷ ārāyappaṭṭapiṉ im muṭivu nērmāṟāka māṟiyuḷḷatu.2 pirāmi eḻuttuk kaḷiṉiṉṟu poṉīciya eḻuttukkaḷum, poṉīciya eḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟu kirēkka, urōmi eḻuttukkaḷum, ivikaḷiṉiṉṟum ēṉiya airōppiya eḻuttukkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟum tamiḻ vaṭṭeḻut tukkaḷum, vaṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiliruntu ik kālat tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷum vaḷarcciyaṭintaṉa. vaṭṭeḻuttu aṇmikkālam variyil mali yāḷattilē kōleḻuttu eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
mattiyatarimakkaḷ iṉattiṉar ellōrukkum moḻiyum eḻuttum oṉṟē
ulakil makkaṭkulam eppaṭi oṉṟāyiruntatō appaṭiyē moḻi yum oṉṟāyiruntateṉpatu moḻinūlār naṉku ārāyntu kāṭṭiya uṇmi. ulakil mikap paḻiya makkaḷ mattiyatari mutal kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷvariyil vāḻntōrāvar.3 in nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiya mutiyamoḻikaḷ ōr aṭimarattiṉiṉṟum kavarviṭṭa komparkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ārāycci vīrar nilināṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. itaṉāl, tamiḻ, ātimoḻikku mika aṇmiyil uḷḷatu eṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. cintunatip paḷḷattākkiṟ kiṭitta muttirikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷōṭu ulakiṉ pala pākaṅ kaḷil vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttukkaḷiyum oppunōkkiya keras pātiriyār kūṟi yiruppatu varumāṟu:
“mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷip pōṉṟaṉa ilipiya vaṉān taratti aṭutta celimā eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉip pērāciriyar ilāṅṭaṉ eṉpavarum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
“mokañcotarō eḻuttukkuṟikaḷ pōṉṟa (spāṉiya eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiyum avipōṉṟa uccarippu olikaḷiyum aipīriya moḻiyiṟ kāṇalām. (im moḻi pāsku eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.)
“etirūskiya (paḻiya ittāliya) moḻi eḻuttukkaḷ ilipiya moḻi iṉattic cārntaṉa. im moḻiyil mokañcotarō eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum uccarippu olikaḷiyum kāṇpatu kaṭiṉamaṉṟu.
“pintiya ilipiya moḻi eḻuttukkaḷum numitiya pēperiya (Numidic and Berberic) eḻuttukkaḷum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa iyalpiṉavē.
“miṉōva (Crete) eḻuttukkaḷ mikap paḻiyaṉa. ivi mokañ cotarō eḻuttukkaḷōṭu kiṭṭiya oṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa. cipiras eḻuttu miṉōva eḻuttiṉ oru piriviṉatē.
“cila moḻikaḷ eḻuttukkaḷ illāmal iruntaṉa. am moḻi kaḷukkuriyōr tirāviṭa makkaḷōṭu ūṭāṭiya piṉ mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷik kiyāṇṭaṉar. piṉpu avarkaḷ av veḻuttukkaḷit tāmē vaḷarcci ceytaṉar.”
“cumēriya moḻikkum mokañcotarō moḻikkum neruṅkiya uṟavu uṇṭu. ik koḷkiyip pērāciriyar ilāṅṭaṉ tam virivuri kaḷil naṉku valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷār.
“āppeḻuttukkaḷ cumēriya eḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟkālaṅ kaḷil vaḷarcciyaṭintaṉa.”
“paḻiya ellam moḻi eḻuttukkaḷ iṉṉum vācikkappaṭāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa.”
“paḻiya cīṉa eḻuttukkum mokañcotarō eḻuttukkum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṇṭu. itu ivvaḷavu kālamum oruvar karuttilum paṭavilli. mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷum paḻiya cīṉa eḻuttuk kaḷum oru tāy vayiṟṟiṟ piṟanta iraṇṭu piḷḷikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟalām. kōyaṅ (Hoang ki. mu. 2500) kūṟṟāl itu valiyuṟukiṉṟatu.
“cāpiya allatu teṉ arāpiya eḻuttukkaḷ, iṉṉum mokañ cotarō eḻuttukkuṟikaḷ cilavaṟṟik kāppāṟṟi vittirukkiṉṟaṉa. kiḻakkut tīvukaḷil eḻuttukkaḷ perumpālum mokañcotarō eḻut tukkaḷippōlavēyirukkiṉṟaṉa: cila oṉṟākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.”1
pērāciriyar ci.ār. kaṉṟar kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
“tuḷiyiṭṭa paḻiya intiya nāṇayaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum aṭi yāḷaṅkaḷum mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷip piṉpaṟṟiyaṉavē. mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu pirāmi eḻuttu māttiramaṉṟu; teṉ arēpiya, miṉōva, eḻuttukkaḷum piṟantaṉa. mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷ teṟku āciyā vaḻiyāka vantu poṉīciya kirēkka urōmi eḻuttukkaḷip piṟappittirukkiṉṟaṉa.”2
“cumēriyam ellam mutaliya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷōṭu oli muṟiyil oṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa. ākavē ṭākṭar cartēci eṉpavar kirēttā, iliciya, cumēriya, ellam moḻikaḷ orē toṭarpuṭiyaṉa veṉṟum, aiciyaṉ tīvukaḷ ciṉṉa āciyā, mecapetēmiyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷ orē nākarika iṇippuṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉaveṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar.” (“tirāviṭa intiyā” pakkam 41-42)
eppaṭi mattiyatari makkaḷum tirāviṭa makkaḷum orē iṉattavar kaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷō, appaṭiyē avarkaḷ moḻikaḷum orē iṉattic cārntaṉa eṉa ik kāla moḻi nūlār ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. moḻi nūlār kūṟiya muṭivukaḷi ellām oruṅku vittu oppunōkkiya velcu eṉṉum āciriyar mattiyatari nāṭukaḷil ātiyil vaḻaṅkiya āriyamallātatum kamattiyam allātatumākiya moḻi, pāskku (Basque) eṉappaṭum eṉṟum, atu tirāviṭa moḻikku neruṅkiya uṟavuḷḷateṉ ṟum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ataṉ curukkam varumāṟu:
“pāskkumoḻi, kākkēciya malippakkaṅkaḷiṟ pēcappaṭum moḻikku iṉamuṭiyatu; orukālattu atu mikavum paravali aṭin tiruntatu; muntiya kamattiya moḻik kūṭṭaṅkaḷākap parantu mattiyatari makkaḷāṟ pēcappaṭṭatu; teṟku airōppāvilum mēṟku āciyāvilum vaḻaṅkiyatu; intiyāviluḷḷa tirāviṭa moḻikku neruṅkiya toṭar puṭiyatu.
mēṟkut, teṟku airōppāvil pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ moḻikkūṭṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruntaṉa. āriyam varutalum, avi maṟin taṉa. paḻiya moḻiyic cārntavi kirēttā (Crete) ilitiya moḻikaḷ, ivi 1pāskku moḻiyic cērntavi ākalām.
maṅkōliyam, pāskku, kākkēciyam mutaliya moḻikaḷi iṇikkum moḻi cumēriyamāka virukkalām. itu meyyākaviruntāl, pāskku, kākkēciyam, tirāviṭam, maṅkōliyam mutaliya moḻikaḷ iṉṉum mikap paḻiya moḻikaḷākum. āriyam cemittiyam kamittiyam mutaliya moḻikaḷit toṭukkum moḻi oṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭāma lirukkalām. kuṭṭiyīṉum vilaṅkukaḷ eppaṭip palli muṉṉaviyiliruntu (ancestors) vaḷarcciyaṭintu pallikku iṉamāyirukkiṉṟaṉavō atu pōṉṟa iṉamē am moḻikaḷukkum im moḻikkum irukkalām.”2
mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷ
iv veḻuttukkaḷip paṟṟik keras pātiriyār veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa cila uṇmikaḷi īṇṭut tarutum. “iv veḻuttukkaḷ ōviya eḻut tukkaḷ toṭarpāṉavi. ivi kiḻakkut tīvukaḷ mutal ispeyiṉ tēcam variyil vaḻaṅkiṉa. ivi ōviya eḻuttukkaḷukkum olimuṟiyil eḻutappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷukkum iṭippaṭṭaṉa; intiya nāṭṭukku ayalil vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttukkaḷukkum cīṉa cumēriya ekiptiya eḻuttukkaḷukkum iṉamuṭiyaṉa. maṉitaṉ, mīṉ, paṟavi, maram, nāṟkāli, mēl, kīḻ eṉpa vaṟṟi aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷāṟ kuṟippiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatu eḷitu.
mokañcotarōvil vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷil pala tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāy mokañ cotarō moḻi eṉak kūṟalām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum mokañ cotarō moḻiccoṟkaḷil pala tamiḻiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē tamiḻ maṟṟiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum mūppu uṭiyatu eṉṟu karutappaṭu kiṉṟatu. maṟṟiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil cila coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tuṉpattil āḻntiruttal eṉṉum poruḷ tarum pakil (Pagil) eṉṉum col kaṉṉaṭattil uḷḷatu. mutalilum kaṭiyilum eṉpatik kuṟikkum kaṭitala, koṭitala eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tuḷu moḻiyil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḻiya tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ pirākūy moḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭalām. kaṇ kal eṉṉum coṟkaḷ kkaṇ (Khan) kkal (Khal) eṉa am moḻiyil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
“yāṉ mokañcotarōp paṭṭiyaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭavaṟṟi olimuṟiyil eḻuti vittiruntu avikaḷi yāḻppāṇam cuvāmi ñāṉappirakācarukku vācittuk kāṭṭiṉēṉ. avar avikaḷiṉ iṭiyē irupattintu ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷ iruppatik kaṇṭu piṭittār. ap pāṭṭukkaḷ palavakip pā iṉaṅkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ aintu, kuṟaḷ veṇpākkaḷ. paṭippavarkaḷ atil oṉṟik kēṭka virumpalām. ākavē avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi īṇṭut tarukiṉṟēṉ.
naṇṟuṟuṟu tūkkuaṭu kārmukilūr vēliorūr
eṭueṭṭu ṟuuyarel irāriri pērkaṭavuḷ
ōr ūr eṉṉum āṇṭi aṭikiṉṟa cattamiṭum uyarac cellum ñāyiṟṟiṉ iraṇṭu vaḻikaḷiyuṭiya paccik (payir?) kaṭavuḷ malimukilkaḷum iṭiyumuṭiya nāṭṭukku veḷiyē uḷḷatu.1
iṉṉoru paṭṭiyattiṟ kāṇpatu,
“kallar mīṉavar kāṉ kaṭer valviṭaṭu
kalkuṟava val kuraṅkar nāl” eṉpatu.
kalanta (uṟavukoṇṭa) nāṭukaḷiṉ makkaḷiṭiyē nālu valiya kuraṅkar tōṉṟum (ammakkaḷiṉ) kōṭṭi valiya kāluḷḷa mīṉavarāṟ piṭikkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ.
tal tā viṭṭil eṉpataṟku viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vīṭu eṉṟu poruḷ. tal viḷaṅkutalik kuṟikkum. mukil eṉpatu maḻiyillāta mukiliyum, karumukil eṉpatu maḻi mukiliyum kuṟikka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kalpēṭ eṉṉum āciriyar kōriyā moḻi ilakkaṇam tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇatti ottiruppati ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2
iṭappeyarum makkaṭpeyarum
muṟkālattē ūrkaḷ perumpālum kōyilic cuṟṟi eḻuntaṉa. avvāṟu eḻunta iṭaṅkaḷ kōyiliṉ peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.1 mēṟku āciyāvilē acur eṉṉum kaṭavuḷiṉ kōyil eḻunta iṭam aciriyā eṉavum, pēpel eṉṉum kōyil tōṉṟiya iṭam pāpilōṉ eṉavum, niṉēv eṉṉum āṟṟut teyvam kōyil koṇṭa iṭam niṉōvā eṉavum peyar peṟṟaṉa. ciṟiya ūrkaḷ pala cērntatu oru nāṭākum. ciṟiya ūrkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ ciṟappāka nāṭṭiṉ peyarāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. oru nāṭṭu makkaḷ iṉṉoru nāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟi ṉāl an nāṭu kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷiṉ peyari ēṟṟalum iyalpu. itu pirit taṉil aṅkiḷar kuṭiyēṟiṉamiyiṉ atu aṅkiḷlāntu (iṅkilāntu) eṉappaṭṭatu pōṉṟa vaḻakku. ātiyil āṇṭa aracaṉiṉ peyarkaḷik koṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷukkup peyar uṇṭāvatumuṇṭu.2
makkaṭ peyariliruntu moḻippeyar
makkaḷ eppeyarāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟārkaḷō ap peyariliruntē moḻippeyar tōṉṟutal iyalpu. cērmaṉi, cērmaṉiyar, cērmaṉiyam; kirīs, kirēkkar, kirēkkam; maliyāḷam, maliyāḷar, maliyāḷam; teluṅkāṉam, teluṅkar, teluṅku pōṉṟaṉa itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
tamiḻccol tōṟṟamum iv vakiyiṉatē
tamiḻ ātiyil moḻikku uṇṭāṉa peyareṉṟum, ap peyari ṉiṉṟum makkaḷukkum nāṭṭukkum peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟum, aṇmik kālattu āciriyarkaḷ piṟaḻa uṇarntamiyāl tamiḻiṉ tōṟṟattip paṟṟip palavāṟu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. makkaṭ peyariliruntē moḻip peyar uṇṭātal viti eṉpati viḷakki āciriyar civarācapiḷḷi avarkaḷ āṅkilattil eḻutiya kaṭṭuriyiṉ pakuti varumāṟu:
“ataṉip (moḻiyip) pēcum makkaḷiliruntu moḻi peyar peṟṟateṉak koḷvatē takuti. moḻippeyariliruntu makkaḷiṉpeyar tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu ēṟpuṭittaṉṟu. caṅkakālap pulavarkaḷiṉ collāṭci yiliruntu tamiḻ eṉpatu moḻip peyarilum pārkkac cātippeyaraḷavil mikap paḻamiyuṭiyateṉat terikiṉṟatu. itu ulakiṉ maṟṟap pākaṅkaḷi luḷḷa makkaḷ varalāṟṟōṭum ottirukkiṉṟatu. ākavē itukāṟum tamiḻc col tōṟṟattippaṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa aṭiyōṭu māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻiṉ tōṟṟattik kūṟavanta ellōrum atu cātiyiyaṉṟi moḻi yiyē kuṟittateṉak koṇṭaṉar. uṇmi itaṟku etirmaṟi. ic colliṉ tōṟṟam kulattoṭarpākavaṉṟi moḻittoṭarpāka iruttal muṭiyātu. anēka cātikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷippōlak kulattukkuriya ip peyarum viḷakkappaṭuttakkūṭāmal irukkiṉṟatu. ic colliṉ tōṟṟat tiṟkuriya kālam ellā varalāṟṟuk kālattiyum tuḷittuc ceṉṟu, nammi iv vulakam muḻumiyilum vāḻum makkaṭkulaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṉ piṟappiyum, avi paravuvatiyuṅ kaṇṭa ōr ulakattukkuk koṇṭu celkiṉṟatu. atu ellā varalāṟṟiyuṅ kaṭantu ceṉṟu maṉitappaḻami yiṉ āḻattiluḷḷa varalāṟṟukku appāṟpaṭṭa kālam oṉṟik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. ak kālattippaṟṟi makkaṭkula ārāycci, kaṉavu kāṇ kiṉṟatēyaṉṟi atippaṟṟiya muṭivu oṉṟum kūṟavilli. av vaki yāṉa tiriyiṉ piṉ ic collum irukkiṉṟatu. im moḻi ulakiluḷḷa ellā moḻikaḷilum paḻamiyuṭiyatu.”1
tamiḻ
tamiḻ eṉṉum col moḻikkup peyar maṭṭumallāmal iṉimi nīrmi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷiyum uṇarttum.2 ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ‘tēṉuṟi tamiḻ’ (kallāṭam), “tamiḻ taḻīiya cāyal” (cintāmaṇi) eṉat tamiḻ iṉimi eṉṉum poruḷil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ic col iṉimiyi uṇart taṟkuriya kāraṇam, ataṉ mūlam mutaliyaṉa aṟiya muṭiyātaṉa. civaṉ, ñāyiṟu, murukaṉ mutaliya kaṭavuḷarkaḷōṭum tamiḻar matattōṭum tamiḻ toṭarpuṭiyateṉpatu tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum paḻiya aitīkam. iv vitīkampaṟṟiyē, “teṉ moḻiyi ulakamelāntoḻutēttum kuṭa muṉikku valiyuṟuttār kol lēṟṟup pākar.” “centamiḻp paramāciri yaṉākiya murukak kaṭavuḷ akattiyarukkut tamiḻ aṟivuṟuttiṉār” eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vākkiyaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
“cantaṉamum puḻukun taṇpaṉi nīruṭaṉē
kontalar caṇpakamuṅ koṇṭu vaṇaṅkiṉēṉ
vantiṭum valviṉinōy māṟṟuva tuṉpatamē
centami ḻākaraṉē civaciva cūriyaṉē.” (paḻiya pāṭal)
“acalē curarpat tiraṉē kuṇatik
karuṇō tayamut tamiḻōṉē.” (tiruppukaḻ)
“cuḷḷeṉ ṟeṟikkuñ cuṭarōṉpāl tōṉṟiyuyir
uḷḷam tulakki yulāvaliṉ-teḷḷupukaḻp
paṇṭip periyārtām pintamiḻi oṇṭamiḻ
oṇṭami ḻeṉmaṉār pōntu”1
ic ceyyuṭkaḷ ñāyiṟṟiṉiṉṟum tamiḻ piṟantateṉṉum vaḻak kuṇmiyi viḷakkuvaṉa. civaṉ, murukaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ ātiyil ñāyiṟṟik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiṉa. ākavē tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar ñāyiṟṟik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya yātō paḻiya col oṉṟiṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉa ūkittal piḻiyākātu. itu orupōtu el eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum piṟantirukkalāmeṉpatip paṇṭit tamiḻar eṉṉum ematu nūliṟ kāṭṭiyuḷḷēm. teluṅku moḻiyi ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ aruḷic ceytār eṉṉum aitīkam āntirarkaḷiṭiyē uṇṭu.
“tamiḻp” peyarkkāraṇam
tamiḻ eṉṉum coṟpiṟappippaṟṟi yām ematu karutti veḷi yiṭukiṉṟēm. itu muṭivāṉataṉṟu; ēṉiyōr kūṟiyavikaḷōṭu op piṭṭu ārāyattakkatu. iv vulaka mattiyil periya pūkaṇṭam (nāvalantīvu) oṉṟu iruntateṉṟum ataṉ mattiyil mēruvum ataṉic cūḻntu iḷāvirutamum uḷḷateṉṟum, ilaṅkit tīvu mērumaliyiṉ oru koṭumuṭi eṉṟum purāṇaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. iḷāvirutam eṉpatil virutam nāṭṭik kuṟikkum. iḷā eṉpatu el eṉpataṉ tiripu. iḷāvirutattiṉ pakutiyākiya ilaṅki īḻameṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. īḻam eṉpataṉ aṭi el eṉṟē tamiḻ oppiyal akarātiyil nallūr cuvāmi ñāṉappirakācar avarkaḷ kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ellam eṉpatu ilaṅkiyiṉ paḻiya peyar. ilaṅki eṉṉum colliṉ aṭiyum el. ilaṅki makkaḷiṉ paḻiya moḻi elu eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. itaṉālum ilaṅkiyiṉ paḻiya peyar el allatu ellam eṉa naṉku tuṇitum. ilaṅkiyi āṇṭa mutal aracaṉ ciṅkamarapiṉaṉ. ākavē ellam ciṅka ellam eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. makkaḷ ciṅka ellar eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. ellam īḻamāṉa pōtu ciṅkellam ciṅkaḷamāyiṟṟu. ḻakaram ḷakaramātalaṉṟi ḷakaram ḻakaramāta lilli eṉac cilar kūṟuvar. cōḷar eṉṟirukka vēṇṭiyatu cōḻar eṉa vaḻaṅkuvati nōkkuka. ḷakaram ḻakaramāka uccarikkappaṭum coṟkaḷ pala moḻiyārāycciyāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mekastiṉas kālattil intiyāviyum ilaṅkiyiyum oru ciṟiya āṟupōṉṟa kaṭal pirittatu. ākavē orukālattil teṉṉintiyā vum ilaṅkiyum oṉṟupaṭṭu iruntaṉa eṉakkoḷḷutal piḻiyākātu. oru kālattil tāmiravarṇi āṟu ilaṅkivariyiṟ ceṉṟateṉak karutap paṭukiṉṟatu. tāpirapēṉ eṉṉum ilaṅkiyiṉ peyar tāmiravarṇi eṉpataṉ tiripu eṉa varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ cilar eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ivi pōṉṟa cāṉṟukaḷāl intiyā ilaṅki eṉṉum iru nāṭukaḷum oṉṟu cērntiruntaṉaveṉṟu tuṇitum. teṉ intiyāvum ilaṅkiyum cērnta nāvalantīviṉ pakuti; muṉpu ellam eṉṟē vaḻaṅkiyiruttal kūṭum. cumēriya makkaḷ teṉṉintiyā allatu ilaṅkiyiliruntu ceṉṟavarkaḷ eṉpatu tuṇivu.1
tamiḻnāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟa cumēriyar tamatu nāṭṭiluḷḷa iṭam oṉṟukkum mali oṉṟukkum ellam eṉṉum peyar koṭuttuḷḷārkaḷ. oru nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu iṉṉoru nāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟum makkaḷ tam nāṭṭu iṭappeyarkaḷ cilavaṟṟit tām kuṭiyēṟiya nāṭṭiṉ iṭaṅ kaḷukkum iṭṭu vaḻaṅkutal iyalpu. itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ vēṇṭā. itaṉālum kiḻakkil ōr ellam iruntateṉpatu teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭum. muṟkālattu iṭappeyarkaḷ perumpālum vaḻipaṭu kaṭavuḷar toṭarpāka uṇṭāyiṉa eṉpatu muṉ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. mēṟku āciyāvil el toṭarpuṭiya nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa peyarkaḷik kāṇalām. cumēriyāviṟ pōlavē kiḻakkilum ellam māttiramaṉṟu, ellam maliyum iruntatu. ataṟkuc civaṉoḷimali eṉpatum paḻiya peyar. ippeyar puttar kālattukku muṟpaṭṭatu. maṉuviṉ pēḻi taṅkiyatākap purāṇaṅ kūṟum vaṭamali ituvē yātal kūṭum.
cumēriyar vaḻipaṭṭa ñāyiṟṟukkaṭavuḷ tāmus2 allatu tāmci eṉappaṭṭār. ip peyaruṭaṉ ik kaṭavuḷ poṉīciyāvilum maṟṟum nāṭu kaḷilum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. tamiḻ eṉṉum col iru coṟkaḷiṉ marūu eṉpatu naṉku aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. ataṉ iṟuti, el eṉpataṉ tiripākiya il. illē piṉ iḻ āyiṟṟu.
tamiḻ, ñāyiṟṟiṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉṉum vaḻakkuṇmiyum, ellam eṉpatu ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭṭa makkaḷiṉ nāṭṭik kuṟittalum, mākaṟal kārttikēya mutaliyār tamiḻ ñāyiṟṟik kuṟikkum oru colli ṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉak kūṟutalum mika ottuḷḷaṉa. uṇmi ivikaḷukku aṇmiyil kiṭakkiṉṟatu. kārttikēya mutaliyār, tamiḻ, tāmam eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉkiṟār. itilum periya uṇmiyirukkiṉṟatu. tāmam eṉṉum col maliyiyum ñāyiṟṟiyum iṉṉum pala poruḷ kaḷiyum kuṟikkum. oru collukkup pala poruḷkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉ atu mikap paḻiyacol eṉak koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. vevvēṟu aṭiyākap piṟantu vevvēṟu poruḷkaḷi uṇarttiya coṟkaḷ pala, kālattil tirintu maruvi uccarippil oru collāka varutaluṇṭu. iv vuṇmi moḻiyārāycciyāl naṉku aṟiyappaṭum. tāmam eṉṉum col mikap paḻiyatāyiruttalālum, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟa cumēriyar ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷit tāmus eṉa vaḻaṅkiyamiyālum ilaṅkiyiluḷḷa ñāyiṟṟu mali ‘tāmam’ eṉavum, am maliyiyuṭiya nāṭu tāmam ellam eṉavum vaḻaṅkiyirukkalāmō eṉpaṉa cintikkattakkaṉa. avvā ṟāyiṉ, tāmam tām eṉa maruvit tā mellamāki, ellam īḻamāṉapōtu tāmīḻamāyp piṉ tamiḻam, tamiḻāyiṟṟeṉak koḷḷalām. civaṉ eṉpatu ñāyiṟṟiṉ paḻiya peyareṉpatu muṉ viḷakkappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟu koḷḷiṉ, tamiḻ, ñāyiṟṟiṉiṉṟum piṟantatu; atu civaṉ murukaṉ mutaliya kaṭavuḷarkaḷōṭum civacittāntattōṭum toṭarpuṭiyatu eṉṟu varum paḻiya vaḻakkuṇmikaḷukku oruvāṟu viḷakkam uṇṭā kiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu kūṟutal tamiḻi ilaṅkiyōṭu toṭarpupaṭuttutaṟ kuriya cūḻcci eṉac cilar karutalām. oru kālattil tamiḻariṉ iruppiṭam 1teṟkilēyē iruntateṉṟum, ilaṅkiyum teṉṉintiyāvum ak kālattu vevvēṟāka irukkavilliyeṉṟum iṉṉum orumuṟi niṉivuṟuttukiṉṟēm.
vaṭakkē vaḻaṅkiya moḻiyiṉ varalāṟu
āriyar intiyāvi aṭivataṉ muṉ intiyā muḻumiyum tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. āriyarkaḷuṭiya vēta pāṭalkaḷ pirākiruta moḻiyil ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. pirākirutam ilakkaṇa varampillāta moḻi. pirākirutat tiṉ tiruttamē camaṟkirutam eṉap peyarpeṟṟatu. āriya makkaḷ pirākiruta moḻiyip pēciṉārkaḷ. vēta pāṭalkaḷil pala tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ pirākirutattōṭu tirāviṭac coṟ kaḷiyum kalantu pēciṉārkaḷ. uṟpattiyil vaṭamoḻiyic cārntaṉa eṉappaṭum vaṭintiya moḻikaḷiṉ amipput tamiḻi ottiruttaliṉ nāḷaṭivil avvāriya makkaḷ tamiḻiyē pēcat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉṟum, avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻil āriyac coṟkaḷ peritum kāṇap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟum koḷḷutal amiyum. ki.mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭil viḷaṅkiya kutamaputtar tam aruḷmoḻikaḷi makata moḻiyil (pāḷiyil) ceytār.1 vaṭamoḻiyilillāta e.o. mutaliya kuṟilkaḷ pāḷiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟamiyāl pāḷi moḻi vaḻaṅkiya nāṭṭil muṉpu tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiyateṉpatum pāḷimoḻikkuriyōr tamiḻareṉpatum muṉ ōriṭattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. puttaruṭiya matakkoḷkikaḷ cāṅkiya matattit taḻuviyavi.2 cāṅkiyamatam vētamatattukku māṟupaṭṭatu. ki. mu. 5ām, 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil putta nūlkaḷ pirāmi eḻuttil eḻutap paṭṭaṉa. marappaṭṭiyi vārntu eṭutta mirutuvāṉa ataṉ uṭpakutiyum ōlikaḷum eḻutap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil poṟikkappaṭṭa puttap paṭṭiyaṅkaḷil maci eṉṉum col kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē, akkālattu eḻutuvataṟku maciyum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu.
acōkar kālattil pāḷi moḻi vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. pāṇiṉiyiṉ ilakkaṇam pirāmi eḻuttil eḻutappaṭṭatu. acōkariṉ paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ pirākirutattil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. puttarum ciṉarum camaṟkirutattip poruṭpaṭuttavilli. avarkaḷ piṟkālattilēyē camaṟkirutam payiṉ ṟaṉar. appoḻutu pirākirutam pāḷikkum camaṟkirutattukkum iṭiyi luḷḷa oruniliyi aṭintiruntatu. ammoḻi puttariṉ varalāṟṟik kūṟum lalitavistarā eṉṉum pāṭalil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu katā moḻi eṉavumpaṭṭatu. ki.pi. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭakkē uḷḷa maturi yil poṟikkappaṭṭa ciṉa paṭṭiyaṅkaḷil pirākirutam kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. itaṟkup piṉ camaṟkirutam vaḻakkil iruntatu. camaṟkirutap paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil ārampikkiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal camaṟkirutam payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantatu. āṉāl, ciṉar itaṉip payaṉpaṭuttavilli. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil putta nūlkaḷ camaṟkirutattil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ki.pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiyāvukku vanta tiyāṉciyāṅ eṉṉum cīṉap pirayāṇi putta caṉṉi yācikaḷ cātāraṇa pēccilum camaṟkirutattip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉa eḻuti yuḷḷāṉ. makamatiyar kālam variyil camaṟkirutam eḻuttu moḻiyāka viruntatu. pañcāpi, inti, kucarātti, marāṭṭi mutaliyaṉa pirākirutatti ṉiṉṟum piṟantaṉavāyiṉum amippil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi ottaṉa.
camaṟkiruta nūlkaḷ tēvanākariyil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. inti, marāṭṭip puttakaṅkaḷ iv veḻuttil acciṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. iv veḻuttil ki.pi. 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa paḻaya paṭṭiyaṅ kaḷum, ki.pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa paḻiya kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āriya varttam allatu intustāṉam eṉappaṭṭa vintattukkum imayattukkum iṭippaṭṭa nāṭṭil ki.mu. 200 variyil āriyam vaḻaṅkiyateṉak kūṟalām. camaṟkiruta nāṭakaṅkaḷil aracarum iruṭikaḷum camakkirutattilum, peṇkaḷum potumakkaḷum pirākirutat tilum pēcuvatāka eḻutappaṭṭiruttal kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē, pirākirutamē makkaḷ moḻiyāyiruntateṉat terikiṉṟatu. camaṟkirutam ilakkiya moḻiyāyiruntataṉṟi orupōtum pēcappaṭavilli. vaṭakkē kāṇappaṭum moḻikaḷuḷ mukkiyamuṭiyaṉa piṉ varuvaṉa: (1) mēṟku inti, (2) kiḻakku inti, (3) irācastāṉi, (4) kucarātti, (5) pañcāpi,
(6) kāsmīri, (7) lākiṉṭā (Lahinda), (8) cinti, (9) marāṭṭi, (10) pīkāri,
(11) oriyā (Orya), (12) vaṅkāḷi, (13) acāmi, (14) intucīṉam.1 (intiyāviṉ tolivil (Further India) vaḻaṅkuvatu.)
tamiḻc caṅkam
taliccaṅkam, iṭiccaṅkam, kaṭiccaṅkameṉa mūvakippaṭṭa caṅkam niruviṉār pāṇṭiyarkaḷ. ivaruḷ taliccaṅkamiruntār akattiyaṉārum, tiripurameritta viricaṭik kaṭavuḷum, kuṉṟameṟinta kumaravēḷum, murañciyūr muṭināka rāyarum nitiyiṉ kiḻavaṉumeṉa it toṭakkattār aiññūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruḷḷiṭṭa nālāyirattu nūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉāreṉpa. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa ettuṇiyō paripāṭalum, mutunāriyum mutukurukum kaḷariyā viriyumeṉa it toṭakkattaṉa. avar nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟiyāṇṭu caṅka miruntāreṉpa. avarkaḷic caṅka mirīiyiṉār kāyciṉavaḻuti mutalākak kaṭuṅkōṉīṟāka eṇpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭa maturi yeṉpa. avarkku nūl akattiyam.
iṉi iṭiccaṅka miruntār akattiyaṉārum tolkāppiyaṉā rum, iruntiyūrk karuṅkōḻiyum, mōciyum, veḷḷūrk kāppiyaṉum, ciṟu pāṇṭaraṅkaṉum, tiriyaṉ māṟaṉum, tuvarikkōṉum kīrantiyu meṉa it toṭakkattār aimpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruḷḷiṭṭa mūvāyirat teḻunūṟṟuvar pāṭiṉāreṉpa. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa kaliyum, kurukum, veṇṭāḷiyum, viyāḻa māliyum, viyāḻamāli yakavalumeṉa it toṭakkattaṉaveṉpa. avarkku nūl akattiyamun tolkāppiyamum, māpurāṇamum, ici nuṇukkamum, pūtapurāṇamumeṉa iviyeṉpa. avar mūvāyiratteḻunūṟṟiyāṇṭu caṅkamiruntāreṉpa. avarkaḷic caṅka mirīiyiṉār veṇṭērc ceḻiyaṉ mutalāka muṭat tirumāṟaṉ īṟāka aimpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiṉār aivar pāṇṭiyareṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu 1kapāṭapurat teṉpa. ak kālattuppōlum pāṇṭiya nāṭṭik kaṭalkoṇṭatu.
“iṉik kaṭiccaṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntār ciṟu mētāviyārum, cēntampūtaṉārum, aṟivuṭi yaraṉārum. peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻārum, iḷan tirumāṟaṉum, maturiyāciriyar nallantuvaṉārum, marutaṉiḷa nākaṉārum, kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉārumeṉa it toṭakkattār nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉāreṉpa. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa, neṭuntoki nāṉūṟum (akanāṉūṟu), kuṟuntokiyum, naṟṟiṇiyum, puṟanāṉūṟum, aiṅkuṟunūṟum, patiṟṟuppattum, nūṟṟimpatu kaliyum, eḻupatu paripāṭalum, kūttum, variyum, ciṟṟiciyum, pēriciyumeṉṟu it toṭakkattaṉa. avarkku nūl akattiyamun tolkāppiyamumeṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻ ārāyntatu āyiratteṇṇūṟ ṟimpatiṟṟiyāṇṭu eṉpa. avar kaḷic caṅka mirīiyiṉār, kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭup pōntirunta muṭattiru māṟaṉ mutalāka ukkirap peruvaḻuti yīṟāka nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiṉār mūvar pāṇṭiyareṉpa” (iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uri.)
itaṉ curukkam:- taliccaṅkam kaṭalkoṇṭa teṉ maturiyiṟ kūṭiṟṟu. ik kaḻakattukku variyaṟukkappaṭṭa uṟuppiṉar 549 pēr. ic caṅkam 89 pāṇṭiyariṉ āṭcik kālattil 4,440 āṇṭu naṭipeṟṟatu. ic caṅkattil nūlkaḷiyō taṉippāṭalkaḷiyō pāṭikkoṇṭu vantu araṅkēṟṟiya pulavar 4,449 pēr. iṭiccaṅkam, teṉ maturiyiṉ aḻivuk kuppiṉ kapāṭapurattil naṭipeṟṟatu. ic caṅka uṟuppiṉar 59 pēr. ic caṅkam 59 pāṇṭiyar āṭciyil 3,780 āṇṭu naṭipeṟṟatu. ic capiyil pāṭalkaḷi araṅkēṟṟiya pulavar 3,700 pēr. ivarkaḷuḷ aivar pāṇṭiya maṉṉar.
kapāṭapurattik kaṭal koṇṭapiṉ caṅkam maturiyiṟ kūṭiṟṟu. ic caṅkam 49 aracar kālattil 1800 āṇṭu naṭipeṟṟatu. ic caṅkattup, pulamiyil araṅkēṟiya pulavariṉ eṇ 449. ivaruḷ mūvar pāṇṭiyar. ic caṅka uṟuppiṉar 49 pēr.
tamiḻccaṅkam 197 aracar kālattil 9,990 āṇṭukaḷ naṭipeṟ ṟirukkiṉṟatu.
caṅka ārāycci
iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviya lurikārar ic caṅka varalāṟṟi ak kāla nūlkaḷil kaṇṭavaṟṟi ātāramākakkoṇṭē eḻutiyuḷḷār eṉa uyttuṇartal vēṇṭum. iv vaki varalāṟukaḷ pala, āṅkāṅku ak kāla nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa eṉpataṟku, “cakarar vēḷvik kutiri nāṭit toṭṭa kalakattuṭpaṭṭuk kumariyāṟum paṉināṭṭōṭu keṭuvataṟku muṉṉum” eṉṟu pērāciriyarum, kaṭalkoṇṭa āṟum maliyum nāṭum ākiyavaṟṟi aṭiyārkku nallārum, “vaṭivē leṟinta vāṉpaki poṟātu paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa” eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, “malitiri yūrntutaṉ maṇkaṭal veḷavaliṉ, meliviṉṟi mēṟceṉṟu mēvārnā ṭiṭampaṭap, puliyoṭu vilnīkkip pukaḻpoṟitta kiḷarkeṇṭi, valiyiṉāl vaṇakkiya vāṭāc cīrtteṉṉavaṉ” eṉa nallantuvaṉārum kūṟumāṟṟāṉ naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. tamiḻ makkaḷ tam varalāṟukaḷi eḻutivippatil karuttuc celuttiṟṟilar eṉak kūṟuvatu tavaṟu tamiḻar tam paṇṭi varalāṟukaḷiyum aracarkaḷiyum paṟṟi eḻuti vittiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu makastīṉas, pakkas mutal alakcāntariṉ paṭiyeṭuppu variyil 154 pāṇṭiyar āṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟum, avarkaḷiṉ āṭcikkālam 6,431 āṇṭu mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷamiyāl1 naṉku tuṇitalākum. mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷilum kōyiṟ kurumārē aracarkaḷiyum avar kālattu nikaḻccikaḷiyum paṟṟi eḻutivittārkaḷ. ak kālaṅkaḷiṉ iṉṉa aracaṉiṉ āṭciyiṉ ittaṉiyāvatu āṇṭu eṉak kālam kuṟik kappaṭ ṭu vantamiyiṉ aracarip paṟṟiya varalāṟukaḷ eḻutappaṭāmal iruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟutal amiyātu. mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil nūlkaḷ kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukaḷil eḻutic cūḷiyiliṭṭuk kāppāṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē, avi maṇ cel mutaliyavikaḷāl aḻikkappaṭātu piḻittaṉa; avi kaḷāl an nāṭukaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷ ōraḷavu aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ekipti yariṉ varalāṟukaḷ perumpālum camātic cuvarkaḷilum camātikaḷuḷ vik kappaṭṭa pipiras nūlkaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻar eḻutap payaṉ paṭuttiya ōli ciṟitu kālattil oṭintum, cel, maṇ mutaliyavikaḷāl uṇṇappaṭṭum, irāmapāṇattāl tuḷikkappaṭṭum aḻintupōkat takkaṉa. mēṟku āciyāvil ki. mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉṉarē periya nūl niliyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa.1 mokañcotarōt tamiḻar nākarikam mēṟku āciyamakkaḷ nākarikattiviṭa mēlāṉatu eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ulaka makkaḷukkē nākarikatti nalkiyavarkaḷ eṉṟu collappaṭum makkaḷ tamatu varalāṟṟi eḻuti viyātiruntaṉar eṉak kūṟamuṭiyātu. nūlkaḷi eḻutip pātukāttu vikkum muṟiyum oru naṭu iṭattiṉiṉṟum parantu ceṉṟa vaḻakkamē yātal vēṇṭum. varalāṟukaḷi eḻutik kāppāṟṟum paḻiya vaḻakkip piṉpaṟṟiyē ilaṅkip putta kurumārum puttar kālam mutal varalāṟukaḷi eḻutik kāppāṟṟiṉar. avikaḷi ātāra mākakkoṇṭē tīpavamicam, makāvamicam mutaliya varalāṟukaḷ virittu eḻutappaṭṭaṉa.2
purāṇaṅkaḷil uṇmi aṇuvaḷavum, poy maliyaḷavum kāṇappaṭuvati nōkkiya cila ārāycciyāḷar tamiḻppulavarkaḷ kūṟi yuḷḷa varalāṟṟuṇmikaḷilum aiyam uṟukiṉṟaṉar. purāṇaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kaṟpaṉik katikaḷukkup poṟuppup pirāmaṇarēyaṉṟit tamiḻp pulavar kaḷallar eṉpati ñāpakattil vittukkoṇṭāl, im mayakkam eḻa vaḻiyilli. tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ uḷḷavaṟṟi uḷḷavāṟē kūṟum iyal piṉareṉpatu caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷāṟ ṟeḷitum. ‘irāmar 11 āyiram āṇṭu aracu purintār, tacarataṉ aṟupatiṉāyiram āṇṭu uyir vāḻntāṉ’ eṉa iyaṟki kku māṟāṉa etiyum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluri kūṟāmi karuttiṟ patikkattakkatu. keratōtacu āciriyar ōr aracaṉukkuḷḷa carācari kālam 33 āṇṭukaḷ eṉak kaṇakkuc ceytuḷḷār. mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷum naṭipeṟṟa kālattil irunta aracariṉ eṇ 197. iv varacar ovvoru varukkum muppatu āṇṭukaḷ īntāl 5970 āṇṭukaḷākum. iv vāṇṭukaḷ cantira aḷaviṉpaṭi kaṇakkucceytu cūriya āṇṭākkiṉ 5470 variyilākum. iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluriyil mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷum 9990 āṇṭukaḷ naṭipeṟṟaṉavākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. paḻiya kālak kaṇakku kaḷil ivvāṟu cila piḻikaḷ nērtal iyalpu. keratōtacu mūṉṟu ekiptiya aracar taṉittaṉi 300 āṇṭukaḷ āṇṭaṉar eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār. iṭiyē cila aracariṉ eṇ viṭappaṭumāyiṉ avarkaḷiṉ āṭcikkālam nīṇṭateṉa k kaṇakkiṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. ilaṅki varalāṟṟil iv vakip piḻi nērntamiyāl cila aracar nīṇṭa kālam āṭcipurintaṉar eṉak karutappaṭṭup piṉpu tiruttappaṭṭatu. kiṟittava maṟiyiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭu maṟintupōkap piṉpu atu ki. mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum ki. mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkumiṭiyil eḻutappaṭṭatu. atil cila aracar 800 allatu 900 āṇṭukaḷ uyirvāḻntavarkaḷākak kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ataṉi nampa muṭiyāviṭiṉum atil kūṟappaṭuvaṉa varalāṟṟu nūlārāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷi ellām vittunōkki iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa kālakkaṇakki ārāytal vēṇṭum. kālakkaṇakku mikutiyāyiruppatāl caṅkam naṭipeṟavilliyeṉak koḷḷutal ākātu. e ṭṭuttoki pattuppāṭṭu mutaliya nūlkaḷip pāṭiya pulavarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ pala kōṇaṅkaḷil palvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil vāḻntōr. ap pāṭalkaḷ, tokukkappaṭuvataṟku ōriṭattil evvāṟu kiṭittaṉa eṉpatu ārāyappaṭutal vēṇṭum. caṅkattē araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭa pāṭalkaḷ aṅku paṭiyeṭukkappaṭṭuk kiṭantamiyālē avikaḷ ōriṭattiṟ kiṭittaṉa veṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkum. caṅka varalāṟu ki. pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum ataṟku muṉṉum paḻiya varalāṟāka viruntateṉpatu1 “kūṭalilāynta oṇ tīntamiḻiṉ tuṟivāy nuḻintaṉiyō” eṉṉum māṇikkavācakariṉ tiruvākkāṉum “naṉpāṭṭup pulavaṉāy caṅkamēṟi naṟkaṉakak kiḻitarumik karuḷiṉāṉkāṇ” eṉṉum tirunāvukkaracar tēvārattāṉum naṉku viḷaṅkum. caṅkattiṉ iṟutik kālam ki. pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil eṉap perumpāluṅ koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu.1
iṭiccaṅkam kapāṭapurattiliruntateṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu tāmira paraṇi āṟṟu mukattuvārattiluḷḷateṉa irāmāyaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. kapāṭa purattiṉ aḻivukkup piṉ pāṇṭiyariṉ talinakaram maṇalūriliruntatu. itu tiruccentūrukkup pakkattil iruntateṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. māpāratam pāṇṭiyariṉ talinakaram maṇalūriliruntatākavē kūṟukiṉṟatu. ataṉ piṉpē ippoḻutuḷḷa maturi pāṇṭiyariṉ talinakarāyiṟṟu. ippōtiya maturi muṉ kaṭampavaṉamāyiruntateṉat tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇaṅ kūṟum. “kiṉṉarampayil kaṭampavāṉattiṉ kīḻcārt, teṉṉaṉ cēkaraṉ eṉṉuṅ kulacēkaraṉāka, maṉṉar cēkara ṉaracu ceytiruppatu maṇa vūr” eṉpatu tiruviḷiyāṭal. mekastiṉacum kuṭaliyarum maturiyik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. kaṭiccaṅka kālattil irunta aracar 49 pēr eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. taṟkāla muṟiyiṉpaṭi ōr aracaṉukku 20 āṇṭu īyiṉ avarkaḷiṉ motta āṭcik kālam 980 āṇṭukaḷākum. ākavē, kaṭiccaṅkam ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil toṭaṅkiṟṟu eṉak kūṟutal tava ṟākātu. muṟikkumuṟi nērnta kaṭalkōṭ kuḻappaṅkaḷāl aracarāl tiraṭṭi vikkappaṭṭa tamiḻnūlkaḷ aḻiveytiṉaveṉṟu uyttuṇartalkūṭum. talamicōttar eṉpavarāl alakcāntiriyāvil tokuttu vikkappaṭṭa 700,000 nūlkaḷ aḻintu pōṉami itaṉōṭu oppunōkkattakkatu. kaṭic caṅkam kulintapiṉ ki.pi. 470il vaccirananti eṉpavariṉ talimiyil tikamparaciṉarāl tamiḻccaṅkam toṭaṅki naṭattappaṭṭatu.
akattiyar
akattiyar taliccaṅkattit talimi tāṅki naṭattiyavar eṉa iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluri kūṟukiṉṟatu. akattiyar eṉṉum viṇmīṉ oṉṟip paripāṭal kūṟukiṉṟatu. maṇimēkaliyil (ki. pi. 200) akattiyar amaramuṉivar eṉac cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟār. “kañca vēṭkiyiṟ kakantaṉ vēṇṭa, amara muṉiva ṉakattiyaṉ ṟaṉātu, karakam kaviḻtta kāvirip pāvi.”
“kaṭavuṭ kaṇiki kātalañ ciṟuvaṉ, antaṇa riruvarum” eṉa varum maṇimēkali aṭikaḷukku “tilōttami piḷḷikaḷākiya vatiṣṭar akattiyar” eṉap poruḷkūṟi “vaciṣṭaṉum akastiyaṉum pirahmā tilōttami yeṉṉum apsarasik kaṇṭa kālattu jaṉit taṉar.” (nīla-camayam) eṉṉum mēṟkōḷiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār ṭākṭar cāmināta aiyaravarkaḷ. purāṇaṅkaḷ vatiṣṭar, akattiyar, vicuvā mittirar mutaliyōriṉ tanti mittiravaruṇaṉ eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.1 mittiravaruṇaṉ tamiḻ marapiṉarē eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar tuṇipu.
tirumantiram ki. pi. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭu aḷaviṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. ataṉakattē akattiyarippaṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
“naṭuvunil lātiv vulakañ carintu
keṭukiṉṟa temperu māṉeṉṉa vīcaṉ
naṭuvuḷa aṅki akattiya nīpōy
muṭukiya viyattu muṉṉireṉ ṟāṉē.” (tirumantiram)
puṟapporuḷ veṇpā mālip pāyirattil,
“maṉṉiya ciṟappiṉ vāṉōr vēṇṭat
teṉmali yirunta cīrcāl muṉivaṉ.”
eṉa varum aṭikaḷum mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi karuttit taḻuvic ceyyappaṭṭa tākum.
iv varalāṟukaḷāl kiṟittuva āṇṭiṉ toṭakkattukku muṉṉar akattiyar varalāṟu kaṟpaṉik katikaḷ palavaṟṟuṭaṉ kalanta paḻaṅkati yāka māṟiyuḷḷateṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
vētapāṭalkaḷ cilavaṟṟic ceytavarum ulōpā muttiriyi maṉiviyākap peṟṟavarumākiya akattiyar oruvar vētakālattil viḷaṅkiṉār. irāmāyaṇattil akattiyar ācciramam kōtāvariyāṟṟukku vaṭakkē uḷḷateṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.2 teṉṉāṭṭil akat tīccuram akattiyāṉ paḷḷi mutaliya ālayaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa.
akattiyar tamiḻukku āti3 ilakkaṇañ ceytāreṉṟum, avariṭat til ataṅkōṭṭāciriyaṉ, tolkāppiyar, paṉampāraṉār, cempūṭcēy, viyāpikar, avinayar, kākki pāṭiṉiyar, turāliṅkar, vāyppiyar, kaḻārampar, naṟṟattar, vāmaṉar eṉṉum paṉṉiruvar kalvi payiṉṟārka ḷeṉṟum varalāṟukaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷukku,
“vīṅkukaṭal uṭutta viyaṉkaṇ ñālattut
tāṅkā nallicit tamiḻkkuviḷak kākeṉa
vāṉōr ēttum vāymoḻip palpukaḻ
āṉāp perumi akattiyaṉ eṉṉum
aruntava muṉivaṉ ākkiya mutaṉūl
poruntak kaṟṟup puritapa uṇarntōr
nallici niṟutta tolkāp piyaṉum.”
eṉṉat tolkāppiya uriyiṟ kāṇappaṭum paṉṉiru paṭalap pāyiram eṉṉum mēṟkōḷiyum,
“teṉmali yirunta cīrcāl muṉivaraṉ
taṉpāṟ ṟaṇṭamiḻ tāviṉ ṟuṇarnta
tuṉṉaruñ cīrttit tolkāppiyaṉ mutaṟ
paṉṉiru pulavarum pāṅkuṟap pakarnta.”
eṉa varum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālip pāyira aṭikaḷiyumaṉṟi vēṟu mēṟkōḷkaḷ kāṇpataritu.
“tamiḻ nāṭṭil akattiyarkaḷ” eṉṉum ārāycci nūl eḻutiya kē. eṉ. civarācapiḷḷi avarkaḷ, akattiyarip paṟṟiya katikaḷ kaṟpaṉi eṉak karutiṉārkaḷ. muṟ kālaṅkaḷil ovvoru kuṭumpattiṉa rum avvakkuṭumpaṅkaḷiṉ āti mutalvarkaḷ peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭ ṭārkaḷ. vicuvāmittirar, vatiṭṭar, akattiyar eṉṉum peyarkaḷum am muṟiyil vaḻaṅkiṉa. ivvāṟu palvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil vāḻnta pala akat tiyarkaḷiṉ katikaḷ ellām oruṅku tiraṇṭu pala kaṭṭukkatikaḷōṭu piṉṉi iṉṟiya akattiyar varalāṟāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟateṉpatu iṟṟi ñāṉṟi ārāycciyāḷaratu tuṇipākum. iññāṉṟu akattiyar peya ruṭaṉ vaḻaṅkum vittiya nūlkaḷ pōṉṟaṉavē muṟkāla akattiyaṅkaḷu māyiruttal kūṭum.
ceṅkōṉ tariccelavil ēḻteṅkanāṭṭu muttūr akattiyaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻp pulavar oruvariṉ peyar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
cumattirā yāvā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil akattiyar vikkirakaṅkaḷ makka ḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. vāyupurāṇam, akattiyar pōṇiyō, kucattīpam, varākatīpam, maliyat tīpaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟāreṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. avar cumattirāvilē uḷḷa malāyu eṉṉum maliyil iruntār eṉṉum varalāṟu aṅku vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ātiyil cantaṉa marattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa akattiyar vikkirakam malāyarāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. ki.pi. 7ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭil kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa akattiyar uruvam vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. akattiyar cili caṭāmuṭi kavacam uruttirākkam mutaliyavikaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.1 akattiyar marapiluḷḷōr palar aracarāyiruntār kaḷeṉat terikiṉṟatu.
mattiya yāvāvilē cāṇṭipāṉ (Chandi Bann) eṉṉumiṭattil uḷḷa civaṉ kōyilil aḻakiya akattiya viṣṇu vikkirakaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cittiya (Scythians) makkaḷiṭiyē akattiricikaḷ eṉṉum oru kūṭṭattiṉar iruntārkaḷ eṉak keratōtacu eṉṉum āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. pāti pāmpum pāti maṉita vaṭivumuṭiyatāyuḷḷa, kekkulis eṉṉum tanti akattiricikaḷiṉ āti mutalvar eṉa avar kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. akattiricikaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭṭa pala āṭavar oru peṇṇi maṇakkum vaḻakkampōṉṟatu, maliyāḷak karikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.2 maliyāḷam muṟkālattil nākanāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. cēra eṉpataṟku nākam eṉṟē cāyaṇar poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷār.3 oru kālattil maliyāḷak karikaḷiṉiṉṟum veḷippōnta akattiyar marapiṉarē akattiricikaḷ eṉac cittiya makkaḷiṭiyum kāṇappaṭṭārkaḷām. cittiya makkaḷum tamiḻari oppat turāṉiyamakkaḷāvar eṉa makkaṭkula nūlār kūṟuvar.
tolkāppiyar
tolkāppiyar eṉpatu tolkāppiyam eṉṉum nūlic ceyta āciriyarukku annūl ceytami kāraṇamāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa oru kāraṇappeyar. itu naṉṉūl ceytāri naṉṉūlār eṉak kūṟutal pōṉṟatoru vaḻakku. ivaratu iyaṟpeyar yāteṉat teriyavilli. āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ivar iyaṟpeyar tiraṇa tūmākkiṉi eṉa ōriṭattuk kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tolkāppiyariṉ iyaṟpeyar pulattiyaṉ (pulam-aṟivu) eṉṟum, appeyari vaṭamoḻippaṭuttiyavarkaḷ ataṉip pul+akam+tīyaṉ eṉap pirittut (tiraṇam+tūmam+akkiṉi) tiraṇa tūmākkiṉi eṉa māṟṟiṉareṉṟum aṟiñar cilar kūṟuvar. itu ik kālam vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷit tamiḻppaṭutti vaḻaṅkuvatupōṉṟatōr muṟkāla vaḻakku. cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiri eṉpatip paritimāl kaliñaṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu itaṟku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. vaṭamoḻiyiṉit teyva moḻi eṉa makkaḷ nampat talippaṭṭa oru kālattil teṉṉāṭṭut tirukkōyiṟ peyarkaḷum makkaṭ peyarkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ṟiruppap paṭṭu vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa. tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattiṟ kāṇappaṭum pāṇṭiya aracarkaḷ peyarkaḷum ivvāṟu māṟṟappaṭṭiruttal nōkkattakkatu. tolkāppiyam ceyyappaṭṭakālattil tiraṇatūmākkiṉi pōṉṟa peyar tōṉṟiyiruttal cālāteṉpatu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷukku naṉku teḷivākum.
tolkāppiyam eṉṉum nūli nuṇuki ārāyumiṭattu atu paṇṭit tamiḻ marapukaḷip pātukāttaṟku eḻunta nūleṉpatu teḷḷi tiṟ pulappaṭum. ik kāraṇam paṟṟiyē tolkāppiyam (tol + kāppu + iyam = paḻamiyik kāppatākiya nūl) eṉṉum peyar an nūlukku iṭap paṭṭatu. kāppiyam eṉpatu kāviyam eṉpataṉ tiripu eṉac cilar vaḻakkiṭuvar. tolkāppiyattil kāviyattukkuriya poruḷkaḷ amintirāmiyiṉ aṉṉōr kūṟṟup poruḷaṉṟeṉa viṭuka.
tolkāppiyam ki. mu. 350kkup piṟpaṭṭa nūlaṉṟeṉpatu varalāṟ ṟāciriyarkaḷ tuṇipu, tolkāppiyattil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷa ōri eṉṉum col kirēkkariṉ “hōrā” eṉṉum colliṉ tiripeṉṟum, varāka mikirar (ki. pi. 550) kālattukkup piṉpē intiya makkaḷ hōrā eṉṉuñ colli vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷeṉṟum ākavē, tolkāppiyam ki. pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṟpaṭṭateṉṟum cilar kūṟuvar. tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟap paṭum ilakkaṇa vitikaḷāl atu cilappatikārattukku muṟpaṭṭa nūl eṉa naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. kirēkkar ki. mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkumuṉ vāṉa nūlippaṟṟi aṟintilar. ataṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉarē mēṟku āciyamakkaḷum tamiḻarum vāṉanūl, cōtiṭanūl eṉpavikaḷil tiṟami peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉar. ākavē, kirēkka makkaḷ vāṉa ārāycci, cōtiṭam eṉpavikaḷukkuriya coṟkaḷip piṟa makkaḷiṭamiruntu peṟṟiruppār kaḷēyaṉṟi ak kalikaḷip paḻamiyē aṟintirunta piṟar kirēkkariṭa miruntu peṟṟirukkamāṭṭārkaḷ. ulaka moḻikaḷil tamiḻccol mūlaṅkaḷ ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvati moḻi ārāycciyāḷar kaṇṭu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kaṟkaḷi eṇṇik kaṇakkiṭutal eṉṉum poruḷil, ilattīṉ moḻiyil vaḻaṅkum kalkulas (calcules) āṅkila moḻiyil vaḻaṅkum kalkulēṭ (Calculate) mutaliya coṟkaḷ kal eṉṉum tamiḻc colliṉiṉṟu piṟan taṉavātali nōkkuvārkku ōri eṉṉum collē kirēkka moḻiyil ceṉṟu hōrā eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉak koḷḷutal iyipuṭiyateṉat tōṉṟum.
tolkāppiyap pāyirattil aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉak kūṟappaṭutaliṉ avar aintira meṉṉum vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇattik kaṟṟiruntār eṉac cilar karutuvar. aintiram eṉṉum ilakkaṇanūl ip poḻutu kāṇappaṭavilli. pāṇiṉikku muṉ aintirameṉṉum vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇamoṉṟu iruntatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. pāṇiṉikkuppiṉ aintirameṉṉum ilakkaṇanūl vaḻaṅkavilli. pāyirattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa aintiram vaṭamoḻi nūlāyiṉ tolkāppiyar pāṇiṉi muṉivarukku (ki.mu. 400) muntiyarāvar. oru moḻikku ilakkaṇam ceyyumiṭattu maṟṟoru moḻikkuriya ilakkaṇattiyum kaṟṟu oppunōkki oṟṟumi vēṟṟumi kāṇutal ciṟanta muṟiyākum.
iṉṉorucārār, aintirameṉpatu ciṉanūl; ākavē, tolkāp piyar ciṉar eṉak kūṟuvar. cilappatikārattil, 1“kappat tintiraṉ kāṭṭiya nūliṉ, meyppāṭ ṭiyaṟki viḷaṅkak kāṇā”, “viṇṇavar kōmāṉ viḻunūl” eṉa intiraṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa oru nūl cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. atu mikap paḻiya kālattu (kaṟpa kālattil) intiraṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa teṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. intiraṉatu talinakarākiya amarāpati kiruṣṇānati mukattuvārattiluḷḷamiyum, intiraṉ teṉṉāṭṭilē civaliṅkam vittup pūcittamiyumākiya varalāṟukaḷ ārāyappaṭal vēṇṭum.
tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭum cila akaccāṉṟukaḷālum,2 ataṉ pāyirattālum tolkāppiyar kālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkelli vēṅkaṭameṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. akanāṉūṟṟil varum māmūlaṉār pāṭalkaḷiṉ,
“paṉipaṭu cōli, vēṅkaṭat tumpar
moḻipeyar tēyattar eṉpa”
“palpukaḻ niṟinta velpōr nantar
cīrmiku pāṭaliṟ kuḻīik kaṅki
munnīr karanta nitiyaṅ kollō”
“kaṉikura licikkum viricelaṟ kaṭuṅkaṇ
muraṇmiku vaṭukar muṉṉuṟa mōriyar
teṉṟici mātira muṉṉiya varaviṟku”
eṉavarum aṭikaḷāl māmūlaṉār kālattukkumuṉ vēṅkaṭattukku vaṭakkē piṟamoḻi vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉṟum māmūlaṉār ki. mu. 300 variyil vāḻntavarāvar eṉṟum varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷ ḷārkaḷ. ākavē, ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭukkumuṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭakku elli vēṅkaṭamāka viruntiruntatu eṉat tuṇital kūṭum. kaliṅka iḷavaracaṉākiya vicayaṉ kālam mutal (ki. mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) tōṉṟi vaḷara ārampitta ciṅkaḷa moḻiyil ilakkiyam mutaṉ mutal ki. pi. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻuntatu. āntira moḻiyilum ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ki. pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal eḻa ārampittaṉa. ākavē iru moḻikaḷiṉ kālamum oṉṟu eṉa (ki. mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) uttēcamākak kūṟalām. ak kālat tilō ataṉ ciṟitu pintiyō tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkelli vēṅkaṭamāyiṟṟu.
“parantupaṭṭa poruṇmiyavākiya māpurāṇam, pūtapurāṇa meṉpaṉa, cil vāḻnāṭ ciṟṟaṟiviṉ mākkaṭku upakārappaṭāmiyiṉ tokuttuc ceyyappaṭṭa vaḻakku nūlākiya tolkāppiyam iṭic caṅkam mutalāka iṉṟukāṟum uḷatāyiṟṟeṉka. kaṭiccaṅkattāruṭ kaḷaviyaṟ poruḷ kaṇṭa kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉār iṭiccaṅkattārkkum kaṭiccaṅkattārkkum nūlāyiṟṟut tolkāppiyam eṉṟārākalāṉum (pērāciriyar uri. mara. 649).
“tolkāp piyampulavar tōṉṟa viritturittār
palkāya ṉārvakuttup paṉṉiṉār-nalyāppuk
kaṟṟār matikkuṅ kalikkākki pāṭiṉiyār
coṟṟārtan nūliṟ ṟokuttu.”
eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟāl tolkāppiyar mutal piṉvanta āciriyarkaḷ ellām muṉ virintu kiṭanta nūlkaḷic curukki nūlkaḷ ceytārkaḷ eṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyiram
tolkāppiyattukkup paṉampāraṉār ceyta ciṟappuppāyira moṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ip pāyiram ārāyccikkuriyatu. tolkāp piyattil nūlkaḷukkup pāyiram ceytaṟku vitikāṇappaṭavilli. caṅka nūlkaḷukkum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyalukkum pāyiram illāmi karut tiṟkoḷḷattakkatu. cilappatikāram maṇimēkali mutaliya nūlkaḷukkup poruḷaṭakkamē pāyiramāka eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vāṉmīkar irāmāyaṇat til kāṇappaṭum pāyiramum iv vakiyiṉatē. itaṉāl cilappatikāra kālattum piṟkālattum vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷiṉ muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟip pāyirañ ceyyappaṭalāyiṟṟu eṉak karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
pāyiram nūlukku iṉṟiyamiyātateṉa iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uri mutal mutal valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu. iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluri nakkīraṉārāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu nīṇṭakālam cevi vaḻakkil vantu ki. pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil eḻuti vikkappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl ki. pi. 7ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭil allatu ataṟkuc ciṟitumuṉ nūlukkup pāyiram iṉṟiyami yātatu eṉṉum koḷki valuvaṭintiruttal vēṇṭum.1
tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyiram, maṇimēkali, cilappatikāram mutaliya nūlkaḷukkuḷḷa pāyiram pōlallātu, nūl, nuvalvōṉ, nuvalun tiṟaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟik kūṟuvaṉavāyumuḷḷatu. ivikaḷi ellām oruṅkuvittu ārāyumiṭattut tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyiram, ki.pi. 5ām allatu 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil irunta paṉampāraṉār eṉṉum pulavar oruvarāl, ak kālam tolkāppiyattip paṟṟi vaḻaṅkiya kaṉṉa parampari varalāṟukaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum tiraṭṭic ceyyappaṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu. pāyirattil tolkāppiyar akattiyariṉ māṇavareṉak kūṟappaṭāmi nōkkattakkatu.
ilakkaṇak kuṟippukaḷ cila
tolkāppiyar viyaṅkōḷ viṉi, taṉmi muṉṉili eṉṉum īriṭattum varāteṉak kūṟuvar. “avaṟṟuḷ-muṉṉili taṉmi yāyī riṭattōṭu, maṉṉātākum viyaṅkōṭ kiḷavi” (tol.col.viṉi.) puṟa nāṉūṟṟiṉ iraṇṭām pāṭṭic ceyta murañciyūr muṭinākarāyar, “vāṉa varampaṉi nīyō peruma” “naṭukkiṉṟi niliyarō” eṉa muṉṉili yiṭattu viyaṅkōḷ viṉiyip payaṉpaṭutti yirukkiṉṟār. itaṉāl murañciyūr muṭinākarāyar tolkāppiyar kālattukkup piṟpaṭṭavareṉa uyttaṟiyalām. tolkāppiyar kālattil viyaṅkōḷviṉi paṭarkki iṭattumaṭṭum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattil atu muṉṉiliyiṭat tum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. naṉṉūlār ataṉi mūviṭattum payaṉpaṭuttiṉār. “kayavoṭu ravvoṟ ṟīṟṟa viyaṅkōḷ, iyalu miṭampā leṅku meṉpa” (naṉ-338). ic cūttirattiṉāl naṉṉūlārukku muṉṉum viyaṅkōḷ taṉmiyiṭattum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉat terikiṉṟatu.
eṭṭut toki pattuppāṭṭu mutaliya nūlkaḷil uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ kaḷ vikuti ēṟkavilli. maṇimēkali cilappatikāram mutaliya nūlkaḷil uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ kaḷ vikuti ēṟkum vaḻakkuk kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. ‘yāṅkaḷum nīṇeṟippaṭarutum’ (cilap.) eṉavum, “nōṉpikaḷ viḻumaṅ koḷḷavum” (maṇi.) eṉavum varutal kāṇka. piṟkālaṅkaḷil kaḷ vikuti uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷuṭaṉum irutiṇi viṉi muṟṟukkaḷuṭaṉum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. tolkāppiyar uyartiṇi eṉṟu koṇṭa taṉmip peyarkaḷ naṉṉūlār kālam mutal viravutiṇi (uyartiṇiyum aḵṟiṇiyum) eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. “taṉmi nāṉkum muṉṉili yintum........potuppeyar” (naṉ. 282)
ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu nikaḻkālattukku māttiram uriyateṉat tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. “pallōr paṭarkki muṉṉili taṉmi, avvayiṉ mūṉṟum nikaḻum kālattu, ceyyumeṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyoṭu koḷḷā” (tol. col. viṉi.) naṉṉūlāriṉpaṭi atu varuṅkālat tukku muriyatākum. “ceyyu nikaḻ petirvum” (naṉ. 145)
naṉṉūlār nikaḻkāla viṉimuṟṟu ceykiṟēṉ, ceykiṉṟēṉ, ceyyāniṉṟēṉ eṉa varum eṉak koḷvar. tēvārattukkum nālāyirap pirapantattukkum muṉ mēṟkuṟittavikaḷil oṉṟēṉum āḷappaṭa villi. vīracōḻiya āciriyar ceyyāniṉṟēṉ eṉpatic ceyyā-niṉ ṟēṉ eṉa iru coṟkaḷākap pirippar. āṉāl, naṉṉūlār irucoṟkaḷi yum oṉṟākakkoṇṭu āniṉṟu nikaḻkālattiṉi uṇarttumeṉṟār.
iv vārāycciyiṉāl tolkāppiyam ellāt tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷukkum pārkka muṟpaṭṭateṉpatum, atu cilappatikāra kālattukkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ maṟpaṭṭateṉpatum naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
urinaṭi varalāṟu
moḻi, vaḻakku ceyyuḷ, eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. makkaḷ oruvarōṭu oruvar uriyāṭuṅkāl āḷum naṭi vaḻakkeṉappaṭum. vaḻakkeṉiṉum, vacaṉa naṭi urinaṭi eṉiṉum okkum.
uḷḷatteḻum uṇarccikaḷukkēṟpa makkaḷāṟṟōṟṟuvikkappaṭum ōcikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyē pāṭaliṉ toṭakkamākum. av vōci, aḷantu ceyyappaṭṭamiyiṉ ceyyuḷ eṉappaṭṭatu. ceyyuḷ eṉpataṟkuc cey yappaṭṭa teṉpatu poruḷ; tūkku eṉpatu ceyyuḷukku maṟṟoru peyar. tūkku eṉpatu aḷavu eṉṉum poruṭṭu. ākavē, tūkku, ceyyuḷ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ orē poruḷaṉavātal kāṇka. pā eṉpatum ceyyuḷi uṇarttum. ōci toṭarpākac ceyyuṭkup ‘pā’ eṉṟu peyar iṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
“pā eṉpatu cēṭpulattirunta kālattum oruvaṉ eḻuttuñ collum teriyāmaṟ pāṭa mōtuṅkāl, avaṉ collukiṉṟa ceyyuḷi vikaṟpittu iṉṉa ceyyuḷeṉṟu uṇartaṟ kētuvākiya parantupaṭṭuc celvatōrōci” eṉap pērāciriyar pāveṉpataṟkup poruḷ virit turittār.
muṟkāla makkaḷ uriyāṭumpōtum eḻutum pōtum āṇṭa naṭikaḷ vevvēṟu vakiyiṉa. pēccunaṭi eppoḻutum vacaṉa naṭi yāka amivuṟṟatu. eḻutuṅkāl eppoḻutum ceyyuḷ naṭiyē ki yāḷappaṭṭatu. itaṟkuk kāraṇam pala ākalām. ceyyuḷ naṭimikac curukkamāṉatu; vacaṉa naṭiyil atikam kūṟavēṇṭiyavaṟṟic ceyyuḷ muṟiyiṟ cila varikaḷiṟ kūṟiviṭalām. ōliyil eḻuttāṇiyāl eḻutavēṇṭiyiruntakālattil iccurukku muṟiyi makkaḷ virumputal iyalpākum. accuccātaṉamillāta muṟkālattil nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭaṟkum, paṭieṭuttaṟkum ceyyuḷ muṟi vāyppaḷittatu.
ik kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉālēyē muṟkāla nūlkaḷ ellām ceyyuḷ vaṭiviṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kālattukkukkālam makkaḷ āḷum coṟkaḷ māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru kālattiṟ peru vaḻakkilirunta coṟkaḷ maṟintupōkap putiya coṟkaḷ vaḻakkil varukiṉṟaṉa. putiya coṟkaḷ vaḻakkil vantakālattil eḻutap paṭum nūlkaḷil paḻiya coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻakkup perumpālum niṉṟuviṭu kiṉṟatu. piṟkālattavarkaḷukku muṟkālattil eḻutappaṭṭa nūlkaḷip payiṉṟu poruḷ viḷaṅkuvatu kaṭumiyākiṉṟatu. ik kāraṇattāl muṟkāla nūlkaḷukku vaḻakku (vacaṉa) muṟiyil poruḷ eḻutappaṭu kiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu eḻutappaṭum poruṭpakutikaḷ uri eṉa vaḻaṅkum. uri eḻutum im muṟiyiliruntē piṟkālattil vacaṉa nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭalāyiṉa.
urinaṭikku aṭivariyaṟi mutaliyaṉa illi. muṟkāla uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ urinaṭikaḷiṉ pala pakutikaḷ ceyyuṭkaḷ pōlavē celkiṉṟaṉa. ak kāla makkaḷiṉ vaḻakku eṉappaṭṭa pēccu naṭi, avvāṟu iruntateṉak kūṟutal amiyātu. av vuriyip pārttu ak kāla urinaṭi ev vakiyiṉateṉa oruvāṟu cintaṉi ceytu aṟintukoḷḷalām.
tolkāppiyam eṉṉum paḻiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūl ki. mu. 350-variyiṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. an nūl uri nūlkaḷip paṟṟic ciṟitu kūṟukiṉṟatu (cū. 485.) avi pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē urinaṭiyi ṉiyaṉṟa pakutikaḷ vittuc ceyyappaṭuvaṉa; cūttirattukkup poruḷ eḻutuvaṉa pōlvaṉa; oḻintupōṉa pāṭalkaḷukkup poruḷ eḻututal pōlvaṉa; cevi vaḻakkilvarum kaṟpaṉik katikaḷ pōlvaṉa; poy eṉappaṭātu ulakiyalōṭiyaṉṟu naki viḷikkakkūṭiya katikaḷ pōlvaṉa.
iv vakiya nūlkaḷ oṉṟēṉum iṉṟu kāṇappaṭavilli. paṟpala kāraṇac ceṟivāl māṇṭa tamiḻ nūlkaḷōṭu aviyuṅkūṭa maṭintaṉa vākalām.
ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eṉṉum aracap pulavar pāṭiya cilappatikārattil urip pāṭṭukkaḷ cila kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. avi aṭivariyaṟiyiṉṟip pāṭṭunaṭiyil amintuḷḷaṉa. avi perumpālum āciriyatti ottuḷḷaṉa. ki.pi. 1ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭaḷavil ceyyappaṭṭa iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uri ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭaḷavil eḻuti vikkappaṭṭatu. av vurinaṭiyiṉ cila pakutikaḷ ceyyuḷnaṭi pōṟṟōṉṟiṉum perumpakuti ciṟuciṟu vacaṉaṅkaḷāka amintuḷḷatu. peruntēvaṉār pāratattiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē, maṇippira vāḷa naṭiyil eḻutappaṭṭa vacaṉa naṭi itaṉpiṉ eḻuntatu. ivvacaṉa naṭi, pārata veṇpāp pāṭiya peruntēvaṉār (ki. pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) ceytatākātu. ki. pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkuppiṉ eḻunta urinaṭikku eṭuttukkāṭṭu aṭiyārkku nallār, pērāciriyar, iḷampūraṇar, nacci ṉārkkiṉiyar, parimēlaḻakar mutalāṉōr urikaḷākum. piṟkāla uri naṭikku eṭuttukkāṭṭu mātavac civañāṉa yōkikaḷiṉ civañāṉapōta māpāṭiyamākum.
tamiḻ uṇarccikku etir uṇarcci
vētakālattil tācukkaḷukkum āriyar kaḷukkum mikunta vayiram uṇṭāyiruntatu. ataṉit toṭarntu tamiḻi āriyattilum tāḻttik kūṟum kaṭciyoṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭil nīṇṭanāḷ toṭarntu varāniṉṟatu. camaya kuravar kālattum iv vaki uṇarcciyirun tamiyiṉālēyē “āriyaṉ kaṇṭāy tamiḻaṉ kaṇṭāy” eṉat tēvārattuṭ kūṟappaṭalāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟu kūṟap paṭṭatu iru kaṭcikaḷiyum cantu ceytal poruṭṭēyākum.
tamiḻi iḻivupaṭuttum koṭumi nāḷukkunāḷ vaḷarvatāyiṟṟu. atiṟpaṭṭu mayaṅkiya makkaḷ tamiḻiṉ uyarviyum ataṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyiyum uṇarātu ataṉi ikaḻvārāyiṉar. kalviyiṟ periya kampaṉē ‘tēvapāṭiyi ṉikkati ceytavar’ eṉa moḻintu vaṭa moḻikkup paṇivu kāṭṭukiṉṟami kāṇka. parañcōti muṉivar, civañāṉa muṉivar kumarakuruparar mutalāyiṉōr am makkaḷiṉ pēraṟiyāmik kiraṅki avarkaḷ maṉaṅkoṇṭu tamiḻiṟ paṟṟaṭiyumāṟu tamiḻiṉ peru miyip pala ariya iṉiya teḷḷiya pāṭalkaḷāl viḷakkip pōntaṉar. pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷi avarkaḷ maṉōṉmaṇīyattiṟ kūṟiya tamiḻt teyva vaṇakkap pāṭalkaḷāl ak kālattut tamiḻukku etirāṉa uṇarcci evvaḷavu muṉittu niṉṟateṉpatu teḷḷitil viḷaṅkum. iṉṟum av vuṇarcci cāmpar pūtta neruppup pōla, palar uḷḷattē karantu kiṭavāmal illi. tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāra uriyil (poruḷ. 490) āriya naṉṟu tamiḻ tī teṉavuritta, kāriyattāṟ kālakkōṭ paṭṭāṉic-cīriya, antaṇ potiyil akattiyaṉā rāṇiyāl, centamiḻē tīrkka cuvā kā” eṉak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa utāraṇac ceyyuḷālum iv vaki uṇarcci tamiḻ nāṭṭil nīṇṭakālam uḷḷateṉpati naṉku aṟika.

taṇṭa miḻccolō maṟupulac coṟkaḷō cāṟṟīr.

tiṭamu ṟuttiyam moḻikketi rākkiya teṉcol
maṭama kaṭkaraṅ keṉpatu vaḻutinā ṭaṉṟō.

1muṉṉirunta pāli moḻiyuṅkīr vāṇamum
tuṉṉuṅ karuppiyilē tōyvataṟku-muṉṉarē
paṇṭikkā lattē paravikoṇṭa muṉṉūḻi
maṇṭalatti lēpēr vaḷanāṭṭiṉ-maṇṭunīrp

pēraviyi lēnūṟ perumakkaḷ cūḻntēttap

paḻamaṟikaṇ muṟiyiṭap pintamiḻp piṉceṉṟa
paccip pacuṅkoṇṭalē. -mī. piḷḷittamiḻ.
caṅkakālattut tamiḻakam
kiruṣṇā natikkukkīḻ uḷḷa nāṭukaḷ mūṉṟu irācciyaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mūvēntaruṭiyavum kuṟunilamaṉṉar eḻuvaruṭiyavum irācciyaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkum. ivarkaḷi aṉṟic ciṟiya celvākkuṭiya talivarkaḷ palarum viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. kaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷ mūvēntarukkuriyavāyiruntaṉa. naṭuppakutikaḷum malikaḷum kuṟunila maṉṉar vacam iruntaṉa. kiḻakkuk kariyil kiruṣṇānatimutal teṟkil irāmanātapurap pakutiyiluḷḷa toṇṭivariyil cōḻa irācciyam iruntatu. iv virācciyattiṉ naṭuvē kāñcikku vaṭakkiluḷḷa tirukkōvalūric cūḻnta malināṭu maliyamāṉ eṉṉum ciṟṟaracaṉiṉ kīḻ iruntatu. cōḻa nāṭṭukkut teṟkil pāṇṭiya irācciyam iruntatu. itu kiḻakku kaṭaṟkariyiliruntu mēṟkuk kaṭaṟkari variyum akaṉ ṟiruntatu. maturi, tirunelvēli, tiruvitāṅkūr, kōyamuttūriṉ pakuti, kocci eṉpaṉa itil aṭaṅkum. iv virācciyattukkuṭpaṭṭirunta potiya maliyi āyum tirunelvēlip pakutiyiluḷḷa koṟkit tuṟi mukattic cūḻnta iṭaṅkaḷi evviyum āṇṭaṉar. paḻaṉimaliyiyum aticcūḻnta nāṭukaḷiyum pēkaṉ āṇṭāṉ. ivaṉuṭiya nāṭu kaḷukku vaṭakkē mēṟkut toṭarccimali ōramākac cēranāṭu iruntatu. itu pālikkāṭṭu veḷi (Palghat gap) vaḻiyākak kōyamut tūrvariyuñ ceṉṟatu. itaṟkuc carinērē (Parallel) teṉ micūril pala ciṟṟaracarkaḷiṉ nāṭukaḷ iruntaṉa. avarkaḷil arayam nāṭṭi iruṅkōvēḷum, paṟampu maliyip pāriyum, takaṭūri (tarumapuri) atiyamāṉum, kolli maliyi ōriyum āṇṭaṉar. iruṅkōvēḷiṉ nāṭṭukku vaṭakkē kaṅkar nāṭukaḷum, teṟkil koṅku nāṭum iruntaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkē (mēṟkuk kariyil) uḷḷa tuḷunāṭu naṉṉaṉ āṇikkuṭ paṭṭiruntatu; kiḻakkil pulliyiṉ nāṭum, vaṭakkil vaṭukar nāṭum iruntaṉa.
muṇṭar
intiyāvilē cūṭiyanākapuriyil muṇṭar (Mundar) eṉṉum oru makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉar vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ pēcum moḻi tamiḻukku uṟavuṭiyatu. kulamuṟiyil avarkaḷ tirāviṭak kūṭṭattic cērntōr. varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ cilar, avarkaḷē āriyarukkum tamiḻa rukkum muṟpaṭṭa intiya makkaḷ eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. uṇmiyil avarkaḷ tamiḻk kūṭṭattiṉariṉ oru piriviṉarē yeṉṟum, avarkaḷ nīṇṭa kālam tamiḻpperuṅkūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum pirintu taṉittu vāḻn tamiyiṉ avarkaḷ moḻi paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ ākiyaṉa māṟupaṭātu vantuḷḷaṉaveṉṟum, avarkaḷiṉ moḻi mikap paḻiya tamiḻākumeṉ ṟum, iṟṟi ñāṉṟi ārāycci vallunar pukalvar.1
2“muṇṭā moḻi (kol) moṉ kimer (Mon Khmer) eṉa vaḻaṅkum. iḵtu intu cīṉamoḻik kūṭṭattic cārntatu. itu makātōk kuṉṟukaḷi lum (Mahado Hills) imayamaliyilum vāḻum makkaḷāṟ pēcappaṭukiṉṟatu. cūṭiya nākapuriyilēyē itu peritum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. im moḻiyip pēcuvōriṉ eṇ 300,000. im moḻikkuriya makkaḷ aṅkum iṅkum citaṟik kāṇappaṭutaliṉ avarkaḷ muṟkālattil mikap parantu vāḻntār kaḷeṉa uyttuṇaralām. cimiṭ eṉṉum pātiriyār (Fr. Schimidt) avarkaḷi āstirēliya toṭarpuṭiya moḻikaḷi vaḻaṅkum makkaḷōṭu iṇittu, ivarkaḷ āciya makkaḷiyum āstirēliya makkaḷiyum iṇikkum caṅkilipōṉṟavarkaḷ eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār. uṇmiyil avarkaḷ avvāṟē uḷar. piṟisluski eṉpār. (J. Przyluski) muṇṭā moḻi toṉmiyuḷḷateṉṟum, paḻiya moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil aḵtu uḷḷateṉṟum, aḵtu iṉṟum malāya āciyāviṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟa teṉṟum kūṟuvar.
muṇṭā moḻikaḷi vaḻaṅkuvōr potuniṟamuṭiya kolliyar (Kols); ivarkaḷ neḷinta (curuṇṭatallāta) talimayiruṭiyar; paḻiya parmiya acām intu cīṉa āstirēliya makkaḷukkum ilaṅki vēṭaruk kum iṉamuṭiyōr. cantāttīviṉ pantiṉ (Bantin) eṉṉum cātiyiṉarum celipicimakkaḷum iv viṉattic cārntōr eṉa vekspoṉṭ (Wxbonds) eṉpār kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. im makkaḷ iruṇṭa niṟamuṭiya tirāviṭa veḷḷattiṟ pukuntu maṟintaṉar. tirāviṭariṉ uṟpattiyi aṟiya muṭiyāmiyiṉāl, ārāycciyāḷar avarkaḷi āstirēliyar, eturuskāṉiyar (paḻiya ittāliya makkaḷ) piṉṉiya ukkiriyar (Finno-ugrians)kaḷōṭu iṇittaṉar. tirāviṭameṉpatu tamiḻ eṉpataṉ uccarippu vēṟupāṭu.”
cūṭiyanākapuriyil muṇṭā moḻiyi vaḻaṅkum ōriyar eṉṉum makkaḷukku kuraṅkukkoṭiyum, atē kulakkuṟiyum (Totem) uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum, avipaṟṟi ivarkaḷ kuraṅkar allatu kuraṅkukaḷ eṉa irāmā yaṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟum ‘muṇṭarum avar nāṭum’1 eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

The name Sin was given first to the land of the Sumerians, the Euphratian Delta. This is called Shinar in the Bible, Singara by Greeks, Sindjar by the Arabian Geographers and was that ruled by the three eyed and two horned wild pull God. This was the three eyed Shiva of the Hindus, whose wife was the weaving Uma, the flax (Uma) Goddess, the Goddess mother of the flax weavers of Asia Minor who become the Egyptian Goddess Neith - Ibid - P. 250.

1. God Varuna was himself an Asura. Mr. Banerji believes that the Asuras were different from Dasus but identical with Dravidians. The Dasus he points out were black, while Asuras were brown or rather golden. This synthesis he points out in the adoption of the Cult of Siva which was the Asura Cult; as a part of Vedic System of worship Varuna and other Asura gods were incorporated into the Vedic Pantheon. The gods of the Asuras and the gods of the Trtsus become one. A social amalgamation came into existence with fruitful results. The Asuras and the Devas were made sons of the same Father God. Many Vedic Kings and Rishis came to have Asura Blood in them as is indicated by the colour. Sages like Vasishta, Agastya and Visvamitra were given the same Father Mitra-varuna. Asura in Aryan means Supreme God - Pre-musalman India....P.P. 172, 174,

1. The Dravidian dialects affected profoundly by sounds the structure, the idiom and the vocobulary of the former in the course of its growth in India on account of the constant influence of the Dravidian tongue. This language lost the subjunctive mood, many infinitive forms and several noun declentions, forgot its richly varied system of real verb tenses and adopted terms of expressions peculiar to the Dravidian idiom...The Dravidian names of things and operations connected with these arts of peace are native and not foreign (i.e. borrowed from Sanskrit) the question has not yet been investigated but on enquiry it will most probably turn out that many Sanskrit words connected with these were borrowed from the Dravidian. -The age of the mantras-P. T. S. Ayengar.

1. ak kāla vīrar payaṉpaṭuttiya paṭikkalaṅkaḷuḷ vaḷitaṭi oṉṟu. itu āstirēliya pūrvakuṭikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttum pūmaṟāṅ eṉṉum taṭiyē eṉa ārāycciyāl terikiṉṟatu. ataṉi ōr ilakki nōkki eṟiyiṉ aḵtu ilakkiṟpaṭṭu mīṇṭum tirumpi varum. ṭākṭar oppēṭ eṉpār itikkuṟittu eḻutiyiruppatu piṉvarumāṟu:
Dr. Oppert says, “this general belief that the bumerang is a weapon peculiar to Australians, but this is by no means the case. It is well known in many parts of India specially in its Southern peninsula. The Tamilian Maravar and Kallar employ it when hunting and throw it after deer. In the Madras Government Museum are shown three boomerangs two ivory ones which came from the armoury of the late Rajah of Tanjore, and a common one which came from Pudukota. The wood of which the bumerang is made is very dark. I possess a fair black wooden and one iron bumerang, which I received from Pudukota. In the arsenal of the Pudukota Rajah is always kept a stock of these sticks. Their name in Tamil is Vali Tadi, ‘bent stick’ as the stick is bent and flat. When thrown a whirling motion is imparted to the weapon which cuses it to return to the place it was thrown. The natives are well acquainted with the peculiar fact - Indo - Aryans - Vol. 2-R. Mitra.

1. There appear to have been a general permit for a learned Brahman to speak his mind in any durbar; and these Brahmans often gave out their opinions most fearlessly. This privilege was similarly accorded also to men of learning - Beginnings of South Indian History, P. 139 - S.K. Aiyengar.
2. First thing in the morning, fire must be lit, a lamp carried round to the master, as they usually rose before the sunrise, then some incense burnt to give divine nourishment of perfume, incense being literally, ‘divine food’ like cooking the morning meal. The master’s door was then opened, obeisance was made to him, with protestations of fidelity, he was anointed, and the divine food of incense put before him. The servant then returned with assurances that he was ready to his master. After the breakfast the servant comes forward agin, goes through the same obeisance and calls on him to wake up in peace, declaring that his word is law, and that he will destroy his enemies. Then the master is washed
perfumed, and dressed in various cloths, anointed and decorated. The ground is sanded for him to walk on when he comes forth. Several offerings of incense and another washing represent the meals and cleaning during the day and the service is over. - Flinders Petrie.

2. After the study of the above one-thousand and eight hundred inscriptions which up to now have been deciphered by the present writer, it is easy to realize that the wave of migrations of the Mediterranean race which as supposed to have been from West to East must now be finally settled as having taken place in the opposite direction, i.e., East to West.
The development of Mohenjo-Daro script, the religion of these two countries and that of Egypt, the titles of kings, the number of Zodiacal constellations
among the Proto Indian people and the relative position of these constellations, the changing of the Proto Indian constellation of the harp (Yal) for Turus (the bull) which might have taken place in Sumer, the tradition of ancient people of Mesopotamia recorded by Berosus, the parallel by Bibilical account in Gen. ii, 1.5 all point to the same conclusion, that the migration of the Mediterranean race commenced from India and extended through South Mesopotamia and Northern Africa; spread through Crete, Cyprus, Greece, Italy and Spin and across the Pyrenees reached Central Europe and the British Isles. This route starting from Ceylon to Ireland is marked by uninterrupted chin of Dolmens and other megaliths that seem to be the relics of the enterprising and highly civilized race which is termed the Mediterranean by the anthropologists and which in India has been quite unreasonably despised under the name Dravidian. - Journal of Indian History, Vol. XVI - Part 1- Fr. Heras.

1. kavu, tukim, oṟicā, iñciper, kapiṟoṉ, ipim, pippili, cintu.
2. There are in all Indo-aryan languages, a considerable number of words which cannot apparently be identified in other; Indo-European languages. This is specially the case in modern vernaculars and the old opinion was that such words had, generally speaking been borrowed from the language of the tribes which inhabited India before the Aryan invation. The steady progress of philological studies in later years has enabled us to trace an overincreasing portion of
such words to Sanskrit, and many scholars now hold that there have hardly been any loan at all. It has however been overlooked that it is sufficient to show that a word is found in Sanskrit or in vedic dialects, in order to prove that it belonged to the original language of the Aryan. The foreign element must reach into the oldest times, and it would be necessary to trace dubeous words not only in Sanskrit, but also in other languages of the Indo-European family. That is exactly what philology has, in many Cases failed to do. There are i.g. a number of verbal roots in Sanskrit which do not occur in other Indo-European forms of speech. The same is the case with a considerable portion of vocabulary. There is how-ever every possibility for the supposition that at least a considerable portion of such words and bases has been borrowed from the Dravidian - Linguistic Survey of India - Vol. IV. pp. 278-9 Grierson.

P.T.S. Aiyengar.
We might see that it would be more correct to describe Indian religion as Dravidian religion stimulated and modified by the ideas of Aryan invaders. For the great deities of Hinduism Siva, Krishna, Rama, Durga and some of its most essential doctrines such a metempsychosis and divine incarnation are either totally unknown to the Veda or obscurely adumbrated in it. The chief characteristics of mature Indian religion are characteristics of an area not of a race, and they are not the characteristics of religion of Persia, Greece or other Aryan lands - Hinduism and Buddhism - P. XV-Sir Charles Elliot.

3. The Dravidian names of things and operations connected with all these arts of peace are native and not foreign (i.e., borrowed from Sanskrit). The question has not yet been investigated, but on enquiry it will most probably turn out
that many Sanskrit words connected with these arts were borrowed from the Dravidian - Life in Ancient India - P.T.S. Iyengar.
The language of the Rig Veda is as yet purely Aryan or Indo-European in its form, structure and spirit, but its phonetics is already affected by the Dravidian and it has already begun to borrow words from Dravidian and Kol. Among words of probable Dravida origin in the Rig Veda, the following may be noted-anu - particle; arani - rubbing wood for fire; Kapi - monkey; Karmara - smith; Kala-time, Kunda-Hole; Gana-Band; nana-several; nila-blue; Puspa - flower; Pujana-worship; Phala-fruit; Bija-seed; Mayura-peafowl; ratri-night; rupa-form-Ibid P. 42.

Their method is to go in fleets of twenty or thirty of these pirate vessels together, and then they form what they call a sea cordon, that is that they drop off till there is an interval of five to six miles between ship and ship, so that they cover something like a hundred miles of sea and no merchant ship can escape them. For when any one corsir sights a vessel a signal is made by fire or smoke, and then the whole of them make for this and scize merchants and plunder them.....But now the merchants are aware of this and go so well manned and armed, and with such great ships and they do not fear the corsirs. 1. Dr. Grierson has recently proved that these languages belong to the same family. The Munda language of the Bengal hills is of a more archic type than those of Madras and the linguistic evidence leads to the conclusion that both races came from the south and that they must have remined apart for a period sufficiently long to admit of the development of those peculiarities which differenciate the languages so called Kolarian or Munda Group from those of the other Dravidians. - Natives of Northern India. - P. 29-W. Crooke B.A.
The proto-Austroloids are responsible for the introduction of neolithic culture and pottery in India but their linguistic legacy is more enduring and important. They are known as the speakers of Austro-Asiatic languages distributed over the widest area from the Punjab to New Zealand and from Madagascar to Easter Island. The Indian variety of the languages is known as the Munda, which accordingly is to be considered the earliest language spoken in India. A consideration of the Munda linguistic areas in India thrown light on the course of proto-Austroloid migrations. These may have been from East to West or from West to East. Munda survives now on the inner Himalayan ranges between Ladukh Sikkim in the west of the Central Provinces and southwards among the Ganjam and Vizagapatam hills but not beyond the Godawari. Munda shows affinity not merely with the languages of south east Asia and the Pacific but also with the agglutinative Sumerian languages. This is considered that the various branches of the Austro-Asiatic family of languages originated at some common centre in central or south East Asia from which it spread in a more or less southerly direction.
The Munda speaking peoples are called by the General names, Mundas, Kolarians, Kols and number of six million comprising Santals, Bhils, Kurumbas, Mundas, Savaras and other minor tribes like the Kuravas of Sirguja and Mirzapur. - Hindu civilization P. 34 - R.K. Mookerjee.

putta kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa yāḻ
(vaṭa intiyāvil vaḻaṅkiyatu; cātakak katikaḷil kūṟappaṭuvatu ivvaki yāḻē.)



ekiptil vaḻaṅkiya yāḻ
(eṉcikiḷōpīṭiyā pirittāṉikkāviṟ kāṇappaṭṭatu)
narampi vil nāṇ pōṉṟu vaḷinta taṭiyil pūṭṭi olikkum muṟiyiliruntu yāḻ uṇṭāyiṟṟu. paḻiya cumēriyā, ayarlāntu, kirīs, urōm, ekiptu, tamiḻakam mutaliya nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiya yāḻkaḷi oppiṭṭu nōkkumiṭattu orē aṭippaṭik karuttiṉiṉṟē yāḻ ceyyum muṟi eḻuntateṉṟu naṉku viḷaṅkum. ici nūl valla em. āpirakām paṇṭitaravarkaḷ, iciyum icik karuvikaḷum tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟē mēṟkut tēcaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟaṉaveṉṟu ārāyntu viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
